## I Disaster / I 灾祸

===Original===
The Throg task force struck the Terran Survey camp a few minutes after dawn, without warning, and with a deadly precision which argued that the aliens had fully reconnoitered and prepared that attack. Eye-searing lances of energy lashed back and forth across the base with methodical accuracy. And a single cowering witness, flattened on a ledge in the heights above, knew that when the last of those yellow-red bolts fell, nothing human would be left alive down there. His teeth closed hard upon the thick stuff of the sleeve covering his thin forearm, and in his throat a scream of terror and rage was stillborn.

More than caution kept him pinned on that narrow shelf of rock. Watching that holocaust below, Shann Lantee could not force himself to move. The sheer ruthlessness of the Throg move-in left him momentarily weak. To listen to a tale of Throgs in action, and to be an eyewitness to such action, were two vastly different things. He shivered in spite of the warmth of the Survey Corps uniform.

===Chinese===
黎明过后不过几分钟，斯罗格人（Throg）的特遣队便向地球勘测队（Terran Survey）的营地发起了打击——事先毫无征兆，而且精准得可怕，这说明外星人早已把这里侦察得通透，进攻也筹划周全。灼人眼目的能量长矛往复横扫，按部就班、分毫不差地掠过整座基地。高地上，一个紧贴岩棚、缩成一团的目击者心里明白：等那些黄红色的能量束落下最后一击，下面便再不会留下一个活人。他一口咬住罩着细瘦小臂的厚厚袖料，喉咙里那声恐惧与狂怒交织的尖叫，还没出口便死在了喉头。

把他按在那条狭窄岩架上动弹不得的，绝不止是谨慎二字。沙恩·兰蒂（Shann Lantee）望着脚下这场浩劫，怎么也逼不动自己的身体。斯罗格人这手突袭的狠绝，让他一时间浑身发软。听人讲斯罗格人如何动手是一回事，亲眼目睹这场面又是另一回事，两者天差地别。尽管勘测队的制服颇为暖和，他还是止不住打颤。

===Original===
As yet he had sighted none of the aliens, only their plate-shaped flyers. They would stay aloft until their long-range weapon cleared out all opposition. But how had they been able to make such a complete annihilation of the Terran force? The last report had placed the nearest Throg nest at least two systems away from Warlock. And a patrol lane had been drawn about the Circe system the minute that Survey had marked its second planet ready for colonization. Somehow the beetles had slipped through that supposedly tight cordon and would now consolidate their gains with their usual speed at rooting. First an energy attack to finish the small Terran force; then they would simply take over.

A month later, or maybe two months, and they could not have done it. The grids would have been up, and any Throg ship venturing into Warlock’s amber-tinted sky would abruptly cease to be. In the race for survival as a galactic power, Terra had that one small edge over the swarms of the enemy. They need only stake out their newfound world and get the grids assembled on its surface; then that planet would be locked to the beetles. The critical period was between the first discovery of a suitable colony world and the erection of grid control. Planets in the past had been lost during that time lag, just as Warlock was lost now.

===Chinese===
直到此刻，他连一个外星人的影子都没见到，只看见他们那些碟形飞行器。它们会一直悬在半空，等远程武器把所有抵抗清除干净。可他们怎么能把地球人的这支队伍消灭得如此干净利落？上一份报告明明写着，最近的斯罗格巢穴离沃洛克（Warlock）至少还隔着两个星系。而且勘测队一宣布喀耳刻星系（Circe system）的第二行星可以殖民，巡逻航线便立刻圈住了整个星系。可这些甲虫人（beetles）不知怎么钻过了那道号称密不透风的封锁线，如今正要以他们一贯的扎根速度，把到手的猎物牢牢巩固。先是一场能量攻击了结这支小小的地球部队，然后，他们只需接管一切。

倘若晚上一个月，或者两个月，他们就绝不可能得手。到那时网格（grid）早已竖起，任何斯罗格飞船只要胆敢闯进沃洛克琥珀色的天空，都会倏然灰飞烟灭。在这场争夺银河强权的生存竞赛里，地球（Terra）对虫群般的敌人就握着这一点小小的优势：只需圈定新发现的世界，把网格在其表面装配起来，这颗行星便对甲虫人彻底锁死。最凶险的时段，是从发现一颗合适的殖民行星到网格防线建起之间的空当。以往就有行星在这个空当里沦陷，正如沃洛克此刻的沦陷。

===Original===
Throgs and Terrans﻿ ﻿… For more than a century now, planet time, they had been fighting their queer, twisted war among the stars. Terrans hunted worlds for colonization, the old hunger for land of their own driving men from the overpopulated worlds, out of Sol’s system to the far stars. And those worlds barren of intelligent native life, open to settlers, were none too many and widely scattered. Perhaps half a dozen were found in a quarter century, and of that six maybe only one was suitable for human life without any costly and lengthy adaption of man or world. Warlock was one of the lucky finds which came so seldom.

Throgs were predators, living on the loot they garnered. As yet, mankind had not been able to discover whether they did indeed swarm from any home world. Perhaps they lived eternally on board their plate ships with no permanent base, forced into a wandering life by the destruction of the planet on which they had originally been spawned. But they were raiders now, laying waste defenseless worlds, picking up the wealth of shattered cities in which no native life remained. And their hidden temporary bases were looped about the galaxy, their need for worlds with an atmosphere similar to Terra’s as necessary as that of man. For in spite of their grotesque insectile bodies, their wholly alien minds, the Throgs were warm-blooded, oxygen-breathing creatures.

===Chinese===
斯罗格人与地球人……以行星时计算，一个多世纪以来，双方一直在群星之间打着这场古怪而扭曲的战争。地球人四处搜求可以殖民的行星——那份对自有土地的古老饥渴，驱使人们离开人满为患的世界，走出太阳系（Sol's system），奔向遥远的星辰。而没有智慧土著、可供移民落脚的行星却为数寥寥，散布各处。二十五年里也许能找出半打，而这六颗中，或许仅有一颗无需对人或对世界作昂贵而漫长的改造，便适合人类生存。沃洛克便是那种难得一遇的幸运发现之一。

斯罗格人是掠食者，靠搜刮来的赃物过活。直到今天，人类也没能查明他们究竟是不是从哪颗母巢星球上繁衍出来的。也许他们永远栖身在碟形飞船里，没有任何固定基地——诞育他们的那颗行星早已毁灭，迫使他们过上了流浪的日子。但如今他们是劫掠者，肆意蹂躏没有防备的世界，拾取那些再无生灵的破碎城市里的财富。他们那些隐蔽的临时基地如环扣般散布在银河各处；他们和人类一样，迫切需要大气与地球相近的行星。因为尽管长着怪诞的虫形身躯，怀着我们全然无从索解的心智，斯罗格人终究是温血、吸氧的生物。

===Original===
After the first few clashes the early Terran explorers had endeavored to promote a truce between the species, only to discover that between Throg and man there appeared to be no meeting ground at all﻿—total differences of mental processes producing insurmountable misunderstanding. There was simply no point of communication. So the Terrans had suffered one smarting defeat after another until they perfected the grid. And now their colonies were safe, at least when time worked in their favor.

It had not on Warlock.

===Chinese===
最初几次交锋过后，早期的地球探险者曾力图促成两个种族之间的休战，结果却发现斯罗格人与人类之间似乎根本不存在任何交汇点——思维方式的全面差异，造成了无法逾越的误解。双方连一个可以沟通的渠道都找不到。于是地球人接连咽下一场又一场灼痛的败绩，直到他们把网格完善为止。如今他们的殖民地是安全的——至少，在时间站在他们一边的时候。

而在沃洛克，时间没有站在他们这边。

===Original===
A last vivid lash of red cracked over the huddle of domes in the valley. Shann blinked, half blinded by that glare. His jaws ached as he unclenched his teeth. That was the finish. Breathing raggedly, he raised his head, beginning to realize that he was the only one of his kind left alive on a none-too-hospitable world controlled by enemies﻿—without shelter or supplies.

He edged back into the narrow cleft which was the entrance to the ledge. As a representative of his species he was not impressive, and now with those shudders he could not master, shaking his thin body, he looked even smaller and more vulnerable. Shann drew his knees up close under his chin. The hood of his woodsman’s jacket was pushed back in spite of the chill of the morning, and he wiped the back of his hand across his lips and chin in an oddly childish gesture.

===Chinese===
最后一道刺目的红光炸裂在谷中那片穹顶上空。沙恩眨了眨眼，强光晃得他半盲。松开牙关时，他双颚酸痛。结束了。他粗重地喘着气，抬起头，开始意识到：在这个由敌人掌控、并不好客的世界上，他是自己这个种族唯一活下来的人——没有住所，没有补给。

他侧身退回通往岩架的那道窄缝。作为本种族的代表，他实在不起眼；此刻一阵阵压制不住的颤抖摇着他单薄的身体，使他显得愈发瘦小、愈发不堪一击。沙恩把膝盖收到下巴底下。尽管清晨寒气袭人，他那件林务员外套的兜帽仍推在脑后；他用手背抹过嘴唇和下巴，那姿势带着一股古怪的稚气。

===Original===
None of the men below who had been alive only minutes earlier had been close friends of his; Shann had never known anyone but acquaintances in his short, roving life. Most people had ignored him completely except to give orders, and one or two had been actively malicious﻿—like Garth Thorvald. Shann grimaced at a certain recent memory, and then that grimace faded into wonder. If young Thorvald hadn’t purposefully tried to get Shann into trouble by opening the wolverines’ cage, Shann wouldn’t be here now﻿—alive and safe for a time﻿—he’d have been down there with the others.

The wolverines! For the first time since Shann had heard the crackle of the Throg attack he remembered the reason he had been heading into the hills. Of all the men on the Survey team, Shann Lantee had been the least important. The dirty, tedious cleanup jobs, the dull routines which required no technical training but which had to be performed to keep the camp functioning comfortably, those had been his portion. And he had accepted that status willingly, just to have a chance to be included among Survey personnel. Not that he had the slightest hope of climbing up to even an S-E-Three rating in the service.

===Chinese===
下面那些几分钟前还活着的人里，没有一个称得上他的挚友；在沙恩短短的漂泊生涯中，他认识的人从来都只是泛泛之交。大多数人除了对他发号施令，根本当他不存在，还有一两个干脆心怀恶意——比如加斯·托尔瓦德（Garth Thorvald）。想起不久前的某件事，沙恩脸上一阵扭曲，随即那副怪相又化作了惊异。若不是小托尔瓦德存心打开狼獾（wolverine）的笼子想害他吃个大亏，沙恩此刻绝不会在这里——暂时活着、暂时安全——他会跟其他人一样，躺在下面。

狼獾！自打听见斯罗格人进攻的爆裂声以来，沙恩头一回想起自己进山的缘由。在这支勘测队里，沙恩·兰蒂原是最无足轻重的一个。那些又脏又累的清扫活计，那些不需要技术训练、却非做不可、营地才能舒舒服服运转下去的枯燥杂务，统统归了他。而他甘愿领受这份地位，只求有机会跻身勘测队的人员之列——倒不是说他存有半分奢望，指望自己在这行里爬到哪怕 S-E-三级（S-E-Three）的评衔。

===Original===
Part of those menial activities had been to clean the animal cages. And there Shann Lantee had found something new, something so absorbing that most of the tiring dull labor had ceased to exist except as tasks to finish before he could return to the fascination of the animal runs.

Survey teams had early discovered the advantage of using mutated and highly trained Terran animals as assistants in the exploration of strange worlds. From the biological laboratories and breeding farms on Terra came a trickle of specialized aides-de-camp to accompany man into space. Some were fighters, silent, more deadly than weapons a man wore at his belt or carried in his hands. Some were keener eyes, keener noses, keener scouts than the human kind could produce. Bred for intelligence, for size, for adaptability to alien conditions, the animal explorers from Terra were prized.

===Chinese===
这些杂务中有一项，是清扫动物笼舍。而沙恩·兰蒂正是在那里找到了某种新东西——一件让他如此着迷的事，以至于大多数累人的枯燥活计都算不得什么了，只剩下一桩桩必须赶完的任务：做完了，好回到那片让他心醉神迷的动物围场去。

勘测队早就发现，用经过突变繁育和严格训练的地球动物充当探索陌生世界的助手，大有好处。从地球的生物实验室和繁育农场里，涓涓流出一支特化的“随行副官”队伍，陪伴人类进入太空。有的是战士，沉默无声，比人系在腰间或握在手中的武器更加致命；有的是比人类所能造就的更敏锐的眼睛、更敏锐的鼻子、更敏锐的侦察员。为了智力、体型、对外星环境的适应力而专门繁育的这些来自地球的动物探险家，个个备受珍视。

===Original===
Wolverines, the ancient “devils” of the northlands on Terra, were being tried for the first time on Warlock. Their caution, a quality highly developed in their breed, made them testers for new territory. Able to tackle in battle an animal three times their size, they should be added protection for the man they accompanied into the wilderness, and their wide ranging, their ability to climb and swim, and above all, their curiosity were assets.

Shann had begun contact by cleaning their cages; he ended captivated by these miniature bears with long bushy tails. And to his unbounded delight the attraction was mutual. Alone to Taggi and Togi he was a person, an important person. Those teeth, which could tear flesh into ragged strips, nipped gently at his fingers, closed without any pressure on arm, even on nose and chin in what was the ultimate caress of their kind. Since they were escape artists of no mean ability, twice he had had to track and lead them back to camp from forays of their own devising.

===Chinese===
狼獾——地球上北方地带自古号称“魔鬼”的动物——是头一回在沃洛克试用。它们的审慎，是这个品系高度发达的秉性，正宜充当新地域的试探者。搏斗起来能撂倒三倍于己的猛兽，它们理应成为随行人踏入荒野时多添的一道保障；而它们巡游范围之广、攀爬泅水之能，尤其是那份好奇心，都是可贵的本钱。

沙恩与它们的交情，是从清扫笼舍开始的；到后来，他已彻底迷上了这些拖着蓬松长尾的小熊模样的家伙。而令他喜出望外的是，这份吸引是相互的。唯独在塔吉（Taggi）和托吉（Togi）眼里，他是一个“人”，一个重要的人物。那副能把肉撕成碎条的牙齿，只轻轻叼他的手指，不带分毫力道地含住他的手臂，甚至碰碰他的鼻子和下巴——这是它们族类最亲昵至极的爱抚。它们又是本领不俗的越狱大师，曾两度自导自演出逃，害得他不得不循迹追踪，把它们领回营地。

===Original===
But the second time he had been caught by Fadakar, the chief of animal control, before he could lock up the delinquents. And the memory of the resulting interview still had the power to make him flush with impotent anger. Shann’s explanation had been contemptuously brushed aside, and he had been delivered an ultimatum. If his carelessness occurred again, he would be sent back on the next supply ship, to be dismissed without an official sign-off on his work record, thus locked out of even the lowest level of Survey for the rest of his life.

That was why Garth Thorvald’s act of the night before had made Shann brave the unknown darkness of Warlock alone when he had discovered that the test animals were gone. He had to locate and return them before Fadakar made his morning inspection; Garth Thorvald’s attempt to get him into bad trouble had saved his life.

===Chinese===
可第二次，他还没来得及把这两个惹祸精关进笼子，就被动物管理主管法达卡（Fadakar）逮了个正着。那场训话留下的记忆，至今仍能让他因无能为力的愤怒而涨红了脸。沙恩的解释被轻蔑地撇到一边，对方给他下了最后通牒：这类疏失若再犯一次，他就会被下一艘补给船遣返，工作记录上没有正式签核便遭辞退——从此这辈子连勘测队最低一级的门都进不去。

正因如此，前一天夜里加斯·托尔瓦德干下的那件事，才害得沙恩在发现试验动物失踪后，独自一人闯进沃洛克陌生的黑暗。他必须赶在法达卡清晨巡查之前找到它们、送它们归笼；加斯·托尔瓦德想让他大祸临头的算计，反倒救了他的命。

===Original===
Shann cowered back, striving to make his huddled body as small as possible. One of the Throg flyers appeared silently out of the misty amber of the morning sky, hovering over the silent camp. The aliens were coming in to inspect the site of their victory. And the safest place for any Terran now was as far from the vicinity of those silent domes as he could get. Shann’s slight body was an asset as he wedged through the narrow mouth of a cleft and so back into the cliff wall. The climb before him he knew in part, for this was the path the wolverines had followed on their two other escapes. A few moments of tricky scrambling and he was out in a cuplike depression choked with brush covered with the purplish foliage of Warlock. On the other side of that was a small cut to a sloping hillside, giving on another valley, not as wide as that in which the camp stood, but one well provided with cover in the way of trees and high-growing bushes.

A light wind pushed among the trees, and twice Shann heard the harsh, rasping call of a clak-clak﻿—one of the bat-like leather-winged flyers that laired in pits along the cliff walls. That present snap of two-tone complaint suggested that the land was empty of strangers. For the clak-claks vociferously and loudly resented encroachment on their chosen hunting territory.

===Chinese===
沙恩缩起身子直往后退，拼命把蜷成一团的身体缩得更小。一架斯罗格飞行器悄无声息地钻出清晨琥珀色的薄雾，悬停在死寂的营地上空。外星人要进来视察他们的胜利现场了。此刻对任何地球人来说，最安全的地方，就是离那片死寂的穹顶越远越好。沙恩瘦小的身板倒成了优势，他从一道石缝的窄口挤进去，回到了崖壁之内。眼前这段攀路他略知一二，因为前两次狼獾出逃，走的正是这条道。手脚并用地攀了一阵，他钻进一处杯状洼地，里面密密匝匝，长满了披着沃洛克紫红色叶片的灌木。洼地另一侧有条小径通向一面斜坡，坡下又是一道山谷——不如扎营那道宽阔，但树木和高秆灌木丛生，遮蔽甚好。

微风在林间穿行，沙恩两次听见克拉克鸟（clak-clak）粗厉刺耳的鸣叫——那是栖在崖壁坑洞里、蝙蝠般长着皮质翅膀的飞兽。此刻这两声双音调的抱怨发作，倒说明这片土地上没有外人。因为克拉克鸟一旦发现自己选定的猎场被侵犯，总会大吵大嚷、抗议不休。

===Original===
Shann hesitated. He was driven by the urge to put as much distance between him and the landing Throg ship as he could. But to arouse the attention of inquisitive clak-claks was asking for trouble. Perhaps it would be best to keep on along the top of the cliff, rather than risk a descent to take cover in the valley the flyers patrolled.

A patch of dust, sheltered by a tooth-shaped projection of rock, gave the Terran his first proof that Taggi and his mate had preceded him, for printed firmly there was the familiar paw mark of a wolverine. Shann began to hope that both animals had taken to cover in the wilderness ahead.

===Chinese===
沙恩犹豫了。一股冲动催着他尽量远离正在降落的斯罗格飞船。可是惹起好事的克拉克鸟的注意，无异于自找麻烦。也许最好还是沿着崖顶继续走，而不是冒险下到谷中藏身——飞行器正在那道山谷上空巡逻。

一处被牙齿状岩突遮蔽的沙地上，让沙恩第一次拿到了证据：塔吉和它的伴侣走在他前头。那儿清晰地印着一枚熟悉的狼獾爪印。沙恩开始期望，两只动物都已在前方的荒野里找到了藏身之处。

===Original===
He licked dry lips. Having left secretly without any emergency pack, he had no canteen, and now Shann inventoried his scant possessions﻿—a field kit, heavy-duty clothing, a short hooded jacket with attached mittens, the breast marked with the Survey insignia. His belt supported a sheathed stunner and bush knife, and seam pockets held three credit tokens, a twist of wire intended to reinforce the latch of the wolverine cage, a packet of bravo tablets, two identity and work cards, and a length of cord. No rations﻿—save the bravos﻿—no extra charge for his stunner. But he did have, weighing down a loop on the jacket, a small atomic torch.

The path he followed ended abruptly in a cliff drop, and Shann made a face at the odor rising from below, even though that scent meant he could climb down to the valley floor here without fearing any clak-clak attention. Chemical fumes from a mineral spring funneled against the wall, warding off any nesting in this section.

===Chinese===
他舔了舔干裂的嘴唇。因为是悄悄出逃，没带应急背包，他连水壶都没有。沙恩清点了一下自己那点可怜的家当：一套野外作业工具、一身耐磨的厚工装、一件缝有连指手套的连帽短夹克，胸口印着勘测队的徽记。腰带上挂着鞘装的一支击晕枪（stunner）和一把丛林刀；衣缝口袋里装着三枚信用币、一截备着加固狼獾笼门的铁丝、一包布拉沃口粮片（bravo tablets）、两张身份与工籍卡，还有一段绳索。没有口粮——布拉沃片除外——击晕枪也没有备用能量块。不过他倒是有一件东西坠在夹克的扣环上：一支小小的原子炬（atomic torch）。

他走的这条路陡然断在一处崖坎前。崖下升起的气味熏得沙恩直做怪相，不过这股味道也意味着：他从这里下到谷底，不必担心克拉克鸟的注意。一眼矿泉的化学烟雾顺崖壁涌上来，把一切筑巢的生灵都从这个地段赶跑了。

===Original===
Shann drew up the hood of his jacket and snapped the transparent face mask into place. He must get away﻿—then find food, water, a hiding place. That will to live which had made Shann Lantee fight innumerable battles in the past was in command, bracing him with a stubborn determination.

The fumes swirled up in a smoke haze about his waist, but he strode on, heading for the open valley and cleaner air. That sickly lavender vegetation bordering the spring deepened in color to the normal purple-green, and then he was in a grove of trees, their branches pointed skyward at sharp angles to the rust-red trunks.

===Chinese===
沙恩掀起夹克的兜帽，啪地扣好透明的面罩。他必须先脱身——然后再找食物、水和一个藏身之所。正是那股求生欲——那股曾驱使沙恩·兰蒂打赢过无数场仗的求生欲——此刻接管了他，以一种顽强的决心支撑着他。

化学烟雾像一圈烟霭，绕着他的腰际打旋，他仍大步向前，直奔开阔的谷地和更干净的空气。泉边那种病恹恹的薰衣草色植被，色泽渐渐加深，变成寻常的紫绿色；随后他走进一片树林，枝条以尖锐的角度从铁锈红的树干上直指天空。

===Original===
A small skitterer burst from moss-spotted ground covering, giving an alarmed squeak, skimming out of sight as suddenly as it had appeared. Shann squeezed between two trees and then paused. The trunk of the larger was deeply scored with scratches dripping viscid gobs of sap, a sap which was a bright froth of scarlet. Taggi had left his mark here, and not too long ago.

The soft carpet of moss showed no paw marks, but he thought he knew the goal of the animals﻿—a lake down-valley. Shann was beginning to plan now. The Throgs had not blasted the Terran camp out of existence; they had only made sure of the death of its occupiers. Which meant they must have some use for the installations. For the general loot of a Survey field camp would be relatively worthless to those who picked over the treasure of entire cities elsewhere. Why? What did the Throgs want? And would the alien invaders continue to occupy the domes for long?

===Chinese===
一只小疾窜兽（skitterer）猛地从斑驳生苔的地被里蹿出，惊叫一声，又像出现时那样倏地没了影。沙恩从两棵树间挤过去，随即停住脚。较大那棵的树干上布满深深的抓痕，一道道淌着黏稠的胶质团块，那树胶泛着猩红的亮沫。塔吉在这里留过记号，而且时间不久。

柔软如毯的苔藓上没有爪印，但他自认猜到了这两只动物的目标——谷下方的一座湖。沙恩这会儿开始盘算起来。斯罗格人没有把地球人的营地轰成齑粉，他们只是确保了营中之人尽数死亡。这就是说，营地设施对他们还有某种用处。要知道，对那些在别处整城整城搜刮财宝的家伙来说，一座勘测野营的寻常物资实在不值几个钱。为什么？斯罗格人想要什么？这些外星入侵者又会在这片穹顶里盘踞多久？

===Original===
Shann did not realize what had happened to him since that shock of ruthless attack. From early childhood, when he had been thrown on his own to scratch a living﻿—a borderline existence of a living﻿—on the Dumps of Tyr, he had had to use his wits to keep life in a scrawny and undersized body. However, since he had been eating regularly from Survey rations, he was not quite so scrawny any more.

His formal education was close to zero, his informal and off-center schooling vast. And that particular toughening process which had been working on him for years now aided in his speedy adaption to a new set of facts, formidable ones. He was alone on a strange and perhaps hostile world. Water, food, safe shelter, those were important now. And once again, away from the ordered round of the camp where he had been ruled by the desires and requirements of others, he was thinking, planning in freedom. Later (his hand went to the butt of his stunner) perhaps later he might just find a way of extracting an accounting from the beetle-faces, too.

===Chinese===
沙恩自己并没有意识到，自那场无情袭击的重击之后，他身上起了什么变化。从记事起，他就被抛在提尔（Tyr）的垃圾场（the Dumps）上自谋生路——一种在生存线上打转的日子——全凭心计，才在皮包骨的瘦小躯壳里保住性命。不过，自从按顿吃上勘测队的口粮，他已不再那么瘦骨嶙峋。

他受过的正规教育近乎于零，那套不上台面的社会学问却广博得很。而这门多年来一直打磨着他的磨砺课，如今正帮他飞快地适应一套新的现实——一套严酷的现实。他孤身一人，身处一个陌生的、也许怀有敌意的世界。水、食物、安全的栖身处，眼下这些才要紧。而且，一旦离开了那个事事受他人意愿与需求支配的营地窠臼，他又一次开始自由地思考、自由地筹划了。将来（他的手摸上击晕枪的枪柄）也许到了将来，他还能找出个法子，跟这些甲虫脑袋好好算一笔账。

===Original===
For the present, he would have to keep away from the Throgs, which meant well away from the camp. A fleck of green showed through the amethyst foliage before him﻿—the lake! Shann wriggled through a last bush barrier and stood to look out over that surface. A sleek brown head bobbed up. Shann put fingers to his mouth and whistled. The head turned, black button eyes regarded him, short legs began to churn water. To his gratification the swimmer was obeying his summons.

Taggi came ashore, pausing on the fine gray sand of the verge to shake himself vigorously. Then the wolverine came upslope at a clumsy gallop to Shann. With an unknown feeling swelling inside him, the Terran went down on both knees, burying both hands in the coarse brown fur, warming to the uproarious welcome Taggi gave him.

===Chinese===
眼下，他必须躲开斯罗格人，也就是说要离营地远远的。前方紫水晶色的叶隙间闪出一抹绿——湖！沙恩扭身钻过最后一道灌木屏障，站定，眺望那片水面。一颗油亮的棕色脑袋冒了出来。沙恩把手指放进嘴里，吹了声口哨。那脑袋转过来，两粒黑纽扣似的眼睛望着他，短短的腿开始划水。令他欣慰的是，那位游泳好手正应着他的召唤而来。

塔吉上了岸，在岸边细软的灰沙上停了停，使劲抖了抖身子。随后这只狼獾迈着笨拙的快步跑上坡，奔向沙恩。一种陌生的情感在胸腔里涨起来，这个地球人双膝跪下，两手深深插进粗糙的棕色皮毛里，尽情领受塔吉给他的这场热烈欢迎。

===Original===
“Togi?” Shann asked as if the other could answer. He gazed back to the lake, but Taggi’s mate was nowhere in sight.

The blunt head under his hand swung around, black button nose pointed north. Shann had never been sure just how intelligent, as mankind measured intelligence, the wolverines were. He had come to suspect that Fadakar and the other experts had underrated them and that both beasts understood more than they were given credit for. Now he followed an experiment of his own, one he had had a chance to try only a few times before and never at length. Pressing his palm flat on Taggi’s head, Shann thought of Throgs and of their attack, trying to arouse in the animal a corresponding reaction to his own horror and anger.

===Chinese===
“托吉呢？”沙恩问道，仿佛对方真能回答似的。他回望湖面，可塔吉的伴侣踪影全无。

他手底下那颗圆钝的脑袋转了过来，黑纽扣似的鼻尖指向北方。若按人类衡量智力的那把尺子，沙恩从来拿不准这两只狼獾到底有多聪明。他渐渐怀疑，法达卡和那些专家都低估了它们，这两只畜生懂的事比人们公认的多。此刻他要做一项自己的实验——此前他只偶尔有过几次尝试的机会，从没来得及从容做过。沙恩把手掌平贴在塔吉头上，心里想着斯罗格人，想着那场袭击，竭力要在它身上激起与自己那份恐惧和愤怒相应的反应。

===Original===
And Taggi responded. A mutter became a growl, teeth gleamed﻿—those cruel teeth of a carnivore to whom they were weapons of aggression. Danger﻿ ﻿… Shann thought “danger.” Then he raised his hand, and the wolverine shuffled off, heading north. The man followed.

They discovered Togi busy in a small cove where a jagged tangle of drift made a mat dating from the last high-water period. She was finishing a hearty breakfast, the remains of a water rat being buried thriftily against future need after the instincts of her kind. When she was done she came to Shann, inquiry plain to read in her eyes.

===Chinese===
塔吉有了回应。低哼化作咆哮，牙齿闪着寒光——那是食肉动物冷酷的牙齿，对它而言便是进攻的武器。危险……沙恩在心里默念“危险”。然后他抬起手，狼獾便碎步跑开，朝北而去。人跟了上去。

他们在一个小湖湾里找到了正忙活的托吉——湾中乱七八糟堆着的浮木残枝，还是上次涨水期留下的，缠成了一张垫子。它刚吃完一顿丰盛的早餐，正按照本族的天性，把吃剩的水鼠仔仔细细地埋起来，以备后需。忙完这些，它走到沙恩跟前，眼里明明白白写着探询。

===Original===
There was water here, and good hunting. But the site was too close to the Throgs. Let one of their exploring flyers sight them, and the little group was finished. Better cover, that’s what the three fugitives must have. Shann scowled, not at Togi, but at the landscape. He was tired and hungry, but he must keep on going.

A stream fed into the cove from the west, a guide of sorts. With very little knowledge of the countryside, Shann was inclined to follow that.

===Chinese===
这里有水，猎物也丰富。可这地方离斯罗格人太近了。只要让他们一架侦察飞行器瞧见，这一小队便全完了。更好的藏身处——这三个逃亡者非找到不可。沙恩皱起眉头，不是冲着托吉，而是冲着这片山川。他又累又饿，却必须继续赶路。

一条小溪从西边注入湖湾，好歹算个向导。对这一带乡野所知甚少，沙恩倾向于就顺着它走。

===Original===
Overhead the sun made its usual golden haze of the sky. A flight of vivid green streaks marked a flock of lake ducks coming for a morning feeding. Lake duck was good eating, but Shann had no time to hunt one now. Togi started down the bank of the stream, Taggi behind her. Either they had caught his choice subtly through some undefined mental contact, or they had already picked that road on their own.

Shann’s attention was caught by a piece of the drift. He twisted the length free and had his first weapon of his own manufacture, a club. Using it to hold back a low sweeping branch, he followed the wolverines.

===Chinese===
头顶上，太阳照例给天空蒙上一层金色的薄霭。几道鲜绿的流影掠过——是一群湖鸭飞来觅早餐。湖鸭肉味甚美，可沙恩眼下没工夫去猎一只。托吉顺着溪岸先行，塔吉跟在后面。要么它们通过某种说不清的心灵感应，隐约领会了他的选择；要么它们原本就自己选定了这条路。

一段浮木引起了沙恩的注意。他把它拧了下来，从此有了第一件出自自己之手的武器——一根木棒。他用木棒拨开一枝低垂横扫的树枝，跟着狼獾走去。

===Original===
Within the half hour he had breakfast, too. A pair of limp skitterers, their long hind feet lashed together with a thong of grass, hung from his belt. They were not particularly good eating, but they were meat and acceptable.

The three, man and wolverines, made their way up the stream to the valley wall and through a feeder ravine into the larger space beyond. There, where the stream was born at the foot of a falls, they made their first camp. Judging that the morning haze would veil any smoke, Shann built a pocket-size fire. He seared rather than roasted the skitterers after he had made an awkward and messy business of skinning them, and tore the meat from the delicate bones in greedy mouthfuls. The wolverines lay side by side on the gravel, now and again raising a head alertly to test the scent on the air, or gaze into the distance.

===Chinese===
不出半个钟头，他也吃上了早餐。两只软塌塌的疾窜兽，长长的后脚用草茎捆在一起，挂在他的腰带上。它们的味道说不上多好，但毕竟是肉，聊胜于无。

一人和两只狼獾溯溪而上，走到谷壁脚下，又穿过一条支谷，进入更开阔的天地。在小溪发源处、一道瀑布脚下，他们扎下了第一个营地。沙恩估摸着晨霭足以遮掩炊烟，便拢起一小堆巴掌大的火。他把疾窜兽剥了皮——那过程笨手笨脚、一片狼藉——然后与其说烤、不如说燎了一下，便贪婪地大口把肉从细嫩的骨头上撕下来。两只狼獾并排卧在砾石上，不时警觉地抬起头，嗅嗅风里的气味，或是望望远方。

===Original===
Taggi made a warning sound deep in the throat. Shann tossed handfuls of sand over the dying fire. He had only time to fling himself face-down, hoping the drab and weathered cloth of his uniform faded into the color of the earth on which he lay, every muscle tense.

A shadow swung across the hillside. Shann’s shoulders hunched, and he cowered again. That terror he had known on the ledge was back in full force as he waited for the beam to lick at him as it had earlier at his fellows. The Throgs were on the hunt﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
塔吉在喉咙深处发出一声警示。沙恩抓起一把把沙子，扬向将熄的火堆。他只来得及扑倒在地，脸朝下伏着，指望制服那灰暗褪色的布料能溶进身下泥土的颜色。他每一块肌肉都绷得紧紧的。

一道影子扫过山坡。沙恩双肩一缩，又蜷伏下去。他在岩架上领略过的那种恐怖，此刻全须全尾地卷土重来——他等着那道光束来舔舐自己，就像它早先舔舐他的同伴们那样。斯罗格人出来狩猎了……

## II Death of a Ship / II 飞船之死

===Original===
That sigh of displaced air was not as loud as a breeze, but it echoed monstrously in Shann’s ears. He could not believe in his luck as that sound grew fainter, drew away into the valley he had just left. With infinite caution he raised his head from his arm, still hardly able to accept the fact that he had not been sighted, that the Throgs and their flyer were gone.

But that black plate was spinning out into the sun haze. One of the beetles might have suspected that there were Terran fugitives and ordered a routine patrol. After all, how could the aliens know that they had caught all but one of the Survey party in camp? Though with all the Terran scout flitters grounded on the field, the men dead in their bunks, the surprise would seem to be complete.
===Chinese===
那声气流被排开的叹息，还不如一阵微风响，可在沙恩耳中却轰然回荡。那声音渐渐微弱下去，退进他刚刚离开的山谷，他简直不敢相信自己的运气。他无限小心地把头从手臂上抬起，仍旧难以接受这个事实：他没有被发现，斯罗格人和他们的飞行器已经走了。

可那黑色的碟影还在打着旋，飞进太阳的薄霭深处。也许某个甲虫人疑心尚有地球逃亡者，便下令来一次例行巡逻。毕竟，外星人又怎么可能知道，勘测队全队只走脱了他一个？何况地球人的侦察飞梭尽数瘫在机场上，人都死在铺位里，这场突袭看上去已是干净利落、再无遗漏。

===Original===
As Shann moved, Taggi and Togi came to life also. They had gone to earth with speed, and the man was sure that both beasts had sensed danger. Not for the first time he knew a burning desire for the formal education he had never had. In camp he had listened, dragging out routine jobs in order to overhear reports and the small talk of specialists keen on their own particular hobbies. But so much of the information Shann had thus picked up to store in a retentive memory he had not understood and could not fit together. It had been as if he were trying to solve some highly important puzzle with at least a quarter of the necessary pieces missing, or with unrelated bits from others intermixed. How much control did a trained animal scout have over his furred or feathered assistants? And was part of that mastery a mental rapport built up between man and animal?
===Chinese===
沙恩一动，塔吉和托吉也跟着活了过来。它们方才窜进隐蔽处藏得飞快，这人确信两只野兽都察觉了危险。他心头又烧起那股渴望——渴望自己从未得到过的正规教育，这已不是头一回了。在营地里他总竖着耳朵，故意把杂活拖得老长，好偷听各种报告，听那些醉心于各自癖好的专家们闲谈。可这样捡来、存进那份可靠记性里的许多信息，他并不理解，也拼合不到一块儿去。这就好比要解一道极其要紧的谜题，必要的拼块却至少缺了四分之一，还掺进了别的谜题里不相干的碎片。一个受过训练的驯兽侦察员，对那些长毛长羽的助手到底能驾驭几分？而那份驾驭里，可有几分靠的是人与兽之间养出来的心灵默契？

===Original===
How well would the wolverines obey him now, especially when they would not return to camp where cages stood waiting as symbols of human authority? Wouldn’t a trek into the wilderness bring about a revolt for complete freedom? If Shann could depend upon the animals, it would mean a great deal. Not only would their superior hunting ability provide all three with food, but their scouting senses, so much keener than his, might erect a slender wall between life and death.

Few large native beasts had been discovered on Warlock by the Terran explorers. And of those four or five different species, none had proved hostile if unprovoked. But that did not mean that somewhere back in the wild lands into which Shann was heading there were no heretofore unknowns, perhaps slyer and as vicious as the wolverines when they were aroused to rage.
===Chinese===
如今这两只狼獾还能听他几分话？尤其是，再不用回到营地里去，面对那些张着口等待的笼子——那可是人类权威的象征。长途跋涉进了荒野，难道不会激起一场争取彻底自由的叛乱？倘若沙恩能指望这两个伙伴，那意义非同小可。不光它们高超的捕猎本领够三个一路吃食，它们那远比他敏锐的侦察感官，或许还能在生与死之间筑起一道薄薄的屏障。

地球探险者在沃洛克发现的大型土生野兽寥寥无几。而那四五种不同的物种里，只要人不犯它，没有一个显出过敌意。可这并不意味着，在沙恩正要前往的荒野深处，就不存在至今未知的家伙——它们兴许更加阴狡，被激得性起时凶起来，不亚于狼獾。

===Original===
Then there were the “dreams,” which had afforded the prime source of camp discussion and dispute. Shann brushed coarse sand from his boots and thought about the dreams. Did they or did they not exist? You could start an argument any time by making a definite statement for or against the peculiar sort of dreaming reported by the first scout to set ship on this world.

The Circe system, of which Warlock was the second of three planets, had first been scouted four years ago by one of those explorers traveling solo in Survey service. Everyone knew that the First-In Scouts were a weird breed, almost a mutation of Terran stock﻿—their reports were rife with strange observations.

So an alarming one concerning Circe (a yellow sun such as Sol) and her three planets was not so rare. Witch, the world nearest in orbit to Circe, was too hot for human occupancy without drastic and too costly world-changing. Wizard, the third out from the sun, was mostly bare rock and highly poisonous water. But Warlock, swinging through space between two forbidding neighbors, seemed to be just what the settlement board ordered.
===Chinese===
再有就是那些「梦」——营地里讨论和争执的头号题目。沙恩拍掉靴子上的粗沙，琢磨起这些梦来。它们到底存不存在？只要你斩钉截铁地表示赞成或反对那头一位降船踏上此星的侦察员报告过的那种怪梦，随时都能挑起一场争论。

喀耳刻星系共有三颗行星，沃洛克位居第二。四年前，受勘测队差遣、单枪匹马独自远航的探险者之一初次勘测了这里。谁都知道，先遣侦察员是一帮怪人，几乎算是地球人种里的一支变种——他们的报告里尽是些离奇的见闻。

因此，一份关于喀耳刻（一颗类似太阳的黄色恒星）及其三颗行星的骇人报告，倒也不算稀奇。女巫星（Witch）是绕喀耳刻最近的行星，热得人类根本无法居住，除非大动干戈改造整个世界，而那代价又太过高昂。男巫星（Wizard）是离太阳的第三颗，遍地裸岩，水也剧毒。唯独沃洛克，在两个凶险的近邻之间穿行于太空，看上去简直像照着定居委员会的订单造出来的，正合适不过。

===Original===
Then the Survey scout, even in the cocoon safety of his well-armed ship, began to dream. And from those dreams a horror of the apparently empty world developed, until he fled the planet to preserve his sanity. There had been a second visit to Warlock in check; worlds so well adapted to human emigration could not be lightly thrown away. And this time there was a negative report, no trace of dreams, no registration of any outside influence on the delicate and complicated equipment the ship carried. So the Survey team had been dispatched to prepare for the coming of the first pioneers, and none of them had dreamed either﻿—at least, no more than the ordinary dreams all men accepted.

Only there were those who pointed out that the seasons had changed between the first and second visits to Warlock. That first scout had planeted in summer; his successors had come in fall and winter. They argued that the final release of the world for settlement should not be given until the full year on Warlock had been sampled.
===Chinese===
后来，那位勘测队侦察员纵然藏身于全副武装的飞船那茧壳般的安全之中，也开始做起梦来。梦来梦去，他对这个表面空寂的世界生出了一种恐惧，直到为了保住清醒的神智而逃离了这颗行星。随后人们又对沃洛克作了一次核查性质的二次造访——如此适合人类移民的世界，不能轻易抛弃。这一回报告是否定的：没有梦的踪影，船上那些精密复杂的仪器也没有记录到任何外来影响的迹象。于是勘测队奉命前来，为首批拓荒者的到来做准备，而队里同样没有人做过那种梦——至少，不比人人习以为常的寻常之梦更多。

只是有人指出，两次造访沃洛克之间，季节已经换了。头一位侦察员降临时正值夏天；他的后继者来时已是秋天和冬天。他们力争说，非得把沃洛克的一整年都采样过，才能最终放行这颗世界、准予定居。

===Original===
But the pressure of Emigrant Control had forced their hands, that and the fear of just what had eventually happened﻿—an attack from the Throgs. So they had speeded up the process of declaring Warlock open. Only Ragnar Thorvald had protested that decision up to the last and had gone back to headquarters on the supply ship a month ago to make a last appeal for a more careful study.

Shann stopped brushing the sand from the tough fabric above his knee. Ragnar Thorvald﻿ ﻿… He remembered back to the port landing apron on another world, remembered with a sense of loss he could not define. That had been about the second biggest day of his short life; the biggest had come earlier when they had actually allowed him to sign on for Survey duty.
===Chinese===
但移民管理局（Emigrant Control）的压力迫使他们不得不如此，再加上对一桩事的恐惧——而那桩事最终恰恰发生了：斯罗格人的袭击。于是他们加快了宣布沃洛克开放的进程。唯有拉格纳·托尔瓦德抗议这项决定，一直抗到最后；一个月前，他乘补给船回了总部，要为更周密的研究作最后一次呼吁。

沙恩停下了手，不再擦拭膝上那块结实的布料。拉格纳·托尔瓦德……他回想起另一个世界上港口的停机坪，心里泛起一种说不出名目的失落感。那差不多是他短短一生中第二重大的日子；最重大的那个来得更早——他们当真准许他签约受雇于勘测队的那天。

===Original===
He had tumbled off the cross-continent cargo carrier, his kit﻿—a very meager kit﻿—slung over his thin shoulder, a hot eagerness expanding inside him until he thought that he could not continue to throttle down that wild happiness. There was a waiting starship. And he﻿—Shann Lantee from the Dumps of Tyr, without any influence or schooling﻿—was going to blast off in her, wearing the brown-green uniform of Survey!
===Chinese===
他从跨洲货运机上一骨碌翻下来，行李——寒酸得可怜的一副行囊——挎在瘦削的肩头，一股滚烫的热望在胸中膨胀，胀得他觉得自己再也按捺不住那股狂野的欢喜。一艘星舰正等着。而他——来自提尔的垃圾场、无门路无学历的沙恩·兰蒂——竟要穿上勘测队棕绿色的制服，随她一道轰然升空了！

===Original===
Then he had hesitated uncertainly, had not quite dared cross the few feet of apron lying between him and that compact group wearing the same uniform﻿—with a slight difference, that of service bars and completion badges and rank insignia﻿—with the unconscious self-assurance of men who had done this many times before.

But after a moment that whole group had become in his own shy appraisal just a background for one man. Shann had never before known in his pinched and limited childhood, his lost boyhood, anyone who aroused in him hero worship. And he could not have put a name to the new emotion that added so suddenly to his burning desire to make good, not only to hold the small niche in Survey which he had already so painfully achieved, but to climb, until he could stand so in such a group talking easily to that tall man, his uncovered head bronze-yellow in the sunlight, his cool gray eyes pale in his brown face.
===Chinese===
可随即他就踌躇起来，没敢跨过隔在他与那一小群人之间的几步停机坪。那些人穿着同样的制服——差别微乎其微，不过是多了服役条章、结业徽章和军衔标识——却带着一种浑然不觉的自信，那是此等场面经过千百回的人才有的从容。

可过了一会儿，在他自己羞怯的打量中，那一整群人便淡成了背景，只为衬托一个人。沙恩在那拮据局促的童年、那失落的少年时代里，从没见识过一个能令他生出英雄崇拜的人。这股突然添上来的新情绪，他说不出名目；它给他那股出人头地的灼热欲望又添了一把火：他不但要保住自己千辛万苦才在勘测队挣下的这个小位置，还要往上爬——爬到有朝一日，也能站在那样一群人当中，同那位高个子男人谈笑自如。那人未戴帽的头颅在阳光下泛着青铜般的黄色，冷静的灰眼睛在棕色脸庞上显得很淡。

===Original===
Not that any of those wild dreams born in that minute or two had been realized in the ensuing months. Probably those dreams had always been as wild as the ones reported by the first scout on Warlock. Shann grinned wryly now at the short period of childish hope and half-confidence that he could do big things. Only one Thorvald had ever noticed Shann’s existence in the Survey camp, and that had been Garth.

Garth Thorvald, a far less impressive﻿—one could say “smudged”﻿—copy of his brother. Swaggering with an arrogance Ragnar never showed, Garth was a cadet on his first mission, intent upon making Shann realize the unbridgeable gulf between a labor hand and an officer-to-be. He had appeared to know right from their first meeting just how to make Shann’s life a misery.
===Chinese===
当然，那一两分钟里生出的那些狂想，在随后的几个月里一桩也没有成真。它们八成从头起，就跟头一位侦察员在沃洛克报告的那些梦一样狂乱。此刻沙恩想起自己那段孩子气的指望、那股自许能成大事的半吊子信心，不由得咧嘴苦笑。勘测营地里只有一个托尔瓦德留意过沙恩的存在——那就是加斯。

加斯·托尔瓦德活脱是他哥哥的一份翻版，只是逊色得多——简直可以说是「走了样」。拉格纳从不摆架子，加斯却昂首阔步、骄横逼人；他是头一回出任务的见习生，一门心思想让沙恩明白：一个卖苦力的帮工与一名未来的军官之间，横着一道永远跨不过去的鸿沟。仿佛从两人初次见面起，他就摸透了窍门，知道怎样才能把沙恩的日子变成煎熬。

===Original===
Now, in this slit of valley well away from the domes, Shann’s fists balled. He pounded them against the earth in a way he had so often hoped to plant them on Garth’s smoothly handsome face, his well-muscled body. One didn’t survive the Dumps of Tyr without learning how to use fists, and boots, and a list of tricks they didn’t teach in any academy. He had always been sure that he could take Garth if they mixed it up. But if he had loosed the tight rein he had kept on his temper and offered that challenge, he would have lost his chance with Survey. Garth had proved himself able to talk his way out of any scrape, even minor derelictions of duty, and he far outranked Shann. The laborer from Tyr had had to swallow all that the other could dish out and hope that on his next assignment he would not be a member of young Thorvald’s team. Though, because of Garth Thorvald, Shann’s toll of black record marks had mounted dangerously high and each day the chance for any more duty tours had grown dimmer.

Shann laughed, and the sound was ugly. That was one thing he didn’t have to worry about any longer. There would be no other assignments for him, the Throgs had seen to that. And Garth﻿ ﻿… well, there would never be a showdown between them now. He stood up. The Throg ship had disappeared; they could push on.
===Chinese===
此刻，在这条远离穹顶群、狭如缝隙的山谷里，沙恩的双拳攥紧了。他把拳头往地上一擂又一擂，那股劲头，正是他多少回盼着砸在加斯那张光洁俊脸上、那副结实身坯上的。在提尔的垃圾场活不下来的人，必定学会了使拳脚、用靴尖，外加一串任何学府都不教的损招。他一直自信，真要混战一场，他能收拾了加斯。可只要他松开一直勒紧脾气的缰绳、递出那封战书，他在勘测队的机会就算完了。加斯早证明过，多大的乱子他都能凭一张嘴滑过去，连小小的玩忽职守也不在话下，何况他的级别远在沙恩之上。这个提尔来的劳工只有全盘咽下对方端上来的一切，只盼下一回派差，别再编进小托尔瓦德的队里。尽管因为加斯·托尔瓦德，沙恩记过簿上的黑账已经涨到了危险的高度，再出勤任差的日子也一天比一天黯淡。

沙恩笑了，那笑声很难听。至少有一桩事，他再不用操心了。他不会再有别的派差了——斯罗格人替他把这条路断得干干净净。至于加斯……唉，他们之间那场迟早要来的摊牌，如今是永远等不到了。他站起身。斯罗格飞船已经无影无踪，可以继续赶路了。

===Original===
He found a break in the cliff wall which was climbable, and he coaxed the wolverines after him. When they stood on the heights from which the falls tumbled, Taggi and Togi rubbed against him, cried for his attention. They, too, appeared to need the reassurance they got from contact with him, for they were also fugitives on this alien world, the only representatives of their kind.

Since he did not have any definite goal in view, Shann continued to be guided by the stream, following its wanderings across a plateau. The sun was warm, so he carried his jacket slung across one shoulder. Taggi and Togi ranged ahead, twice catching skitterers, which they devoured voraciously. A shadow on a sunbaked rock sent the Terran skidding for cover until he saw that it was cast by one of the questing falcons from the upper peaks. But that shook his confidence, so he again sought cover, ashamed at his own carelessness.
===Chinese===
他在崖壁上找到一道可以攀爬的裂口，连哄带劝地把狼獾引了上来。当他们站上瀑布倾泻而下的高处时，塔吉和托吉挨着他蹭来蹭去，叫着讨他的关注。看来它们也需要从他身上得到那份安抚——在这个异星的世界上，它们同样是逃亡者，是各自种族仅存的代表。

既然心里没有明确的目标，沙恩便继续让小溪引路，跟着它蜿蜒穿过一片高地。日头暖和，他把外套搭在一侧肩头。塔吉和托吉在前头四散巡行，两回逮住疾窜兽，都狼吞虎咽地吃了下去。晒得滚烫的岩石上晃过一道影子，惊得这个地球人急忙滑向隐蔽处，直到看清那不过是高峰上下来盘旋觅食的一只隼投下的。可这一惊还是动摇了他的胆气，于是他又寻了处掩护躲进去，为自己的大意暗自羞愧。

===Original===
In the late afternoon he reached the far end of the plateau, faced a climb to peaks which still bore cones of snow, now tinted a soft peach by the sun. Shann studied that possible path and distrusted his own powers to take it without proper equipment or supplies. He must turn either north or south, though he would then have to abandon a sure water supply in the stream. Tonight he would camp where he was. He had not realized how tired he was until he found a likely half-cave in the mountain wall and crawled in. There was too much danger in fire here; he would have to do without that first comfort of his kind.

Luckily, the wolverines squeezed in beside him to fill the hole. With their warm furred bodies sandwiching him, Shann dozed, awoke, and dozed again, listening to night sounds﻿—the screams, cries, hunting calls, of the Warlock wilds. Now and again one of the wolverines whined and moved uneasily.
===Chinese===
近傍晚时，他到了高地尽头，面前是一段攀登，通往仍顶着雪锥的群峰——此刻雪色正被夕阳染上柔和的桃红。沙恩端详着那条可能走得通的路，不敢信自己缺了装备补给还能闯得过去。他必须往北或往南转，尽管那样就得放弃溪水这条十拿九稳的水源。今夜他就在原地扎营。直到他在山壁上寻见一个像是半截洞穴的凹处、爬将进去，他才发觉自己已经累到了什么地步。在这里生火太危险；他只得舍弃人类这第一样安慰。

幸好两只狼獾挤进来贴着他，把洞穴填满了。夹在它们温热的毛躯之间，沙恩打了盹，醒来，又打盹，听着沃洛克荒野的夜之声——尖叫、啼哭、狩猎的呼号。狼獾时不时呜咽一声，不安地挪动身子。

===Original===
Fingers of sun picked at Shann through a shaft among the rocks, striking his eyes. He moved, blinked blearily awake, unable for the first few seconds to understand why the smooth plasta wall of his bunk had become rough red stone. Then he remembered. He was alone and he threw himself frantically out of the cave, afraid the wolverines had wandered off. Only both animals were busy clawing under a boulder with a steady persistence which argued there was a purpose behind that effort.

A sharp sting on the back of one hand made that purpose only too clear to Shann, and he retreated hurriedly from the vicinity of the excavation. They had found an earth-wasp’s burrow and were hunting grubs, naturally arousing the rightful inhabitants to bitter resentment.
===Chinese===
阳光的手指从岩石间的一道竖缝里探进来，挠着沙恩，直刺他的眼睛。他动了动，睡眼惺忪地眨醒，头几秒钟竟不明白，铺位那光滑的普拉斯塔（plasta）舱壁怎么变成了粗糙的红石头。随即他想起来了。他孤身一人，便发疯似的冲出洞穴，生怕狼獾自己逛远了。谁知两只野兽正忙着在一块巨石底下刨挖，不紧不慢、锲而不舍，那架势分明表明这番辛苦背后有名堂。

手背上猛地一记刺痛，那名堂立刻明明白白，沙恩赶忙从挖掘现场周围撤了下来。它们找着了一窝土蜂（earth-wasp），正掏幼虫吃呢，自然也惹得穴宅的正主儿们怨愤交加。

===Original===
Shann faced the problem of his own breakfast. He had had the immunity shots given to all members of the team, and he had eaten game brought in by exploring parties and labeled “safe.” But how long he could keep to the varieties of native food he knew was uncertain. Sooner or later he must experiment for himself. Already he drank the stream water without the aid of purifiers, and so far there had been no ill results from that necessary recklessness. Now the stream suggested fish. But instead he chanced upon another water inhabitant which had crawled up on land for some obscure purpose of its own. It was a sluggish scaled thing, an easy victim to his club, with thin, weak legs it could project at will from a finned and armor-plated body.
===Chinese===
沙恩面对起自己的早餐问题。全队成员都打过的免疫针他也打了，勘探分队带回、贴着「安全」标签的野味他也吃过。可他叫得出名目的这几样土产食物能撑多久，实在没个准数。早晚他得亲自试验。他已经不用净化剂、直接喝上了溪水，到如今，这番必要的莽撞尚未招来任何恶果。眼下，溪水让他想到了鱼。不想他却撞上了另一位水族——它为了某种只有它自己才清楚的缘故爬上了岸。那是个披鳞的迟钝家伙，一对细弱的小腿能随意从带鳍的装甲躯体里探出来，一棒下去便轻松毙命。

===Original===
Shann offered the head and guts to Togi, who had abandoned the wasp nest. She sniffed in careful investigation and then gulped. Shann built a small fire and seared the firm greenish flesh. The taste was flat, lacking salt, but the food eased his emptiness. Enheartened, he started south, hoping to find water sometime during the morning.

By noon he had his optimism justified with the discovery of a spring, and the wolverines had brought down a slender-legged animal whose coat was close in shade to the dusky purple of the vegetation. Smaller than a Terran deer, its head bore, not horns, but a ridge of stiffened hair rising in a point some twelve inches about the skull dome. Shann haggled off some ragged steaks while the wolverines feasted in earnest, carefully burying the head afterward.
===Chinese===
沙恩把头和内脏让给了早撇下蜂巢的托吉。它仔细地嗅探了一番，随即一口吞下。沙恩拢起一小堆火，把紧实的淡绿色肉燎了燎。味道寡淡，缺盐，可食物到底缓解了他的饥肠。他壮起精神朝南进发，指望上午的什么时候能寻着水。

到中午，他的乐观得到了报偿——发现了一眼泉；狼獾们也扑倒了一只细腿的兽，那身皮毛的颜色同暗紫色植被的色调几乎一般深浅。它比地球的鹿小，头上顶的不是角，而是一列硬毛凝成的脊冠，在头骨穹顶上拢起约十二英寸的一簇尖。沙恩胡乱剁下几条参差不齐的肉排，狼獾们则放开肚子大吃了一顿，事后还仔细地把兽头埋了。

===Original===
It was when Shann knelt by the spring pool to wash that he caught the clamor of the clak-claks. He had seen or heard nothing of the flyers since he had left the lake valley. But from the noise now rising in an earsplitting volume, he thought there was a sizable colony nearby and that the inhabitants were thoroughly aroused.

He crept on his hands and knees to nearby brush cover, heading toward the source of that outburst. If the claks were announcing a Throg scouting party, he wanted to know it.

Lying flat, with branches forming a screen over him, the Terran gazed out on a stretch of grassland which sloped at a fairly steep angle to the south and which must lead to a portion of countryside well below the level he was now traversing.
===Chinese===
就在沙恩跪在泉池边洗漱时，他听见了克拉克鸟的喧噪。离开湖谷以来，飞行器的动静他再没见过、也没听见过。可此刻那噪声越涨越高、震耳欲聋，他料想附近有一片不小的聚居地，而住户们已经全然惊起了。

他手脚并用，爬进附近的灌木丛，朝那阵骚动的源头挪去。要是克拉克鸟通报的是一支斯罗格侦察队，他非得知道不可。

他平平地伏着，头顶枝柯交叠成一道帘幕。这个地球人向外望去：一片草地以相当陡的坡度向南倾斜，想必通往比他此刻所走的地带低得多的一带乡野。

===Original===
The clak-claks were skimming back and forth, shrieking their staccato war cries. Following the erratic dashes of their flight formation, Shann decided that whatever they railed against was on the lower level, out of his sight from that point. Should he simply withdraw, since the disturbance was not near him? Prudence dictated that; yet still he hesitated.

He had no desire to travel north, or to try and scale the mountains. No, south was his best path, and he should be very sure that route was closed before he retreated.
===Chinese===
克拉克鸟来回掠飞，尖声叫着一连串短促的战号。循着它们忽东忽西的队形冲刺看去，沙恩断定，它们叫骂的对头在低处，从这个位置望不见。骚乱既然不在近旁，他干脆撤走不就得了？谨慎是这么吩咐的；可他仍旧犹豫。

他无意北行，也不打算试着翻越群山。不，南方才是最好的去路，退却之前，他务必先弄确实那条路是不是已被封死。

===Original===
Since any additional fuss the clak-claks might make on sighting him would be undistinguished in their now general clamor, the Terran crawled on to where tall grass provided a screen at the top of the slope. There he stopped short, his hands digging into the earth in sudden braking action.

Below, the ground steamed from a rocket flare-back, grasses burned away from the fins of a small scoutship. But even as Shann rose to one knee, his shout of welcome choked in his throat. One of those fins sank, canting the ship crookedly, preventing any new takeoff. And over the crown of a low hill to the west swung the ominous black plate of a Throg flyer.
===Chinese===
反正克拉克鸟此刻已闹成一片，再多几声发现他的叫嚷也听不出个所以然，这地球人便接着往前爬，爬到坡顶长草可作掩蔽的地方。到那儿他猛地刹住，双手一把抠进土里，生生止住了前冲的势头。

下方，火箭喷焰回烧过的地面还在蒸腾热气，一艘小型侦察飞船的尾翅四周，草已烧秃。可就在沙恩单膝撑起的那一瞬，那声欢呼哽在了喉咙里。飞船的一枚尾翅正在下陷，把船身翘得歪歪斜斜，再不可能重新起飞。而西边一座小丘的顶上，斯罗格飞行器那张不祥的黑碟，正悠悠荡了过来。

===Original===
The Throg ship came up in a burst of speed, and Shann waited tensely for some countermove from the scout. Those small speedy Terran ships were prudently provided with weapons triply deadly in proportion to their size. He was sure that the Terran ship could hold its own against the Throg, even eliminate the enemy. But there was no fire from the slanting pencil of the scout. The Throg circled warily, obviously expecting a trap. Twice it darted back in the direction from which it had come. As it returned from its second retreat, another of its kind showed, a black coin dot against the amber of the sky.

Shann felt sick inside. Now the Terran scout had lost any advantage and perhaps all hope. The Throgs could box the other in, cut the downed ship to pieces with their energy beams. He wanted to crawl away and not witness this last disaster for his kind. But some stubborn core of will kept him where he was.
===Chinese===
斯罗格飞船猛然加速逼近，沙恩紧张地等着侦察船的回手。那种小巧神速的地球飞船，向来谨慎地配着武器，就体型而论杀伤力足有三倍之多。他确信那艘地球飞船足可同斯罗格人分庭抗礼，甚至能把敌人送终。可那支铅笔般斜倚的侦察船身上，没有半点火光。斯罗格飞机警惕地兜着圈，显然在提防一个陷阱。它两次朝来路疾退。待它第二回退罢归来时，又一架同类现身了，衬着琥珀色的天空，像一枚小小的黑色钱币。

沙恩心里一阵发呕。此刻地球侦察船已尽失优势，或许也失尽了一切指望。斯罗格人能把它围死，用能量束把这艘跌落的飞船切成碎片。他想爬开，不去目睹本种族的这最后一场灾难。可意志深处某个固执的核，把他钉在了原地。

===Original===
The Throgs began to circle while beneath them the flock of clak-claks screamed and dived at the slanting nose of the Terran ship. Then that same slashing energy he had watched quarter the camp snapped from the far plate across the stricken scout. The man who had piloted her, if not dead already (which might account for the lack of defense), must have fallen victim to that. But the Throg was going to make very sure. The second flyer halted, remaining poised long enough to unleash a second bolt﻿—dazzling any watching eyes and broadcasting a vibration to make Shann’s skin crawl when the last faint ripple reached his lookout post.

What happened then the overconfident Throg was not prepared to take. Shann cried out, burying his face on his arm, as pinwheels of scarlet light blotted out normal sight. There was an explosion, a deafening blast. He cowered, blind, unable to hear. Then, rubbing at his eyes, he tried to see what had happened.
===Chinese===
斯罗格人开始绕圈，脚底下那群克拉克鸟冲着地球飞船倾斜的船首尖啸俯冲。紧接着，他曾亲眼看着把营地剁成碎块的那种劈斩能量，从远处那艘碟船上劈出，横扫过受创的侦察船。驾船的人即便先前没有死（这或许正解释了为何毫无抵抗），此刻也必遭了毒手。可斯罗格人还要把事情做到十足。第二架飞行器停住，悬定了片刻，足够再轰出一击——那道光华晃瞎了每一双观看的眼睛，激起的震荡传开去，待最后一丝微澜抵达沙恩的瞭望处，仍激得他皮肤发麻。

接下来发生的事，那自信过头的斯罗格人可没料到。沙恩大叫一声，把脸埋进手臂——猩红的光轮风车般打转，把正常的视界整个抹掉了。一声爆炸，震耳欲聋。他缩成一团，又瞎又聋。然后他揉着眼睛，想看看出了什么事。

===Original===
Through watery blurs he made out the Throg ship, not swinging now in serene indifference to Warlock’s gravity, but whirling end over end across the sky as might a leaf tossed in a gust of wind. Its rim caught against a rust-red cliff, it rebounded and crumpled. Then it came down, smashing perhaps half a mile away from the smoking crater in which lay the mangled wreckage of the Terran ship. The disabled scout pilot must have played a last desperate game, making of his ship bait for a trap.

The Terran had taken one Throg with him. Shann rubbed again at his eyes, just barely able to catch a glimpse of the second ship flashing away westward. Perhaps it was only his impaired sight, but it appeared to him that the Throg followed an erratic path, either as if the pilot feared to be caught by a second shot, or because that ship had also suffered some injury.
===Chinese===
透过水汪汪的泪光，他辨出了那艘斯罗格飞船——它再不能那般安然无视沃洛克的重力、从容摆动了，而是像狂风里卷起的一片落叶，头尾翻滚着横过天空。它的边缘磕在铁锈红的崖壁上，弹起来，随即皱缩变形。然后它栽落下来，砸在离那个冒烟的弹坑约半英里远处——坑里躺着地球飞船血肉模糊的残骸。那艘残废侦察船的驾驶员，临了必定下了一着孤注一掷的险棋，把自己的船做了饵，布下陷阱。

那个地球人临走拉上了一个斯罗格人垫背。沙恩又揉了揉眼睛，恰好来得及瞥见第二架飞船向西面闪电般遁走。或许只是他的视力受了损，可在他看来，那斯罗格飞船飞得歪歪斜斜，要么是驾驶员生怕再挨上一发，要么就是那艘船也挂了彩。

===Original===
Acid smoke wreathed up from the valley making Shann retch and cough. There could be no survivor from the Terran scout, and he did not believe that any Throg had lived to crawl free of the crumpled plate. But there would be other beetles swarming here soon. They would not dare to leave the scene unsearched. He wondered about that scout. Had the pilot been aiming for the Survey camp, the absence of any rider beam from there warning him off so that he made the detour which brought him here? Or had the Throgs tried to blast the Terran ship in the upper atmosphere, crippling it, making this a forced landing? But at least this battle had cost the Throgs, settling a small portion of the Terran debt for the lost camp.

The length of time between Shann’s sighting of the grounded ship and the attack by the Throgs had been so short that he had not really developed any strong hope of rescue to be destroyed by the end of the crippled ship. On the other hand, seeing the Throgs take a beating had exploded his subconscious acceptance of their superiority. He might not have even the resources of a damaged scout at his command. But he did have Taggi, Togi, and his own brain. Since he was fated to permanent exile on Warlock, there might just be some way to make the beetles pay for that.
===Chinese===
酸烟从谷里一缕缕卷上来，呛得沙恩干呕咳嗽。地球侦察船上不可能有幸存者，他也不信有哪个斯罗格人能活着从皱成一团的碟壳里爬出来。但很快就会有别的甲虫人蜂拥而至。他们不敢不把现场搜个底朝天。他琢磨起那艘侦察船来。驾驶员原是不是冲着勘测营地去的？营地方向不见引导波束（rider beam）发出、向他示警，他这才绕道，落到了这里？还是斯罗格人在高层大气里就轰击了地球飞船，把它打残，逼成了这场迫降？但至少这一仗让斯罗格人付了代价——为失陷的营地，替地球人讨还了一小笔债。

从沙恩望见那艘落地的飞船，到斯罗格人发动攻击，中间的工夫太短，他还来不及真正生出什么强烈的获救指望，也就谈不上被这艘残船的覆灭碾碎。另一方面，眼看斯罗格人挨了揍，他潜意识里对他们高人一等的认定，也砰然炸了个粉碎。他手头兴许连一艘受损侦察船的家底都没有。可他有塔吉、托吉，还有他自己的脑子。既然命定要流放沃洛克一世，说不定就真有什么法子，能让甲虫人为此付出代价。

===Original===
He licked his lips. Real action against the aliens would take a lot of planning. Shann would have to know more about what made a Throg a Throg, more than all the wild stories he had heard over the years. There *had* to be some way a Terran could move effectively against a beetle-head. And he had a lot of time, maybe the rest of his life to work out a few answers. That Throg ship lying wrecked at the foot of the cliff﻿ ﻿… perhaps he could do a little investigating before any rescue squad arrived. Shann decided such a move was worth the try and whistled to the wolverines.
===Chinese===
他舔了舔嘴唇。要对这些外星人来真格的，得好好筹划一番。沙恩必须更透彻地了解斯罗格人何以成为斯罗格人——光靠这些年听来的那些离奇传说可不够。总*得*有那么一种办法，能让地球人实实在在地对付一个甲虫脑袋。而他有的是时间——也许余下的一生——来琢磨出几个答案。崖脚底下躺着的那艘斯罗格飞船残骸……赶在救援队到来之前，他未尝不能去做一番小小的查探。沙恩认定这一趟值得一试，便朝狼獾打了个呼哨。

## III To Close Ranks / III 收拢阵线

===Original===
Shann made his way at an angle to avoid the smoking pit cradling the wreckage of the Terran ship. There were no signs of life about the Throg plate as he approached. A quarter of its bulk was telescoped back into the rest, and surely none of the aliens could have survived such a smash, tough as they were reputed to be with those horny carapaces serving them in place of more vulnerable human skin.

He sniffed. There was a nauseous odor heavy on the morning air, one which would make a lasting impression on any human nose. The port door in the black ship stood open, perhaps having burst in the impact against the cliff. Shann had almost reached it when a crackle of chain lightning beat across the ground before him, turning the edge of the buckled entrance panel red.

===Chinese===
沙恩斜插着绕路而行，避开那个还冒着烟的大坑——地球人飞船的残骸正卧在坑里。走近时，那艘斯罗格碟形飞船四周不见半点生命迹象。船体整整四分之一被撞得缩叠进其余部分，任凭这些外星人素以坚韧著称——他们那角质甲壳顶替了人类更为脆弱的皮肤——这样一场猛撞之下也断无幸存之理。

他抽了抽鼻子。清晨的空气里沉甸甸地弥漫着一股令人作呕的气味，任何人类的鼻子都会对它留下终生难忘的印象。黑船的舷门敞开着，也许是撞上崖壁时震开的。沙恩刚要走到近前，一道链状闪电噼啪炸过他面前的地面，把扭曲变形的舱门边缘烧得通红。

===Original===
Shann dropped to the ground, drawing his stunner, knowing at the same moment that such a weapon was about as much use in meeting a blaster as a straw wand would be to ward off a blazing coal. A chill numbness held him as he waited for a second blast to charr the flesh between his shoulders. So there had been a Throg survivor, after all.

But as moments passed and the Throg did not move in to make an easy kill, Shann collected his wits. Only one shot! Was the beetle injured, unable to make sure of even an almost defenseless prey? The Throgs seldom took prisoners. When they did﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
沙恩扑倒在地，抽出击晕枪，同时心里明白：拿这样一件武器去会爆能枪，就跟用一根麦秆小棒去挡一团烧红的炭火差不多。一阵冰凉的麻木攫住了他，他等着第二发把自己肩胛之间的皮肉烧成焦炭。原来终究还是有一个斯罗格人活了下来。

可是一连过了好几个瞬间，那斯罗格人并没有逼上来轻松了结猎物，沙恩这才定下神来。只开了一枪！莫非这甲虫人受了伤，连对付一个几乎毫无反抗之力的猎物都拿不准了？斯罗格人很少抓俘虏。真要抓的时候……

===Original===
The Terran’s lips tightened. He worked his hand under his prone body, feeling for the hilt of his knife. With that he could speedily remove himself from the status of Throg prisoner, and he would do it gladly if there was no hope of escape. Had there been only one charge left in that blaster? Shann could make half a dozen guesses as to why the other had made no move, but that shot had come from behind him, and he dared not turn his head or otherwise make an effort to see what the other might be doing.

Was it only his imagination, or had that stench grown stronger during the last few seconds? Could the Throg be creeping up on him? Shann strained his ears, trying to catch some sound he could interpret. The few clak-claks that had survived the blast about the ship were shrieking overhead, and Shann made one attempt at counterattack.

===Chinese===
地球人抿紧了嘴唇。他把手探到伏地的身子底下，摸索刀柄。有了它，他就能干脆利落地摆脱「斯罗格俘虏」这个身份；倘若逃生无望，他会心甘情愿地这么做。那把爆能枪莫非只剩一发能量了？对方为何按兵不动，沙恩能猜出半打缘由；只是那一枪是从他背后打来的，他不敢回头，也不敢设法看清对方在做什么。

是错觉吗，还是那股恶臭在这最后几秒里愈发浓重了？斯罗格人会不会正悄悄向他潜近？沙恩竖起耳朵，想捕捉一丝他能判读的声响。飞船四周那场爆炸后幸存下来的几只克拉克鸟正在头顶尖啸。沙恩试着发起一次反击。

===Original===
He whistled the wolverines’ call. The pair had not been too willing to follow him down into this valley, and they had avoided the crater at a very wide circle. But if they would obey him now, he just might have a chance.

There! That *had* been a sound, and the smell *was* stronger. The Throg must be coming to him. Again Shann whistled, holding in his mind his hatred for the beetle-head, the need for finishing off that alien. If the animals could pick either thoughts or emotions out of their human companion, this was the time for him to get those unspoken half-orders across.

===Chinese===
他吹出唤狼獾的呼哨。那一对先前本就不大情愿跟他下到这山谷里来，还绕了个极大的圈子避开坠机坑。可只要它们此刻肯听他号令，他就还有一线机会。

那儿！那*确实*是动静，气味也*真的*更浓了。斯罗格人必定正朝他过来。沙恩再次吹响呼哨，心里凝聚着对那甲虫脑袋的仇恨，凝聚着必须干掉那个外星人的决意。倘若动物能从它们的人类伙伴身上捕捉到念头或情绪，此刻正是把那些没说出口的半个命令传达过去的时机。

===Original===
Shann slammed his hand hard against the ground, sent his body rolling, his stunner up and ready.

And now he could see that grotesque thing, swaying weakly back and forth on its thin legs, yet holding a blaster, bringing that weapon up to center it on him. The Throg was hunched over and perhaps to Taggi presented the outline of some four-footed creature to be hunted. For the wolverine male sprang for the horn-shelled shoulders.

===Chinese===
沙恩猛地一掌击地，身子就地翻滚，击晕枪抬起待发。

这下他能看见那怪物了——细瘦的长腿支撑着虚弱摇晃的身子，却仍握着一支爆能枪，正把它抬起来对准自己。斯罗格人佝偻着身子，在塔吉眼里，那轮廓或许正像一头可供猎杀的四足兽。公狼獾果然朝那对角质甲壳的肩膀扑了上去。

===Original===
Under that impact that Throg sagged forward. But Taggi, outraged at the nature of creature he had attacked, squalled and retreated. Shann had had his precious seconds of distraction. He fired, the core of the stun beam striking full into the flat dish of the alien’s “face.”

That bolt, which would have shocked a mammal into insensibility, only slowed the Throg. Shann rolled again, gaining a temporary cover behind the wrecked ship. He squirmed under metal hot enough to scorch his jacket and saw the reflection of a second blaster shot which had been fired seconds late.

===Chinese===
这一撞之下，斯罗格人向前瘫倒。可塔吉弄清自己攻击的是何等怪物之后，又惊又怒，尖叫着退了回来。沙恩得到了那宝贵的几秒分神之机。他开火了，击晕束的核心不偏不倚打进外星人那张扁平如盘的「脸」。

这一击若落在哺乳动物身上，早把它震得不省人事，却只是拖慢了斯罗格人。沙恩再次翻滚，躲到飞船残骸后面取得片刻掩护。他匍匐钻过烫得能烤焦外套的金属，瞥见了迟了几秒射出的第二发爆能枪弹光的反照。

===Original===
Now the Throg had him tied down. But to get at the Terran the alien would have to show himself, and Shann had one chance in fifty, which was better than that of three minutes ago﻿—when the odds had been set at one in a hundred. He knew that he could not press the wolverines in again. Taggi’s distaste was too manifest; Shann had been lucky that the animal had made one abortive attack.

===Chinese===
眼下他被斯罗格人火力钉住了。但外星人要够到这个地球人，就必须现身，而沙恩手里握着五十分之一的胜算——总比三分钟前强，那时赔率还是百分之一。他知道自己没法再驱狼獾上阵了。塔吉的嫌恶太明显；这畜生肯扑上去闹那么一场半途而废的攻击，已是沙恩的侥幸。

===Original===
Perhaps the Terran’s escape and Taggi’s action had made the alien reckless. Shann had no clue to the thinking processes of the nonhuman, but now the Throg staggered around the end of the plate, his digits, which were closer to claws than fingers, fumbling with his weapon. The Terran snapped another shot from his stunner, hoping to slow the enemy down. But he was trapped. If he turned to climb the cliff at his back, the beetle-head could easily pick him off.

A rock hurtled from the heights above, striking with deadly accuracy on the domed, hairless head of the Throg. His armored body crashed forward, struck against the ship, and rebounded to the ground. Shann darted forward to seize the blaster, kicking loose the claws which still grasped it, before he flattened back to the cliff, the strange weapon over his arm, his heart beating wildly.

===Chinese===
也许是地球人的逃脱和塔吉的举动让外星人失去了谨慎。这非人生物的思维过程沙恩无从捉摸，可此刻那斯罗格人已经摇摇晃晃绕过碟形飞船的一端，他那与其说是手指、不如说更近于爪子的指头正笨拙地摆弄武器。地球人又疾射出一记击晕束，指望拖慢敌人。可他仍被困着。若转身攀上背后的崖壁，甲虫脑袋能轻轻松松把他撂倒。

一块石头从高处飞坠而下，分毫不差地砸在斯罗格人那颗圆顶般光秃秃的脑袋上。他披甲的躯体轰然前扑，撞上飞船，又弹回地面。沙恩抢步上前夺下爆能枪，踢开仍攥着它不放的爪子，随即退回崖壁贴平——那件古怪的武器横在臂弯里，心脏狂跳不止。

===Original===
That rock had not bounded down the mountainside by chance; it had been hurled with intent and aimed carefully at its target. And no Throg would kill one of his fellows. Or would he? Suppose orders had been issued to take a Terran prisoner and the Throg by the ship had disobeyed? Then, why a rock and not a blaster bolt?

Shann edged along until the upslanted, broken side of the Throg flyer provided him with protection from any overhead attack. Under that shelter he waited for the next move from his unknown rescuer.

===Chinese===
那块石头绝不是碰巧从山坡上弹滚下来的；它是被人有意掷出、仔细瞄准了目标的。而斯罗格人不会杀害自己的同类。当真不会吗？假如下达过抓一名地球俘虏的命令，而飞船边这个斯罗格人抗命不从呢？可那样的话，为什么用石头而不用爆能枪？

沙恩侧身挪动，直到斯罗格飞行器那向上斜翘、断裂的一侧替他挡住任何来自头顶的攻击。在这处掩蔽之下，他等着那位身份不明的救援者走出下一步。

===Original===
The clak-claks wheeled closer to earth. One lit boldly on the carapace of the inert Throg, shuffling ungainly along that horny ridge. Cradling the blaster, the Terran continued to wait. His patience was rewarded when that investigating clak-clak took off uttering an enraged snap or two. He heard what might be the scrape of boots across rock, but that might also have come from horny skin meeting stone.

Then the other must have lost his footing not too far above. Accompanied by a miniature landslide of stones and earth, a figure slid down several yards away. Shann waited in a half-crouch, his looted blaster covering the man now getting to his feet. There was no mistaking the familiar uniform, or even the man. How Ragnar Thorvald had reached that particular spot on Warlock or why, Shann could not know. But that he was there, there was no denying.

===Chinese===
克拉克鸟盘旋着贴近地面。一只斗胆落在那具一动不动的斯罗格人甲壳上，笨拙地沿角质棱脊蹒跚挪动。地球人抱枪在怀，继续等待。他的耐心得了回报——那只前来查探的克拉克鸟发出一两声怒气冲冲的脆鸣，振翅飞走。他听见一声像是靴底蹭过岩石的动静，不过那也可能只是角质皮肤碰上石头。

接着，上方不远处那人想必是失了脚。随着一阵石块泥土的小规模滑坡，一条人影滑落到几码开外。沙恩半蹲着严阵以待，缴来的爆能枪罩住那个正在起身的人。那身眼熟的制服绝不会认错，连人也绝不会认错。拉格纳·托尔瓦德（Ragnar Thorvald）是怎么到达沃洛克这个地点的、又是为了什么，沙恩无从知晓。可他就在这里，这一点无可否认。

===Original===
Shann hurried forward. It had been when he caught his first sight of Thorvald that he realized just how deep his unacknowledged loneliness had bit. There were two Terrans on Warlock now, and he did not need to know why. But Thorvald was staring back at him with the blankness of non-recognition.

“Who are you?” The demand held something close to suspicion.

===Chinese===
沙恩快步迎上前去。正是在第一眼望见托尔瓦德的刹那，他才明白自己那从不肯承认的孤独咬得有多深。如今沃洛克上有两个地球人了，何须再问为什么。可托尔瓦德回望他的眼神却是一片空白，全无认出的意思。

「你是谁？」这一句问话里带着近乎猜疑的意味。

===Original===
That note in the other’s voice wiped away a measure of Shann’s confidence, threatened something which had flowered in him since he had struck into the wilderness on his own. Three words had reduced him again to Lantee, unskilled laborer.

“Lantee. I’m from the camp﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
对方嗓音里的那一点腔调，抹去了沙恩几分自信，也威胁着自他孤身闯入荒野以来在他心里开出的某种东西。短短三个字，就又把他打回了原形——兰蒂，无技艺的苦工。

「兰蒂。我是营地来的……」

===Original===
Thorvald’s eagerness was plain in his next question: “How many of you got away? Where are the rest?” He gazed past Shann up the plateau slope as if he expected to see the personnel of the camp sprout out of the cloak of grass along the verge.

“Just me and the wolverines,” Shann answered in a colorless voice. He cradled the blaster on his hip, turned a little away from the officer.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德的急切在下一个问题里表露无遗：「你们逃出来多少人？其余人在哪儿？」他的目光越过沙恩，投向高原的上坡，仿佛指望看见营地的人员从坡沿那一袭草色斗篷下破土而出。

「就我和两只狼獾。」沙恩答道，声音平板无色。他把爆能枪挎上胯侧，微微侧身避开军官。

===Original===
“You﻿ ﻿… and the wolverines?” Thorvald was plainly startled. “But﻿ ﻿… where? How?”

“The Throgs hit very early yesterday morning. They caught the rest in camp. The wolverines had escaped from their cage, and I was out hunting them﻿ ﻿…” He told his story baldly.

===Chinese===
「你……和狼獾？」托尔瓦德分明吃了一惊。「可是……在哪儿？怎么回事？」

「斯罗格人昨天一大早动的手。其余人都被堵在了营地里。狼獾从笼子里跑了出去，我正在外面追它们……」他把经过干巴巴地讲了一遍。

===Original===
“You’re sure about the rest?” Thorvald had a thin steel of rage edging his voice. Almost, Shann thought, as if he could turn that blade of rage against one Shann Lantee for being yet alive when more important men had not survived.

“I saw the attack from an upper ridge,” the younger man said, having been put on the defensive. Yet he had a right to be alive, hadn’t he? Or did Thorvald believe that he should have gone running down to meet the beetle-heads with his useless stunner? “They used energy beams﻿ ﻿… didn’t land until it was all over.”

===Chinese===
「其余的人，你确定？」托尔瓦德的声音里镶着一层薄钢似的怒意。沙恩几乎觉得，那道怒刃简直要调转来对付他沙恩·兰蒂——只因比他重要的人都没能活下来，他却还活着。

「我从上面一道山脊看见了那场袭击。」年轻人被逼出了守势，答道。可他有权活着，不是吗？还是说，托尔瓦德认为他本该拿着那把没用的击晕枪冲下去会一会甲虫脑袋？「他们用的是能量光束……直到一切都结束了才降落。」

===Original===
“I knew there was something wrong when the camp didn’t answer our enter-atmosphere signal,” Thorvald said absently. “Then one of those platters jumped us on braking orbit, and my pilot was killed. When we set down on the automatics here I had just time to rig a surprise for any trackers before I took to the hills﻿—”

“The blast got one of them,” Shann pointed out.

===Chinese===
「营地不回应我们的入大气层信号时，我就知道出了事。」托尔瓦德心不在焉地说。「接着一艘那样的碟形飞船在制动轨道上伏击了我们，我的驾驶员阵亡了。靠自动系统在这里降落之后，我只来得及给可能的追踪者布下一份惊喜，便进了山——」

「那一炸报销了他们一个。」沙恩指出。

===Original===
“Yes, they’d nicked the booster rocket; she wouldn’t climb again. But they’ll be back here to pick over the remains.”

Shann looked at the dead Throg. “Thanks for taking a hand.” His tone was as chill as the other’s this time. “I’m heading south﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「是啊，他们击伤了助推火箭，她再也爬不上去了。不过他们还会回到这儿来翻检残骸的。」

沙恩看了看那具斯罗格死尸。「多谢搭了把手。」这一次，他的口气跟对方的一样冷。「我要往南走……」

===Original===
And, he added silently, I intend to keep on that way. The Throg attack had dissolved the pattern of the Survey team. He didn’t owe Thorvald any allegiance. And he had been successfully on his own here since the camp had been overrun.

“South,” Thorvald repeated. “Well, that’s as good a direction as any right now.”

===Chinese===
而且，他默默补上一句，我打算就这么走下去。斯罗格人的袭击已经把勘测队的建制冲得烟消云散。他不欠托尔瓦德任何效忠。况且自营地陷落以来，他孤身一人在这里也一直应付得下来。

「往南。」托尔瓦德重复道。「也好，眼下这个方向不比任何方向差。」

===Original===
But they were not united. Shann found the wolverines and patiently coaxed and wheedled them into coming with him over a circuitous route which kept them away from both ships. Thorvald went up the cliff, swung down again, a supply bag slung over one shoulder. He stood watching as Shann brought the animals in.

Then Thorvald’s arm swept out, his fingers closing possessively about the barrel of the blaster. Shann’s own hold on the weapon tightened, and the force of the other’s pull dragged him partly around.

===Chinese===
但两人并未同心。沙恩找到狼獾，耐着性子连哄带劝，让它们绕开两艘飞船、迂回着跟他走。托尔瓦德登上崖顶，又折返下来，一只补给袋挎在肩头。他站在一旁，看沙恩把牲口们带过来。

这时托尔瓦德的手臂横扫而出，五指一扣，摆出据为己有的架势攥住爆能枪的枪管。沙恩握枪的手也随之收紧，对方拉拽的力道把他带得半转了个身。

===Original===
“Let’s have that﻿—”

“Why?” Shann supposed that because it had been the other’s well-aimed rock which had put the Throg out of commission permanently, the officer was going to claim their only spoils of war as personal booty, and a hot resentment flowered in the younger man.

===Chinese===
「把那个给我——」

「凭什么？」沙恩心想，只因把斯罗格人永久撂倒的那块好准头的石头是对方掷的，这位军官就要把他们唯一一件战利品收作私人缴获；一股火辣辣的怨愤在年轻人心里开了花。

===Original===
“We don’t take that away from here.” Thorvald made the weapon his with a quick twist.

To Shann’s utter astonishment, the Survey officer walked back to kneel beside the dead Throg. He worked the grip of the blaster under the alien’s lax claws and inspected the result with the care of one arranging a special and highly important display. Shann’s protest became vocal. “We’ll need that!”

===Chinese===
「这东西不能从这儿带走。」托尔瓦德手腕一旋，利落地把武器归了自己。

令沙恩错愕不已的是，勘测军官竟走回去跪在那具斯罗格死尸旁边。他把爆能枪的握把塞进外星人松弛的爪子底下，又像布置一件特殊而极其要紧的展品似的，仔细检视成果。沙恩的抗议出了声：「我们会需要它的！」

===Original===
“It’ll do us far more good right where it is﻿ ﻿…” Thorvald paused and then added, with impatience roughening his voice as if he disliked the need for making any explanations, “There is no reason for us to advertise our being alive. If the Throgs found a blaster missing, they’d start thinking and looking around. I want to have a breathing spell before I have to play quarry in one of their hunts.”

Put that way, his action did make sense. But Shann regretted the loss of an arm so superior to their own weapons. Now they could not loot the plateship either. In silence he turned and started to trudge southward, without waiting for Thorvald to catch up with him.

===Chinese===
「它待在原地，对我们的用处大得多……」托尔瓦德顿了顿，又接着说了下去，不耐烦把嗓音磨得粗粝，仿佛他厌恶居然非解释不可，「我们没有任何理由昭告天下自己还活着。要是斯罗格人发现少了一支爆能枪，他们就会开始琢磨、开始四处搜查。我还想在被迫去当他们哪场围猎里的猎物之前，先喘上一口气。」

这么一说，他的做法倒确实有理。可沙恩仍为失去一件远胜他们现有装备的枪械而痛惜。这下连碟形飞船也没法打劫了。他默默转身，迈步向南跋涉而去，并不等托尔瓦德赶上。

===Original===
Once away from the blasted area, the wolverines ranged ahead at their clumsy gallop, which covered ground at a surprising rate of speed. Shann knew that their curiosity made them scouts surpassing any human and that the men who followed would have ample warning of any danger to come. Without reference to his silent trail companion, he sent the animals toward another strip of woodland which would give them cover against the coming of any Throg flyer.

As the hours advanced he began to cast about for a proper night camp. The woods ought to give them a usable site.

===Chinese===
一离开那片炸毁区，狼獾便撒开那副笨拙的疾驰在前头游弋，占地之快出人意料。沙恩知道，那份好奇心使它们成为胜过任何人类的斥候，跟随其后的人对任何将至的危险都能得到充分的预警。他没有征询那沉默赶路同伴的意见，径自把两头牲口赶向另一条林带——那能掩护他们躲过任何斯罗格飞行器的到来。

随着时辰推移，他开始四下物色一处合适的夜营地。这片树林该能给他们一个可用的所在。

===Original===
“This is a water wood,” Thorvald said, breaking the silence for the first time since they had left the wrecks.

Shann knew that the other had knowledge, not only of the general countryside, but of exploring techniques which he himself did not possess, but to be reminded of that fact was an irritant rather than a reassurance. Without answering, the younger man bored on to locate the water promised.

===Chinese===
「这是一片近水的树林。」托尔瓦德开口打破了沉默，这是他们离开残骸以来的头一回。

沙恩知道对方不仅有这一带乡野的学识，还掌握他自己不具备的勘探技术；可被人在这一点上提醒，非但不是宽慰，反倒叫人窝火。年轻人一言不发，闷头往前赶，去找那话里许下的水源。

===Original===
The wolverines found the small lake first and were splashing along its shore when the Terrans caught up. Thorvald went to work, but to Shann’s surprise he did not unstrap the force-blade ax at his belt. Bending over a sapling, he pounded away with a stone at the green wood a few inches above the root line until he was able to break through the slender trunk. Shann drew his own knife and bent to tackle another treelet when Thorvald stopped him with an order: “Use a stone on that, the way I did.”

===Chinese===
狼獾先找到了小湖，两个地球人赶上来时，它们正沿着湖岸蹚水戏浪。托尔瓦德动手干活了，可让沙恩意外的是，他并没有解下腰间那把力场斧（force ax）。他俯身对着一株幼树，抡起石头在根线上方几英寸处一个劲地捶砸那湿青的木身，直到把纤细的树干砸断。沙恩拔出自己的刀，正弯腰去对付另一棵小树，托尔瓦德一声喝止了他：「用石头砸，照我那样。」

===Original===
Shann could see no reason for such a laborious process. If Thorvald did not want to use his ax, that was no reason that Shann could not put his heavy belt knife to work. He hesitated, ready to set the blade to the outer bark of the tree.

“Look﻿—” again that impatient edge in the officer’s tone, the need for explanation seeming to come very hard to the other﻿—“sooner or later the Throgs might just trace us here and find this camp. If so, they are *not* going to discover any traces to label us Terran﻿—”

===Chinese===
沙恩看不出有什么必要如此费事。托尔瓦德不想动用自己的斧头是一回事，但这并不等于沙恩就不能让他那把沉重的腰带刀上阵。他犹豫着，刀刃已经对准了树皮外层。

「听着——」军官的语气里又带出那道不耐烦的锋刃，看样子解释的必要让他分外难受——「斯罗格人早晚有可能追踪到这儿，发现这座营地。真到那时，他们*绝不能*发现任何能把我们标记成地球人的痕迹——」

===Original===
“But who else could we be?” protested Shann. “There is no native race on Warlock.”

Thorvald tossed his improvised stone ax from hand to hand.

===Chinese===
「可我们还能是谁？」沙恩抗辩道。「沃洛克上没有土著种族。」

托尔瓦德把那柄临时凑合的石斧在两手之间抛来抛去。

===Original===
“But do the Throgs know that?”

The implications, the possibilities, in that idea struck home to Shann. Now he began to understand what Thorvald might be planning.

===Chinese===
「可斯罗格人知道吗？」

这念头里的含义和种种可能，重重击中了沙恩。他开始明白托尔瓦德打的算盘了。

===Original===
“Now there is going to be a native race.” Shann made a statement instead of a question and saw that the other was watching him with a new intentness, as if he had at last been recognized as a person instead of rank and file and very low rank at that﻿—Survey personnel.

“There is going to be a native race,” Thorvald affirmed.

===Chinese===
「现在就要有一个土著种族了。」沙恩说出的是陈述句，不是问句。他看见对方正以一种新的专注打量自己，仿佛他终于被承认是一个「人」，而非行伍中的一员——而且还是军衔顶低的那种——勘测队人员。

「就要有一个土著种族了。」托尔瓦德予以确认。

===Original===
Shann resheathed his knife and went to search the pond beach for a suitable stone to use in its place. Even so, he made harder work of the clumsy chopping than Thorvald had. He worried at one sapling after another until his hands were skinned and his breath came in painful gusts from under aching ribs. Thorvald had gone on to another task, ripping the end of a long tough vine from just under the powdery surface of the thick leaf masses fallen in other years.

===Chinese===
沙恩收刀入鞘，去湖滩上寻觅一块合用的石头取而代之。即便如此，这笨拙的砍活他干得也比托尔瓦德吃力。他跟一株又一株幼树死磕，直到双手磨破了皮，气息一阵阵从酸痛的肋下艰难地涌出来。托尔瓦德已转去做另一件事：从往年落积的厚厚叶堆那粉状的表层底下，撕拽出一根坚韧长藤的末端。

===Original===
With this the officer lashed together the tops of the poles, having planted their splintered butts in the ground, so that he achieved a crudely conical erection. Leafy branches were woven back and forth through this framework, with an entrance, through which one might crawl on hands and knees, left facing the lakeside. The shelter they completed was compact and efficient but totally unlike anything Shann had ever seen before, certainly far removed from the domes of the camp. He said so, nursing his raw hands.

===Chinese===
军官把劈裂的杆脚插进地里，用这根藤条把杆顶捆扎在一处，扎出一个粗陋的圆锥形棚架。带叶的枝条在骨架间纵横编缀，留下一个面朝湖边的入口——人得手脚并用地爬进爬出。他们完工的这处栖身棚紧凑而实用，却与沙恩平生所见过的任何东西都全然不同，自然更与营地的那些穹顶相去十万八千里。他这么说了，一面揉着自己那双磨破的手。

===Original===
“An old form,” Thorvald replied, “native to a primitive race on Terra. Certainly the beetle-heads haven’t come across its like before.”

“Are we going to stay here? Otherwise it is pretty heavy work for one night’s lodging.”

===Chinese===
「一种古老的形制。」托尔瓦德答道，「是地球上一个原始种族的本土样式。甲虫脑袋们肯定从没见过这样的东西。」

「我们要在这儿久待吗？不然的话，为一宿落脚，这活计也未免太重了。」

===Original===
Thorvald tested the shelter with a sharp shake. The matted leaves whispered, but the framework held.

“Stage dressing. No, we won’t linger here. But it’s evidence to support our play. Even a Throg isn’t dense enough to believe that natives would make a cross-country trip without leaving evidence of their passing.”

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德猛地一摇，测试棚屋。缠结的叶片簌簌低语，骨架却稳稳当当。

「布景罢了。不，我们不会在这儿逗留。但它是撑起我们这出戏的凭证。再蠢的斯罗格人也不至于相信，土著横穿旷野走上一遭，竟会不留半点过路的痕迹。」

===Original===
Shann sat down with a sigh he made no effort to suppress. He had a vision of Thorvald traveling southward, methodically erecting these huts here and there to confound Throgs who might not ever chance upon them. But already the Survey officer was busy with a new problem.

“We need weapons﻿—”

===Chinese===
沙恩坐下来，发出一声怎么也懒得压制的叹息。他脑中浮出一幅图景：托尔瓦德一路南行，沿途有条不紊地竖起一座座这样的棚屋，去迷惑那些也许根本无缘撞见它们的斯罗格人。可勘测军官已经在忙着琢磨下一个难题了。

「我们需要武器——」

===Original===
“We have our stunners, a force ax, and our knives,” Shann pointed out. He did not add, as he would have liked that they could have had a blaster.

“Native weapons,” Thorvald countered with his usual snap. He went back to the beach and crawled about there, choosing and rejecting stones picked out of the gravel.

===Chinese===
「我们有击晕枪、一把力场斧，还有刀。」沙恩指出。他没有添上一句心里很想说的话——他们本来是能有一支爆能枪的。

「土著武器。」托尔瓦德惯常那般干脆地反驳。他回到湖滩，在砾石间爬来爬去，挑挑拣拣。

===Original===
Shann scooped out a small pit just before their hut and set about the making of a pocket-sized fire. He was hungry and looked longingly now and again to the supply bag Thorvald had brought with him. Dared he rummage in that for rations? Surely the other would be carrying concentrates.

“Who taught you how to make a fire that way?” Thorvald was back from the pond, a selection of round stones about the size of his fist resting between his chest and his forearm.

===Chinese===
沙恩在棚屋跟前刨出一个小坑，动手拢起一小堆巴掌大的火。他饿了，不时眼巴巴地瞟向托尔瓦德带来的那只补给袋。敢去翻找吃食吗？对方总该带着浓缩口粮吧。

「谁教你的这种生火法？」托尔瓦德从池边回来了，胸口与小臂之间兜着一捧挑好的、拳头大小的圆石。

===Original===
“It’s regulation, isn’t it?” Shann countered defensively.

“It’s regulation,” Thorvald agreed. He set down his stones in a row and then tossed the supply bag over to his companion. “Too late to hunt tonight. But we’ll have to go easy on those rations until we can get more.”

===Chinese===
「这是条例里有的，不是吗？」沙恩带着守势回了一句。

「是条例里的。」托尔瓦德认了。他把圆石一排排码好，然后把补给袋抛给同伴。「今晚打猎是来不及了。可这些口粮得省着吃，直到我们能弄到更多。」

===Original===
“Where?” Did Thorvald know of some supply cache they could raid?

“From the Throgs,” the other answered matter of factly.

===Chinese===
「从哪儿弄？」托尔瓦德是不是知道哪处藏着可供劫用的补给？

「从斯罗格人那儿。」对方答得平平常常。

===Original===
“But they don’t eat our kind of food﻿ ﻿…”

“All the more reason for them to leave the camp supplies untouched.”

===Chinese===
「可他们不吃我们这路人吃的东西……」

「正因如此，营地的补给他们更会原封不动地留着。」

===Original===
“The camp?”

For the first time Thorvald’s lips curved in a shadow smile which was neither joyous nor warming. “A native raid on an invaders’ camp. What could be more natural? And we’d better make it soon.”

===Chinese===
「营地？」

托尔瓦德的嘴角头一回弯起一个阴影般的微笑，既无欢喜，也无暖意。「土著袭击入侵者的营地。还有比这更顺理成章的吗？而且我们最好尽快动手。」

===Original===
“But how can we?” To Shann what the other proposed was sheer madness.

“There was once an ancient service corps on Terra,” Thorvald answered, “which had a motto something like this: ‘The improbable we do at once; the impossible takes a little longer.’ What did you think we were going to do? Sulk around out here in the bush and let the Throgs claim Warlock for one of their pirate bases without opposition?”

===Chinese===
「可我们怎么干得成？」在沙恩看来，对方的提议纯属疯狂。

「地球上从前有一支古老的勤务部队。」托尔瓦德答道，「他们的座右铭大意是这么说的：『不大可能的事，我们立刻去办；不可能的事，也只需多花一点时间。』你以为我们打算干什么？在这荒郊野岭里怄气干耗，任凭斯罗格人把沃洛克占作他们的一个海盗基地，而无人反抗？」

===Original===
Since that was the only future Shann had visualized, he was ready enough to admit the truth, only some shade of tone in the officer’s voice kept him from saying so aloud.

===Chinese===
既然沙恩原先所能设想的未来也不过如此，他本大可以承认这一层，只是军官话音里某种说不清道不明的色调，让他没把这话大声说出来。

## IV Sortie / IV 出击

===Original===
Five days later they came up from the south so that this time Shann’s view of the Terran camp was from a different angle. At first sight there had been little change in the general scene. He wondered if the aliens were using the Terran dome shelters themselves. Even in the twilight it was easy to pick out such landmarks as the com dome with the shaft of a broadcaster spearing from its top and the greater bulk of the supply warehouse.

“Two of their small flyers down on the landing field﻿ ﻿…” Thorvald materialized from the shadow, his voice a thread of whisper.

===Chinese===
五天后，他们从南边上来，于是这一次，沙恩是从另一个角度望见地球人营地的。乍看之下，整片景象几乎没什么变化。他揣摩着，那些外星人是不是正拿地球人的穹顶住所自用。即便天色已入暮，也不难辨认出那些地标：通讯穹顶——顶上一根广播天线杆直刺天际——还有体量更大的补给仓库。

“他们那两架小型飞行器落在降落场上了……”托尔瓦德从阴影里现出身形，嗓音细若一缕耳语。

===Original===
By Shann’s side the wolverines were moving restlessly. Since Taggi’s attack on the Throg neither beast would venture near any site where they could scent the aliens. This was the nearest point to which the men could urge either animal, which was a disappointment, for the wolverines would have been an excellent addition to the surprise sortie they planned for tonight, halving the danger for the men.

Shann ran his fingers across the coarse fur on the animals’ shoulders, exerting a light pressure to signal them to wait. But he was not sure of their obedience. The foray was a crazy idea, and Shann wondered again why he had agreed to it. Yet he had gone along with Thorvald, even suggested a few modifications and additions of his own, such as the contents of the crude leaf sack now resting between his knees.

===Chinese===
沙恩身边，两只狼獾躁动不安地挪动。自打塔吉扑袭过那个斯罗格人，这两只家伙说什么也不肯再靠近任何嗅得出外星人气味的地方。这里已是两个人能把它们往前劝到的极限——着实叫人失望，因为若能带上狼獾，今晚策划的这场出其不意的突袭必会如虎添翼，两人承担的风险也能减半。

沙恩的手指抚过两只动物肩上的粗毛，轻轻使了点劲儿，示意它们等候。可他没把握它们是否听话。这场奔袭本就是个疯狂的念头，沙恩又一次纳闷，自己当时怎么就答应了。然而他还是跟着托尔瓦德干了起来，甚至主动提出过几处修改和补充——比如此刻搁在他两膝之间的这只粗制叶袋里装的东西。

===Original===
Thorvald flitted away, seeking his own post to the west. Shann was still waiting for the other’s signal when there arose from the camp a sound to chill the flesh of any listener, a wail which could not have come from the throat of any normal living thing, intelligent being or animal. Ululating in ear-torturing intensity, the cry sank to a faint, ominous echo of itself, to waver up the scale again.

The wolverines went mad. Shann had witnessed their quick kills in the wilds, but this stark ferocity of spitting, howling rage was new. They answered that challenge from the camp, streaking out from under his hands. Yet both animals skidded to a stop before they passed the first dome and were lost in the gloom. A spark glowed for an instant to his right; Thorvald was ready to go, so Shann had no time to try and recall the animals.

===Chinese===
