托尔瓦德闪身而去，到西边寻找自己的位置。沙恩还在等对方的信号，营地里却升起一个声音，任谁听了都会寒彻肌肤——那是一声哀嚎，绝不可能出自任何寻常生物的喉咙，无论是智慧种族还是野兽。嚎声凄厉刺耳，低伏下去时化作自身一缕微弱而不祥的回响，随即又颤巍巍地攀高。

狼獾疯了。沙恩在荒野里见过它们扑杀猎物的迅疾，可这般喷着涎沫、长嚎暴怒的赤裸凶态却是头一回见。它们应下营地传来的那声挑战，从他手底下一窜而出。然而还没跑过第一座穹顶，两只动物便急急刹住，随即没入昏暗之中。他右侧有一点火星倏然一亮——托尔瓦德已准备动手，沙恩再没工夫去追回它们了。

===Original===
He fumbled for those balls of soaked moss in his leaf bag. The chemical smell from them blotted out that alien mustiness which the wind brought from the campsite. Shann readied the first sopping mess in his sling, snapped his fire sparker at it, and had the ball awhirl for a toss almost in one continuous movement. The moss burst into fire as it curved out and fell.

To a witness it might have seemed that the missile materialized out of the air, the effect being better than Shann had hoped.

A second ball for the sling﻿—spark﻿ ﻿… out﻿ ﻿… down. The first had smashed on the ground near the dome of the com station, the force of impact flattening it into a round splatter of now fiercely burning material. And his second, carefully aimed, lit two feet beyond.

===Chinese===
他在叶袋里摸索出那几团浸透的苔藓球。球上散出的化学气味，压过了风从营地吹来的那股外星霉味。沙恩把第一团湿漉漉的玩意儿装上投石索（sling），打着打火器引燃，几乎一气呵成地抡圆掷出。苔藓球划出一道弧线，坠地时轰然起火。

在旁观者眼里，那枚弹体简直像凭空从空气里凝出来的——效果比沙恩指望的还要好。

投石索装上第二团——点火……掷出……落下。第一团砸在通讯站穹顶近旁的地面上，撞击之力把它摊成一摊圆形的东西，此刻正烧得凶猛。而他小心瞄准的第二团，落在两英尺开外。

===Original===
Another wail tearing at the nerves. Shann made a third throw, a fourth. He had an audience now. In the light of those pools of fire the Throgs were scuttling back and forth, their hunched bodies casting weird shadows on the dome walls. They were making efforts to douse the fires, but Shann knew from careful experimentation that once ignited the stuff he had skimmed from the lip of one of the hot springs would go on burning as long as a fraction of its viscid substance remained unconsumed.

Now Thorvald had gone into action. A Throg suddenly halted, struggled frantically, and toppled over into the edge of a fire splotch, legs looped together by the coils of the curious weapon Thorvald had put together on their first night of partnership. Three round stones of comparable weight had each been fastened at the end of a vine cord, and those cords united at a center point. Thorvald had demonstrated the effectiveness of his creation by bringing down one of the small “deer” of the grasslands, an animal normally fleet enough to feel safe from both human and animal pursuit. And those weighted ropes now trapped the Throg with the same efficiency.

===Chinese===
又一声嚎叫撕扯着人的神经。沙恩掷出第三团、第四团。这会儿他有观众了。在一汪汪火光的照映下，斯罗格人来回疾窜，弓耸的身躯在穹顶壁上投下幢幢怪影。他们正奋力扑火，但沙恩经过细心试验早有数：他从一处温泉边沿撇来的那种东西一经点燃，只要那黏稠浆体还有一小点没烧尽，就会一直烧下去。

这时托尔瓦德也出手了。一个斯罗格人猛地收步，拼命挣扎，一头栽进一片火迹的边缘——几圈绳索缠住了他的腿，那正是托尔瓦德在他们结伙的第一夜拼就的那件古怪武器。三块分量相仿的圆石，各自系在一根藤索的末端，几根藤索再汇结于中心一点。托尔瓦德曾用它放倒草原上的一种小“鹿”（deer），当场验证了这件造物的效力——那种动物四蹄如飞，寻常自信无论人类还是野兽都追它不上。而此刻，这几根系石绳索又以同样的高效困住了这个斯罗格人。

===Original===
Having shot his last fireball, Shann ran swiftly to take up a new position, downgrade and to the east of the domes. Here he put into action another of the primitive weapons Thorvald had devised, a spear hurled with a throwing stick, giving it double range and twice as forceful penetration power. The spears themselves were hardly more than crudely shaped lengths of wood, their points charred in the fire. Perhaps these missiles could neither kill nor seriously wound. But more than one thudded home in a satisfactory fashion against the curving back carapace or the softer front parts of a Throg in a manner which certainly shook up and bruised the target. And one of Shann’s victims went to the ground, to lie kicking in a way which suggested he had been more than just bruised.

Fireballs, spears Thorvald had moved too. And now down into the somewhat frantic melee of the aroused camp fell a shower of slim weighted reeds, each provided with a clay-ball head. The majority of those balls broke on landing as the Terrans had intended. So, through the beetle smell of the aliens, spread the acrid, throat-parching fumes of the hot spring water. Whether those fumes had the same effect upon Throg breathing apparatus as they did upon Terran, the attackers could not tell, but they hoped such a bombardment would add to the general confusion.

===Chinese===
抛完最后一个火球，沙恩飞快跑去抢占新位置——在穹顶群的下坡、偏东一侧。在这里，他启用了托尔瓦德设计的又一件原始武器：借投矛器（throwing stick）掷出的长矛，射程加倍，穿透力也强上一倍。矛本身不过是粗粗削成的木棍，矛尖在火里炙得焦硬。这些武器也许既杀不了命，也造不成重伤。但不止一支矛令人满意地闷闷砸中斯罗格人弓曲的背甲或较软的前身，顿教目标震荡受创、乌青一片。沙恩手下的一个受害者更扑倒在地，躺着乱蹬乱踢——那副样子，可不像只挨了几块淤伤。

火球、长矛——托尔瓦德那边也换上了新招。此刻，一阵细长带坠的苇矛又落进惊起的营地那片乱作一团的混战之中，每根苇杆顶上都安着一枚陶丸。那些陶丸多半一落地便应声碎裂，正合两个地球人的心意。于是，透过外星人身上那股甲虫气味，温泉水辛辣呛喉的烟气四下漫开。这些烟气对斯罗格人的呼吸器官是否也像对地球人那样奏效，进攻者无从知晓，但他们指望这轮“弹幕”能给本已够乱的场面再添几分乱。

===Original===
Shann began to space the hurling of his crude spears with more care, trying to place them with all the precision of aim he could muster. There was a limit to their amount of varied ammunition, although they had dedicated every waking moment of the past few days to manufacture and testing. Luckily the enemy had had none of their energy beams at the domes. And so far they had made no move to lift their flyers for retaliation blasts.

But the Throgs were pulling themselves into order. Blaster fire cut the dusk. Most of the aliens were now flat on the ground, sending a creeping line of fire into the perimeter of the camp area. A dark form moved between Shann and the nearest patch of burning moss. The Terran raised a spear to the ready before he caught a whiff of the pungent scent emitted by a wolverine hot with battle rage. He whistled coaxingly. With the Throgs eager to blast any moving thing, the animals were in danger if they prowled about the scene.

===Chinese===
沙恩开始更讲究地掌握粗矛掷出的间隔，竭尽自己所能集聚的瞄准功夫，力求投投落点精准。他们五花八门的弹药终究存量有限，尽管过去这几天，他们把醒着的每一刻都花在了制造和试验上。所幸敌人没在穹顶上架起能量炮。而且到眼下为止，他们也没有升空飞行器实施报复性轰击的动静。

可斯罗格人正在收拢阵脚。爆能枪的火力切开了暮色。大多数外星人此刻平贴在地，朝营区外围射出一条匍匐推进的火线。一团黑影在沙恩与最近一片燃烧的苔藓之间移动。地球人先端矛待发，随后才嗅到一缕刺鼻的气味——那是一只狼獾战怒正炽时发出的体味。他打出一声哄劝的呼哨。斯罗格人正巴不得轰掉一切会动的东西，动物们若在这一带逡巡，可就危险了。

===Original===
That blunt head moved. Shann caught the glint of eyes in a furred mask; it was either Taggi or his mate. Then a puff of mixed Throg and chemical scent from the camp must have reached the wolverine. The animal coughed and fled westward, passing Shann.

Had Thorvald had time and opportunity to make his planned raid on the supply dome? Time during such an embroilment was hard to measure, and Shann could not be sure. He began to count aloud, slowly, as they had agreed. When he reached one hundred he would begin his retreat; on two hundred he was to run for it, his goal the river a half mile from the camp.

===Chinese===
那颗钝脑袋动了。沙恩瞥见毛面上闪着一点眼光——不是塔吉，便是它的老伴。随后，营地里那股混着斯罗格气味与化学气味的味道，想必随一阵风钻进了狼獾的鼻子。那动物咳了一声，打沙恩身边掠过，向西逃去。

托尔瓦德有没有时间、有没有机会对补给穹顶下手，实施他计划中的那场偷袭？这般混战里的时间难于估量，沙恩说不准。他开始照事先的约定，缓缓地出声计数。数到一百，他便开始撤退；数到两百，就得没命地跑——目标是离营地半英里的那条河。

===Original===
The stream would take the fugitives to the sea where fjords cut the coastline into a ragged fringe offering a wealth of hiding places. Throgs seldom explored any territory on foot. For them to venture into that maze would be putting themselves at the mercy of the Terrans they hunted. And their flyers could comb the air above such a rocky wilderness without result.

Shann reached the count of one hundred. Twice a blaster bolt singed ground within distance close enough to make him wince, but most of the fire carried well above his head. All of his spears were gone, save for one he had kept, hoping for a last good target. One of the Throgs who appeared to be directing the fire of the others was facing Shann’s position. And on pure chance that he might knock out that leader, Shann chose him for his victim.

===Chinese===
这条河将载着逃亡者们入海——海边，一道道峡湾（fjord）把海岸线割成一绺参差的须边，有的是藏身之处。斯罗格人极少徒步勘察任何地界。他们若冒险闯进那座迷宫，无异于把自己交到所追猎的地球人手里任凭摆布。而他们的飞行器在那片礁石莽原上空梳篦来去，也终将一无所获。

沙恩数到了一百。爆能枪的能量束两度灼过近旁的地面，近得让他咧嘴缩身，但大部分火力都远远高过他的头顶飞了过去。他的矛已全部掷完，只留下一支，指望着最后一个好靶子。一个看样子正指挥同伴射击的斯罗格人，恰恰面对着沙恩的方位。沙恩纯赌一线机会——兴许能敲掉那个头目——便选中他当了自己的猎物。

===Original===
The Terran had no illusions concerning his own marksmanship. The most he could hope for, he thought, was to have the primitive weapon thud home painfully on the other’s armored hide. Perhaps, if he were very lucky, he could knock the other from his clawed feet. But that chance which hovers over any battlefield turned in Shann’s favor. At just the right moment the Throg stretched his head up from the usual hunched position where the carapace extended over his wide shoulders to protect one of the alien’s few vulnerable spots, the soft underside of his throat. And the fire-sharpened point of the spear went deep.

Throgs were mute, or at least none of them had ever uttered a vocal sound to be reported by Terrans. This one did not cry out. But he staggered forward, forelimbs up, clawed digits pulling at the wooden pin transfixing his throat just under the mandible-equipped jaw, holding his head at an unnatural angle. Without seeming to notice the others of his kind, the Throg came on at a shambling run, straight at Shann as if he could actually see through the dark and had marked down the Terran for personal vengeance. There was something so uncanny about that forward dash that Shann retreated. As his hand groped for the knife at his belt his boot heel caught in a tangle of weed and he struggled for balance. The wounded Throg, still pulling at the spear shaft protruding above the swelling barrel of his chest, pounded on.

===Chinese===
对自己的枪法，这个地球人从不存幻想。他想，最好的指望，也不过是让这件原始武器闷闷地砸在对方的装甲皮壳上，教它吃痛。运气再好些，或许能把对手从那双带爪的脚上掀翻。可任何战场上空都悬着的那点运气，这回偏向了沙恩。恰在那一瞬，那斯罗格人从惯常的弓伏姿态里探起了头——平日里，背甲覆着他宽阔的双肩，护住这种外星人寥寥几处弱点之一：喉头的软腹——而火中炙出尖锋的矛头，深深扎了进去。

斯罗格人是哑的，至少就地球人所报，从没有哪一个发出过一点嗓音。这一只也没有喊出声。他踉跄着向前，前肢高举，带爪的指头扯着那根洞穿喉咙的木楔——楔子正卡在生着大颚的颔下，把他的脑袋拗成一个不自然的角度。那斯罗格人仿佛全没理会自己的同类，一瘸一拐地跑着直冲过来，直取沙恩，倒像他真能看穿黑暗，已把这个地球人记下，要向他讨还个人的血债。这一路猛冲透着说不出的邪气，沙恩不由得退了。他伸手去摸腰间的刀，靴跟却绊进一团乱草，他趔趄着找平衡。那受伤的斯罗格人仍旧扯着从自己鼓凸的桶状胸膛上戳出来的矛杆，咚咚地步步逼近。

===Original===
Shann sprawled backward and was caught in the elastic embrace of a bush, so he did not strike the ground. He fought the grip of prickly branches and kicked to gain solid earth under his feet. Then again he heard that piercing wail from the camp, as chilling as it had been the first time. Spurred by that, he won free. But he could not turn his back on the wounded Throg, keeping rather a sidewise retreat.

Already the alien had reached the dark beyond the rim of the camp. His progress now was marked by the crashing through low brush. Two of the Throgs back on the firing line started up after their leader. Shann caught a whiff of their odor as the wounded alien advanced with the single-mindedness of a robot.

===Chinese===
沙恩仰面栽倒，被一丛灌木弹性的怀抱兜住，总算没磕在地上。他挣开多刺枝条的纠缠，蹬踏着想找到脚下坚实的地面。恰在这时，营地那边又响起那声穿耳的哀嚎，同第一回一样令人胆寒。受这一激，他挣脱了出来。但他不敢把后背亮给那个受伤的斯罗格人，宁可侧着身子后退。

那外星人已经抵达营地边缘之外的黑暗。他此刻的行进，全凭撞开矮树丛的哗啦声来标记。火力线那头的两个斯罗格人一跃而起，追赶他们的头领。受伤的外星人抱着机器人般的一根筋步步进逼，沙恩又闻到了他们那股气味。

===Original===
It would be best to head for the river. Tall grass twisted about the Terran’s legs as he began to run. In spite of the gloom, he hesitated to cross that open space. At night Warlock’s peculiar vegetation displayed a very alien attribute﻿—ten﻿ ﻿… twenty varieties of grass, plant, and tree emitted a wan phosphorescence, varying in degree, but affording each an aura of light. And the path before Shann now was dotted by splotches of that radiance, not as brilliant as the chemical-born flames the attackers had kindled in the camp, but as quick to betray the unwary who passed within their dim circles. And there had never been any reason to believe that Throg powers of sight were less than human; there was perhaps some evidence to the contrary. Shann crouched, charting the clumps ahead for a zigzag course which would take him to at least momentary safety in the river bed.

===Chinese===
还是奔河边去最妥。高草缠绕着地球人的双腿，他开始奔跑。尽管天色晦暗，要横穿那片开阔地，他仍不免踌躇。入夜之后，沃洛克特有的草木会显出一种十足异星的品性——十来种……二十来种草、灌、树都散发着惨淡的磷光，明暗程度不一，却各自笼出一圈光晕。此刻沙恩面前的路径上，正星星点点布着那种光斑，虽不及进攻者在营地点起的化学火焰那般明亮，却一样会立刻出卖踏入那圈微光的不小心的人。何况，从来就没有理由相信斯罗格人的视力不如人类，说不定还有相反的证据。沙恩伏低身子，把前方一丛丛光点看在眼里，规划出一条之字形的路线，好带他抵达河床中——至少是片刻的安全。

===Original===
Perhaps a mile downstream was the transport the Terrans had cobbled together no earlier than this afternoon, a raft Thorvald had professed to believe would support them to the sea which lay some fifty Terran miles to the west. But now he had to cover that mile.

The wolverines? Thorvald? There was one lure which might draw the animals on to the rendezvous. Taggi had brought down a “deer” just before they had left the raft. And instead of allowing both beasts to feast at leisure, Shann had lashed the carcass to the shaky platform of wood and brush, putting it out to swing in the current, though still moored to the bank.

Wolverines always cached that part of the kill which they did not consume at the first eating, usually burying it. He had hoped that to leave the carcass in such a way would draw both animals back to the raft when they were hungry. And they had not fed particularly well that day.

===Chinese===
下游约一英里处，泊着两个地球人今天下午才拼凑出来的那只“运输船”——一只木筏（raft）。托尔瓦德口口声声说自己相信，这筏子载得动他们，一路漂到西边约五十地球英里外的大海。可是眼下，这一英里路，他得先走完。

狼獾呢？托尔瓦德呢？要说引那两只动物赴会，倒有一样诱饵。就在他们离开木筏之前，塔吉刚放倒过一只“鹿”。沙恩没有让两只家伙从容饱餐，而是把那具尸首捆在木头枝条搭成的、摇摇晃晃的平台上，推到水流里荡着，只是仍系在岸边。

狼獾素有窖藏的习惯——头一顿吃不完的猎获，它们总会存起来，通常是埋进土里。他指望这样搁下那具尸首，等两只动物饿了，自会回到木筏上来。何况那天，它们也没吃得特别饱。

===Original===
Thorvald? Well, the Survey officer had made it very plain during the past five days of what Shann had come to look upon as an uneasy partnership that he considered himself far abler to manage in the field, while he had grave doubts of Shann’s efficiency in the direction of survival potential.

The Terran started along the pattern of retreat he had laid out to the river bed. His heart pounded as he ran, not because of the physical effort he was expending, but because again from the camp had come that blood-freezing howl. A lighter line marked the lip of the cut in which the stream was set, something he had not foreseen. He threw himself down to crawl the last few feet, hugging the earth.

===Chinese===
至于托尔瓦德？唉，过去这五天里——这段关系，沙恩已渐渐看成一门别扭的搭伙——这位勘测队军官把话挑得再明白不过：论野外本事，他自视远高一筹；而对沙恩在“生存潜质”方面的能耐，他则疑虑重重。

地球人循着自己拟好的撤退路线，朝河床进发。他一路奔跑，心口咚咚直撞，倒不是因为耗费体力，而是营地那边又传来了那声冻僵血液的嚎叫。一道较亮的线勾出了那条嵌着溪水的河沟的唇沿——这是他没预见到的。他扑倒在地，紧贴泥土，爬完最后几英尺。

===Original===
That very pale luminescence was easily accounted for by what lay below. Shann licked his lips and tasted the sting of sap smeared on his face during his struggle with the bushes. While the strip of meadow behind him now had been spotted with light plants, the cut below showed an almost solid line of them stringing willow-wise along the water’s edge. To go down at this point was simply to spotlight his presence for any Throg on his trail. He could only continue along the upper bank, hoping to finally find an end to the growth of luminescent vegetation below.

Shann was perhaps five yards from the point where he had come to the river, when a commotion behind made him freeze and turn his head cautiously. The camp was half hidden, and the fires there must be dying. But a twisting, struggling mass was rolling across the meadow in his general direction.

===Chinese===
那道极淡的微光，缘由一看便知，就在沟底。沙恩舔舔嘴唇，尝到一丝与灌木丛搏斗时抹上脸的树汁的辛辣。他身后那条草带如今已星星点点缀着发光植物（light plant），脚下的沟里却几乎是密实的一线，柳树似的沿水缘排开。从这里下沟，无异于给自己打上聚光灯，把行踪亮给任何尾随而至的斯罗格人。他只能沿着高岸继续走，只盼前头终能让这片发光的草木到头。

沙恩离他来到河边的那处地方约莫还有五码，身后一阵骚动让他僵住，小心地回过头去。营地隐去了一半，那边的火想必正在熄灭。可一团扭动挣扎的东西，正朝他这个大方向滚过草场而来。

===Original===
Thorvald fighting off an attack? The wolverines? Shann drew his legs under him, ready to erupt into a counteroffensive. He hesitated between drawing stunner or knife. In his brush with the injured Throg at the wreck the stunner had had little impression on the enemy. And now he wondered if his blade, though it was super-steel at its toughest, could pierce any joint in the armored bodies of the aliens.

There was surely a fight in progress. The whole crazily weaving blot collapsed and rolled down upon three bright light plants. Dull sheen of Throg casing was revealed﻿ ﻿… no sign of fur, or flesh, or clothing. Two of the aliens battling? But why?

===Chinese===
是托尔瓦德在击退袭击？还是狼獾？沙恩把双腿收拢到身下，蓄势待发，随时准备暴起反攻。他在拔击晕枪还是拔刀之间迟疑。在残骸旁与那个受伤斯罗格人的那次纠缠里，击晕枪对敌人几乎没留下什么印象。此刻他又犯疑：自己这把刀——尽管是再坚韧不过的超级钢——能不能扎进外星人装甲躯体的任何一处关节。

那边分明有一场搏斗正在进行。那团歪歪扭扭的乱影整个塌倒，滚压上三株明亮的发光植物。露出了斯罗格甲壳的哑光……却不见皮毛、血肉，也不见衣物。两个外星人在自相残杀？可为什么？

===Original===
One of those figures got up stiffly, bent over the huddle still on the ground, and pulled at something. The wooden shaft of Shann’s spear was wanly visible. And the form on the ground did not stir as that was jerked loose. The Throg leader dead? Shann hoped so. He slid his knife back into the sheath, tapped the hilt to make sure it was firmly in place, and crawled on. The river, twisting here and there, was a promising pool of dusky shadow ahead. The bank of willow-things was coming to an end, and none too soon. For when he glanced back again he saw another Throg run across the meadow, and he watched them lift their fellow, carrying him back to camp.

The Throgs might seem indestructible, but he had put an end to one, aided by luck and a very rough weapon. With that to bolster his self-confidence to a higher notch, Shann dropped by cautious degrees over the bank and down to the water’s edge. When his boots splashed into the oily flood he began to tramp downstream, feeling the pull of the water, first ankle high and then about his calves. This early in the season they did not have to fear floods, and hereabouts the stream was wide and shallow, save in mid-current at the center point.

===Chinese===
其中一条身影直挺挺地爬起来，俯向仍瘫在地上的那团，拽住什么东西往外拉。沙恩那支矛的木杆隐约可见。矛被猛地拽脱时，地上的身形纹丝不动。那个斯罗格人头目死了？沙恩但愿如此。他把刀插回鞘中，敲敲柄头确认插牢，继续向前爬。小河弯来绕去，在前方积成一汪诱人的暗影池塘。那排柳状植物的堤岸眼看要到头了，也正是时候——因为他再回头张望时，正看见又一个斯罗格人奔过草场，随后他们抬起同伴，把他抬回了营地。

斯罗格人看上去也许刀枪不入，可他到底送走了一个——靠运气帮忙，外加一件粗陋得很的武器。这份战绩把他的自信又垫高一级。沙恩小心翼翼、一级一级地翻下堤岸，下到水边。靴子噼啪蹚进油汪汪的河水，他便开始沿下游跋涉，觉出水流的拽力，先是没踝，继而又围上腿肚。时值季初，还不用怕洪水，这一带的河又宽又浅，唯有正中央的主流是例外。

===Original===
Twice more he had to skirt patches of light plants, and once a young tree stood bathed in radiance with a pinkish tinge instead of the usual ghostly gray. Within the haze which tented the drooping branches, flitted small glittering, flying things; and the scent of its half-open buds was heavy on the air, neither pleasant nor unpleasant in Shann’s nostrils, merely different.

He dared to whistle, a soft call he hoped would carry along the cut between the high banks. But, though he paused and listened until it seemed that every cell in his thin body was occupied in that act, he heard no answering call from the wolverines, nor any suggestion that either the animals or Thorvald were headed in the direction of the raft.

===Chinese===
他又两次绕开发光植物的斑块，还有一回，一株小树浸在光华里，泛着淡红，而非惯常那种幽灵似的灰白。低垂的枝条笼在光雾帐篷里，有细小的、亮闪闪的飞虫般的东西翩跹出没；半绽的花蕾气味沉沉地压在空气上，在沙恩鼻中谈不上好闻也谈不上难闻，只是别具一格。

他大着胆子打了个呼哨——一声轻唤，指望它能顺着高岸之间的河沟传出去。可是，他停下来听了又听，直听到仿佛自己瘦削身体里的每一个细胞都投入了倾听，仍没听见狼獾的回应，也没有半点迹象表明动物或托尔瓦德正朝木筏的方向去。

===Original===
What was he going to do if none of the others joined him downstream? Thorvald had said not to linger there past daylight. Yet Shann knew that unless he actually sighted a Throg patrol splashing after him he would wait until he made sure of the others’ fate. Both Taggi and Togi were as important to him as the Survey officer. Perhaps more so, he told himself now, because he understood them to a certain degree and found companionship in their undemanding company which he could not claim from the man.

Why *did* Thorvald insist upon their going on to the seashore? To Shann’s mind his own first plan of holing up back in the eastern mountains was better. Those heights had as many hiding places as the fjord country. But Thorvald had suddenly become so set on this westward trek that he had given in. As much as he inwardly rebelled when he took them, he found himself obeying the older man’s orders. It was only when he was alone, as now, that he began to question both Thorvald’s motives and his authority.

===Chinese===
要是其余几位一个也不来下游同他会合，他打算怎么办？托尔瓦德说过，天一亮就别在那里耽搁。可沙恩心里有数：除非亲眼看见一支斯罗格巡逻队蹚着水追来，否则他一定等到弄清其余几位的下落为止。塔吉和托吉在他心里的分量，同那位勘测队军官一般重。此刻他甚至对自己说，或许还更重些——因为对它们，他多少还能懂得几分；同它们作伴，它们无所苛求，他却能得着一份相依，这是从那个人身上讨不来的。

托尔瓦德到底*为什么*非要他们继续赶往海岸？在沙恩看来，自己起初那个退回东部山区躲藏的方案更好。那片高地的藏身之处，不比峡湾地带少。可托尔瓦德忽然铁了心要走这趟西行，他只好让步。尽管每次领受命令时他心里都在造反，他却发现自己次次服从了这位年长者的号令。只有独处的时候——比如眼下——他才开始怀疑托尔瓦德的动机，连同他的权威。

===Original===
Three sprigs of a light bush set in a triangle. Shann paused and then climbed out on the bank, shaking the water from his boots as Taggi might shake such drops from a furred limb. This was the sign they had set to mark their rendezvous point, but﻿ ﻿…

Shann whirled, drawing his stunner. The raft was a dark blob on the surface of the water some feet farther on. And now it was bobbing up and down violently. That was not the result of any normal tug of current. He heard an indignant squeal and relaxed with a little laugh. He need not have worried about the wolverines; that bait had drawn them all right. Both of them were now engaged in eating, though they had to conduct their feast on the rather shaky foundation of the makeshift transport.

===Chinese===
三小枝发光的灌木，插成一个三角。沙恩停了停，随即爬上岸，甩掉靴子上的水，那架势活像塔吉抖落毛腿上的水珠。这正是他们约定标出会合点的记号，可是……

沙恩霍地转身，拔出击晕枪。木筏是往前几英尺外水面上的一团黑影，此刻正剧烈地上下颠簸。那绝不是水流寻常拖拽的结果。他听见一声愤愤的尖叫，松了口气，轻轻笑出声来。关于狼獾，他是白操心了——那饵把它们引得妥妥帖帖。两个家伙这会儿正埋头大嚼，只是这场盛宴，须在那只临时“运输船”相当摇晃的底子上进行。

===Original===
They paid no attention as he waded out, pulling at the anchor cord as he went. The wind must have carried his familiar scent to them. As the water climbed to his shoulders Shann put one hand on the outmost log of the raft. One of the animals snarled a warning at being disturbed. Or had that been at him?

Shann stood where he was, listening intently. Yes, there was a splashing sound from upstream. Whoever followed his own recent trail was taking no care to keep that pursuit a secret, and the pace of the newcomer was fast enough to spell trouble.

===Chinese===
他蹚水出去时，它们理也不理；他一路走，一路收拽锚绳。风想必早把他熟悉的气味送到了它们那儿。水漫到肩头时，沙恩一只手搭上木筏最外层的圆木。一只狼獾受了搅扰，低吼一声警告。要么——那一声原是冲他来的？

沙恩立在原地，凝神细听。没错，上游有蹚水声。无论谁在循着他新留下的踪迹赶来，都全不打算把这场追踪瞒住，而那来人的脚程，快得足以预示麻烦。

===Original===
Throgs? Tensely the Terran waited for some reaction from the wolverines. He was sure that if the aliens had followed him, both animals would give warning. Save when they had gone wild upon hearing that strange wail from the camp, they avoided meeting the enemy.

But from all sounds the animals had not stopped feeding. So the other was no beetle-head. On the other hand, why would Thorvald so advertise his coming, unless the need for speed was greater than caution? Shann drew taut the mooring cord, bringing out his knife to saw through that tough length. A figure passed the three-sprig signal, ran onto the raft.

===Chinese===
是斯罗格人？地球人紧张地等着狼獾作出反应。他确信，若是外星人跟了来，两只动物必定告警。除了听见营地那声怪嚎而发狂的那一回，它们素来避免同敌人照面。

可听动静，动物们根本没停下进食。这样看来，来者不是甲虫脑袋。但话说回来，托尔瓦德为什么要把自己的到来张扬得人尽皆知？除非赶路的急迫已经压倒了谨慎。沙恩拽紧系筏的藤索，抽出刀去锯那段坚韧的绳索。一条身影掠过三枝信号的标记，奔上了木筏。

===Original===
“Lantee?” The call came in a hoarse, demanding whisper.

“Here.”

“Cut loose. We have to get out of here!”

===Chinese===
“兰蒂？”唤声嘶哑，是一句不由分说的低语。

“在这儿。”

“砍断绳。我们得离开这儿！”

===Original===
Thorvald flung himself forward, and together the men scrambled up on the raft. The mangled carcass plunged into the water, dislodged by their efforts. But before the wolverines could follow it, the mooring vine snapped, and the river current took them. Feeling the raft sway and begin to spin, the wolverines whined, crouched in the middle of what now seemed a very frail craft.

Behind them, far away but too clear, sounded that eerie howling, topping the sigh of the night wind.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德一个箭步抢上，两人一起爬上木筏。那具被撕烂的鹿尸被他们这一挤，翻落水中。可还没等狼獾追上去，系泊的藤索应声而断，河水便把他们带走了。木筏晃起来、打起转，狼獾呜呜哀叫，蜷伏在这只如今看来单薄至极的筏子中央。

身后远处，却又清晰得过分，那骇人的嚎叫再度响起，盖过了夜风的叹息。

===Original===
“I saw﻿—” Thorvald gasped, pausing as if to catch full lungfuls of air to back his words, “they have a ‘hound!’ That’s what you hear.”

===Chinese===
“我看见了——”托尔瓦德喘着气顿了顿，仿佛要吸足满肺的空气来给下面的话撑腰，“他们有一头‘猎犬’（hound）！你听见的就是它。”

## V Pursuit / V 追击

===Original===
As the raft revolved slowly it also slipped downstream at a steadily increasing pace, for the current had them in hold. The wolverines pressed close to Shann until the musky scent of their fur, their animal warmth, enveloped him. One growled deep in its throat, perhaps in answer to that wind-borne wail.
===Chinese===
木筏缓缓打着转，顺流滑下的速度越来越快——急流已经把他们攥在了手心。狼獾紧紧挤在沙恩身边，皮毛的麝香气和兽体的暖意把他裹了个严实。其中一只在喉咙深处低吼了一声，也许是在回应那随风飘来的嚎叫。

===Original===
“Hound?” Shann asked.

Beside him in the dark Thorvald was working loose one of the poles they had readied to help control the raft’s voyaging. The current carried them along, but there was a need for those lengths of sapling to fend them free from rocks and water-buried snags.

“What hound?” the younger man demanded more sharply when there came no immediate answer.
===Chinese===
“猎犬？”沙恩问。

黑暗中，托尔瓦德正在他身边抽出一根撑杆——那是他们预先备好，用来协助掌控木筏行程的家伙。水流载着他们前行，可还得靠这几根树苗似的长杆，才撑得开岩石和水下的暗桩。

“什么猎犬？”见没人马上回答，年轻人追问了一句，语气更冲了。

===Original===
“The Throgs’ tracker. But why did they import one?” Thorvald’s puzzlement was plain in his tone. He added a moment later, with some of his usual firmness, “We may be in for bad trouble now. Use of a hound means an attempt to take prisoners﻿—”

“Then they do not know that we are here, as Terrans, I mean?”

Thorvald seemed to be sorting out his thoughts when he replied to that. “They could have brought a hound here just on chance that they might miss one of us in the initial mop-up. Or, if they believe we are natives, they could want a specimen for study.”
===Chinese===
“斯罗格人的追踪兽。可他们为什么要运一头到这儿来？”托尔瓦德话音里的困惑再明白不过。片刻之后他又开口，恢复了几分惯常的果决：“现在我们恐怕要碰上大麻烦了。动用猎犬，意味着他们想抓活的——”

“这么说，他们并不知道我们在这儿？我是说，不知道我们是地球人？”

托尔瓦德回答之前，似乎先把思绪理了一遍。“他们把猎犬带来，也可能只是防着初步清剿时漏掉我们哪一个。再不，就是他们拿我们当了土著，想弄一个活体标本回去研究。”

===Original===
“Wouldn’t they just blast down Terrans on sight?”

Shann saw the dark blot which was Thorvald’s head shake in negation.

“They might need a live Terran﻿—badly and soon.”
===Chinese===
“见了地球人，他们难道不会当场轰掉？”

沙恩看见托尔瓦德那颗头颅的黑影摇了摇，表示否定。

“他们可能需要一个活的地球人——要得很急，越快越好。”

===Original===
“Why?”

“To operate the camp call beam.”

Shann’s momentary bewilderment vanished. He knew enough of Survey procedure to guess the reason for such a move on the part of the aliens.
===Chinese===
“要活的干什么？”

“操纵营地的呼叫波束。”

沙恩一闪而过的茫然顿时消散。勘测队的规程他懂得不少，足以猜出外星人这一步棋的用意。

===Original===
“The settler transport?”

“Yes, the ship. She won’t planet here without the proper signal. And the Throgs can’t give that. If they don’t take her, their time’s run out before they have even made a start here.”

“But how could they know that the transport is nearly due? When we intercept their calls they’re pure gibberish to us. Can they read our codes?”
===Chinese===
“移民运输舰？”

“对，就是那艘船。没有正确的信号，她不会降落到这里。而斯罗格人发不出那个信号。要是抓不下她，他们在这儿连头都没开，日子就先到头了。”

“可他们怎么会知道运输舰快到了？我们截听他们的通讯，只听得一锅乱码。他们能破我们的密码？”

===Original===
“The supposition is that they can’t. Only, concerning Throgs, all we know is supposition. Anyway, they do know the routine for establishing a Terran colony, and we can’t alter that procedure except in small nonessentials,” Thorvald said grimly. “If that transport doesn’t pick up the proper signal to set down here on schedule, her captain will call in the patrol escort﻿ ﻿… then exit one Throg base. But if the beetle-heads can trick the ship in and take her, then they’ll have a clear five or six more months here to consolidate their own position. After that it would take more than just one patrol cruiser to clear Warlock; it will require a fleet. So the Throgs will have another world to play with, and an important one. This lies on a direct line between the Odin and Kulkulkan systems. A Throg base on such a trade route could eventually cut us right out of this quarter of the galaxy.”
===Chinese===
“推测是不能。只不过，关于斯罗格人，我们所知道的一切全都只是推测。反正，建立地球殖民地的那套规程他们清楚得很，除了些无关紧要的小节，我们改不动它。”托尔瓦德说得冷硬。“运输舰要是收不到让它按时降落的正确信号，舰长就会召来护航的巡逻舰……然后，一个斯罗格基地就此退场。可要是甲虫脑袋们能把那艘船诓进来、一举拿下，他们就能在这儿干干净净再待上五六个月，从容经营自家的阵地。到那时，要清剿沃洛克，一艘巡逻舰就不顶事了，得整支舰队来。这样斯罗格人又多了一个可以摆弄的世界，还是个要紧的世界。这颗行星正卡在奥丁（Odin）星系与库库尔坎（Kulkulkan）星系之间的直通航线上。在这样一条商路上设一处斯罗格基地，迟早会把我们整个挤出银河系的这一片。”

===Original===
“So you think they want to capture us in order to bring the transport in?”

“By our type of reasoning, that would be a logical move﻿—*if* they know we are here. They haven’t too many of those hounds, and they don’t risk them on petty jobs. I’d hoped we’d covered our trail well. But we had to risk that attack on the camp﻿ ﻿… I needed the map case!” Again Thorvald might have been talking to himself. “Time﻿ ﻿… and the right maps﻿—” he brought his fist down on the raft, making the platform tremble﻿—“that’s what I have to have now.”
===Chinese===
“所以你认为，他们想抓住我们，是为了把运输舰引进来？”

“照我们这路人的推理，这倒是合乎逻辑的一步——*如果*他们知道我们在这儿的话。那种猎犬他们没有多少条，不会拿去干小差事。我原指望我们已经把行迹掩盖得够好了。可我们不得不冒那次袭击营地的险……我需要那只地图盒！”托尔瓦德这话又一次像在自言自语。“时间……还有对路的地图——”他一拳砸在筏面上，砸得整个平台直颤——“这才是我现在非有不可的东西。”

===Original===
Another patch of light-willows stretched along the riverbanks, and as they sailed through that ribbon of ghostly radiance they could see each other’s faces. Thorvald’s was bleak, hard, his eyes on the stream behind them as if he expected at any moment to see a Throg emerge from the surface of the water.

“Suppose that thing﻿—” Shann pointed upstream with his chin﻿—“follows us? What is it anyway?” Hound suggested Terran dog, but he couldn’t stretch his imagination to believe in a working cooperation between Throg and any mammal.
===Chinese===
河岸两侧又延展开一片光柳（light-willow），木筏驶过那条幽幽发光的光带时，两人能看清彼此的脸了。托尔瓦德的脸冷峻而坚硬，两眼盯着身后的水路，仿佛随时等着一个斯罗格人从水面上冒出来。

“假如那东西——”沙恩用下巴朝上游点了点——“追了上来呢？它到底是什么？”猎犬这个词让人联想到地球的狗，可他无论如何没法想象，斯罗格人竟能同任何哺乳动物共事。

===Original===
“A rather spectacular combination of toad and lizard, with a few other grisly touches, is about as close as you can get to a general description. And that won’t be too accurate, because like the Throgs its remote ancestors must have been of the insect family. If the thing follows us, and I think we can be sure that it will, we’ll have to take steps. There is always this advantage﻿—those hounds cannot be controlled from a flyer, and the beetle-heads never take kindly to foot slogging. So we won’t have to expect any speedy chase. If it slips its masters in rough country, we can try to ambush it.” In the dim light Thorvald was frowning. “I flew over the territory ahead on two sweeps, and it is a queer mixture. If we can reach the rough country bordering the sea, we’ll have won the first round. I don’t believe that the Throgs will be in a hurry to track us in there. They’ll try two alternatives to chasing us on foot. One, use their energy beams to rake any suspect valley, and since there are hundreds of valleys all pretty much alike, that will take some time. Or they can attempt to shake us out with a dumdum should they have one here, which I doubt.”
===Chinese===
“大致说，它算是蟾蜍和蜥蜴一场颇为触目的联姻，再添几笔别的可怖点缀——这描述顶多也就到这个份上。而且未必准确，因为和斯罗格人一样，它远古的祖先必定也出自昆虫一族。那东西要是追上来——我想这一点大可以放心，它一定会的——我们就得拿出对策。好在有一条优势：那种猎犬没法从飞行器上遥控，而甲虫脑袋又向来不肯吃徒步行军这份苦。所以我们不必担心会有什么迅雷般的追击。它要是被主人甩在崎岖地带掉了队，我们倒可以试试伏它一手。”幽暗的光里，托尔瓦德皱着眉。“前面的地界我驾机来回扫过两趟，那是个古怪的混合体。只要能抵达滨海的崎岖地带，第一回合就算我们赢了。我不信斯罗格人会急着追踪我们进那片地方。除了徒步追赶，他们还有两条路可试。其一，用能量束把每一条可疑的山谷都犁一遍——好在成百条山谷条条都差不多，这得耗些时日。其二，要是他们手头有达姆武器（dumdum），也可以用它把我们震出来，不过我怀疑他们没带来。”

===Original===
Shann tensed. The stories of the effects of the Throg’s dumdum weapon were anything but pretty.

“And to get a dumdum,” Thorvald continued as if he were discussing a purely theoretical matter and not a threat of something worse than death, “They’ll have to bring in one of their major ships. Which they will hesitate to do with a cruiser near at hand. Our own danger spot now is the section we should strike soon after dawn tomorrow if the rate of this current is what I have timed it. There is a band of desert on this side of the mountains. The river gorge deepens there and the land is bare. Let them send a ship over and we could be as visible as if we were sending up flares﻿—”
===Chinese===
沙恩绷紧了。关于斯罗格人达姆武器效力的种种传闻，可没有一样是好听的。

“而要把达姆武器运来，”托尔瓦德接着说，那口气仿佛在讨论一件纯理论的事，而不是一样比死还可怕的威胁，“他们就得调一艘主力舰过来。巡逻舰近在咫尺，他们不会轻易这么做。我们眼下的险地，是明天拂晓后不久便会撞上的那一段——如果这段流速和我计的时不差的话。山脉的这一侧有一条沙漠带。到了那里河峡愈切愈深，地表寸草不生。让他们派一艘飞船过来，我们就显眼得跟自己放信号弹上天一样——”

===Original===
“How about taking cover now and going on only at night?” suggested Shann.

“Ordinarily, I’d say yes. But with time pressing us now, no. If we keep straight on, we could reach the foothills in about forty hours, maybe less. And we have to stay with the river. To strike across country there without good supplies and on foot is sheer folly.”
===Chinese===
“现在先寻个地方藏起来，只在夜里赶路，行不行？”沙恩提议。

“按常理，我会说行。可眼下时间逼人，不行。照直赶路，大约四十个小时，兴许还不到，就能到山麓丘陵。而且我们必须跟着河走。补给不足，又要靠两条腿，想在那样的地界横穿旷野，纯属蠢行。”

===Original===
Two days. With perhaps the Throgs unleashing their hound on land, combing from their flyers. With a desert﻿ ﻿… Shann put out his hands to the wolverines. The prospect certainly didn’t seem anywhere near as simple as it had the night before when Thorvald had planned this escape. But then the Survey officer had left out quite a few points which were not pertinent. Was he also leaving out other essentials? Shann wanted to ask, but somehow he could not.
===Chinese===
两天。也许斯罗格人会在陆地上放出猎犬，会驾着飞行器来回梳篦。外加一片沙漠……沙恩朝狼獾伸出手去。这番前景，怎么看都比头天夜里托尔瓦德筹划这场逃亡时显得复杂得多。只是当时那位勘测队军官略去了不少无关紧要的枝节。他是不是也略去了别的要紧东西？沙恩想问，可不知怎么就是问不出口。

===Original===
After a while he dozed, his head resting on his knees. He awoke, roused out of a vivid dream, a dream so detailed and so deeply impressed in a picture on his mind that he was confused when he blinked at the riverbank visible in the half-light of early dawn.

Instead of that stretch of earth and ragged vegetation now gliding past him as the raft angled along, he should have been fronting a vast skull stark against the sky﻿—a skull whose outlines were oddly inhuman, from whose eyeholes issued and returned flying things while its sharply protruding lower jaw was lapped by water. In color that skull had been a violent clash of bloodred and purple. Shann blinked again at the riverbank, seeing transposed on it still that ghostly haze of bone-bare dome, cavernous eyeholes and nose slit, fanged jaws. That skull was a mountain, or a mountain was a skull﻿—and it was important to him; he must locate it!
===Chinese===
过了一阵，他头枕膝盖打起了盹。再醒来，是从一场鲜活的梦里被拽出来的——那梦细致入微，像一幅图画深深烙进他脑中，以致他望着拂晓微光里隐约可辨的河岸时，一时竟分不清身在何处。

木筏斜斜前行，此刻正从他身边滑过去的，本该是一片荒土和参差的草木；他本该面对的，是一座兀然衬在天幕上的巨大骷髅——那骷髅的轮廓怪异得不似人类，眼窟窿里有会飞的东西出出进进，尖利凸出的下颌被水浪舔舐着。那骷髅的颜色，是血红与紫色的一场狂烈冲撞。沙恩又朝河岸眨了眨眼，那幽灵似的影像仍叠印在上头：裸露的穹顶状颅骨、深陷的眼窟和鼻缝、生着獠牙的颌骨。那骷髅是一座山，或者说，一座山就是一具骷髅——而它对他事关重大；他必须找到它！

===Original===
He moved stiffly, his legs and arms cramped but not cold. The wolverines stirred on either side of him. Thorvald continued to sleep, curled up beyond, the pole still clasped in his hands. A flat map case was slung by a strap about his neck, its thin envelope between his arm and his body as if for safekeeping. On the smooth flap was the Survey seal, and it was fastened with a finger lock.

Thorvald had lost some of the bright hard surface he had shown at the spaceport where Shann had first sighted him. There were hollows in his cheeks, sending into high relief those bone ridges beneath his eye sockets, giving him a faint resemblance to the skull of Shann’s dream. His face was grimed, his field uniform stained and torn. Only his hair was as bright as ever.

Shann smeared the back of his hand across his own face, not doubting that he must present an even more disreputable appearance. He leaned forward cautiously to look into the water, but that surface was not quiet enough to act as a mirror.
===Chinese===
他僵着身子挪动了一下，手脚都发麻，倒不冷。狼獾在他两侧动了动。托尔瓦德还睡着，蜷在稍远处，手里仍攥着撑杆。一只扁平的地图盒用带子挎在他脖子上，薄薄的盒套夹在手臂和身侧之间，像是护得格外经心。光滑的盒盖上打着勘测队的徽印，扣的是一道指码锁。

托尔瓦德在太空港初次落进沙恩眼中时那层明亮的硬壳，如今已剥落了几分。他双颊塌陷，眼眶下的骨棱愈发凸出，竟隐隐有几分像沙恩梦里的那具骷髅。他一脸尘垢，野战制服又是污渍又是破口。唯独那头头发，还一如既往地亮。

沙恩用手背抹了把脸，毫不怀疑自己这副尊容只会更加不像样。他小心地探身朝水面看去，可那水面晃荡个不停，当不得镜子。

===Original===
Getting to his feet as the raft bobbed under his shift of weight, Shann studied the territory now about them. He could not match Thorvald’s inches, just as he must have a third less bulk than the officer, but standing, he could sight something of what now lay beyond the rising banks of the cut. That grass which had been so thick in the meadowlands around the camp had thinned into separate clumps, pale lavender in color. And the scrawniness of stem and blade suggested dehydration and poor soil. The earth showing between those clumps was not of the usual blue, but pallid, too, bleached to gray, while the bushes along the stream’s edge were few and smaller. They must have crossed the line into the desert Thorvald had promised.
===Chinese===
沙恩站起身，体重一移，木筏跟着颠了颠。他打量起四周的地界。他比不得托尔瓦德的个头，块头想来也比军官小上三分之一，可站着，便能望见河谷两岸之后的一些景象了。营地四周草甸上曾经那样茂密的草，已经稀成一丛一丛，颜色是淡淡的薰衣草紫。茎叶的干瘦说明缺水，也说明土质贫瘠。草丛间裸露的土壤不是惯常的那种蓝色，同样苍白，褪成了灰色，溪边的灌木又少又小。他们必定已经越过界线，进了托尔瓦德说过的那片沙漠。

===Original===
Shann edged around to face west. There was light enough in the sky to sight tall black pyramids waiting. They had to reach those distant mountains, mountains whose feet on the other side were resting in sea water. He studied them carefully, surveying each peak he could separate from its fellows.

Did the skull lie among them? The conviction that the place he had seen in his dream was real, that it was to be found on Warlock, persisted. Not only was it a definite feature of the landscape somewhere in the wild places of this world, but it was also necessary for him to locate it. Why? Shann puzzled over that, with a growing uneasiness which was not quite fear, not yet, anyway.
===Chinese===
沙恩侧过身子面向西边。天光已够亮，望得见一座座高耸的黑色金字塔正候在那里。他们必须抵达那些远山——大山的另一侧，山脚就浸在海水中。他细细端详，把能从群峰中分辨出来的每一座都打量了一遍。

那具骷髅就藏在它们中间吗？梦中所见之地确有其物、就坐落在沃洛克某处——这个信念始终盘踞不去。它不但是这颗世界蛮荒之地某处一处实实在在的地貌，而且他非找到它不可。为什么？沙恩为此暗自纳闷，心头的不安一分分加重——还算不上恐惧，眼下还谈不上。

===Original===
Thorvald moved. The raft tilted and the wolverines became growly. Shann sat down, one hand out to the officer’s shoulder in warning. Feeling that touch Thorvald shifted, one hand striking out blindly in a blow which Shann was just able to avoid while with the other he pinned the map case yet tighter to him.

“Take it easy!” Shann urged.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德动了。木筏一歪，狼獾呜噜噜低吼起来。沙恩坐下去，一只手伸向军官的肩头示警。触到这一下，托尔瓦德翻了个身，一只手没头没脑地挥出一拳——沙恩堪堪躲过——另一只手却把地图盒搂得更紧了。

“放松点！”沙恩急声道。

===Original===
The other’s eyelids flicked. He looked up, but not as if he saw Shann at all.

“The Cavern of the Veil﻿—” he muttered. “Utgard﻿ ﻿…” Then his eyes did focus and he sat up, gazing around him with a frown.

“We’re in the desert,” Shann announced.
===Chinese===
对方的眼皮颤了颤。他抬起眼来，却像是压根没看见沙恩。

“幕之洞窟（the Cavern of the Veil）……”他喃喃道。“尤特加德……”随后他的目光当真聚了焦，坐起身，皱着眉环顾四周。

“我们到沙漠了。”沙恩宣告。

===Original===
Thorvald got up, balancing on feet planted a little apart, looking to the faded expanse of the waste spreading from the river cut. He stared at the mountains before he squatted down to fumble with the lock of the map case.

The wolverines were growing restless, though they still did not try to move about too freely on the raft, greeting Shann with vocal complaint. He and Thorvald could satisfy their hunger with a handful of concentrates from the survival kit. But those dry tablets could not serve the animals. Shann studied the terrain with more knowledge than he had possessed a week earlier. This was not hunting land, but there remained the bounty of the river.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德站起身，两脚略分开站稳，望向河槽之外铺展开去的、一片褪色的荒原。他对着群山凝望了一阵，才蹲下来摸弄地图盒的锁扣。

狼獾们越来越不安生，虽然还没在筏上放肆乱动，却冲着沙恩呜呜咽咽地抗议。他和托尔瓦德从求生包里抓几把浓缩口粮，就能对付了肚子里的饥火。可那些干巴巴的口粮片喂不了这两个家伙。沙恩打量这片地形，眼力已比一周前老到。这不是猎场，好在河里还有天的恩赐。

===Original===
“We’ll have to feed Taggi and Togi,” he broke the silence abruptly. “If we don’t, they’ll be into the river and off on their own.”

Thorvald glanced up from one of the tough, thin sheets of map skin, again as if he had been drawn back from some distance. His eyes moved from Shann to the unpromising shore.

“How? With what?” he wanted to know. Then the real urgency of the situation must have penetrated his mental isolation. “You have an idea﻿—?”
===Chinese===
“总得喂喂塔吉和托吉，”他陡然打破沉默，“不然它们就要下河，自奔前程去了。”

托尔瓦德从一张结实纤薄的图皮纸上抬起眼，又一次像是从很远的地方被拽了回来。他的目光从沙恩移向那片没什么指望的河岸。

“怎么喂？拿什么喂？”他想知道。随后，局势真正的紧迫想必终于渗进了他那层心不在焉的壳。“你有主意了——？”

===Original===
“There’s those fish we found them eating back by the mountain stream,” Shann said, recalling an incident of a few days earlier. “Rocks here, too, like those the fish were hiding under. Maybe we can locate some of them here.”

He knew that Thorvald would be reluctant to work the raft in shore, to spare time for such hunting. But there would be no arguing with hungry wolverines, and he did not propose to lose the animals for the officer’s whim.
===Chinese===
“山溪边上，咱们不是撞见它们吃过一种鱼嘛，”沙恩说，想起几天前的一桩事。“这儿也有石头，跟那些鱼藏身的石头一个样。兴许在这儿也能摸出几条来。”

他知道，托尔瓦德会不情愿把木筏撑向岸边，不情愿匀出工夫来干打猎这档子事。可饿着肚子的狼獾是没法讲理的，他也犯不上为了军官的一念之差，就把这两个伙伴丢了。

===Original===
However, Thorvald did not protest. They poled the raft out of the main pull of the current, sending it in toward the southern shore in the lee of a clump of light-willows. Shann scrambled ashore, the wolverines after him, sniffling along at his heels while he overturned likely looking rocks to unroof some odd underwater dwellings. The fish with the rudimentary legs were present and not agile enough even in their native element to avoid well-clawed paws which scooped them neatly out of the river shallows. There was also a sleek furred creature with a broad flat head and paddle-equipped forepaws, rather like a miniature seal, which Taggi appropriated before Shann had a chance to examine it closely. In fact, the wolverines wrought havoc along a half-mile section of bank before the Terran could coax them back to the raft.
===Chinese===
然而托尔瓦德没有反对。他们把木筏撑出主流，送它贴着南岸一丛光柳的背风处靠过去。沙恩爬上岸，狼獾跟在后面，抽着鼻子贴他的脚跟转；他把一块块看着有戏的石头翻开，掀掉一座座水下巢穴的顶。那种长着雏形腿的鱼果然在，即便在自家地界也够不机灵，躲不开那双利爪森森的脚掌，被干净利落地从河边浅水里捞了出来。还有一头皮毛油光水滑的兽，宽扁的脑袋，前爪带蹼，活像一头缩小版的海豹——沙恩还没来得及细看，就被塔吉占了去。事实上，在这个地球人把两只狼獾哄回筏上之前，它们已沿着半英里长的河岸大闹了一场。

===Original===
As they hunted, Shann got a better idea of the land about the river. It was sere, the vegetation dwindling except for some rough spikes of things pushing through the parched ground like flayed fingers, their puffed redness in contrast to the usual amethystine coloring of Warlock’s growing things. Under the climbing sun that whole stretch of country was revealed in a stark bareness which at first repelled, and then began to interest him.

He discovered Thorvald standing on the upper bluff, looking out toward the waiting mountains. The officer turned as Shann urged the wolverines to the raft, and when he jumped down the drop to join them, Shann saw he carried a map strip unrolled in his hand.
===Chinese===
一路翻找，沙恩把河两岸的地界看了个更真切。满目枯焦，草木凋零，只有一些粗砺的尖刺状怪东西戳出干裂的地面，像剥了皮的手指，肿胀发红，同沃洛克草木惯常的紫水晶色形成刺目的对照。爬高的太阳底下，那一整片乡野袒露出一种彻骨的荒赤，起初令人望而生厌，渐渐地却又勾起他的兴味。

他发现托尔瓦德站在上方的高崖上，遥望那些静候的群山。沙恩往回赶狼獾时，军官转过身来；待他跳下崖坎回到筏边，沙恩看见他手里展开着一卷地图条。

===Original===
“The situation is not as good as we hoped,” he told the younger man. “We’ll have to leave the river to cross the heights.”

“Why?”

“There’re rapids﻿—ending in a falls.” The officer squatted down, spreading out the strip and making stabs at it with a nervous finger tip. “Here we have to leave. This is all rough ground. But lying to the south there’s a gap which may be a pass. This was made from an aerial survey.”
===Chinese===
“形势不如我们指望的那么好，”他对年轻人说，“我们得离开河，翻过高地。”

“为什么？”

“前面有急流——尽头挂着瀑布。”军官蹲下身，摊开图条，指尖紧张地点点戳戳。“这儿我们就得弃河了。这一带全是崎岖地。但偏南有一道豁口，没准是条隘口。这图是根据航测绘的。”

===Original===
Shann knew enough to realize to what extent such a guide could go wrong. Main features of the landscape would be clear enough from aloft, but there might be unsurmountable difficulties at ground level which were not distinguishable from the air. Yet Thorvald had planned this journey as if he had already explored their escape route and that it was as open and easy as a stroll down Tyr’s main transport way. Why was it so necessary that they try to reach the sea? However, since he had no objection to voice except a dislike for indefinite information, Shann did not question the other’s calm assumption of command, not yet, anyway.
===Chinese===
沙恩懂得够多了，明白这样的向导能错到什么地步。从高处看，地貌的主要特征一清二楚，可地面上或许横着无法逾越的障碍，从空中根本分辨不出来。然而托尔瓦德筹划这趟行程时的那副笃定，仿佛他早已把他们的逃生路线踏勘过一遍，仿佛那条路像沿提尔的主干运输道散步一样畅通好走。他们为什么非得抵达海边不可？不过，除了不喜欢这种没个准信的消息，他也提不出别的异议，于是沙恩没有质问对方那副理所当然的发号施令——眼下还没有。

===Original===
As they embarked and worked back into the current, Shann studied his companion. Thorvald had freely listed the difficulties lying before them. Yet he did not seem in the least worried about their being able to win through to the sea﻿—or if he was, his outer shell of unconcern remained uncracked. Before their first day together had ended, the younger Terran had learned that to Thorvald he was only another tool, to be used by the Survey officer in some project which the other believed of primary importance. And his resentment of the valuation was under control so far. He valued Thorvald’s knowledge, but the other’s attitude chilled and rebuffed his need for something more than a half partnership of work.

Why had Thorvald come back to Warlock in the first place? And why had it been necessary for him to risk his life﻿—perhaps more than his life if their theory was correct concerning the Throgs’ wish to capture a Terran﻿—to get that set of maps from the plundered camp? When he had first talked of that raid, his promised loot had been supplies to fill their daily needs; there had been no mention of maps. By all signs Thorvald was engaged on some mission. And what would happen if he, Shann, suddenly stopped being the other’s obedient underling and demanded a few explanations here and now?
===Chinese===
两人上筏，重新撑回主流。沙恩端详起他的同伴来。托尔瓦德把面前的困难罗列了个痛快。可对他们能否闯到海边，他似乎丝毫不担心——就算担心，那层满不在乎的外壳也没裂开一丝缝。共处的头一天还没过完，这个年轻的地球人就明白了：对托尔瓦德来说，自己不过是又一件工具，供这位勘测队军官用在某项他自认为头等要紧的计划里。对这身价，他心头的怨气至今还摁得住。他珍视托尔瓦德的学识，可对方这副态度，冷冷地回绝了他想要的东西——想要一份不止于干活搭伙的情分。

托尔瓦德当初究竟为什么回沃洛克来？为什么他非得拿性命去冒——倘若他们关于斯罗格人想抓地球人的推测不差，那凶险还不止送命——去那座遭劫掠的营地里取那套地图？最初说起那场突袭时，他许下的战利品不过是些填日常所需的补给；地图的事只字未提。种种迹象都表明，托尔瓦德身负着什么差事。那么，倘若他沙恩突然不再做这俯首帖耳的下属，当场就要几条解释，又会怎么样？

===Original===
Only Shann knew enough about men to also know that he would not get any information out of Thorvald that the latter was not ready to give, and that such a showdown, coming prematurely, would only end in his own discomfiture. He smiled wryly now, remembering his emotions when he had first seen Ragnar Thorvald months ago. As if the officer ever considered the likes, dislikes﻿—or dreams﻿—of one Shann Lantee. No, reality and dreams seldom approached each other. Dreams﻿ ﻿…
===Chinese===
只是，沙恩对人也看得够多了，知道托尔瓦德不打算给的，自己一个字也掏不出来；这样一场摊牌来得太早，只会落得自己下不来台。此刻他想起几个月前初见拉格纳·托尔瓦德时自己的心绪，不由得苦笑。仿佛那位军官会把一个沙恩·兰蒂的好恶——或者梦——放在心上似的。不，现实和梦难得彼此靠近。梦……

===Original===
“On any of those shoreline maps,” he asked suddenly, “do they have marked a mountain shaped like a skull?”

Thorvald thrust with his pole. “Skull?” he repeated, a little absently, as he so often did in answer to Shann’s questions unless they dealt with some currently important matter.
===Chinese===
“那些海岸线图上，”他突然问，“有没有标过一座形状像骷髅的山？”

托尔瓦德撑了一杆。“骷髅？”他重复了一遍，有点心不在焉——每逢沙恩发问，只要不涉眼下的要紧事，他多半是这副样子。

===Original===
“A queer sort of skull,” Shann said. Just as vividly as when he had first awakened, he could picture that skull mountain with the flying things about its eye sockets. And that, too, was odd; dream impressions usually faded with the passing of waking hours. “It has a protruding lower jaw and the waves wash that﻿ ﻿… red-and-purple rock﻿—”
===Chinese===
“一种怪模怪样的骷髅，”沙恩说。一如初醒时那般鲜明，他仍能想见那座骷髅山，眼窝四周绕着会飞的东西。这一点也怪——梦里的印象，通常随着清醒时辰的流逝便淡去了。“它下颌凸出，海浪拍打着那儿……红色和紫色的岩石——”

===Original===
“What?”

He had Thorvald’s complete attention now.

“Where did you hear about it?” That demand followed quickly.
===Chinese===
“什么？”

这下，托尔瓦德的注意力全在他身上了。

“你是在哪儿听说它的？”紧跟着便是这句逼问。

===Original===
“I didn’t hear about it. I dreamed of it last night. I stood there right in front of it. There were birds﻿—or things flying like birds﻿—going in and out of the eyeholes﻿—”

“What else?” Thorvald leaned across his pole, his eyes alive, avid, as if he would pull the reply he wanted out of Shann by force.
===Chinese===
“我不是听说的。我昨晚梦见了它。我就站在它跟前。有鸟——或者说像鸟一样飞的东西——在眼窟窿里进进出出——”

“还有什么？”托尔瓦德俯在撑杆上，两眼放光，热切得近乎贪婪，仿佛要硬从沙恩嘴里把想听的答话拽出来。

===Original===
“That was all I remember﻿—the skull mountain.” He did not add his other impression, that he was meant to find that skull, that he *must* find it.

“Nothing﻿ ﻿…” Thorvald paused, and then spoke slowly, with a visible reluctance. “Nothing else? No cavern with a green veil﻿—a wide green veil﻿—strung across it?”

Shann shook his head. “Just the skull mountain.”
===Chinese===
“我记得的就这些——那座骷髅山。”他没有说出另一层感受：冥冥中他就是该找到那具骷髅，他*必须*找到它。

“没有……”托尔瓦德顿了顿，然后才慢慢开口，带着掩不住的不情愿。“没有别的了？没有一座洞窟，窟口横张着一道绿幕——很宽的绿幕——？”

沙恩摇头。“就那座骷髅山。”

===Original===
Thorvald looked as if he didn’t quite believe that, but Shann’s expression must have been convincing, for he laughed shortly.

“Well, there goes one nice neat theory up in smoke!” he commented. “No, your skull doesn’t appear on any of our maps, and so probably my cavern does not exist either. They may both be smoke screens﻿—”
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德的神情像是并不怎么信，可沙恩的表情想必够有说服力，他短促地笑了一声。

“好啊，一个漂亮齐整的理论就这么化烟了！”他评了一句。“不，你那座骷髅哪张图上都没有，这么说，我的洞窟多半也不存在。它们俩兴许都是烟幕——”

===Original===
“What﻿—?” But Shann never finished that query.

A wind was rising in the desert to blow across the slit which held the river, carrying with it a fine shifting of sand which coasted down into the water as a gray haze, coating men, animals, and raft, and sighing as snow sighs when it falls.
===Chinese===
“什么——？”可这个问题，沙恩终究没能问完。

沙漠里起风了，风刮过夹着河流的那道狭谷，卷来一阵细软的流沙。沙尘像一层灰雾滑进水里，落满人、兽和木筏，簌簌地叹息着，像雪落下时的声息。

===Original===
Only that did not drown out another cry, a thin cry, diluted by the miles of land stretching behind them, but yet carrying that long ululating howl they had heard in the Throg camp. Thorvald grinned mirthlessly.

“The hound’s on trail.”

He bent to the pole, using it to aid the pace of the current. Shann, chilled in spite of the sun’s heat, followed his example, wondering if time had ceased to fight on their side.
===Chinese===
可这风声没能盖住另一声呼号——细细的一声，被身后绵延数英里的旷野稀释得淡了，却仍带着他们在斯罗格营地里听到过的那种悠长起伏的嚎叫。托尔瓦德咧嘴一笑，笑里没有半点暖意。

“猎犬追上来了。”

他俯身撑杆，借它给流速再添一把力。沙恩顶着日头的暑热仍觉寒意侵体，也学着样撑起杆来，心里寻思着，时间是不是已经不再站在他们这一边了。

## VI The Hound / VI “猎犬”

===Original===
The sun was a harsh ball of heat baking the ground and then, in some odd manner, drawing back that same fieriness. In the coolness of the eastern mountains Shann would not have believed that Warlock could hold such heat. The men discarded their jackets early as they swung to dip the poles. But they dared not strip off the rest of their clothing lest their skin burn. And again gusts of wind now drove sand over the edge of the cut to blanket the water.

Shann wiped his eyes, pausing in his eternal push-push, to look at the rocks which they were passing in threatening proximity. For the slash which held the river had narrowed. And the rock of its walls was naked of earth, save for sheltered pockets holding the drift of sand dust, while boulders of all sizes cut into the path of the flowing water.
===Chinese===
太阳成了一团酷烈的火球，炙烤着大地，随后又不知怎的，把那股炽烈收了回去。在东部山地的凉爽里待过之后，沙恩绝不相信沃洛克竟能热成这样。两人早早脱下外衣，抡起撑杆撑筏。可余下的衣裳他们不敢再脱，生怕皮肤灼伤。而阵风又一回回把沙子刮过裂谷边沿，给河面罩上一层尘毯。

沙恩抹了把眼睛，在那没完没了的一撑一撑之间歇了口气，打量起近得逼人、擦身而过的岩石。夹着河水的这道裂口已经变窄。谷壁的岩石寸土不挂，只有背风的坑洼里积着沙尘，而大大小小的巨砾都探进了流水的水道。

===Original===
He had not been mistaken; they were going faster, faster even than their efforts with the poles would account for. With the narrowing of the bed of the stream, the current was taking on a new swiftness. Shann said as much and Thorvald nodded.

“We’re approaching the first of the rapids.”
===Chinese===
他没有看错；他们行进得越来越快，甚至快过了两人撑杆出的力气所能解释的程度。随着河床收窄，水流正添上一股新的迅疾。沙恩把这层意思说了出来，托尔瓦德点了点头。

“我们快到第一段急流了。”

===Original===
“Where we get off and walk around,” Shann croaked wearily. The dust gritted between his teeth, irritated his eyes. “Do we stay beside the river?”

“As long as we can,” Thorvald replied somberly. “We have no way of transporting water.”

Yes, a man could live on very slim rations of food, continue to beat his way over a bad trail if he had the concentrate tablets they carried. But there was no going without water, and in this heat such an effort would finish them quickly. Always they both listened for another cry from behind, a cry to tell them just how near the Throg hunting party had come.
===Chinese===
“到了那儿，我们就下筏，从旁边绕过去。”沙恩的声音沙哑而疲惫。尘沙在牙缝里硌着，又磨得他双眼发疼。“我们还贴着河走吗？”

“能贴多久贴多久。”托尔瓦德答得沉郁。“我们没办法运水。”

是啊，凭着随身带的那些浓缩口粮片，一个人可以把伙食压到极省，在糟糕的路上一程程硬闯下去。可没有水就寸步难行，况且在这种酷热里，那样的苦撑很快就会把他们的命送掉。两人始终竖着耳朵，等身后传来下一声嚎叫——那声嚎叫会告诉他们，斯罗格人的猎杀队已经逼到多近。

===Original===
“No Throg flyers yet,” Shann observed. He had expected one of those black plates to come cruising the moment the hound had pointed the direction for their pursuers.

“Not in a storm such as this.” Thorvald, without releasing his hold on the raft pole, pointed with his chin to the swirling haze cloaking the air above the cut walls. Here the river dug yet deeper into the beginning of a canyon. They could breathe better. The dust still sifted down but not as thickly as a half hour earlier. Though over their heads the sky was now a grayish lid, shutting out the sun, bringing a portion of coolness to the travelers.

The Survey officer glanced from side to side, watching the banks as if hunting for some special mark or sign. At last he used his pole as a pointer to indicate a rough pile of boulders ahead. Some former landslide had quarter dammed the river at that point, and the drift of seasonal floods was caught in and among the rocky pile to form a prickly peninsula.
===Chinese===
“斯罗格飞行器还没露面。”沙恩说。他原以为，那头“猎犬”刚替追兵指明方向，一架黑色碟形飞船就会巡弋过来。

“这样的风暴天气里，它们不会来。”托尔瓦德一手不离撑杆，用下巴朝裂谷上空那片打着旋的沙霾一指。到了这里，河流正往一道峡谷的雏形里越凿越深。他们呼吸畅快了些。尘沙仍在一层层筛落，只是已不及半个钟头前那么厚。头顶的天空则成了一面灰蒙蒙的盖子，遮死太阳，给两个赶路人送来几分凉意。

勘测队军官左顾右盼，审视着两岸，仿佛在搜寻什么特殊的标记或征兆。最后他拿撑杆当指针，朝前方一堆乱石嶙峋的巨砾一指。早先某场山崩在那里把河道拦去了四分之一，季节性洪水的冲积物卡进石堆之间、积在石堆之上，堆成一座尖刺横生的小半岛。

===Original===
“In there﻿—”

They brought the raft to shore, fighting the faster current. The wolverines, who had been subdued by the heat and the dust, flung themselves to the rocks with the eagerness of passengers deserting a sinking ship for certain rescue. Thorvald settled the map case more securely between his arm and side before he took the same leap. When they were all ashore he prodded the raft out into the stream again, pushing the platform along until it was sucked by the current past the line of boulders.
===Chinese===
“进那儿——”

他们跟更急的水流较着劲，把木筏弄到岸边。热气和尘沙早把两只狼獾折磨得蔫头耷脑，这会儿却争先恐后地扑上岩石，那股急切劲儿，活像乘客弃了将沉的船、扑向十拿九稳的生路。托尔瓦德先把地图盒（map case）在臂弯与肋侧之间夹得更牢，才跟着纵身一跃。等全都上了岸，他把木筏重新捅进水流，推着这平台顺水漂去，直到它被激流吸走、卷过那道巨砾连线。

===Original===
“Listen!”

But Shann had already caught that distant rumble of sound. It was steady, beating like some giant drum. Certainly it did not herald a Throg ship in flight and it came from ahead, not from their back trail.

“Rapids﻿ ﻿… perhaps even the falls,” Thorvald interpreted that faint thunder. “Now, let’s see what kind of a road we can find here.”
===Chinese===
“听！”

可沙恩早已捕捉到那阵遥远的隆隆声。它平稳持续，像某面巨鼓在擂。那绝不是斯罗格飞船飞临的先声，而且它来自前方，不是来自他们身后的来路。

“急流……说不定还有瀑布。”托尔瓦德解读着那阵隐隐的雷声，“现在，来看看咱们在这儿能找到一条什么样的路。”

===Original===
The tongue of boulders, spiked with driftwood, was firmly based against the wall of the cut. But it sloped up to within a few feet of the top of that gap, more than one landslide having contributed to its fashioning. The landing stage paralleled the river for perhaps some fifty feet. Beyond it water splashed a straight wall. They would have to climb and follow the stream along the top of the embankment, maybe being forced well away from the source of the water.

By unspoken consent they both knelt and drank deeply from their cupped hands, splashing more of the liquid over their heads, washing the dust from their skins. Then they began to climb the rough assent up which the wolverines had already vanished. The murk above them was less solid, but again the fine grit streaked their faces, embedding itself in their hair.
===Chinese===
那条巨砾垒成的石舌，浮木尖刺林立，牢牢抵在裂谷的谷壁上。但它向上斜升，离那道裂口的顶端只差几英尺——塑造它的山崩不止一次。那级登陆台地顺着河延展约五十英尺。再往前，河水冲溅着一面笔直的陡壁。他们将不得不攀登上去，沿着堤岸顶上循河而行，说不定还得被迫远远离开水源。

两人心照不宣地一齐跪下，掬水痛饮，又泼了更多河水在头上，洗去皮肤上的尘沙。随后他们开始攀登那道粗粝的陡坡——狼獾早已打那儿消失了。头顶的昏黑没那么浓实了，可细沙又一道道糊上他们的脸，钻进头发里。

===Original===
Shann paused to scrape a film of mud from his lips and chin. Then he made the last pull, bracing his slight body against the push of the wind he met there. A palm struck hard between his shoulders, nearly sending him sprawling. He had only wits enough left to recognize that as an order to get on, and he staggered ahead until rock arched over him and the sand drift was shut off.

His shoulder met solid stone, and having rubbed the sand from his eyes, Shann realized he was in a pocket in the cliff walls. Well overhead he caught a glimpse of natural amber sky through a slit, but here was a twilight which thickened into complete darkness.
===Chinese===
沙恩停下来，刮掉嘴唇和下巴上的一层泥垢。随后他做了最后一把攀拉，用瘦小的身板顶住那儿迎面扑来的劲风。一只手掌重重拍在他双肩之间，险些把他拍个趔趄。他仅剩的神志只够认出那是一记“往前走”的命令，便踉跄着朝前挪，直到岩石拱上头顶，风沙被隔在了外面。

肩膀抵上坚实的石头。沙恩揉掉眼里的沙子，才明白自己正身处崖壁间的一个凹洞。头顶高处，他透过一线裂隙瞥见一小片天然的琥珀色天空，可脚下这里暮色沉沉，正凝成一片漆黑。

===Original===
There was no sign of wolverines. Thorvald moved along the pocket southward, and Shann followed him. Once more they faced a dead end. For the crevice, with the sheer descent to the river on the right, the cliff wall at its back, came to an abrupt stop in a drop which caught at Shann’s stomach when he ventured to look down.

If some battleship of the interstellar fleet had aimed a force beam across the mountains of Warlock, cutting down to what lay under the first envelope of planet-skin, perhaps the resulting wound might have resembled that slash. What had caused such a break between the height on which they stood and the much taller peak beyond, Shann could not guess. But it must have been a cataclysm of spectacular dimensions. There was certainly no descending to the bottom of that cut and reclimbing the rock face on the other side. The fugitives would either have to return to the river with all its ominous warnings of trouble to come, or find some other path across that gap which now provided such an effective barrier to the west.
===Chinese===
狼獾不见踪影。托尔瓦德沿着凹洞往南走，沙恩跟在后面。他们又一次迎面撞上绝路。这条石缝右侧是直落河面的绝壁，背后是崖墙，尽头猝然断成一道垂直的跌落——沙恩壮着胆子往下望了一眼，胃里便猛地一揪。

倘若有哪艘星际舰队的战列舰，把一道力场光束（force beam）横扫过沃洛克的群山，直切进行星外壳第一层覆盖之下的深处，那么造出的伤口，或许就和这道劈裂有几分相像。在他们立足的高地与远处那座高得多的山峰之间，究竟是什么造成了这样一道断裂，沙恩猜不透。但那必是一场规模骇人的天崩地裂。想下到裂谷谷底、再攀上对面的岩壁，是绝无可能了。两个逃亡者要么回到那条河边——连同它那预示着祸事将临的种种不祥——要么另寻一条路，跨过这道如今成了西面天堑的裂口。

===Original===
“Down!” Just as Thorvald had pushed him out of the murk of the dust storm into the crevice, so now did that officer jerk Shann from his feet, forcing him to the floor of the half cave from which they had partially emerged.

A shadow moved across the bright band of sunlit sky.

“Back!” Thorvald caught at Shann again, his greater strength prevailing as he literally dragged the younger man into the dusk of the crevice. And he did not pause, nor allow Shann to do so, even when they were well undercover again. At last they reached the dark hole in the southern wall which they had passed earlier. And a push from Thorvald sent his companion into that.
===Chinese===
“趴下！”正像先前托尔瓦德把他从沙暴的昏黑里推进石缝那样，此刻这位军官又是一把将沙恩拽离双脚，硬按回他们刚钻出来一半的那个半洞的洞底。

一道影子掠过那一条洒满阳光的亮空。

“退后！”托尔瓦德又一把揪住沙恩，凭着一身更大的力气，硬是把年轻人拖进石缝的昏暗里。即便重新藏得严严实实之后，他也没有停步，也不许沙恩停。最后他们退到南壁上那个黑黢黢的洞口——就是先前经过的一处。托尔瓦德一推，把同伴送了进去。

===Original===
Then a blow greater than any the Survey officer had aimed at him struck Shann. He was hurled against a rough wall with impetus enough to explode the air from his lungs, the ensuing pain so great that he feared his ribs had given under that thrust. Before his eyes fire lashed down the slit, searing him into temporary blindness. That flash was the last thing he remembered as thick darkness closed in, shutting him into the nothingness of unconsciousness.
===Chinese===
接着，一记比勘测队军官任何一次出手都沉重的打击落在了沙恩身上。他被掀到一面粗粝的岩壁上，那股冲力把肺里的空气全撞了出来，随之而来的剧痛让他生怕肋骨已在那一撞之下折断。眼前，烈焰顺着石缝抽落，灼得他霎时失明。浓黑四面合拢，把他关进无意识的虚无——那道闪光便是他记得的最后一幕。

===Original===
It hurt to breathe; he was slowly aware first of that pain and then the fact that he *was* breathing, that he had to endure the pain for the sake of breath. His whole body was jarred into a dull torment as a weight pressed upon his twisted legs. Then strong animal breath puffed into his face. Shann lifted one hand by will power, touched thick fur, felt the rasp of a tongue laid wetly across his fingers.

Something close to terror engulfed him for a second or two when he knew that he could not see! The black about him was colored by jagged flashes of red which he somehow guessed were actually inside his eyes. He groped through that fire-pierced darkness. An animal whimper from the throat of the shaggy body pressed against him; he answered that movement.
===Chinese===
呼吸是痛的；他先慢慢觉出这份痛，再觉出自己*确实*还在呼吸这个事实——为了呼吸，他必须忍住这份痛。一个重物压着他扭歪的双腿，把他周身震成一片钝钝的苦楚。接着，一股浓重的兽息喷上他的脸。沙恩凭着意志抬起一只手，摸到厚厚的毛，又感到一条舌头湿漉漉地拉过手指的粗粝触感。

当他明白自己看不见时，一种近乎恐怖的感觉把他吞没了一两秒！四周的黑里闪着一道道锯齿状的红光，他不知怎么就猜到，那些其实是在他自己的眼睛里面。他在那被火星刺穿的黑暗里摸索。紧贴着他的那具毛茸茸的身躯，喉咙里发出一声兽类的呜咽；他回应了那一下蹭动。

===Original===
“Taggi?”

The shove against him was almost enough to pin him once more to the wall, a painful crush on his aching ribs, as the wolverine responded to his name. That second nudge from the other side must be Togi’s bid for attention.

But what had happened? Thorvald had hurled him back just after that shadow had swung over the ledge. That shadow! Shann’s wits quickened as he tried to make sense of what he could remember. A Throg ship! Then that fiery lash which had cut after them could only have resulted from one of those energy bolts such as had wiped out the others of his kind at the camp. But he was still alive﻿—!
===Chinese===
“塔吉？”

狼獾听见自己的名字，用力一拱，几乎又把他钉回岩壁上，酸痛的肋骨被挤得一阵剧痛。另一侧顶来的第二天，准是托吉在讨他的关注。

可究竟出了什么事？那道影子掠过岩架之后，托尔瓦德立刻把他掼了回来。那道影子！沙恩竭力理清自己记得的一切，脑子飞快地转起来。一艘斯罗格飞船！那么，随后朝他们劈来的那道火鞭，只能是那种能量光束的产物——当初在营地里灭尽了他其余同类的，正是这种东西。可他居然还活着——！

===Original===
“Thorvald?” He called through his personal darkness. When there was no answer, Shann called again, more urgently. Then he hunched forward on his hands and knees, pushing Taggi gently aside, running his hands over projecting rocks, uneven flooring.

His fingers touched what could only be cloth, before they met the warmth of flesh. And he half threw himself against the supine body of the Survey officer, groping awkwardly for heartbeat, for some sign that the other was still living.

“What﻿—?” The one word came thickly, but Shann gave something close to a sob of relief as he caught the faint mutter. He squatted back on his heels, pressed his forearm against his aching eyes in a kind of fierce will to see.
===Chinese===
“托尔瓦德？”他穿透自己那一片黑暗喊道。没有应答，沙恩又喊了一遍，更急了。随后他弓起身子手脚并用地往前爬，轻轻把塔吉拨到一边，双手探过一块块凸岩、一段段凹凸不平的地面。

他的手指先碰到一样只能是布料的东西，随即触到血肉的温热。他半个身子扑向那位仰卧的勘测队军官，笨拙地摸索心跳，摸索对方还活着的任何迹象。

“什么——？”一个词含混地吐出来，可一听见这句微弱的咕哝，沙恩便发出一声近似呜咽的宽慰。他后仰坐回脚跟，把小臂压上酸痛的双眼，带着一股近乎凶狠的意志想要看见。

===Original===
Perhaps that pressure did relieve some of the blackout, for when he blinked again, the complete dark and the fiery trails had faded to gray, and he was sure he saw dimly a source of light to his left.

The Throg ship had fired upon them. But the aliens could not have used the full force of their weapon or neither of the Terrans would still be alive. Which meant, Shann’s thoughts began to make sense﻿—sense which brought apprehension﻿—the Throgs probably intended to disable rather than kill. They wanted prisoners, just as Thorvald had warned.

How long did the Terrans have before the aliens would come to collect them? There was no fit landing place hereabouts for their flyer. The beetle-heads would have to set down at the edge of the desert land and climb the mountains on foot. And the Throgs were not good at that. So, the fugitives still had a measure of time.
===Chinese===
也许那股按压当真驱散了几分黑翳，因为他再眨眼时，那彻头彻尾的黑暗和一条条火痕已褪成灰色，而且他确信自己隐约看见左侧有一处光源。

斯罗格飞船朝他们开了火。但外星人不可能动用武器的全力，否则两个地球人谁也活不成。这就意味着——沙恩的思绪开始理出个所以然，一种令人不安的所以然——斯罗格人多半意在致残，而非取命。他们要俘虏，正如托尔瓦德警告过的那样。

外星人来收捕他们之前，两个地球人还有多少时间？这一带没有适合他们飞行器降落的场地。甲虫脑袋们只能降落在荒漠边缘，再徒步翻山越岭。而翻山越岭恰恰是斯罗格人不擅长的。所以，逃亡者还有一小段时日。

===Original===
Time to do what? The country itself held them securely captive. That drop to the southwest was one barrier. To retreat eastward would mean running straight into the hands of the hunters. To descend again to the river, their raft gone, was worse than useless. There was only this side pocket in which they sheltered. And once the Throgs arrived, they could scoop the Terrans out at their leisure, perhaps while stunned by a controlling energy beam.

“Taggi? Togi?” Shann was suddenly aware that he had not heard the wolverines for some time.
===Chinese===
可这一小段时日拿来做什么？这片山川本身就稳稳地囚禁着他们。西南那道断崖是一道屏障。往东退，等于径直撞进猎杀者的手心。再下到河边去，木筏已失，比无用还不如。剩下的只有他们藏身的这个侧凹洞。而斯罗格人一旦赶到，大可以从从容容把地球人掏出去——说不定掏的时候，人还被一道控御能量光束震得瘫软。

“塔吉？托吉？”沙恩猛然发觉，已经有一阵子没听见狼獾的动静了。

===Original===
He was answered by a weirdly muffled call﻿—from the south! Had the animals found a new exit? Was this niche more than just a niche? A cave of some length, or even a passage running back into the interior of the peaks? With that faint hope spurring him, Shann bent again over Thorvald, able now to make out the other’s huddled form. Then he drew the torch from the inner loop of his coat and pressed the lowest stud.

His eyes smarted in answer to that light, watered until tears patterned the grime and dust on his cheeks. But he could make out what lay before them, a hole leading into the cliff face, the hole which might furnish the door to escape.
===Chinese===
回答他的，是一声怪异发闷的呼唤——从南边传来！动物们找到新的出口了？这个凹穴不只是个凹穴？是一处颇有深度的洞窟，还是一条通进群山腹内的通道？那一线微弱的希望鞭策着他，沙恩再度俯向托尔瓦德——现在他已能辨出对方蜷缩的身形。于是他从夹克内里的扣环上摘下原子炬，按下最低的那颗按钮。

那点光刺得他双眼生疼，泪水涌出，在颊上的污垢尘沙间冲出两道泪痕。但他已能看清面前的景象：一个通进崖壁的洞口——那个也许能充当逃生之门的洞口。

===Original===
The Survey officer moved, levering himself up, his eyes screwed tightly shut.

“Lantee?”

“Here. And there’s a tunnel﻿—right behind you. The wolverines went that way﻿ ﻿…”
===Chinese===
勘测队军官动了动，撑起身子，双眼紧闭。

“兰蒂？”

“在这儿。还有一条隧道——就在你正后方。狼獾往那边去了……”

===Original===
To his surprise there was a thin ghost of a smile on Thorvald’s usually straight-lipped mouth. “And we’d better be away before visitors arrive?”

So he, too, must have thought his way through the sequence of past action to the same conclusion concerning the Throg movements.
===Chinese===
让他意外的是，托尔瓦德那两片素来抿成直线的嘴唇上，浮起一丝浅淡的笑影。“那么，咱们最好赶在‘访客’光临之前上路？”

看来他也把先前那一系列变故在心里过了一遍，对斯罗格人的动向得出了同样的结论。

===Original===
“Can you see, Lantee?” The question was painfully casual, but a note in it, almost a reaching for reassurance, cut for the first time through the wall which had stood between them from their chance meeting by the wrecked ship.

“Better now. I couldn’t when I first came to,” Shann answered quickly.
===Chinese===
“你看得见吗，兰蒂？”这一问问得随意得近乎生硬，可话音里有一丝近乎讨要安抚的意味，头一回穿透了那堵横亘在两人之间的墙——自他们在坠毁的飞船残骸旁偶然相遇起，这堵墙便一直立在那儿。

“现在好些了。刚醒过来那会儿看不见。”沙恩赶紧回答。

===Original===
Thorvald opened his eyes, but Shann guessed that he was as blind as he himself had been, He caught at the officer’s nearer hand, drawing it to rest on his own belt.

“Grab hold!” Shann was giving the orders now. “By the look of that opening we had better try crawling. I’ve a torch on at low﻿—”

“Good enough.” The other’s fingers fumbled on the band about Shann’s slim waist until they gripped tight at his back. He started on into the opening, drawing Thorvald by that hold with him.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德睁开眼睛，可沙恩猜，他跟自己方才一样瞎。他抓住军官离得近的那只手，把它拉过来搭在自己的腰带上。

“抓牢了！”这回轮到沙恩发号施令。“看那洞口的样子，咱们最好爬着走。我的原子炬开着，调在低档——”

“这就够了。”对方的手指在沙恩瘦削腰间的腰带上摸索，一直摸到他背后才扣紧。他率先进向洞口，凭着这一握，把托尔瓦德带着同行。

===Original===
Luckily, they did not have to crawl far, for shortly past the entrance the fault or vein they were following became a passage high enough for even the tall Thorvald to travel without stooping. And then only a little later he released his hold on Shann, reporting he could now see well enough to manage on his own.

The torch beam caught on a wall and awoke from there a glitter which hurt their eyes﻿—a green-gold cluster of crystals. Several feet on, there was another flash of embedded crystals. Those might promise priceless wealth, but neither Terran paused to examine them more closely or touch their surfaces. From time to time Shann whistled. And always he was answered by the wolverines, their calls coming from ahead. So the men continued to hope that they were not walking into a trap from which the Throgs could extract them.
===Chinese===
所幸他们没有爬多远，因为刚过入口，他们循行的那道断层——或者说岩脉——便展宽成一条通道，高得连高个子的托尔瓦德走起来也无须弯腰。又过了没多久，他便松开抓着沙恩的手，说自己如今看得够清楚，能自己走了。

原子炬的光束扫上一面岩壁，从那儿唤醒一片刺得人眼痛的闪光——一簇绿金色的水晶。再往前几英尺，又有一处嵌在岩里的晶簇熠熠一亮。这些或许预示着无价的财富，但两个地球人谁也没停下细看，更没伸手去碰它们的晶面。沙恩不时打一声呼哨，而狼獾每次都从前方回应。于是两人继续抱着希望：自己走进的不是一口任由斯罗格人瓮中捉鳖的陷阱。

===Original===
“Snap off your torch a moment!” Thorvald ordered.

Shann obeyed. The subdued light vanished. Yet there was still light to be seen﻿—ahead and above.

“Front door,” Thorvald observed. “How do we get up?”
===Chinese===
“把原子炬关一下！”托尔瓦德命令道。

沙恩照办。微光熄灭。可前方、上方，仍有光可见。

“正门。”托尔瓦德说，“可咱们怎么上去？”

===Original===
The torch showed them that, a narrow ladder of ledges branching off when the passage they followed took a turn to the left and east. Afterward Shann remembered that climb with wonder that they had actually made it, though their advance had been slow, passing the torch from one to another to make sure of their footing.

Shann was top man when a last spurt of effort enabled him to draw himself out into the open, his hands raw, his nails broken and torn. He sat there, stupefied with his own weariness, to stare about.
===Chinese===
原子炬把答案照给他们看：一条由层层岩阶凑成的窄梯，在他们循行的通道向左转东的拐处分岔而出。事后沙恩回想那段攀爬，还惊异于他们居然真爬了上去——虽然前进得极慢，原子炬在两人手中传递，好踩实每一个落脚点。

沙恩凭着一鼓最后的气力把自己拖出洞口、进入露天，成了最上面的人——他双手血肉模糊，指甲断裂撕损。他坐在那里，被自己的疲惫砸得木然发怔，四下呆望。

===Original===
Thorvald called impatiently, and Shann reached for the torch to hold it for the officer. Then Thorvald crawled out; he, too, looked around in dull surprise.

On either side, peaks cut high into the amber of the sky. But this bowl in which the men had found refuge was rich in growing things. Though the trees were stunted, the grass grew almost as high here as it did on the meadows of the lowlands. Quartering the pocket valley, galloped the wolverines, expressing in that wild activity their delight in this freedom.

“Good campsite.”
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德不耐烦地喊他，沙恩伸手取过原子炬，替军官照着。托尔瓦德随后爬了出来；他也一样，怔怔地惊讶四顾。

两侧的山峰高高切入琥珀色的天空。但两人避入的这处碗状谷地却草木丰茂。树木虽然矮曲，草却长得几乎同低地草甸上一般高。两只狼獾纵横驰骋，来回扫荡着这口袋状的小谷，用那份撒野的欢腾，倾吐着对这份自由的喜悦。

“好营地。”

===Original===
Thorvald shook his head. “We can’t stay here.”

And, to underline that gloomy prophesy, there issued from that hole through which they had just come, muffled and broken, but still threatening, the howl of the Throgs’ hound.

The Survey officer caught the torch from Shann’s hold and knelt to flash it into the interior of the passage. As the beam slowly circled that opening, he held out his other arm, measuring the size of the aperture.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德摇头。“我们不能待在这儿。”

而仿佛要给这句阴郁的预言添上着重号，从他们方才钻出来的那个洞里，传出了斯罗格人那头“猎犬”的嚎叫——发闷、断续，却依旧杀气逼人。

勘测队军官从沙恩手里拿过原子炬，跪下来朝通道内部照射。光束在那个洞口里缓缓环照时，他伸出另一条手臂，丈量着洞径的大小。

===Original===
“When that thing gets on a hot scent”﻿—he snapped off the beam﻿—“the beetle-heads won’t be able to control it. There will be no reason for them to attempt to. Those hounds obey their first orders: kill﻿—or capture. And I think this one operates on ‘capture.’ So they’ll loose it to run ahead of their party.”

“And we move to knock it out?” Shann relied now on the other’s experience.
===Chinese===
“那东西一旦咬上滚烫的嗅迹”——他把光束啪地关掉——“甲虫脑袋们就管不住它了。他们也犯不着再想去管。那种‘猎犬’只遵从最初下达的命令：杀——或者捕。而我想这一头领的是‘捕’。所以他们会放开它，让它跑在大部队前头。”

“那我们设法把它打瘫？”此刻，沙恩全指望对方的经验了。

===Original===
Thorvald rose. “It would need a blaster on full power to finish off a hound. No, we can’t kill it. But we can make it a doorkeeper to our advantage.” He trotted down into the valley, Shann beside him without understanding in the least, but aware that Thorvald did have some plan. The officer bent, searched the ground, and began to pull from under the loose surface dirt one of those nets of tough vines which they had used for cords. He thrust a double handful of this hasty harvest into Shann’s hold with a single curt order: “Twist these together and make as thick a rope as you can!”

Shann twisted, discovering to his pleased surprise that under pressure the vines exuded a sticky purple sap which not only coated his hands, but also acted as an adhesive for the vines themselves so that his task was not nearly as formidable as it had first seemed. With his force ax Thorvald cut down two of the stunted trees and stripped them of branches, wedging the poles into the rocks about the entrance of the hole.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德站起身。“要了结一头‘猎犬’，得用爆能枪开到全功率。不行，我们杀不了它。但我们可以让它当个看门人，反过来为我所用。”他小跑着下到谷里，沙恩跟在旁边，全然摸不着头脑，却明白托尔瓦德心里确有章法。军官俯身搜寻地面，开始从疏松的表土底下，拽出一张他们先前用作绳索的坚韧藤网。他把满满两把仓促的收成塞进沙恩手里，只丢下一句简短的命令：“把这些拧到一块儿，能拧多粗就拧多粗！”

沙恩拧了起来，又惊又喜地发现：藤条一受压榨便渗出黏黏的紫色汁液，不但糊了他两手，还顺势充当了藤条彼此间的黏合剂，于是这桩活计远不像初看上去那么棘手。托尔瓦德则用他那把力场斧砍倒两棵矮曲的树，削尽枝杈，把两根长杆楔进洞口四周的岩缝里。

===Original===
They were working against time, but on Thorvald’s part with practiced efficiency. Twice more that cry of the hunter arose from the depths behind them. As the westering sun, almost down now, shone into the valley hollow Thorvald set up the frame of his trap.

“We can’t knock it out, any more than we can knock out a Throg. But a beam from a stunner ought to slow it up long enough for this to work.”
===Chinese===
他们是在跟时间赛跑，不过托尔瓦德那边干得娴熟利落。身后深处又两次升起那猎手的嚎叫。当西沉的太阳——此刻已将将落尽——照进谷中洼地时，托尔瓦德支好了陷阱的架子。

“我们打不瘫它，就像我们打不瘫一个斯罗格人。不过击晕枪的一束射线，该能让它迟缓那么一小会儿，够这套机关奏效了。”

===Original===
Taggi burst out of the grass, approaching the hole with purpose. And Togi was right at his heels. Both of them stared into that opening, drooling a little, the same eagerness in their pose as they had displayed when hunting. Shann remembered how that first howl of the Throg hound had drawn both animals to the edge of the occupied camp in spite of their marked distaste for its alien masters.

“They’re after it too.” He told Thorvald what he had noted on the night of their sortie.
===Chinese===
塔吉从草丛里蹿出，直奔那个洞口，托吉紧随其后。两个家伙死死盯住洞口，垂着点涎水，那副姿态里的渴望，同它们捕猎时表现得一般无二。沙恩记起，斯罗格“猎犬”最初那声嚎叫，是怎样把两只动物引到被占营地的边上——尽管它们对营地那些外星主人嫌恶至极。

“它们也想逮它。”他把出击那晚注意到的这桩事讲给托尔瓦德听。

===Original===
“Maybe they can keep it occupied,” the other commented. “But we don’t want them to actually mix with it; that might be fatal.”

A clamor broke out in the interior passage. Taggi snarled, backing away a few steps before he uttered his own war cry.

“Ready!” Thorvald jumped to the net slung from the poles; Shann raised his stunner.
===Chinese===
“没准它们能缠住它。”对方评道，“可我们不能让它们真跟它斗成一团——那可能是致命的。”

通道深处炸开一阵喧嚣。塔吉低吼着退开几步，随后发出它自己的战嚎。

“预备！”托尔瓦德跃身扑向吊在两杆之间的网；沙恩举起了击晕枪。

===Original===
Togi underlined her mate’s challenge with a series of snarls rising in volume. There was a tearing, scrambling sound from within. Then Shann fired at the jack-in-the-box appearance of a monstrous head, and Thorvald released the deadfall.

The thing squalled. Ropes beat, growing taut. The wolverines backed from jaws which snapped fruitlessly. To Shann’s relief the Terran animals appeared content to bait the now imprisoned﻿—or collared﻿—horror, without venturing to make any close attack.
===Chinese===
托吉用一串声浪渐高的咆哮，给她伴侣的挑战添上重音。里面传出一阵撕扯蹬爬的动静。随即，一颗巨怪的头颅如玩偶匣（jack-in-the-box）里蹦出的玩偶般猛然探现，沙恩朝它开了火，托尔瓦德放落了那具吊网陷阱。

那东西惨嚎起来。绳索乱甩，随即绷紧。狼獾从一副徒劳空咬的巨颚前退开。让沙恩松了口气的是，这两只地球动物似乎满足于逗弄那头如今已被困住——或者说已被套牢——的怪物，并不冒险发起近身攻击。

===Original===
But he reckoned that too soon. Perhaps the stunner had slowed up the hound’s reflexes, for those jaws stilled with a last shattering snap, the toad-lizard mask﻿—a head which was against all nature as the Terrans knew it﻿—was quiet in the strangle leash of the rope, the rest of the body serving as a cork to fill the exit hole. Taggi had been waiting only for such a chance. He sprang, claws ready. And Togi went in after her mate to share the battle.
===Chinese===
可他料得太早了。也许是击晕枪迟滞了“猎犬”的反应，那副巨颚最后空咬出一声震响，便寂然不动了；那张蟾蜍蜥蜴（toad-lizard）般的脸——一颗在地球人看来全然逆乎自然的头颅——在绳索的勒束下归于安静，余下的躯体则成了一只塞子，堵死了出口。塔吉等的就是这样一个机会。它扑了上去，利爪张开。托吉紧随伴侣冲入，共赴这场战斗。

## VII Unwelcome Guide / VII 不受欢迎的向导

===Original===
There was a small eruption of earth and stone as the hound came alive, fighting to reach its tormentors. The resulting din was deafening. Shann, avoiding by a hand’s breadth a snap of jaws with power to crush his leg into bone powder and mangled flesh, cuffed Togi across her nose and buried his hands in the fur about Taggi’s throat as he heaved the male wolverine back from the struggling monster. He shouted orders, and to his surprise Togi did obey, leaving him free to yank Taggi away. Perhaps neither wolverine had expected the full fury of the hound.

Though he suffered a slash across the back of one hand, delivered by the overexcited Taggi, in the end Shann was able to get both animals away from the hole, now corked so effectively by the slavering thing. Thorvald was actually laughing as he watched his younger companion in action.

“This ought to slow up the beetles! If they haul their little doggie back, it’s apt to take out some of its rage on them, and I’d like to see them dig around it.”

===Chinese===
泥土碎石猛地迸溅开来——“猎犬”活了过来，拼命要去够那些折磨它的仇家。随之而起的喧嚣震耳欲聋。那一口利颌只要咬实，足以把沙恩的腿碾成骨粉烂肉；他险之又险地避过一咬，反手一掌横拍在托吉的鼻梁上，又把双手深深插进塔吉喉部的皮毛，将公狼獾从那挣扎的怪物跟前硬拽回来。他大声喝令，出乎意料，托吉竟真的服从了，他这才腾出手把塔吉也拽开。多半是两只狼獾谁都没料到“猎犬”会爆发出如此全部的狂怒。

尽管兴奋过头的塔吉在他一只手背上划开一道口子，沙恩最终还是把两只野兽都带离了洞口——那淌着涎水的怪物此刻正严严实实地堵在洞里，活像个塞子。托尔瓦德望着年轻同伴这一番手脚，竟自放声笑了。

「这总该拖住甲虫人们的脚步了！他们要是把自家小狗崽拽回去，它多半要拿他们撒气——他们围着它刨土的架势，我倒真想瞧瞧。」

===Original===
Considering that the monstrous head was swinging from side to side in a collar of what seemed to be immovable rocks, Shann thought Thorvald right. He went down on his knees beside the wolverines, soothing them with hand and voice, trying to get them to obey his orders willingly.

“Ha!” Thorvald brought his mud-stained hands together with a clap, the sharp sound attracting the attention of both animals.

Shann scrambled up, swung out his bleeding hand in the simple motion which meant to hunt, being careful to signal down the valley westward. Taggi gave a last reluctant growl at the hound, to be answered by one of its ear-torturing howls, and then trotted off, Togi tagging behind.

===Chinese===
想想那颗怪头正卡在一圈仿佛纹丝不动的岩石中间左摇右晃，沙恩觉得托尔瓦德说得在理。他跪到狼獾身旁，用手抚、用声音哄，想让它们心甘情愿听从号令。

「哈！」托尔瓦德啪地一声拍响沾满泥污的双手，这清脆的一响把两只野兽的注意力都引了过去。

沙恩一跃而起，甩出那只流血的手，比出一个简单的手势——意思是去狩猎，同时小心地把方向指向西边的谷下游。塔吉朝“猎犬”不情愿地低吼了最后一声，换回一声撕磨耳朵的嚎叫，随后小跑着去了，托吉跟在后头。

===Original===
Thorvald caught Shann’s slashed hand, inspecting the bleeding cut. From the aid packet at his belt he brought out powder and a strip of protecting plasta-flesh to cleanse and bind the wound.

“You’ll do,” he commented. “But we’d better get out of here before full dark.”

The small paradise of the valley was no safe campsite. It could not be so long as that monstrosity on the hillside behind them roared and howled its rage to the darkening sky. Trailing the wolverines, the men caught up with the animals drinking from a small spring and thankfully shared that water. Then they pushed on, not able to forget that somewhere in the peaks about must lurk the Throg flyer ready to attack on sight.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德捉过沙恩那只挨划的手，察看流血的伤口。他从腰间急救包里取出药粉和一条护创的普拉斯塔人造皮，把伤口清洗包扎好。

「你还顶用。」他评了一句，「不过我们最好赶在天黑透之前离开这里。」

这处小小的山谷天堂绝非安全的宿营地。只要身后山坡上那怪物还在朝渐暗的天空咆哮泄愤，它就安全不了。两人跟在狼獾后面赶路，追上它们时，它们正就着一眼小泉喝水，两人也感激地分享了几口。随后他们继续前行，心里始终不敢忘记：四周群峰间的什么地方，必定潜伏着那艘一被看见就会攻击的斯罗格飞行器。

===Original===
Only darkness could not be held off by the will of men. Here in the open there was no chance to use the torch. As long as they were within the valley boundaries the phosphorescent bushes marked a path. But by the coming of complete darkness they were once more out in a region of bare rock.

The wolverines had killed a brace of skitterers, consuming hide and soft bones as well as the meager flesh which was not enough to satisfy their hunger. However, to Shann’s relief, they did not wander too far ahead. And as the men stopped at last on a ledge where a fall of rock gave them some limited shelter both animals crowded in against the humans, adding the heat of their bodies to the slight comfort of that cramped resting place.

From time to time Shann was startled out of a troubled half sleep by the howl of the hound. Luckily that sound never seemed any louder. If the Throgs had caught up with their hunter, and certainly they must have done so by now, they either could not, or would not free it from the trap.
