Shann dozed again, untroubled by any dreams, to awake hearing the shrieks of clak-claks. But when he studied the sky he was able to sight none of the cliff-dwelling Warlockian bats.

===Chinese===
只是黑暗不会听凭人的意志止步。在这露天野地里，没有机会动用原子炬。只要还不出山谷地界，发光的灌木便一路标出路径。可等到夜色完全合拢，他们又走进了遍地裸岩的地带。

狼獾们逮住了一对疾窜兽，连皮带软骨嚼了个精光，那点可怜的肉却填不满它们的饥肠。不过让沙恩宽慰的是，它们并没有窜到前头太远。当两人终于在一道岩檐下停脚——塌落的石块给他们提供了些许遮蔽——两只野兽也紧紧挤到人身旁，把自身的暖意添进这处逼仄栖身地仅有的一点舒适里。

“猎犬”的嚎叫不时把沙恩从纷乱不安的半睡半醒中惊起。所幸那声音听着始终没有更近。斯罗格人若已追上他们的猎兽——此刻肯定早就追上了——那他们要么不能、要么不愿把它从陷阱里弄出来。沙恩又打起盹，没有被任何梦搅扰，醒来时听见的是克拉克鸟的尖啸。可他细细察看天空，却一只崖栖的沃洛克蝙蝠也没找到。

===Original===
“More likely they are paying attention to our friend back in the valley,” Thorvald said dryly, rightly reading Shann’s glance to the clouds overhead. “Ought to keep them busy.”

Clak-claks were meat eaters, only they preferred their chosen prey weak and easy to attack. The imprisoned hound would certainly attract their kind. And those shrill cries now belling through the mountain heights ought to draw everyone of their species within miles.

“There it is!” Thorvald, pulling himself to his feet by a rock handhold, gazed westward, his gaunt face eager.

===Chinese===
「多半是去关照咱们留在山谷里的那位朋友了。」托尔瓦德干巴巴地说，他没看错沙恩投向头顶云天的目光。「够它们忙活的了。」

克拉克鸟是食肉动物，只是它们偏爱挑软弱好欺的猎物下手。被困的“猎犬”必定招它们垂青。此刻回荡在群峰之间的声声尖啸，理该把方圆数英里内的每一只同类都招来。

「在那儿！」托尔瓦德攀着一块凸岩站起身，朝西凝望，瘦削的脸上满是急切。

===Original===
Shann, expecting no less than a cruising Throg ship, searched for cover on their perch. Perhaps if they flattened themselves behind the fall of stones, they might be able to escape attention. Yet Thorvald made no move into hiding. And so Shann followed the line of the other’s fixed stare.

Before and below them lay a maze of heights and valleys, sharp drops, and saw-toothed rises. But on the far rim of that section of badlands shone the green of a Warlockian sea rippling on to the only dimly seen horizon. They were now within sight of their goal.

===Chinese===
沙恩满心以为又来了一艘巡弋的斯罗格飞船，急忙在栖身的高处找起掩蔽。若是伏到那堆塌石后面，或许能躲过对方的耳目。可托尔瓦德却没有半点要藏的意思。于是沙恩便顺着对方凝望的方向望去。

他们面前和脚下，是一大片高峰与深谷、陡崖与锯齿状山脊交织的迷宫。但在那片荒原劣地的远缘，闪耀着沃洛克海的碧绿，层层涟漪推向隐约可见的天际线。他们的目标已经在望了。

===Original===
Had they had one of the exploration sky-flitters from the overrun camp, they could have walked its beach sands within the hour. Instead, they fought their way through a Devil-designed country for the next two days. Twice they had narrow escapes from the Throg ship﻿—or ships﻿—which continued to sweep across the rugged line of the coast, and only a quick dive to cover, wasting precious time cowering like trapped animals, saved them from discovery. But at least the hound did not bay again on the tangled trail they left, and they hoped that the trap and the clak-claks had put that monster permanently out of service.

===Chinese===
倘若他们手头有一架营地失陷时留下的勘测飞梭，不出一小时便能踏上那片海滩的沙地。可他们没有，只能一路苦斗，穿过这片魔鬼设计的国土，整整走了两天。斯罗格飞船——也许不止一艘——继续沿崎岖的海岸线来回巡扫，他们两次死里逃生，全靠迅速扑进掩体，像落网的困兽一样缩着身子虚耗宝贵时光，才免于暴露。但至少“猎犬”再没有循着他们留下的一路乱迹吠叫，他们指望陷阱和克拉克鸟已经让那怪物永远歇了业。

===Original===
On the third day they came down to one of those fjords which tongued inland, fringing the coast. There had been no lack of hunting in the narrow valleys through which they had threaded, so both men and wolverines were well fed. Though animal fur wore better than the now tattered uniforms of the men.

“Now where?” Shann asked.

===Chinese===
第三天，他们下到一条峡湾边——这样的峡湾探进内陆，沿海岸缀成一排。他们一路穿行的窄谷里猎物不缺，人和狼獾都吃得饱饱的。虽说兽皮也比两人如今褴褛的制服更经磨。

「现在去哪儿？」沙恩问。

===Original===
Would he now learn the purpose driving Thorvald on to this coastland? Certainly such broken country afforded good hiding, but no better concealment than the mountains of the interior.

The Survey officer turned slowly around on the shingle, studying the heights behind them as well as the angle of the inlet where the wavelets lapped almost at their battered boot tips. Opening his treasured map case, he began a patient checking of landmarks against several of the strips he carried. “We’ll have to get on down to the true coast.”

Shann leaned against the trunk of a conical branched mountain tree, pulling absently at the shreds of wine-colored bark being shed in seasonal change. The chill they had known in the upper valleys was succeeded here by a humid warmth. Spring was becoming a summer such as this northern continent knew. Even the fresh wind, blowing in from the outer sea, had already lost some of the bite they had felt two days before when its salt-laden mistiness had first struck them.

===Chinese===
这回他能弄明白托尔瓦德执意奔向这片海岸的目的了吗？这样支离破碎的地界固然好藏身，可未必比内陆的群山更能掩蔽行踪。

勘测队军官在砾石滩上缓缓转过身，端详他们身后的高峰，也端详这道水湾的走向——细浪几乎舔上他们那双破烂靴子的靴尖。他打开视若珍宝的地图盒，拿着随身携带的几幅条图，开始耐心地逐一核对地标。「我们得再往下走，走到真正的海岸去。」

沙恩倚着一棵圆锥形分枝的山地树干，心不在焉地扯着一条条酒红色的树皮——正逢换季，树皮片片剥落。高谷里领教过的那股寒意，到这里已换作一阵潮润的暖意。春天正在变成这北方大陆惯有的那种夏天。就连从外海吹来的清风，也磨去了几分两天前含盐的水雾初初扑面时的那股凛冽。

===Original===
“Then what do we do there?” Shann persisted.

Thorvald brought over the map, his black-rimmed nail tracing a route down one of the fjords, slanting out to indicate a lace of islands extending in a beaded line across the sea.

“We head for these.”

===Chinese===
「那到了那儿又做什么？」沙恩追问。

托尔瓦德把地图拿过来，用乌黑的指甲沿一道峡湾描出一条路线，又斜斜指出去，点向一串岛屿——它们连成一线念珠横贯海面，宛如一条花边。

「我们就奔这些岛去。」

===Original===
To Shann that made no sense at all. Those islands﻿ ﻿… why, they would offer less chance of establishing a safe base than the broken land in which they now stood. Even the survey scouts had given those spots of sea-encircled earth the most cursory examination from the air.

“Why?” he asked bluntly. So far he had followed orders because they had for the most part made sense. But he was not giving obedience to Thorvald as a matter of rank alone.

“Because there is something out there, something which may make all the difference now. Warlock isn’t an empty world.”

===Chinese===
在沙恩看来，这简直毫无道理可言。那些岛……嘿，在那上头建立安全营地的指望，还不如他们此刻立足的这片破碎高地。就连勘测队的侦察员，也不过从空中对那些四面环海的弹丸之地做过最粗略的查看。

「为什么？」他问得直白。到如今他都服从命令，是因为那些命令大体上说得出道理。可他服从托尔瓦德，并不是单凭军阶。

「因为那边有一样东西，一样眼下可能扭转全局的东西。沃洛克并不是一颗空荡荡的星球。」

===Original===
Shann jerked free a long thong of loose bark, rolling it between his fingers. Had Thorvald cracked? He knew that the officer had disagreed with the findings of the team and had been an unconvinced minority of one who had refused to subscribe to the report that Warlock had no native intelligent life and therefore was ready and waiting for human settlement because it was technically an empty world. But to continue to cling to that belief without a single concrete proof was certainly a sign of mental imbalance.

And Thorvald was regarding him now with frowning impatience. You were supposed to humor delusions, weren’t you? Only, could you surrender and humor a wild idea which might mean your death? If Thorvald wanted to go island-hopping in chance of discovering what never had existed, Shann need not accompany him. And if the officer tried to use force, well, Shann was armed with a stunner, and had, he believed, more control over the wolverines. Perhaps if he merely gave lip agreement to this project﻿ ﻿… Only he didn’t believe, noting the light deep in those gray eyes holding on him, that anybody could talk Thorvald out of this particular obsession.

“You don’t believe me, do you?” The impatience arose hotly in that demand.

===Chinese===
沙恩猛地扯下一条长长的松皮，在指间搓着。托尔瓦德该不会是疯了吧？他知道这位军官一向不认同全队的结论，是唯一没被说服、拒绝在那份报告上署名的少数派——报告宣称沃洛克没有土著智慧生命，因而是一颗现成的空星，正虚位以待人类前来定居。可是连一件具体证据都没有，还要死抱着那个信念不放，那准是精神失衡的征兆。

而托尔瓦德此刻正皱着眉，不耐烦地打量他。人家犯了妄想，你不是该顺着哄吗？只是，一个兴许要你命的狂想，你也能俯首迁就吗？托尔瓦德要想跳岛碰运气，去找那从来不存在的东西，沙恩大可不必奉陪。军官若想动硬的，好啊，沙恩手里也有一支击晕枪，而且他自信更能号令那两只狼獾。要不，只对这个计划口头敷衍一番……只是，瞧着那双灰眼睛深处锁定自己的光，他不信这世上谁劝得动托尔瓦德放下这个执念。

「你不信我，对不对？」那声逼问里腾起一股火气。

===Original===
“Why shouldn’t I?” Shann tried to temporize. “You’ve had a lot of exploration experience; you should know about such things. I don’t pretend to be any authority.”

Thorvald refolded the map and placed it in the case. Then he pulled at the sealing of his blouse, groping in an inner secret pocket. He uncurled his fingers to display his treasure.

On his palm lay a coin-shaped medallion, bone-white but possessing an odd luster which bone would not normally show. And it was carved. Shann put out a finger, though he had a strange reluctance to touch the object. When he did he experienced a sensation close to the tingle of a mild electric shock. And once he had made that contact, he was also impelled to pick up that disk and examine it more closely.

===Chinese===
「我凭什么不信？」沙恩想拖一时是一时。「你勘探经验丰富，这种事该你懂。我可不敢冒充权威。」

托尔瓦德把地图重新叠好，放回盒中。接着他扯开军上衣的封襟，往内里一个暗袋中摸索。他摊开手指，亮出他的珍藏。

他掌心躺着一枚硬币形状的圆牌（medallion），骨白色，泛着一种骨头通常不会有的异样光泽。上面还镌着花纹。沙恩伸出一根手指，心里却莫名生出一股不愿触碰那物件的抵触。指尖一搭上去，他便感到一阵近似轻微电击的麻刺。而这一触之后，他又被一股冲动攫住，想把那圆盘拿起来细看。

===Original===
The carved pattern was very intricate and had been done with great delicacy and skill, though the whorls, oddly shaped knobs, ribbon tracings, made no connected design he could determine. After a moment or two of study, Shann became aware that his eyes, following those twists and twirls, were “fixed,” that it required a distinct effort to look away from the thing. Feeling some of that same alarm as he had known when he first heard the wailing of the Throg hound, he let the disk fall back into Thorvald’s hold, even more disturbed when he discovered that to relinquish his grasp required some exercise of will.

“What is it?”

===Chinese===
镌纹繁复之极，刻工精巧娴熟，只是那些涡纹、形状古怪的圆突和缎带似的细线，拼不出一幅他能辨认的连贯图案。端详片刻之后，沙恩察觉自己的目光已被那些盘旋缭绕的纹路「定」住了——要把视线从那东西上挪开，竟得花一番实实在在的力气。他心里涌起初闻斯罗格“猎犬”哀嚎时那股警惧，赶紧让圆盘落回托尔瓦德手中；待发现自己连松开手指都须动用意志，他愈发惊惶不安了。

「这是什么？」

===Original===
Thorvald restored the coin to his hiding place.

“You tell me. I can say this much, there is no listing for anything even remotely akin to this in the Archives.”

Shann’s eyes widened. He absently rubbed the fingers which had held the bone coin﻿—if it was a coin﻿—back and forth across the torn front of his blouse. That tingle﻿ ﻿… did he still feel it? Or was his imagination at work again? But an object not listed in the exhaustive Survey Archives would mean some totally new civilization, a new stellar race.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德把那圆牌放回藏处。

「你来说吧。我只能告诉你一点：档案库（the Archives）里查不到任何与这东西有一丝近似之处的记录。」

沙恩睁大了眼睛。他怔怔地摩挲着捏过那枚骨圆牌的手指——倘若它当真算枚硬币的话——在撕破的军上衣前襟上来回蹭着。那阵麻刺……他还感觉得到么？还是又是想象力在作祟？可一件在包罗万象的勘测队档案库里都查不到的物件，便意味着某种全然陌生的文明，一个新的星际种族。

===Original===
“It is definitely a created article,” the Survey officer continued. “And it was found on the beach of one of those sea islands.”

“Throg?” But Shann already knew the answer to that.

“Throg work﻿—*this*?” Thorvald was openly scornful. “Throgs have no conception of such art. You must have seen their metal plates﻿—those are the beetle-heads’ idea of beauty. Have those the slightest resemblance to this?”

===Chinese===
「这确凿是件人造物。」勘测队军官接着说，「而且，它是在其中一座海岛的海滩上发现的。」

「斯罗格人的？」可沙恩心里早知道答案。

「斯罗格人的活计——*这个*？」托尔瓦德一脸毫不掩饰的讥诮。「斯罗格人对这种艺术毫无概念。他们的金属板面你总见过——那便是甲虫脑袋心目中的美。那玩意儿跟这个有半点相像吗？」

===Original===
“Then who made it?”

“Either Warlock has﻿—or once had﻿—a native race advanced enough in a well-established form of civilization to develop such a sophisticated type of art, or there have been other visitors from space here before us and the Throgs. And the latter possibility I don’t believe﻿—”

“Why?”

===Chinese===
「那是谁造的？」

「要么沃洛克现在有——或曾经有过——一个土著种族，文明形态成熟稳固，发达到了足以孕育这般精妙艺术的程度；要么便是在我们和斯罗格人之前，另有太空来客到过此地。而后一种可能，我不信——」

「为什么？」

===Original===
“Because this was carved of bone or an allied substance. We haven’t been quite able to identify it in the labs, but it is basically organic material. It was found exposed to the weather and yet it is in perfect condition, could have been carved any time within the past five years. It has been handled, yes, but not roughly. And we have come across evidences of no other star-cruising races or species save ourselves and the Throgs. No, I say this was made here on Warlock, not too long ago, and by intelligent beings of a very high grade of civilization.”

“But they would have cities,” protested Shann. “We’ve been here for months, explored all over this continent. We would have seen them or some traces of them.”

“An old race, maybe,” Thorvald mused, “a very old race, perhaps in decline, reduced to a remnant in numbers with good reason to retire into hiding. No, we’ve discovered no cities, no evidence of a native culture past or present. But this﻿—” he touched the front of his blouse﻿—“was found on the shore of an island. We may have been looking in the wrong place for our natives.”

===Chinese===
「因为这东西是用骨头或相近材质雕成的。我们在实验室里始终未能完全鉴定出来，但它基本上是有机材料。它被发现时暴露在风吹日晒之下，品相却完好无损，雕刻年代不出过去五年之内。它被人摆弄过，这不假，但动作并不粗鲁。而除我们和斯罗格人之外，我们从未撞见过任何其他星际航行种族或物种的踪迹。不，我说，这东西就是在沃洛克本地造的，为时不久，出自一种文明程度极高的智慧生命之手。」

「可他们总该有城市吧。」沙恩提出异议。「我们在这儿待了好几个月，整个大陆都勘遍了。真有的话，我们总该见过他们本人，或者见过些痕迹。」

「也许是个古老的种族，」托尔瓦德沉吟道，「一个非常古老的种族，或许正在衰落，人口凋零成一脉残裔，自有一万个遁世藏匿的理由。不错，我们没发现过城市，没发现土著文化既往或现今的任何证据。可这个东西——」他碰了碰军上衣的前襟——「是在一座岛的海滩上找到的。我们找土著，也许一直找错了地方。」

===Original===
“The sea﻿ ﻿…” Shann glanced with new interest at the green water surging in wavelets along the edge of the fjord.

“Just so, the sea!”

“But scouts have been here for more than a year, one team or another. And nobody saw anything or found any traces.”

===Chinese===
「海……」沙恩重新来了兴致，望向峡湾边缘翻涌细浪的绿水。

「正是，海！」

「可侦察队一队接一队，在这儿待了一年多。谁也没见过什么，没找到过什么痕迹。」

===Original===
“All four of our base camps were set inland, our explorations along the coast were mainly carried out by flitter, except for one party﻿—the one which found this. And there may be excellent local reasons why any native never showed himself to us. For that matter, they may not be able to exist on land at all, any more than we could live without artificial aids in the sea.”

“Now﻿—?”

“Now we must make a real attempt to find them if they do exist anywhere near here. A friendly native race could make all the difference in the world in any struggle with the Throgs.”

===Chinese===
「我们的四座基地营地全设在内陆，沿海的勘察多半是乘飞梭进行的，只有一支小队是例外——找到这东西的正是他们。至于土著为何从不向我们露面，也许有着非常充分的本地缘由。说不定他们根本无法在陆地上生存，就像我们不用人工装备便活在海里一样。」

「现在——？」

「现在，如果他们当真存在于这附近什么地方，我们就必须真正下一番功夫去找他们。在与斯罗格人的任何较量中，一个友好的土著种族都可能彻底扭转大局。」

===Original===
“Then you did have more than the dreams to back you when you argued with Fenniston!” Shann cut in.

Thorvald’s eyes were on him again. “When did you hear that, Lantee?”

To his great embarrassment, Shann found himself flushing. “I heard you, the day you left for Headquarters,” he admitted, and then added in his own defense, “Probably half the camp did, too.”

===Chinese===
「这么说，你当年跟芬尼斯顿（Fenniston）争的时候，除了那些『梦』，手里还捏着别的凭据！」沙恩插嘴道。

托尔瓦德的目光又落回他身上。「你什么时候听说的，兰蒂？」

沙恩发觉自己红了脸，窘得不行。「你动身去总部那天，我听见的。」他认了，随即又替自己辩上一句，「多半大半个营地也都听见了。」

===Original===
Thorvald’s gathering frown flickered away. He gave a snort of laughter. “Yes, I guess we did rather get to the bellowing point that morning. The dreams﻿—” he came back to the subject﻿—“Yes, the dreams were﻿—are﻿—important. We had their warning from the start. Lorry was the First-In Scout who charted Warlock, and he is a good man. I guess I can break secret now to tell you that his ship was equipped with a new experimental device which recorded﻿—well, you might call it an ‘emanation’﻿—a radiation so faint its source could not be traced. And it registered whenever Lorry had one of those dreams. Unfortunately, the machine was very new, very much in the untested stage, and its performance when checked later in the lab was erratic enough so the powers-that-be questioned all its readings. They produced a half dozen answers to account for that tape, and Lorry only caught the recording as long as he was on a big bay to the south.

“Then when two check flights came in later, carrying perfected machines and getting no recordings, it was all written off as a mistake in the first experiment. A planet such as Warlock is too big a find to throw away when there was no proof of occupancy. And the settlement boys rushed matters right along.”

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德眉间聚起的怒意一闪而散。他噗地哼笑一声。「是啊，那天早上我们大概确实吵到咆哮的地步了。那些『梦』——」他回到正题——「不错，那些『梦』当时是——现在也是——要紧的。我们从一开始就得了它们的警告。为沃洛克绘制星图的先遣侦察员是洛里（Lorry），那人是个靠得住的角色。我想现在可以破例向你泄一回密：他的船上装了一台新式的实验性装置，能记录下——嗯，你可以称之为一种『散发』——一种微弱得无法追溯来源的辐射。每当洛里做那种梦，它就有反应。不幸的是，那台机器非常新，还完全处在未经检验的阶段，后来在实验室里复核性能时，表现又飘忽得可以，以致当权的那几位把它所有的读数都打上了问号。他们给那卷记录带编排出六种解释，而洛里也只是在南方一座大海湾上空时，才捕捉到过那段记录。

「后来两趟复核飞行抵达，装的是改良完善的机器，却什么也没录到，于是整桩事被一笔勾销，算作头一次实验出的错。像沃洛克这样的行星是个天大的发现，在对方的占据拿不出证据的情况下，谁也舍不得扔掉。于是搞定居事务的那伙人便急急忙忙把事情办了下去。」

===Original===
Shann recalled his own vivid dream of the skull-rock set in the lap of water﻿—this sea? And another small point fell into place to furnish the beginning of a pattern. “I was asleep on the raft when I dreamed about that skull-mountain,” he said slowly, wondering if he were making sense.

===Chinese===
沙恩回想自己那个鲜活的梦：一座骷髅山安卧在水的怀抱里——就是这片海么？又一个小点归了位，凑出一幅图案的开端。「我梦见那座骷髅山的时候，正在木筏上睡觉。」他缓缓说道，心里没底这话讲不讲得通。

===Original===
Thorvald’s head came up with the alert stance of Taggi on a strong game scent.

“Yes, on the raft you dreamed of a skull-rock. And I of a cavern with a green veil. Both of us were on water﻿—water which had an eventual connection with the sea. Could water be a conductor? I wonder﻿ ﻿…” Once again his hand went into his blouse. He crossed the strip of gravel beach and dipped fingers into the water, letting the drops fall on the carved disk he now held in his other hand.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德霍地抬起头，那副警觉劲儿，活像塔吉嗅到浓烈猎物气息时的模样。

「不错，你在木筏上梦见了骷髅山，我梦见的则是一座张着绿幕的洞窟。咱们俩都在水上——而那水，归根结底都与这片海相通。水会不会是种导体？我在想……」他的手又一次伸进军上衣。他穿过那条砾石沙滩，把手指浸进水里，再让水珠滴落到另一只手托着的那个刻纹圆盘上。

===Original===
“What are you doing?” Shann could see no purpose in that.

Thorvald did not answer. He had pressed wet hand to dry now, palm to palm, the coin cupped tightly between them. He turned a quarter circle, to face the still distant open sea.

“That way.” He spoke with a new odd tonelessness.

===Chinese===
「你在干什么？」沙恩看不出这一手有什么名堂。

托尔瓦德没有答话。他两掌相合，湿掌对干掌，把那枚圆牌紧紧扣在当中。他转过四分之一个圆，面向依然遥远的外海。

「那边。」他开口，声音里带着一种新异的平板，听不出丝毫起伏。

===Original===
Shann stared into the other’s face. All the eager alertness of only a moment earlier had been wiped away. Thorvald was no longer the man he had known, but in some frightening way a husk, holding a quite different personality. The younger Terran answered his fear with an attack from the old days of rough in-fighting in the Dumps of Tyr. He brought his right hand down hard in a sharp chop across the officer’s wrists. The bone coin spun to the sand and Thorvald stumbled, staggering forward a step or two. Before he could recover balance Shann had stamped on the medallion.

Thorvald whirled, his stunner drawn with a speed for which Shann gave him high marks. But the younger man’s own weapon was already out and ready. And he talked﻿—fast.

===Chinese===
沙恩直直盯进对方的脸。短短一刻之前还满面的急切警醒，已被抹得干干净净。托尔瓦德不再是他认识的那个人，而是以某种骇人的方式成了一具空壳，壳里装着一个截然不同的人格。年轻的地球人应付恐惧的法子，是使出当年在提尔垃圾场里粗野近身斗殴的老一套——抢先出手。他右手化作手刀，狠狠劈过军官的双腕。那枚骨圆牌旋着飞落沙地，托尔瓦德一个趔趄，踉跄着朝前抢出一两步。还没等他稳住身形，沙恩已一脚踩住了那枚圆牌。

托尔瓦德旋身回转，击晕枪出鞘之快，沙恩在心里给他打了个高分。但年轻人自己的枪也早已在手待发。同时他开了口——说得飞快。

===Original===
“That thing’s dangerous! What did you do﻿—what did it do to you?”

His demand got through to a Thorvald who was himself again.

“What was *I* doing?” came a counter demand.

===Chinese===
「那东西危险！你刚才干了什么——它对你做了什么？」

这一连串逼问，钻进了已然回魂的托尔瓦德耳中。

「*我*刚才在干什么？」对方反问。

===Original===
“You were acting like a mind-controlled.”

Thorvald stared at him incredulously, then with a growing spark of interest.

“The minute you dripped water on that thing you changed,” Shann continued.

===Chinese===
「你那副样子，活像个精神受控者（mind-controlled）。」

托尔瓦德难以置信地瞪着他，随即眼中渐次燃起一星兴味。

「你把水滴上那东西的一瞬间，人就变了。」沙恩接着说。

===Original===
Thorvald reholstered his stunner. “Yes,” he mused, “why *did* I want to drip water on it? Something prompted me﻿ ﻿…” He ran his still damp hand up the angle of his jaw, across his forehead as if to relieve some pain there. “What else did I do?”

“Faced to the sea and said ‘that way,’ ” Shann replied promptly.

“And why did you move in to stop me?”

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德把击晕枪收回枪套。「是啊，」他若有所思，「*我*当时为什么偏偏想往上面滴水？有什么东西在撺掇我……」他抬手顺着下颌的棱角往上抹，抹过前额，那手还潮着，仿佛要压平那里的什么疼痛。「我还做了什么？」

「转过身朝着海，说了声『那边』。」沙恩应声答道。

「那你又为什么要上来拦我？」

===Original===
Shann shrugged. “When I first touched that thing I felt a shock. And I’ve seen mind-controlled﻿—” He could have bitten his tongue for betraying that. The world of the mind-controlled was very far from the life Thorvald and his kind knew.

“Very interesting,” commented the other. “For one of so few years you seem to have seen a lot, Lantee﻿—and apparently remembered most of it. But I would agree that you are right about this little plaything; it carries a danger with it, being far less innocent than it looks.” He tore off one of the fluttering scraps of rag which now made up his sleeve. “If you’ll just remove your foot, we’ll put it out of business for now.”

He proceeded to wrap the disk well in his bit of cloth, taking care not to touch it again with his bare fingers while he stowed it away.

===Chinese===
沙恩耸耸肩。「我头一回碰那东西时，就挨过一下麻震。何况我见过精神受控者——」话一出口，他就恨不能咬掉自己的舌头。精神受控者的世界，与托尔瓦德和他那一类人所过的生活，相隔不知多少重天。

「有意思。」对方评道。「年纪这么轻，见识倒不少啊，兰蒂——而且看样子大半还记得。不过这个小小玩物，我同意你的看法；它随身带着危险，远不像外表那样无害。」他扯下一条如今权当衣袖、正随风飘舞的破布条。「麻烦把脚挪开，咱们先叫它歇业。」

他动手用那片布把圆盘裹了个严实，收起来的时候，小心翼翼不再让光手指碰它。

===Original===
“I don’t know what we have in this﻿—a key to unlock a door, a trap to catch the unwary. I can’t guess how or why it works. But we can be reasonably sure it’s not just some carefree maiden’s locket, nor the equivalent of a credit to spend in the nearest bar. So it pointed me to the sea, did it? Well, that much I am willing to allow. Maybe we’ll be able to return it to the owner, *after* we learn who﻿—or what﻿—that owner is.”

Shann gazed down at the green water, opaque, not to be pierced to the depths by human sight. Anything might lurk there. Suddenly the Throgs became normal when balanced against an unknown living in the murky depths of an aquatic world. Another attack on the Throg-held camp could be well preferred to such exploration as Thorvald had in mind. Yet Shann did not voice any protest as the Survey officer faced again in the same direction as the disk had pointed him moments before.

===Chinese===
「我不知道咱们手里这算个什么——一把能开某扇门的钥匙，还是一个专逮粗心人的陷阱。它怎么起作用、为何起作用，我猜不透。但有一点咱们大可放心：它总不会只是哪位无忧无虑的少女的项链坠盒，也不是一枚能拿去最近的酒吧花掉的信用币。这么说，它给我指了海的方向，是么？好吧，这一点我认账。也许咱们还能把它物归原主——*等*咱们弄清那位主是谁、或者是何物之后。」

沙恩俯视那片绿水，水色滞重不透，人眼望不进它的深处。那里头什么都可能潜伏着。同栖身于水世界幽暗深渊里的未知之物一比，斯罗格人忽然显得正常起来。比起托尔瓦德心里盘算的这种勘探，再去攻打一次斯罗格人盘踞的营地，倒不失为更好的差事。然而当勘测队军官重新朝方才圆盘指引的方向转过身去时，沙恩没有出声提出半句异议。

## VIII Utgard / VIII 尤特加德

===Original===
A wind from the west sprang up an hour before sunset, lashing waves inland until their spray was a salt mist in the air, a mist to sodden clothing, plaster hair to the skull, leaving a brine slime across the skin. Yet Thorvald hunted no shelter, in spite of the promise in the rough shoreline at their backs. The sand in which their boots slipped and slid was coarse stuff, hardly finer than gravel, studded with nests of drift﻿—bone-white or grayed or pale lavender﻿—smoothed and stored by the seasons of low tides and high, seasonal storms and hurricanes. A wild shore and a forbidding one, to arouse Shann’s distrust, perhaps a fitting goal for that disk’s guiding.

===Chinese===
日落前一小时，西风骤起，把浪头抽打进内陆，浪花化作空气里的一团盐雾——这雾能浸透衣裳，把头发黏贴在头骨上，给皮肤蒙上一层咸黏的涎沫。可托尔瓦德并不寻觅遮蔽，尽管身后那条嶙峋的海岸线看似不乏可资利用之处。他们脚下的沙地尽是粗粒，比砾石细不了多少，上面嵌着一窝窝浮木——骨白色、灰白色，或是淡紫色——被潮起潮落的季候、被一年年的风暴与飓风磨得溜圆、码得齐整。一片荒凉的海岸，一片令人生畏的海岸，激起了沙恩的不信任；而它，也许正是那只圆盘指引而来的恰切终点。

===Original===
Shann had tasted loneliness in the mountains, experienced the strange world of the river at night lighted by the wan radiance of glowing shrubs and plants, forced the starkness of the heights. Yet there had been through all that journeying a general resemblance to his own past on other worlds. A tree was a tree, whether it bore purple foliage or was red-veined. A rock was a rock, a river a river. They were equally hard and wet on Warlock or Tyr.

But now a veil he could not describe, even in his own thoughts, hung between him and the sand over which he walked, between him and the sea which sent spray to wet his torn clothing, between him and that wild wrack of long-ago storms. He could put out his hand and touch sand, drift, spray; yet they were a setting where something lay hidden behind that setting﻿—something watched, calculatingly, with intelligence, and a set of emotions and values he did not, could not share.

===Chinese===
在群山之中，沙恩尝过孤独的滋味；在幽光灌木和植物照出的微茫里，他体验过夜间河流的诡奇世界；他也领教过高地的荒冷。然而一路行来，那些地方同他过去在别的行星上的经历总还有几分相像。树总是树，不管它长着紫色的叶子还是有红的叶脉。石头总是石头，河总是河。无论在沃洛克还是提尔，它们一样坚硬、一样湿冷。

可是此刻，一道他无法形容的帷幕——哪怕在自家心里也形容不出——垂落在他与脚下沙地之间，他与打湿他破衣裳的浪沫之间，他与那片久远风暴遗下的荒凉残骸之间。他可以伸出手，摸到沙子、浮木、浪花；然而它们只是一层布景，布景背后藏着什么东西——什么东西正在注视着，工于心计地、带着心智，还带着一套他无法共通、也决计共通不了的情感与价值。

===Original===
“… storm coming.” Thorvald paused in the buffeting of wind and spray, watching the fury of the tossing sea. The sun was still a pale smear just above the horizon. And it gave light enough to make out that trickle of islands melting out to obscurity.

“Utgard﻿—”

“Utgard?” Shann repeated, the strange word holding no meaning for him.

===Chinese===
“……风暴要来了。”托尔瓦德在风浪的扑打中顿住话头，望着怒海翻腾的凶势。太阳还悬在地平线上方，一道苍白的抹痕。那点光亮，恰够人辨认出那一串渐渐隐没进苍茫里的小岛。

“尤特加德——”

“尤特加德？”沙恩重复了一遍，这个古怪的词在他听来毫无意义。

===Original===
“Legend of my people.” Thorvald smeared spray from his face with one hand. “Utgard, those outermost islands where dwell the giants who are the mortal enemies of the old gods.”

===Chinese===
“我族人的古老传说。”托尔瓦德抬手抹去脸上的浪沫。“尤特加德，那些最外缘的岛屿，巨人们的居所——他们与古老诸神是不共戴天的死敌。”

===Original===
Those dark lumps, most of them bare rock, only a few crowned with stunted vegetation, might well harbor *anything*, Shann decided, giants or the malignant spirits of any race. Perhaps even the Throgs had their tales of evil things in the night, beetle monsters to people wild, unknown lands. He caught at Thorvald’s arm and suggested a practical course of action.

===Chinese===
沙恩暗自断定，那些黑黢黢的岛坨——大都只是秃岩，只有寥寥几座顶着蓬乱的矮曲草木——里面藏下*任何东西*都有可能：巨人也好，随便哪个种族的邪灵也好。兴许连斯罗格人也有自己夜里恶物的传说，用甲虫怪物来充作蛮荒未知之地的居民。他一把攥住托尔瓦德的手臂，提出了一个切合实际的做法。

===Original===
“We’ll need shelter before the storm strikes.” To Shann’s relief the other nodded.

===Chinese===
“风暴砸下来之前，咱们得先找到藏身处。”让沙恩松了口气的是，对方点了点头。

===Original===
They trailed back across the beach, their backs now to the sea and Utgard. That harsh-sounding name did so well fit the line of islands and islets, Shann repeated it to himself. Here the beach was narrow, a strip of blue sand-gravel walled by wave-worn boulders. And from that barrier of stones piled into a breastwork by chance, interwoven with bone-bare drift, arose the first of the cliffs. Shann studied the terrain with increasing uneasiness. To be caught between a sea, whipped inland by a storm wind, and that cliff would be a risk he did not like to consider, as ignorant of field lore as he was. They must locate some break nearer than the fjord, down which they had come. And they must find it soon, before the daylight was gone and the full fury of bad weather struck.

===Chinese===
他们折返回身穿过海滩，这下背对着大海和尤特加德了。那个发音粗厉的名字，跟这一列大岛小屿实在般配，沙恩在心里又默念了一遍。这里的海滩很窄，一条蓝色的沙砾带，外侧垒着一堵浪蚀巨石砌成的墙。从那道恰似垒成胸墙的石垒、连同编插其间的白骨般光秃的浮木之后，拔起了第一道崖壁。沙恩打量着这片地形，心里越发不安。一头是被狂风抽打进内陆的大海，一头是那道悬崖——夹在中间会是什么下场，以他对野外门道的无知，他连想都不愿去想。他们必须找到一处比来路那道峡湾更近的豁口，而且必须尽快，赶在天光灭尽、坏天气的全部凶威砸下来之前。

===Original===
In the end the wolverines discovered an exit, just as they had found the passage through the mountain. Taggi nosed into a darker line down the face of the cliff and disappeared, Togi duplicating that feat. Shann trailed them, finding the opening a tight squeeze.

He squirmed into dimness, his outstretched hands meeting a rough stone surface sloping upward. After gaining a point about eight feet above the beach he was able to look back and down through the seaward slit. Open to the sky the crevice proved a doorway to a narrow valley, not unlike those which housed the fjords, but provided with a thick growth of vegetation well protected by the high walls.

===Chinese===
到头来还是狼獾们找到了出口，正如当初穿山的通路也是它们寻出来的。塔吉把鼻子拱进崖壁上一道颜色更深的细缝，一闪身没了影，托吉如法炮制。沙恩跟了上去，发现那洞口挤得很。

他扭着身子钻进昏暗里，伸出的双手触到一面斜向上行的粗砺岩面。爬到高出海滩约八英尺的地方，他已经能透过朝海的那道窄缝回身下望了。这道裂缝上接青天，竟是一扇门户，通向一条狭谷——与那些容着峡湾的谷地颇有几分相像，只是谷中草木繁茂，被两侧高墙护得严严实实。

===Original===
Working as a now well-rehearsed team, the men set up a shelter of saplings and brush, the back to the slit through which wind was still able to tear a way. Walled in by stone and knowing that no Throg flyer would attempt to fly in the face of the coming storm, they dared make a fire. The warmth was a comfort to their bodies, just as the light of the flames, men’s age-old hearth companion, was a comfort to the fugitives’ spirits. Those dancing spears of red, for Shann at least, burned away that veil of otherworldliness which had enwrapped the beach, providing in the night an illusion of the home he had never really known.

===Chinese===
两人如今搭档得已是娴熟，架起树苗和枝条搭出一座窝棚，背抵着那道仍能让风撕开一条路来的窄缝。四下有石壁围护，又明知斯罗格人的飞行器决不敢顶着将至的风暴起飞，他们便斗胆生起了火。暖意抚慰着他们的身体，正如火光——人类亘古以来炉边的伙伴——抚慰着这两个逃亡者的心神。那一枝枝舞动的红焰，至少对沙恩而言，烧穿了裹住海滩的那层非人间的迷雾，在黑夜里造出一种家的幻象——一个他其实从来不曾有过的家。

===Original===
But the wind and the weather did not keep truce very long. A wailing blast around the upper peaks produced a caterwauling to equal the voices of half a dozen Throg hounds. And in their poor shelter the Terrans not only heard the thunderous boom of surf, but felt the vibration of that beat pounding through the very ground on which they lay. The sea must have long since covered the beach over which they had come and was now trying its strength against the rock of the cliff barrier. They could not talk to each other over that din, although shoulder touched shoulder.

===Chinese===
可风与天气没能休战多久。峰顶四周一声呼啸的狂飙，引出一阵凄厉的尖嚎，抵得上六头斯罗格“猎犬”齐声狂吠。在他们那座寒酸的窝棚里，两个地球人不但听见惊涛的雷鸣，还觉出那轰击的震颤，透过他们身下的地面一阵阵传来。大海想必早已淹过他们来时的那片海滩，此刻正拿崖壁屏障的岩石试它的膂力。隔着那片喧嚣，他们没法交谈，虽说肩挨着肩。

===Original===
The last flush of amber vanished from the sky with the speed of a dropped curtain. Tonight no period of twilight divided night from day, but their portion of Warlock was plunged abruptly into darkness. The wolverines crowded into their small haven, whining deep in their throats. Shann ran his hands along their furred bodies, trying to give them a reassurance he himself did not feel. Never before when on stable land had he been so aware of the unleashed terrors nature could exert, the forces against which all mankind’s controls were as nothing.

===Chinese===
最后一抹琥珀色从天上消失了，快得像一道骤然垂落的幕布。今夜没有薄暮时分来划分昼夜，他们所在的这一角沃洛克蓦地沉入黑暗。狼獾们挤进这个小避风港，喉咙深处呜呜低哼。沙恩用手顺着它们毛茸茸的身子抚过去，想给它们一点自己都感受不到的宽慰。身在安稳的陆地上，他却从未如此真切地意识到，大自然一旦撒开缰绳能施出怎样可怖的威能——在那种力量面前，人类的一切掌控都不值一提。

===Original===
Time could no longer be measured by any set of minutes or hours. There was only darkness, the howling winds, and the salty rain which must be in part the breath of the sea driven in upon them. The comforting fire vanished, chill and dankness crept up to cramp their bodies, so that now and again they were forced to their feet, to swing arms, stamp, drive the blood into faster circulation.

Later came a time when the wind died, no longer driving the rain bullet-hard against and through their flimsy shelter. Then they slept in the thick unconsciousness of exhaustion.

===Chinese===
时间再不能用分秒或钟点来计量了。只剩下黑暗、呼啸的狂风，还有咸涩的雨——那雨想必有一半是大海的呼吸，被风驱赶着泼进他们藏身之处。给人安慰的火熄灭了，寒气与潮气悄悄爬上来，冻得他们身子发僵，害得他们不时被迫起身，抡抡胳膊、跺跺脚，催动血液加快流转。

后来，风终于衰歇了，不再把雨点像子弹似的硬邦邦地砸透他们那单薄的窝棚。于是他们沉沉睡去，坠入筋疲力尽换来的深沉的无知觉。

===Original===
A red-purple skull﻿—and from its eye sockets the flying things﻿—kept coming﻿ ﻿… going﻿ ﻿… Shann trod on an unsteady foundation which dipped under his weight as had the raft of the river voyage. He was drawing nearer to that great head, could see now how waves curled about the angle of the lower jaw, slapping inward between gaps of missing teeth﻿—which were really broken fangs of rock﻿—as if the skull now and then sucked reviving moisture from the water. The aperture marking the nose was closer to a snout, and the hole was dark, dark as the empty eye sockets. Yet that darkness was drawing him past any effort to escape he could summon. And then that on which he rode so perilously was carried forward by the waves, grated against the jawbone, while against his own fighting will his hands arose above his head, reaching for a hold to draw his shrinking body up the stark surface to that snout-passage.

===Chinese===
一具红紫色的骷髅——还有眼窝里那些会飞的东西——不住地涌来……退去……沙恩脚下踩着一片不稳的根基，随着他的体重一沉一沉，恰似河上航行时那只木筏。他正朝那颗巨硕的头颅靠近，如今已能看清波浪如何卷上下颌的转角，从缺牙的豁缝间往里拍击——那些“牙齿”其实是崩断的岩尖——仿佛骷髅时时从水里吮吸几分回春的水汽。标出鼻孔的那处开口更近乎一张兽吻，洞里黑沉沉，同空空的眼窝一样黑。然而那片黑暗正牵引着他，任他使出什么挣脱的力气也是枉然。接着，载着他如此险象环生的那方物事被浪头推着前行，磕上了颌骨；而他那双手竟违逆他自己奋抗的意志，高举过头，去够可以攀抓的地方，要把他瑟缩的身子拖上那光秃秃的岩面，拖向兽吻深处的通道。

===Original===
“Lantee!” A hand jerked him back, broke that compulsion﻿—and the dream. Shann opened his eyes with difficulty, his lashes seemed glued to his cheeks.

He might have been surveying a submerged world. Thin streamers of fog twined up from the earth as if they grew from seeds planted by the storm. But there was no wind, no sound from the peaks. Only under his stiff body Shann could still feel that vibration which was the sea battering against the cliff wall.

===Chinese===
“兰蒂！”一只手猛地把他拽了回来，击碎了那股摄力——也击碎了梦。沙恩艰难地睁开眼睛，睫毛像是黏在了脸颊上。

他这情形，倒像是在勘察一座没入水底的世界。一缕缕薄雾从地面盘旋而上，仿佛是从风暴播下的种子里长出来的。可是没有风，峰峦间也无声息。只有僵硬的身体底下，沙恩仍能觉出那阵震颤——那是大海在捶击崖壁。

===Original===
Thorvald was crouched beside him, his hand still urgent on the younger man’s shoulder. The officer’s face was drawn so finely that his features, sharp under the tanned skin, were akin to the skull Shann still half saw among the ascending pillars of fog.

“Storm’s over.”

Shann shivered as he sat up, hugging his arms to his chest, his tattered uniform soggy under that pressure. He felt as if he would never be warm again. When he moved sluggishly to the pit where they had kindled their handful of fire the night before he realized that the wolverines were missing.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德蹲在他身旁，一只手仍急切地按在年轻人肩上。军官的脸绷得瘦削，晒黑的皮肤底下五官棱角分明，竟与那具骷髅有几分相像——在袅袅升起的雾柱之间，沙恩还隐约看得见它。

“风暴过去了。”

沙恩坐起身时打了个寒噤，双臂紧抱在胸前，破烂的制服被这一挤更是湿漉漉地贴在身上。他觉得自己仿佛再也暖和不起来了。他拖着沉重的步子走向前夜拢起那一小堆火的火坑，这才发觉狼獾不见了。

===Original===
“Taggi﻿—?” His voice sounded rusty in his own ears, as if some of the moisture thick in the air about them had affected his vocal cords.

“Hunting.” Thorvald’s answer was clipped. He was gathering a handful of sticks from the back of their lean-to, where the protection of their own bodies had kept that kindling dry. Shann snapped a length between his hands, dropped it into the pit.

===Chinese===
“塔吉——？”在他自己听来，这嗓音锈涩得很，仿佛四周空气里那股浓重的湿气已经侵蚀了他的声带。

“打猎去了。”托尔瓦德的回答简短干脆。他正从窝棚背面收拢一小把枯枝——那里靠他们自己的身子挡着，引火柴才保持干燥。沙恩两手折断一截，丢进坑里。

===Original===
When they did coax a blaze into being they stripped, wringing out their clothing, propping it piece by steaming piece on sticks by the warmth of the flames. The moist air bit at their bodies and they moved briskly, striving to keep warm by exercise. Still the fog curled, undisturbed by any shaft of sun.

===Chinese===
等他们好歹哄起一蓬火，两人便脱光衣服，把衣裳拧干，一件件冒着热气架在火边暖处的枝条上。湿冷的空气咬噬着他们的皮肉，两人动作麻利，指望靠活动取暖。雾仍在盘旋缭绕，不见一线日光来搅扰。

===Original===
“Did you dream?” Thorvald asked abruptly.

“Yes.” Shann did not elaborate. Disturbing as his dream had been, the feeling that it was not to be shared was also strong, as strong as some order.

===Chinese===
“你做梦了？”托尔瓦德突兀地问。

“做了。”沙恩没有细说。那场梦固然扰人，可有一种感觉同样强烈：它不可与人分享——强烈得如同一道命令。

===Original===
“And so did I,” Thorvald said bleakly. “You saw your skull-mountain?”

“I was climbing it when you awoke me,” Shann returned unwillingly.

===Chinese===
“我也做了。”托尔瓦德的声音冷冷的。“你见到你那座骷髅山了？”

“你叫醒我的时候，我正往上爬呢。”沙恩不情愿地答道。

===Original===
“And I was going through my green veil when Taggi took off and wakened me. You are sure your skull exists?”

“Yes.”

“And so am I that the cavern of the veil is somewhere on this world. But why?” Thorvald stood up, the firelight marking plainly the lines between his tanned arms, his brown face and throat, and the paleness of his lean body. “Why do we dream those particular dreams?”

===Chinese===
“塔吉跑出去、把我惊醒的时候，我正穿过我那道绿幕。你敢肯定你那具骷髅真有其物？”

“肯定。”

“我也一样肯定，幕之洞窟就在这颗行星的某处。可这是为什么？”托尔瓦德站起身，火光清清楚楚地勾出几道界线：晒黑的手臂、棕色的脸庞和脖颈，同他精瘦身躯的苍白之间泾渭分明。“为什么我们偏要做那些特定的梦？”

===Original===
Shann tested the dryness of a shirt. He had no reason to try and explain the wherefore of those dreams, only was he certain that he would sometime, somewhere, find that skull, and that when he did he would climb to the doorway of the snout, pass behind to depths where the flying things might nest﻿—not because he wanted to make such an expedition, but because he must.

===Chinese===
沙恩试了试衬衫干没干。那些梦的缘由，他无意尝试解释；他只是确信，终有一天，在某个地方，他会找到那具骷髅；而一旦找到，他就会爬上兽吻的门口，钻进背后那些深处，那些会飞的东西也许就在那里筑巢——不是因为他想进行这样一场远征，而是因为他必须去。

===Original===
He drew his hands across his ribs, where pressure still brought an aching reminder of the crushing force of the energy whip the Throgs had wielded. There was no extra flesh on his body, yet muscles slid easily under the skin, a darker skin than Thorvald’s, deepening to a warm brown where it had been weathered. His hair, unclipped now for a month, was beginning to curl about his head in tight dark rings. Since he had always been the youngest or the smallest or the weakest in the world of the Dumps, of the Service, of the Team, Shann had very little personal vanity. He did possess a different type of pride, born of his own stubborn achievement in winning out over a long roster of discouragements, failures, and adverse odds.

===Chinese===
他双手抹过肋侧——那里的皮肉一经按压，仍会隐隐作痛，提醒着斯罗格人挥动能量鞭（energy whip）时那股碾碎一切的力道。他身上没有一两多余的肉，肌肉却在皮下滑动自如；那皮肤比托尔瓦德的黑些，经风吹日晒的地方更晕出温暖的棕色。他的头发已经一个月没剪，开始在头颅四周蜷成一个个紧致的黑圈。在垃圾场、在勘测队、在外勤队的天地里，他从来都是最年幼、最瘦小、最没分量的那一个，所以沙恩没什么自惜形貌的虚荣心。他倒拥有另一种自豪，那是从一场场挫折、失败与逆势的漫长清单里固执地闯出头来，才挣下的自豪。

===Original===
“Why do we dream?” he repeated Thorvald’s question. “No answer, sir.” He gave the traditional reply of the Service recruit. And a little to his surprise Thorvald laughed with a tinge of real amusement.

===Chinese===
“为什么我们会做梦？”他重复了一遍托尔瓦德的问题。“答不上来，长官。”他给出的是勘测队新兵的传统答话。而让他有点意外的是，托尔瓦德笑了，笑声里带着几分真正的兴味。

===Original===
“Where do you come from, Lantee?” He asked as if he were honestly interested.

“Tyr.”

“Caldon mines.” The Survey officer automatically matched planet to product. “How did you come into Survey?”

===Chinese===
“你是哪里人，兰蒂？”他问得像是当真心生兴趣。

“提尔。”

“卡尔登矿区（Caldon mines）。”勘测军官习惯成自然，一下就把行星对上了它的出产。“你是怎么进勘测队的？”

===Original===
Shann drew on his shirt. “Signed on as casual labor,” he returned with a spark of defiance. Thorvald had joined the Service the right way as a cadet, then a Team man, finally an officer, climbing that nice even ladder with every rung ready for him when he was prepared to mount it. What did his kind know about the labor Barracks where the dull-minded, the failures, the petty criminals on the run, lived hard under a secret social system of their own? It had taken every bit of physical endurance and energy, every fraction of stubborn will Shann could summon, for him to survive his first three months in those barracks﻿—unbroken and still eager to be Survey. He could still wonder at the unbelievable chance which had rescued him from that merely because Training Center had needed another odd hand to clean cages and feed troughs for the experimental animals.

===Chinese===
沙恩套上衬衫。“以临时工的身份签的约。”他答道，话里迸出一星挑衅。托尔瓦德入队走的是正途：先当见习生，再做外勤队员，最后成了军官，沿着那道漂亮平整的梯子往上爬，每级横档都在他准备登攀时早早候着他。他那一类人，哪里晓得劳工营房是什么样的地方——脑子迟钝的、一事无成的、亡命在逃的小罪犯们，就在那里依着一套自家的隐秘规矩苦熬度日。沙恩能撑过在那些营房里的头三个月，靠的是他所能唤起的全部体力和精力、所能拧出的每一分倔强的意志——撑过来了，没垮掉，还一心想留在勘测队。至今他仍会纳罕，把他从那里捞出来的那个机会竟好得难以置信，而缘由不过是训练中心（Training Center）缺个打杂的帮手，去清洗笼子、给实验动物添食槽罢了。

===Original===
And from the center he made a Team, because when working in a smaller group his push and attention to duty had been noticed and had paid off. Three years it had taken, but he *had* made Team stature. Not that that meant anything now. Shann pulled his boots on over the legs of rough dried coveralls and glanced up, to find Thorvald watching him with a new, questioning directness the younger man could not understand.

===Chinese===
后来他从训练中心升进了外勤队，因为在较小的小组里干活时，他那股冲劲和尽忠职守的态度被人看在了眼里，得了回报。前后花了三年，可他*确实*挣到了外勤队员的身份。尽管如今这身份已算不得什么。沙恩把靴子套上粗糙干硬的连体工作裤的裤腿，抬眼一望，正撞见托尔瓦德在盯着他，目光带着一种崭新的、探问似的直率，让年轻人摸不着头脑。

===Original===
Shann sealed his blouse and stood up, knowing the bite of hunger, dull but persistent. It was a feeling he had had so many times in the past that now he hardly gave it a second thought.

“Supplies?” He brought the subject back to the present and the practical. What did it matter why or how one Shann Lantee had come to Warlock in the first place?

===Chinese===
沙恩扣好上衣站起身，觉出饥饿的啃噬——钝钝的，却不依不饶。这种感觉他从前经历过太多回，如今几乎懒得多想。

“补给？”他把话题拉回到眼下、拉回到实际。一个沙恩·兰蒂当初究竟为什么、又是怎么来到沃洛克的，这有什么要紧？

===Original===
“What we have left of the concentrates we had better keep for emergencies.” Thorvald made no move to open the very shrunken bag he had brought from the scoutship.

He walked over to a rocky outcrop and tugged loose a yellowish tuft of plant, neither moss nor fungi but sharing attributes of both. Shann recognized it without enthusiasm as one of the varieties of native produce which could be safely digested by Terran stomachs. The stuff was almost tasteless and possessed a rather unpleasant odor. Consumed in bulk it would satisfy hunger for a time. Shann hoped that with the wolverines to aid they could go back to hunting soon.

===Chinese===
“剩下的那点浓缩口粮，咱们最好留着应急。”托尔瓦德没有动手去开他从侦察船上带来的那只瘪得厉害的袋子。

他走到一处凸岩旁，扯下一蓬泛黄的植物——既非苔藓也非菌类，却兼有两者的品性。沙恩认得它，提不起半点兴致：这是本土出产里地球人的肠胃能安全消化的品种之一。那玩意儿几乎没有味道，还有股相当难闻的气味。大口吞下去，倒也能顶一阵子饿。沙恩只盼着有狼獾帮忙，他们能很快重新打上猎。

===Original===
However, Thorvald showed no desire to head inland where they might expect to locate game. He disagreed with Shann’s suggestion for tracking Taggi and Togi when those two emerged from the underbrush obviously well fed and contented after their early morning activity.

When Shann protested with some heat, the other countered: “Didn’t you ever hear of fish, Lantee? After a storm such as last night’s, we ought to discover good pickings along the shore.”

But Shann was also sure that it was not only the thought of food which drew Thorvald back to the sea.

===Chinese===
然而，托尔瓦德毫无深入内陆的意愿——本该到那边才有指望寻到猎物。等塔吉和托吉从灌木丛里钻出来——清早的一番忙碌显然让它们吃得肚皮溜圆、心满意足——沙恩提议循着这两只狼獾的路线走，托尔瓦德却不赞成。

沙恩带着几分火气抗辩，对方回敬道：“你就没听说过鱼吗，兰蒂？经过昨晚那样一场风暴，咱们该能在岸边捡到不少好东西。”

可沙恩同样确信，把托尔瓦德拽回海边的，绝不仅仅是食物这一桩。

===Original===
They crawled back through the bolt hole. The beach of gravel-sand had vanished save for a narrow ribbon of land just at the foot of the cliffs, where the water curled in white lace about the barrier of boulders. There was no change in the dullness of the sky; no sun broke through the thick lid of clouds. And the green of the sea was ashened to gray which matched that overcast until one could strain one’s eyes trying to find the horizon, unable to mark the dividing line here between air and water.

===Chinese===
他们从那条逃生洞（bolt hole）爬了出去。砾沙的海滩不见了，只剩崖脚下一窄条地，海水在巨石垒成的屏障四周卷起白色的蕾丝。阴沉的天空没有半点起色；厚厚的云盖不见太阳钻透。海的绿也灰成了死灰，与那层阴翳浑然一色，逼得人眯酸了眼去找地平线，却怎么也辨不出此处天与水的分界线。

===Original===
Utgard was a broken necklace, the outermost island-beads lost, the inner ones more isolated by the rise in water, more forbidding. Shann let out a startled hiss of breath.

===Chinese===
尤特加德成了一串断裂的项链——最外缘的岛珠没了踪影，靠里的几颗被上涨的水面隔得愈发孤绝，愈发森然。沙恩倒抽一口冷气，嘶地出了声。

===Original===
The top of a nearby rock detached itself, drew up into a hunched thing of armor-plated scales and heavy wide-jawed head. A tail cracked into the air; a double tail split into equal forks for halfway down its length. A leg lifted as a forefoot, webbed, clawed for a new hold. This sea beast was the most formidable native thing he had sighted on Warlock, approaching in its ugliness the hound of the Throgs.

===Chinese===
近旁一块岩石的顶上忽然剥离下来，隆起成一团佝偻的东西——周身披着鳞甲，顶着一颗沉重的宽颚巨头。一条尾巴啪地甩上半空；竟是双尾，从中分作均等的两叉，直叉到半长处。一条腿抬起来充当前脚，带蹼带爪，去攀新的落点。这头海兽是他在沃洛克见过的最可畏的土产生物，论狰狞丑怪，已快赶上斯罗格人的“猎犬”了。

===Original===
Breathing in labored gusts, the thing slapped its tail down on the stones with a limpness which suggested that the raising of that appendage had overtaxed its limited supply of strength. The head sank forward, resting across one of the forelimbs. Then Shann sighted the fearsome wound in the side just before one of the larger hind legs, a ragged hole through which pumped with every one of those breaths a dark purplish stream, licked away by the waves as it trickled slickly down the rock.

“What is that?”

===Chinese===
那东西一口口喘着粗气，尾巴软塌塌地拍在石面上——看那有气无力的样子，方才把那条附属物竖起来，只怕已耗尽了它本就不多的力气。头颅向前垂落，枕在一只前肢上。这时沙恩才看见它体侧、恰在一条较大的后腿前方的那道可怕创伤——一个参差不齐的窟窿，随着每一次喘息，从中涌出一股暗紫色的血流，滑腻腻地淌下岩石，被浪头舔去。

“那是什么？”

===Original===
Thorvald shook his head. “Not on our records,” he replied absently, studying the dying creature with avid attention. “Must have been driven in by the storm. This proves there is more in the sea then we knew!”

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德摇了摇头。“档案里没有记载。”他心不在焉地答道，一双眼睛却贪婪地紧盯那垂死的生物。“准是让风暴驱进来的。这说明海里的东西，比我们所知的还要多！”

===Original===
Again the forked tail lifted and fell, the head, raised from the forelimb, stretching up and back until the white underfolds of the throat were exposed as the snout pointed almost vertically to the sky. The jaws opened and from between them came a moaning whistle, a complaint which was drowned out by the wash of the waves. Then, as if that was the last effort, the webbed, clawed feet relaxed their grip of the rock and the scaled body slid sidewise, out of their sight, into the water. There was a feather of spume to mark the plunge and nothing else.

===Chinese===
那条分叉的尾巴又扬起、落下；头颅从前肢上抬起来，向上向后伸展，直到吻部几乎垂直指天，喉部的白色褶皮尽数袒露。双颚张开，从中发出一声呜咽似的呼哨——一声哀诉，旋即被浪涛的冲刷淹没。然后，仿佛那是最后一口气力，带蹼带爪的脚松开了抓握的岩石，鳞甲的躯体侧滑下去，出了他们的视野，滑进水里。只有一羽浪花标出它坠落的地方，再无其他。

===Original===
Shann, watching to see if the reptile would surface again, sighted another object, a rounded shape floating on the sea, bobbing lightly as had their river raft.

“Look!”

===Chinese===
沙恩盯着水面，想看那头爬虫会不会再浮上来，却望见了另一样东西——一个圆滚滚的形体漂在海上，轻轻颠簸，恰似他们河上那只木筏。

“看！”

===Original===
Thorvald’s gaze followed his pointing finger and then before Shann could protest, the officer leaped outward from their perch on the cliff to the broad rock where the scaled sea dweller had lain moments earlier. He stood there, watching that drifting object with the closest attention, as Shann made the same crossing in his wake.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德的目光顺着他指的方向望去，随即，不等沙恩来得及出声反对，军官已从崖上的落脚处纵身一跃，跳上那块宽石——片刻之前那头鳞甲海兽还躺在上面。他站在那儿，目不转睛地盯着那个漂流物；沙恩跟在他身后，也作同样一跃。

===Original===
The drifting thing was oval, perhaps some six feet long and three wide, the mid point rising in a curve from the water’s edge. As far as Shann could make out in the half-light the color was a reddish-brown, the surface rough. And he thought by the way that it moved that it must be flotsam of the storm, buoyant enough to ride the waves with close to cork resiliency. To Shann’s dismay his companion began to strip.

===Chinese===
那漂流物是椭圆形的，长约六英尺、宽约三英尺，中段从水线处弓起一道弧。就着那点微光，沙恩能辨出的也就这么多：颜色红褐，表面粗糙。而从它随波的动作来看，他断定那必是风暴抛下的漂浮残骸，浮力十足，几乎像软木一般有弹性地在浪上起落。让沙恩心头发紧的是，他的同伴竟开始脱衣服了。

===Original===
“What are you going to do?”

“Get that.”

===Chinese===
“你要干什么？”

“把它弄回来。”

===Original===
Shann surveyed the water about the rock. The forked tail had sunk just there. Was the Survey officer mad enough to think he could swim unmenaced through a sea which might be infested with more such creatures? It seemed that he was, for Thorvald’s white body arched out in a dive. Shann waited, half crouched and tense, as though he could in some way attack anything rising from the depths to strike at his companion.

===Chinese===
沙恩察看那块岩石四周的水面。那条分叉的尾巴就沉在那一带。这位勘测军官莫非疯了，竟以为自己能安然无恙地游过一片也许到处潜伏着此类生物的海？看来还真疯了——托尔瓦德白晃晃的身子已经弓身跃出，一头扎进水里。沙恩半蹲着候在那里，全身绷紧，仿佛自己真能出手拦下任何从深处蹿起来袭击同伴的东西似的。

===Original===
A brown arm flashed above the surface. Thorvald swam strongly toward the floating object. He reached it, his outstretched hand rasping across the surface. And it responded so quickly to that touch that Shann guessed it was even lighter and easier to handle than he had first thought.

===Chinese===
一条棕色的手臂在水面上闪了一下。托尔瓦德奋力朝那漂浮物游去。他到了近旁，伸出的手擦过它的表面。那东西一经触碰便应声而动，沙恩由此猜到，它比他起初想的还要轻、还要好摆弄。

===Original===
Thorvald headed back, herding the thing before him. And when he climbed out on the rock, Shann was pulling up his trophy. They flipped the find over, to discover it hollow. They had, in effect, a ready-made craft not unlike a canoe with blunted bows. But the substance was surely organic: Was it shell? Shann speculated, running his finger tips over the irregular surface.

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德往回游，把它赶在前头。等他爬上岩石，沙恩正把这件战利品往上拖。两人把捡来的东西翻了个身，发现里面是空的。实际上，他们到手的是一艘现成的小舟，颇像一条两端船首皆钝的独木舟。可这材料分明是有机物：这是壳吗？沙恩一边琢磨，一边用指尖摩挲那高低不平的表面。

===Original===
The Survey officer dressed. “We have our boat,” he commented. “Now for Utgard﻿—”

Use this frail thing to dare the trip to the islands? But Shann did not protest. If the officer determined to try such a voyage, he would do it. And neither did the younger man doubt that he would accompany Thorvald.

===Chinese===
勘测军官穿好衣服。“咱们有船了。”他评了一句。“这就去尤特加德——”

乘这么个单薄玩意儿，就敢去闯那趟海岛之行？可沙恩没有提出异议。军官既然拿定主意要试这一程，他就一定会去试。而且年轻人也毫不怀疑，自己会跟着托尔瓦德一起去。

## IX One Alone / IX 独自一人

===Original===
Once again the beach was a wide expanse of shingle, drying fast under a sun hotter than any Shann had yet known on Warlock. Summer had taken a big leap forward. The Terrans worked in partial shade below a cliff overhang, not only for the protection against the sun’s rays, but also as a precaution against any roving Throg air patrol.

Under Thorvald’s direction the curious shell dragged from the sea﻿—if it were a shell, and the texture as well as the general shape suggested that﻿—was equipped with a framework to act as a stabilizing outrigger. What resulted was certainly an odd-looking craft, but one which obeyed the paddles and rode the waves easily.
===Chinese===
海滩又一次成了一大片开阔的砾石滩，在一轮烈日下飞快地晒干——这太阳的毒辣，胜过沙恩此前在沃洛克领教过的任何一轮。夏天猛地朝前跨了一大步。两个地球人在一处崖檐下的半荫里干活，既为遮挡日头，也为提防斯罗格巡逻飞行器四处游弋。

在托尔瓦德的指挥下，那具从海里拖来的古怪壳体——倘若那算得上一枚贝壳，而它的质地与大致轮廓也确实如此暗示——装上了一副框架，充作稳定船身的舷外支架（outrigger）。造出来的东西模样固然古怪，却肯听船桨的使唤，破浪而行轻松自如。

===Original===
In the full sunlight the outline of islands was clear-cut﻿—red-and-gray-rock above an aquamarine sea. The Terrans had sighted no more of the sea monsters, and the major evidence of native life along the shore was a new species of clak-claks, roosting in cliff holes and scavenging along the sands, and various queer fish and shelled things stranded in small tide pools﻿—to the delight of the wolverines, who fished eagerly up and down the beach, ready to investigate all debris of the storm.

“That should serve.” Thorvald tightened the last lashing, straightening up, his fists resting on his hips, to regard the craft with a measure of pride.

Shann was not quite so content. He had matched the Survey officer in industry, but the need for haste still eluded him. So the ship﻿—such as it was﻿—was ready. Now they would be off to explore Thorvald’s Utgard. But a small and nagging doubt inside the younger man restrained his enthusiasm over such a voyage. Fork-tail had come out of the section of ocean which they must navigate in this very crude transport. And Shann had no desire to meet an uninjured and alert fork-tail in the latter’s own territory.
===Chinese===
烈日晴光之下，群岛的轮廓历历分明——一片蓝绿色的海上，耸起一座座红灰相间的岩岛。地球人再没望见那些海中巨怪，沿岸本土生命的主要踪迹，是一种新种的克拉克鸟：它们栖在崖洞里，在沙滩上拣食腐物；还有各色怪鱼和带壳的生物，搁浅在一个个小潮池里——这可乐坏了两只狼獾，它们沿着海滩上下游走扑捞，劲头十足，风暴遗下的一切残屑都逃不过它们的查探。

“这下能用了。”托尔瓦德收紧最后一道绑索，直起腰，双拳抵在胯上，带着几分得意打量这条船。

沙恩可没这么心满意足。论干活的勤勉，他不输那位勘测队军官，可这番赶工的必要何在，他始终捉摸不透。如今船——姑且算它是条船——已经就绪。他们就要动身，去探托尔瓦德的尤特加德了。但一缕小小的、纠缠不去的疑虑压在这年轻人心里，按住了他对这趟航程的热乎劲儿。叉尾兽正是从那片海域里冒出来的，而他们就得驾着这么一条粗陋的船横渡。沙恩可不想在叉尾兽自家的地界上，撞见一条毫发无伤、警醒活泛的叉尾兽。

===Original===
“Which island do we head for?” Shann kept private his personal doubts of their success. The outmost tip of that chain was only a distant smudge lying low on the water.

“The largest﻿ ﻿… that one with trees.”

Shann whistled. Since the night of the storm the wolverines were again more amenable to the very light discipline he tried to keep. Perhaps the fury of that elemental burst had tightened the bond between men and animals, both alien to this world. Now Taggi and his mate padded toward him in answer to his summons. But would the wolverines trust the boat? Shann dared not risk their swimming, nor would he agree to leaving them behind.
===Chinese===
“我们朝哪座岛去？”沙恩把心底对成败的疑虑按住不提。岛链最远的那一端，只是低低伏在水线上、远远一抹淡淡的影子。

“最大的那座……有树的那座。”

沙恩打了个呼哨。自风暴之夜起，两只狼獾对他勉强维持的那点松散约束，又服帖了些。也许是那场天地之怒的肆虐，把人与兽之间的纽带勒得更紧了——对这个世界的原生态而言，他们双方都是异乡客。塔吉和它的伴侣应声迈着轻步朝他走来。可狼獾信得过这条船吗？沙恩不敢让它们冒险泅水，也决计不肯把它们撇下。

===Original===
Thorvald had already stored their few provisions on board. And now Shann steadied the craft against a rock which served them as a wharf, while he coaxed Taggi gently. Though the wolverine protested, he at last scrambled in, to hunch at the bottom of the shell, the picture of apprehension. Togi took longer to make up her mind. And at length Shann picked her up bodily, soothing her with quiet speech and stroking hands, to put her beside her mate.

The shell settled under the weight of the passengers, but Thorvald’s foresight concerning the use of the outrigger proved right, for the craft was seaworthy. It answered readily to the dip of their paddles as they headed in a curve, keeping the first of the islands between them and the open sea for a breakwater.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德已经把他们寥寥的一点口粮搬上了船。此刻沙恩把船稳在一块权充码头的岩石旁，柔声哄劝塔吉。狼獾虽一路抗议，末了还是爬了进去，缩在壳底，一副惶惶然的模样。托吉拿主意拿得更久。最后沙恩索性把它整个儿抱起来，轻声细语地安抚，一下下顺着毛摩挲，把它安置到伴侣身边。

壳船被乘客的分量压得一沉，但托尔瓦德在舷外支架上头的先见得到了印证——这条船经得起风浪。船桨起落，它应声而动。他们划出一道弧线，让岛链的第一座岛横在自己与外海之间，权当防波堤。

===Original===
From the air, Thorvald’s course would have been a crooked one, for he wove back and forth between the scattered islands of the chain, using their lee calm for the protection of the canoe. About two thirds of the group were barren rock, inhabited only by clak-claks and creatures closer to true Terran birds in that they wore a body plumage which resembled feathers, though their heads were naked and leathery. And, Shann noted, the clak-claks and the birds did not roost on the same islands, each choosing their own particular home while the other species did not invade that territory.

The first large-sized island they approached was crowned by trees, but it had no beach, no approach from sea level. Perhaps it might be possible to climb to the top of the cliff walls. But Thorvald did not suggest that they try it, heading on toward the next large outcrop of land and rock.
===Chinese===
若从空中俯瞰，托尔瓦德的航线必定弯弯曲曲，因为他在岛链散布的诸岛之间穿来绕去，借各岛背风处的平静水面护住独木舟。群岛中约莫三分之二是光秃秃的岩石，住户只有克拉克鸟，还有一种更接近地球真鸟的生物——它们身上披着近似羽毛的羽衣，脑袋却光裸坚韧，皮质一片。沙恩还注意到，克拉克鸟同那些鸟从不共居一岛，各挑各的家园，另一个种类也决不越界进犯。

他们驶近的头一座大岛顶着满头树木，却没有海滩，从海面的高度无路可登。崖壁顶上或许爬得上去。可托尔瓦德并不提议一试，径直朝下一处大片隆起的岛陆与岩石驶去。

===Original===
Here white lace patterned in a ring well out from the shore to mark a circle of reefs. They nosed their way patiently around the outer circumference of that threatening barrier, hunting the entrance to the lagoon. Within, there were at least two beaches with climbable ascents to the upper reaches inland. Though Shann noted that the vegetation showing was certainly not luxuriant, the few trees within their range of vision being pallid growths, rather like those they had sighted on the fringe of the desert. Leather-headed flyers wheeled out over their canoe, coasting on outspread wings to peer down at the Terran invaders in a manner which suggested intelligent curiosity.

A full flock gathered to escort them as they continued along the outer line of the reef. Thorvald impatiently dug his paddle deeper. They had explored more than half of the reef now without chancing on an entrance channel.
===Chinese===
在这里，一道白色花边远远地沿岸绣成一环，标出一圈礁石。他们耐着性子绕那道凶险屏障的外缘探行，寻找潟湖（lagoon）的入口。屏障之内，至少有两片海滩，有可以攀登的坡道通向岛内高处。只是沙恩看出，显露的植被实在谈不上丰茂，视野内寥寥几株树木尽是些苍白羸弱的植株，倒同他们在大漠边缘见过的那些颇为相像。几只皮头鸟（leather-headed flyer）兜着圈子飞出独木舟上空，平展开双翼滑翔，俯瞰这两位闯入的地球人，那副神气透着一种有智性的好奇。

整整一大群鸟聚拢来，护送他们沿礁环外缘继续行进。托尔瓦德不耐烦地把桨插得更深。此刻他们已探过礁环的一大半，仍未撞上一条入口水道。

===Original===
“Regular fence,” Shann commented. One could begin to believe that the barrier had been deliberately reared to frustrate visitors. Hot sunshine, reflected back from the surface of the waves, burned their exposed skin, so they dared not discard their ragged clothing. And the wolverines were growing increasingly restless. Shann did not know how much longer the animals would consent to their position as passengers without raising active protest.

“How about trying the next one?” he asked, knowing at the same time his companion was not in any mood to accept such a suggestion with good will.

The officer made no reply, but continued to use his steer paddle in a fashion which spelled out his stubborn determination to find a passage. This was a personal thing now, between Ragnar Thorvald of the Terran Survey and a wall of rock, and the man’s will was as strongly rooted as those water-washed stones.
===Chinese===
“简直是道围墙。”沙恩评论道。人简直要相信，这道屏障是存心筑起来败访客的兴的。灼热的阳光从浪面上反射回来，烧灼着他们裸露的皮肤，两人不敢脱下那身破衣烂衫。狼獾也越来越躁动难安。沙恩说不准，这两个家伙还会乖乖当多久乘客，才奋起抗议。

“换下一座试试怎么样？”他问，同时心里明白，同伴眼下绝没有好脾气接受这样的提议。

军官不作答，只管继续摆他那支舵桨，那架势明明白白写出他非要找到一条通道不可的执拗决心。如今这成了一桩私人恩怨，结在地球勘测队的拉格纳·托尔瓦德与一堵岩墙之间；而这人的意志，同那些任水冲刷的石头一样，扎得纹丝不动。

===Original===
On the southwestern tip of the reef they discovered a possible opening. Shann eyed the narrow space between two fanglike rocks dubiously. To him that width of water lane seemed dangerously limited, the sudden slam of a wave could dash them against either of those pillars, with disastrous results, before they could move to save themselves. But Thorvald pointed their blunt bow toward the passage with seeming confidence, and Shann knew that as far as the officer was concerned, this was their door to the lagoon.

Thorvald might be stubborn, but he was not a fool. And his training and skill in such maneuvers was proved when the canoe rode in a rising swell in and by those rocks to gain the safety, in seconds, of the calm lagoon. Shann sighed with relief, but ventured no comment.

Now they must paddle back along the inner side of the reef to locate the beaches, for fronting them on this side of the well-protected island were cliffs as formidable as those which guarded the first of the chain at which they had aimed.
===Chinese===
在礁环的西南端，他们发现了一处可能的豁口。沙恩朝两块獠牙般的礁石之间那条窄缝投去狐疑的一瞥。在他看来，那条水道宽度之局促可谓凶险：一记浪头猛然砸来，不等他们回天，就能把船摔在其中任意一根石柱上，落个灾难性的下场。可托尔瓦德带着一副胸有成竹的样子，把钝船头对准那条水道，沙恩心里明白：在这位军官看来，这就是他们进潟湖的门。

托尔瓦德固执归固执，人却不傻。独木舟乘着一浪高过一浪的涌浪，在那些礁石之间擦身而过，数秒之间便驶入平静潟湖的安稳怀抱——他在这类机动上受过的训练和练出的本事就此得到证明。沙恩如释重负地叹了口气，但没敢多嘴。

眼下他们得沿礁环内侧往回划，去找那几片海滩，因为在这座深得屏障庇护的岛上，迎着他们这一侧的，是同镇守岛链头一座岛的那些崖壁一样险峻的绝壁。

===Original===
Shann glanced now and then over the side of the boat, hoping in these shallows to sight the sea bed or some of the inhabitants of these waters. But there was no piercing that green murk. Here and there nodules of rock projected inches or feet above the surface, awash in the wavelets, to be avoided by the voyagers. Shann’s shoulders ached and burned, his muscles were unaccustomed to the steady swing of the paddles, and the fire of the sun stabbed easily through only two layers of ragged cloth to his skin. He ran a dry tongue over dryer lips and gazed eagerly ahead in search of the first of the beaches.

What was so important about this island that Thorvald *had* to make a landing here? The officer’s stories of a native race which they might turn against the Throgs to their own advantage was thin, very thin indeed. Especially now, as Shann weighed an unsupported theory against that ache in his shoulders, the possibility of being marooned on the inhospitable shore ahead, against the fifty probable dangers he could total up with very little expenditure of effort. A small nagging doubt of Thorvald’s obsession began to grow in his mind. How could Shann even be sure that that carved disk and Thorvald’s hokus-pokus with it had been on the level? On the other hand what motive would the officer have for trying such an act just to impress Shann?
===Chinese===
沙恩时不时朝船舷外瞥上一眼，指望在这片浅水里望见海底，或是这些水域的哪位住户。可那片绿色的浑浊，任什么也透不进去。四处有瘤状的礁石探出水面几英寸乃至几英尺，浸在细浪里，都得由赶路人一一避让。沙恩的肩膀又酸又烫，他的肌肉还不惯船桨这般不停的抡摆；而烈日之火轻而易举便刺透仅有的两层破布，扎上他的皮肤。他用干舌头舔了舔更干的嘴唇，急切地凝望前方，搜寻头一片海滩的踪影。

这座岛到底有什么要紧，让托尔瓦德*非*在此登陆不可？军官那套关于本土种族的说辞——说他们或许能为地球人所驱使、倒戈对付斯罗格人——实在单薄，单薄得很。尤其是此刻，沙恩把一个无凭无据的假说，同肩膀的酸痛、同困死在前方那片荒凉海岸上的可能放在一头，再同他毫不费力就能数出的五十种凶险放在另一头，两下一掂，更觉如此。对托尔瓦德那股痴迷，他心里一缕嘀咕不休的疑窦开始越长越大。沙恩又怎能确定，那块刻纹圆盘，连同托尔瓦德摆弄它时耍的那套玄虚把戏，句句是实？可话说回来，军官若单单为了镇住沙恩而演这么一出，图的又是什么？

===Original===
The beach at last! As they headed the canoe in that direction the wolverines nearly brought disaster on them. The animals’ restlessness became acute as they sighted and scented the shore and knew that they were close. Taggi reared, plunged over the side of the craft, and Shann had just time to fling his weight in the opposite direction as a counterbalance when Togi followed. They splashed shoreward while Thorvald swore fluently and Shann grabbed to save the precious supply bag. In a shower of gravel the animals made land and humped well up on the strand before pausing to shake themselves and splatter far and wide the burden of moisture transported by their shaggy fur.

Ashore, the canoe became a clumsy burden and, light as the craft was, both of the men sweated to get it up on the beach without snagging the outrigger against stones and brush. With the thought of a Throg patrol in mind they worked swiftly to cover it.
===Chinese===
海滩总算到了！他们把独木舟朝那个方向调转时，两只狼獾险些酿成大祸。岸一入眼、气味一入鼻，这两个家伙便知道地头已近，躁动顿时到了顶点。塔吉人立而起，一头翻过船舷；托吉跟着要跳，沙恩堪堪来得及把全身的分量甩向另一头压舱配重。它们扑腾着朝岸边游去，托尔瓦德那头脏话骂得滔滔不绝，沙恩则一把抢住那只金贵的口粮袋。砾石飞溅之中，两只野兽上了岸，一路拱到滩头高处才停下抖毛，把那身乱蓬蓬皮毛驮来的水珠子甩得四下开花。

上了岸，独木舟便成了一件笨拙的重负。船虽轻，两人还是出了一身汗，才把它弄上海滩，一路没让舷外支架磕上石头和灌木。心里惦着斯罗格巡逻队，他们手脚麻利地动手把船掩藏起来。

===Original===
Taggi raised an egg-patterned snout from a hollow and licked at the stippling of greenish yolk matting his fur. The wolverines had wasted no time in sampling the contents of a wealth of nesting places beginning just above the high-water mark, cupping two to four tough-shelled eggs in each. Treading a path among those clutches, the Terrans climbed a red-earthed slope toward the interior of the island.

They found water, not the clear running of a mountain spring, but a stalish pool in a stone-walled depression on the crest of a rise, filled by the bounty of the rain. The warm liquid was brackish, but satisfied in part their thirst, and they drank eagerly.

The outer cliff wall of the island was just that, a wall, for there was an inner slope to match the outer. And at the bottom of it a showing of purple-green foliage where plants and stunted trees fought for living space. But there was nothing else, though they quartered that growing section with the care of men trying to locate an enemy outpost.
===Chinese===
塔吉从一个坑洼里抬起那张沾满蛋花斑点的口鼻，舔着黏结在皮毛上、星星点点的青绿蛋黄。高潮线以上，密密的鸟巢一眼望不到头，每窝里都拢着两到四枚硬壳蛋，两只狼獾当下便开工翻检品尝起来。地球人从一窝窝鸟蛋之间踩出一条道，登上一道红土坡，朝岛的内里走去。

他们找到了水——不是山泉那种清澈的活水，而是一汪半死不活的水塘，卧在一道高冈顶上石砌般的洼地里，全靠雨水的慷慨注满。温吞的液体带着咸涩，却也解了几分渴，两人俯下身痛饮。

岛外侧的崖壁名副其实，就是一堵墙，因为墙内侧还有一道与外壁相称的斜坡。坡底露出一片紫绿色的草木，植物与矮树在那里争抢立锥之地。可除此外再无他物，尽管他们拿出搜寻敌方前哨的细致，把那片草木区来回篦了个遍。

===Original===
That night they camped in the hollow, roasted eggs in a fire, and ate the fishy-tasting contents because it was food, not because they relished what they swallowed. Tonight no cloud bank hung overhead. A man, gazing up, could see the stars. The stars and other things, for over the distant shore of the mainland they sighted the cruising lights of a Throg ship and waited tensely for that circle of small sparkling points to swing out toward their own hiding hole.

“They haven’t given up,” Shann stated what was obvious to them both.
===Chinese===
当夜他们在洼地里宿营，生火烤蛋，吃下那些带着鱼腥味的蛋内容物——只因它是食物，不是因为咽得香甜。今夜头顶没有云幕。人只要仰起头，就能望见星星。星星，还有别的东西：大陆远岸上空，他们望见一艘斯罗格飞船游弋的灯火，便紧张地盯着，等看那圈小小的光点会不会朝他们这处藏身之所荡过来。

“他们没有死心。”沙恩道出了两人心里都明白的事。

===Original===
“The settler transport,” Thorvald reminded him. “If they do not take a prisoner to talk her in and allay suspicion, then﻿—” he snapped his fingers﻿—“the Patrol will be on their tails, but quick!”

So just by keeping out of Throg range, they were, in a way, still fighting. Shann settled back, his tender shoulders resting against a tree hole. He tried to count the number of days and nights lying behind him now since that early morning when he had watched the Terran camp die under the aliens’ weapons. But one day faded into another so that he could remember only action parts clearly﻿—the attack on the grounded scoutship, the sortie they had made in turn on the occupied camp, the dust storm on the river, the escape from the Throg ship in the mountain crevice, and their meeting with the hound. Then that storm which had driven them to seek cover after their curious experience with the disk. And now this day when they had safely reached the island.
===Chinese===
“移民运输舰呗。”托尔瓦德提醒他。“要是他们抓不到一个俘虏去把船诓进来、按住对方的疑心，那么——”他打了个响指——“巡逻舰队立刻就会咬住他们的尾巴，快得很！”

这么说来，只要不进斯罗格人的火力圈，他们某种意义上就仍在战斗。沙恩往后靠去，让酸疼的肩膀抵住一个树洞。他试着数一数，从那个清晨——他眼睁睁看着地球人的营地死在外星人的武器之下——到如今，身后已经积了多少个日夜。可日子与日子早已糊作一片，他只清楚记得那些干出动静来的段落：对迫降侦察船的那场攻击，他们对被占营地回敬的出击，河上的沙暴，在山缝里躲过斯罗格飞船的脱身，同“猎犬”的遭遇。再后来，是圆盘上那番古怪经历之后、逼得他们觅地藏身的那场风暴。还有今天，他们平安登上了这座岛。

===Original===
“Why this island?” he asked suddenly.

“That carved piece was found here on the edge of this valley,” Thorvald returned matter-of-factly.
===Chinese===
“为什么偏偏是这座岛？”他突然问。

“那件刻纹的东西，就是在这儿、这道谷地的边上找到的。”托尔瓦德答得就事论事。

===Original===
“But today we found nothing at all﻿—”

“Yet this island supplies us with a starting point.”
===Chinese===
“可今天我们什么也没找见——”

“但这座岛给我们提供了一个起点。”

===Original===
A starting point for what? A detailed search of all the islands, great and small, in the chain? And how did they dare continue to paddle openly from one to the next with the Throgs sweeping the skies? They would have provided an excellent target today as they combed that reef for an hour or more. Wearily, Shann spread out his hands in the very faint light of their tiny fire, poked with a finger tip at smarting points which would have been blisters had those hands not known a toughening process in the past. More paddling tomorrow? But that was tomorrow, and at least they need not worry tonight about any Throg attack once they had doused the fire, an action which was now being methodically attended to by Thorvald. Shann pushed down on the bed of leaves he had heaped together. The night was quiet. He could hear only the murmur of the sea, a lulling croon of sound to make one sleep deep, perhaps dreamlessly.
===Chinese===
起点？什么的起点？把岛链里大大小小的岛挨个细搜一遍？斯罗格人正扫荡着天空，他们怎么还敢明晃晃地从一个岛划到下一个岛？今天他们在那道礁环上篦了一个多钟头，就是再好不过的靶子。沙恩疲惫地在那小堆火将熄的微光里摊开双手，用指尖戳戳那些灼痛的点位——若非这双手早先吃过一番磨砺的苦，此刻早已满是水泡。明天还要划船？可那是明天的事，至少今夜只要灭了火，他们便不必担心斯罗格人来袭——托尔瓦德此刻正一板一眼地料理这桩事。沙恩往自己拢好的那摊树叶铺上按了按。夜很静。他只听得见海的低语，一支催眠曲般的呢喃，让人睡得深沉，或许无梦。

===Original===
Sun struck down, making a dazzle about him. Shann turned over drowsily in that welcome heat, stretching a little as might a cat at ease. Then he really awoke under the press of memory, and the need for alertness rode him once more. Beaten-down grass, the burnt-out embers of last night’s fire were beside him. But of Thorvald and the wolverines there were no signs.

Not only did he now lie alone, but he was possessed by the feeling that he had not been deserted only momentarily, that Taggi, Togi and the Survey officer were indeed gone. Shann sat up, got to his feet, breathing faster, a prickle of uneasiness spreading in him, bringing him to that inner slope, up it to the crest from which he could see that beach where last night they had concealed the canoe.
===Chinese===
阳光当头砸下，在他周身晃起一片光斑。沙恩在那团受欢迎的暖意里昏昏沉沉翻了个身，像只安适的猫似的微微伸了个懒腰。随即记忆压了下来，他才算真正醒透，警觉的必要又一次骑上了他的脊背。压伏的草、昨夜火堆烧尽的余烬，都在他身边。可托尔瓦德和狼獾，半点踪影也没有。

如今他不只是独自躺着；一种感觉还攫住了他：自己并非只是被一时撇下——塔吉、托吉和那位勘测队军官是真走了。沙恩坐起身，站直，呼吸急促起来，一阵不安的刺麻在周身漫开，催他奔向那道内侧斜坡，一路登上冈顶——从那里能望见昨晚藏船的那片海滩。

===Original===
Those lengths of brush and tufts of grass they had used for a screen were strewn about as if tossed in haste. And not too long before﻿ ﻿…

For the canoe was out in the calm waters within the reef, the paddle blade wielded by its occupant flashing brightly in the sun. On the shingle below, the wolverines prowled back and forth, whining in bewilderment.

“Thorvald﻿—!”
===Chinese===
他们用作遮掩的那些枝条和草束散落四下，仿佛是慌忙间扯掉的。而且就在不久之前……

独木舟已经驶在礁环之内的平静水面上，船上人挥动的桨叶在日光下闪闪发亮。下方砾石滩上，两只狼獾来回逡巡，困惑地呜咽。

“托尔瓦德——！”

===Original===
Shann put the full force of his lungs into that hail, hearing the name ring from one of the small peaks at his back. But the man in the boat did not turn his head; there was no change in the speed of that paddle dip.

Shann leaped down the outer slope to the beach, skidding the last few feet, saving himself from going headfirst into the water only by a painful wrench of his body.
===Chinese===
沙恩把满肺的力气都灌进这声呼喊，听得那名字从他背后一座小峰上撞出回响。可船上人没有回头；桨叶起落的速度分毫未变。

沙恩顺着外侧坡直奔海滩跃下，最后几英尺是滑下去的，全靠身子忍痛猛一拧，才没一头栽进水里。

===Original===
“Thorvald!” He tried calling again. But that head, bright under the sun did not turn; there was no answer. Shann tore at his clothes and kicked off his boots.

He did not think of the possibility of lurking sea monsters as he plunged into the water, swam for the canoe edging along the reef, plainly bound for the sea gate to the southwest. Shann was not a powerful swimmer. His first impetus gave him a good start, but after that he had to fight for each foot he gained, and the fear grew in him that the other would reach the reef passage before he could catch up. He wasted no more time trying to hail Thorvald, putting all his breath and energy into the effort of overtaking the craft.
===Chinese===
“托尔瓦德！”他又试着喊了一声。可那颗在阳光下亮晃晃的脑袋还是没有转过来；没有回应。沙恩扯掉衣裳，踢脱靴子。

他一头扎进水里，朝那条贴着礁缘蹭行的独木舟游去——船分明是要奔西南面那道海门（sea gate）出海。潜藏着海怪的可能，他想都没想。沙恩算不得一个有力气的泳者。头一股冲劲让他抢了个好开局，可此后每一英尺都得靠拼抢，他心里的恐惧越涨越高：怕对方赶在他追上之前，先一步到了礁环通道。他不再费工夫呼喊托尔瓦德，把全部气息和气力都砸进追赶这条船上。

===Original===
And he almost made it, his hand actually slipping along the log which furnished the balancing outrigger. As his fingers tightened on the slimy wood he looked up, and loosed that hold again in time perhaps to save his life.

For when he ducked to let the water cover his head in an impromptu half dive, Shann carried with him a vivid picture, a picture so astounding that he was a little dazed.
===Chinese===
而他差一点就够着了，手已经真真切切滑上了那根充作配重舷外支架的圆木。指尖在那滑腻的木头上收紧的一刻，他抬眼望去，又及时松开了抓握——这一松，多半救了他的命。

因为就在他临时起意扎下半个猛子、让水没过头顶的那一瞬，沙恩的脑海带下来一幅鲜活的画面，一幅骇人得让他微微发懵的画面。

===Original===
Thorvald had stopped paddling at last, because that paddle had to be put to another use. Had Shann not released his hold on the log and gone under water, that crudely fashioned piece of wood might have broken his skull. He saw only too clearly the paddle raised in both hands as an ugly weapon, and Thorvald’s face, convulsed in a spasm of rage which made it as inhuman as a Throg’s.

Sputtering and choking, Shann fought up to the air once more. The paddle was back at the task for which it had been carved, the canoe was underway again, its occupant paying no more attention to what lay behind than if he *had* successfully disposed of the man in the water. To follow would be only to invite another attack, and Shann might not be so lucky next time. He was not good enough a swimmer to try any tricks such as oversetting the canoe, not when Thorvald was an expert who could easily finish off a fumbling opponent.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德终于停了划桨——因为那支桨得派上别的用场了。若不是沙恩松开圆木潜进水里，那件做工粗陋的木器多半已经敲碎了他的脑壳。他看得再清楚不过：那支桨被双手高举，成了一件凶相毕露的武器；托尔瓦德的脸在狂怒的痉挛中扭曲，扭曲得不带半点人味，活像一张斯罗格人的脸。

沙恩噗噗地喷水呛咳，重新拼出水面。那支桨又干回了它被凿出来时承担的本行，独木舟再度前行，船上人对身后之物连眼角都不再光顾，仿佛他*当真*已把水里那人收拾干净了。再追上去无异于自请再挨一记，而下一回沙恩未必还有这样的运气。他这点水性，玩不起掀翻独木舟一类的花招——何况托尔瓦德是个行家，收拾一个手忙脚乱的对手易如反掌。

===Original===
Shann swam wearily to shore where the wolverines waited, unable yet to make sense of that attack in the lagoon. What had happened to Thorvald? What motive had led the other to leave Shann and the animals on this island, the island Thorvald had called a starting point in his search for the natives of Warlock? Or had every bit of that tall tale been invented by the Survey officer for some obscure purpose of his own, certainly no sane purpose? Against that logic Shann could only set the carved disk, and he had only Thorvald’s word that that had been discovered here.

He dragged himself out of the water on his hands and knees and lay, winded and gasping. Taggi came to lick his face, nuzzle him, making a small, bewildered whimpering. While above, the leather-headed birds called and swooped, fearful and angry for their disturbed nesting place. The Terran retched, coughed up water, and then sat up to look around.
===Chinese===
沙恩疲惫地游向岸边，狼獾正在那里等他；潟湖上那场袭击，他一时还理不出头绪。托尔瓦德到底出了什么事？是什么缘由，让那人把沙恩和两只动物撂在这座岛上——这座被托尔瓦德称作搜寻沃洛克土著之起点的岛？又或者，那套耸人听闻的说辞从头到尾，全是勘测队军官为他自己某个晦暗不明的目的编造出来的——一个绝非神志清醒的目的？同这套逻辑相抗，沙恩手里只有那块刻纹圆盘，而它是在这里发现的，也不过是托尔瓦德的一面之词。

他手脚并用地从水里爬上岸，趴着直喘，上气不接下气。塔吉过来舔他的脸，用鼻子拱他，发出细细的、困惑的呜咽。头顶上，皮头鸟叫着俯冲，为巢场被扰又惊又怒。这个地球人干呕几声，咳出呛下的水，然后坐起来四下张望。

===Original===
The spread of lagoon was bare. Thorvald must have rounded the south point of land and be very close to the reef passage, perhaps through it by now. Not stopping for his clothes, Shann started up the slope, crawling part of the way on his hands and knees.

He reached the crest again and got to his feet. The sun made an eye-dazzling glitter of the waves. But under the shade of his hands Shann saw the canoe again, beyond the reef, heading on out along the island chain, not back to shore as he had expected. Thorvald was still on the hunt, but for what? A reality which existed, or a dream in his own disturbed brain?
===Chinese===
潟湖的辽阔水面空空荡荡。托尔瓦德必是已经绕过岛南端，离礁环通道很近了，此刻或许已经穿了出去。沙恩顾不上穿衣，便顺着坡往上赶，有一段路是手脚并用爬上去的。

他重新登上冈顶，站起身。太阳把浪头晃出一片刺眼的粼光。但在手掌遮出的荫翳下，沙恩又望见了那条独木舟——它已在礁环之外，沿着岛链继续向外去，而不是像他料想的那样折回岸边。托尔瓦德仍在追猎。可他追的是什么？一件实实在在存在的东西，还是他自己那颗纷乱脑袋里的一个梦？

===Original===
Shann sat down. He was very hungry, for that adventure in the lagoon had sapped his strength. And he was a prisoner along with the wolverines, a prisoner on an island which was half the size of the valley which held the Survey camp. As far as he knew, his only supply of drinkable water was that tank of evil-smelling rain which would be speedily evaporated by a sun such as the one now beating down on him. And between him and the shore was the sea, a sea which harbored such creatures as the fork-tail he had watched die.

Thorvald was still steadily on course, not to the next island in the chain, a small, bare knob, but to the one beyond that. He could have been hurrying to a meeting. Where and with what?

Shann got to his feet, started down to the beach once more, sure now that the officer had no intention of returning, that he was again on his own with only his wits and strength to keep him alive﻿—alive and somehow free of this water-washed prison.
===Chinese===
沙恩坐了下来。他饿得厉害，潟湖里那场折腾早榨干了他的力气。他同狼獾一道成了囚徒，囚在一座岛上——这座岛，只有容纳勘测营地的那道谷地一半大。据他所知，他唯一的饮用水源，就是那一箱气味难闻的雨水，而这样一轮正烤在他头顶的烈日，很快就会把它蒸个精光。他与大陆之间隔着海——一片养着叉尾兽那种东西的海，他可亲眼看过一条叉尾兽是怎么死的。

托尔瓦德仍稳稳循着航线，目标不是岛链里的下一座岛——那座小小的秃岩瘤——而是再过去的那一座。他那副样子，倒像是在赶赴一场会合。去哪里？会什么？

沙恩站起身，再度朝海滩走下去。此刻他已经断定，那位军官根本不打算回来了；他又成了孤身一人，只剩自己的机敏和气力保他活命——活着，还得想法子逃出这座四面是水的牢狱。

## X A Trap for a Trapper / X 给设陷人的陷阱

===Original===
Shann took up the piece of soft chalklike stone he had found and drew another short white mark on the rust-red of a boulder well above tide level. That made three such marks, three days since Thorvald had marooned him. And he was no nearer the shore now than he had been on that first morning! He sat where he was by the boulder, aware that he should be up, trying to climb to the less accessible nests of the sea birds. The prisoners, man and wolverines, had cleaned out all those they had discovered on beach and cliffs. But at the thought of more eggs, Shann’s stomach knotted in pain and he began to retch.

There had been no sign of Thorvald since Shann had watched him steer between the two westward islands. And the younger Terran’s faint hope that the officer would return had died. On the shore a few feet away lay his own pitiful attempt to solve the problem of escape.
===Chinese===
沙恩捡起他先前找到的那块白垩似的软石，在一块远高出潮线的巨砾的铁锈红石面上，又划下一道短短的白痕。这样已经有三道了——托尔瓦德把他孤零零甩在这座岛上，已经三天。而他此刻离那道海岸，仍旧同头一个早晨一样遥远！他坐在巨砾旁没动，心里明白自己该起身，去攀爬那些更难企及的海鸟巢。这群囚徒——一人两狼獾——早把滩上崖间发现的鸟巢掏了个精光。可一想到还要再吃鸟蛋，沙恩的胃就绞成一团痛楚，他开始干呕起来。

自从沙恩望着托尔瓦德驾船从那两座向西的岛屿之间驶过，就再没有见着他的半点踪影。这个年轻地球人心底那点盼军官回来的微弱希望，也早已死透了。几英尺外的滩头上，躺着他解决逃生难题的可悲尝试。

===Original===
The force ax had vanished with Thorvald, along with all the rest of the meager supplies which had been the officer’s original contribution to their joint equipment. Shann had used his knife on brush and small trees, trying to put together some kind of a raft. But he had not been able to discover here any of those vines necessary for binding, and his best efforts had all come to grief when he tried them in a lagoon launching. So far he had achieved no form of raft which would keep him afloat longer than five minutes, let alone support three of them as far as the next island.

Shann pulled listlessly at the framework of his latest try, fully disheartened. He tried not to think of the unescapable fact that the water in the rain tank had sunk to only an inch or so of muddy scum. Last night he had dug in the heart of the interior valley where the rankness of the vegetation was a promise of moisture, to uncover damp clay and then a brackish ooze. Far too little to satisfy both him and the animals.
===Chinese===
力场斧随托尔瓦德一同消失了，军官最初带来的那点可怜补给，也一并没了影。沙恩用刀砍灌木和小树，想拼出某种筏子来。可他在这儿找不到那些绑扎必需的藤蔓，而他最得意的几件成品，一下潟湖（lagoon）试航就全军覆没。到目前为止，他没能造出一只能让他在水面上撑过五分钟的筏子，更别说载着他们三个撑到下一座岛了。

沙恩有气无力地扯了扯他最新一次尝试的架子，彻底灰了心。他尽量不去想那个逃不掉的事实：雨水箱（rain tank）里的水已经只剩一英寸上下的一层泥浆。昨夜他在内谷深处挖过，那里草木的疯长意味着水汽，结果只挖出湿黏土，继而又是一摊半咸的淤泥。要喂饱他和两只野兽，实在差得太远。

===Original===
There were surely fish somewhere in the lagoon. Shann wondered if the raw flesh of sea dwellers could supply the water they needed. But lacking net, line, or hooks, how did one fish? Yesterday, using his stunner, he had brought down a bird, to discover the carcass so rank even the wolverines, never dainty eaters, refused to gnaw it.

The animals prowled the two beaches, and Shann guessed they hunted shell dwellers, for at times they dug energetically in the gravel. Togi was busied in this way now, the sand flowing from under her pumping legs, her claws raking in good earnest.
===Chinese===
潟湖里总归是有鱼的。沙恩纳闷，海中生物的生肉能不能补上他们需要的水分。可是没有网、没有钓线、也没有钩子，这鱼怎么个捕法？昨天他用击晕枪打下一只鸟，却发现那尸体腥臊得连素来不挑嘴的狼獾都不肯下口。

两只野兽在两片滩地间逡巡，沙恩猜它们是在猎贝，因为它们时不时在砾石里起劲地刨挖。托吉此刻正忙这个，沙子从她刨动的腿间飞溅，爪子认认真真地耙着。

===Original===
And it was Togi’s excavation which brought Shann a first ray of hope. Her excitement was so marked that he believed she was in quest of some worthwhile game and he moved across to inspect the pit. A patch of brown, which had been skimmed bare by one raking paw, made him shout.

Taggi shambled downslope, going to work beside his mate with an eagerness as open as hers. Shann hovered at the edge of the pit they were rapidly enlarging. The brown patch was larger, disclosing itself as a hump doming up from the gravel. The Terran did not need to run his hands over that rough surface to recognize the nature of the find. This was another shell such as had come floating in after the storm to form the raw material of their canoe.

However, as fast as the wolverines dug, they did not appear to make correspondingly swift headway in uncovering their find as might reasonably be expected. In fact, a witness could guess that the shell was sinking at a pace only a fraction slower than the burrowers were using to free it. Intrigued by that, Shann went back to the waterline, secured one of the lengths he had been trying to weave into his failures, and returned to use it as a makeshift shovel.
===Chinese===
而正是托吉的这一挖，给沙恩捎来了第一线希望。她兴奋得如此明显，沙恩认定她在追猎什么值当的活物，便走过去察看那个坑。一只耙扒的爪子掠过，扫出一小块裸露的棕色，沙恩顿时大喊出声。

塔吉蹒跚着冲下坡来，到配偶身旁并肩刨个不停，那股热切同她的一般外露。沙恩守在坑边——那坑正被它们飞快扩大。棕色的斑块越来越宽，露出了真容：一个从砾石里穹起的大圆包。这个地球人不必伸手去摩挲那粗糙的表面，也认得出这一发现是什么路数。这又是一具壳，同风暴后漂来的那具一样——正是那具壳，成了他们独木舟的原材料。

然而，狼獾刨得再快，揭开这发现的速度却不见相应的长进，实在不合常理。事实上，旁观者都能猜到：那壳正在往下沉，速度只比两个掘坑者把它挖出来的速度慢上一线。沙恩被勾起了好奇心，回到水边，抽出一根他一直试图编进那些失败品里的木料，拿回来权当铲子使。

===Original===
Now, with three of them at the digging, the brown hump was uncovered, and Shann pried down around its edge, trying to lever it up and over. To his amazement, his tool was caught and held, nearly jerked from his hands. To his retaliating tug the obstruction below-ground gave way, and the Terran sprawled back, the length of wood coming clear, to show the other end smashed and splintered as if it had been caught between mashing gears.

For the first time he understood that they were dealing not with an empty shell casing buried by drift under this small beach, but with a shell still inhabited by the Warlockian to whom it was a natural covering, and that that inhabitant would fight to continue ownership. A moment’s examination of that splintered wood also suggested that the shell’s present wearer appeared well able to defend itself.
===Chinese===
