这下挖土的成了三个，棕色的大圆包终于露了出来。沙恩沿着它的边缘往下撬，想把它撬翻过来。令他惊愕的是，他的工具被死死咬住，差点从手里夺走。他奋起回夺，地下的阻碍物松了口，地球人仰面栽倒，那根木料脱手而出——另一头已被砸得稀烂、迸满裂茬，仿佛被碾磨的齿轮咬过一般。

他头一回明白过来：他们打交道的，并不是被浮木压埋在这片小滩下的一具空壳，而是一具仍有主人的壳——对壳里那个沃洛克生灵来说，这是它与生俱来的护身甲，而且那位住户为了保住产权，是要动真格的。再看一眼那根迸裂的木料，也足以说明：壳的现任穿戴者，看来完全有本事自卫。

===Original===
Shann attempted to call off the wolverines, but they were out of control now, digging frantically to get at this new prey. And he knew that if he pulled them away by force, they were apt to turn those punishing claws and snapping jaws on him.

It was for their protection that he returned to digging, though he no longer tried to pry up the shell. Taggi leaped to the top of that dome, sweeping paws downward to clear its surface, while Togi prowled around its circumference, pausing now and then to send dirt and gravel spattering, but treading warily as might one alert for a sudden attack.
===Chinese===
沙恩试图把狼獾唤回来，可它们此刻哪里还听管束，发狂似的刨着，非要够到这新猎物不可。他心里清楚，若硬拽它们走，那惩罚性的利爪和咔嚓咬合的颌骨，多半就要冲他来了。

正是为了护着它们，他重新回到挖掘里去，虽然不再试图撬那具壳。塔吉纵身跳上圆顶，爪子向下扫掠，清扫壳面；托吉则绕着四周逡巡，不时停下刨几爪，扬得泥土砾石四溅，脚下却踩得小心翼翼，像是随时提防着一记突袭。

===Original===
They had the creature almost clear now, though the shell still rested firmly on the ground, and they had no notion of what it might protect. It was smaller, perhaps two thirds the size of the one which Thorvald had fashioned into a seagoing craft. But it could provide them with transportation to the mainland if Shann was able to repeat the feat of turning it into an outrigger canoe.

Taggi joined his mate on the ground and both wolverines padded about the dome, obviously baffled. Now and then they assaulted the shell with a testing paw. Claws raked and did not leave any marks but shallow scratches. They could continue that forever, as far as Shann could see, without solving the problem in the least.

He sat back on his heels and studied the scene in detail. The excavation holding the shelled creature was some three yards above the high-water mark, with a few more feet separating that from the point where lazy waves now washed the finer sand. Shann watched the slow inward slip of those waves with growing interest. Where their combined efforts had failed to win this odd battle, perhaps the sea itself could now be pressed into service.
===Chinese===
此刻那生灵几乎被刨得干干净净，只是壳仍稳稳地杵在地上，谁也说不准壳里护着的是什么。它小一些，大约只有托尔瓦德改造成航海小舟的那具的三分之二大。可只要沙恩能故技重施、把它改成一艘舷外支架（outrigger）独木舟，它就能载他们回大陆去。

塔吉跳回地上，站到配偶身旁，两只狼獾围着圆顶轻步打转，显然一筹莫展。它们不时伸出一只爪子试探着攻打那壳。利爪耙过，除几道浅浅的划痕，什么也没留下。在沙恩看来，这么耗到天荒地老，也丝毫解决不了问题。

他往后坐下，蹲踞在脚跟上，细细打量起眼前的局面。困着这带壳生灵的土坑，高出高潮线约三码，再隔几英尺，才是懒洋洋的波浪当前淘洗细沙的地方。沙恩望着那一波波海水缓缓向里漫涌，兴致越来越浓。他们合力赢不下这场古怪的战斗，那么现在，也许可以拉大海本身来当帮工。

===Original===
Shann began his own excavation, a trough to lead from the waterline to the pit occupied by the obstinate shell. Of course the thing living in or under that covering might be only too familiar with salt water. But it had placed its burrow, or hiding place, above the reach of the waves and so might be disconcerted by the sudden appearance of water in its bed. However, the scheme was worth trying, and he went to work doggedly, wishing he could make the wolverines understand so they would help him.

They still prowled about their captive, scrapping at the sand about the shell casing. At least their efforts would keep the half-prisoner occupied and prevent its escape. Shann put another piece of his raft to work as a shovel, throwing up a shower of sand and gravel while sweat dampened his tattered blouse and was salt and sticky on his arms and face.

He finished his trench, one which ran at an angle he hoped would feed water into the pit rapidly once he knocked away the last barrier against the waves. And, splashing out into the green water, he did just that.
===Chinese===
沙恩开始挖他自己的工程：一道水槽，从水边一直通到那具顽固的壳盘踞的土坑。当然，住在那护甲里头或底下的东西，说不定对咸水熟得很。可它把自己的洞穴、或者说藏身之处，安在了浪头够不着的高处，那么一旦水突然灌进它的床铺，它多半会方寸大乱。不管怎样，这法子值得一试。他闷头苦干起来，只恨不得能让狼獾听懂，好来搭把手。

两只狼獾仍在它们的俘虏四周围转，刨着壳座周围的沙子。至少它们这一手能拖住这半个囚徒，不让它跑了。沙恩又拿一块筏子材料当铲子使，沙土砾石扬得像下雨，汗水浸透了他褴褛的衬衫，在手臂和脸上结成又咸又黏的一层。

他的沟渠挖成了，那角度是他特意选的，只盼一旦敲开最后一道挡浪的堤，水能飞快灌进坑里。随后他哗啦哗啦蹚进绿水，一锤子敲开了。

===Original===
His calculations proved correct. Waves lapped, then flowed in a rapidly thickening stream, puddling out about the shell as the wolverines drew back, snarling. Shann lashed his knife fast to a stout length of sapling, so equipping himself with a spear. He stood with it ready in his hand, not knowing just what to expect. And when the answer to his water attack came, the move was so sudden that in spite of his preparation he was caught gaping.

For the shell fairly erupted out of the mess of sand and water. A complete fringe of jointed, clawed brown limbs churned in a forward-and-upward dash. But the water worked to frustrate that charge. For one of the pit walls crumbled, overbalancing the creature so that the fore end of the shell lifted from the ground, the legs clawing wildly at the air.
===Chinese===
他的盘算分毫不差。波浪先是舔舐，随即汇成一股迅速加粗的水流，在壳的四周汪出一片水洼。狼獾咆哮着退开了。沙恩把刀牢牢绑上一截粗壮的幼树干，给自己装备出一杆矛来。他握矛在手，严阵以待，却又说不好等着他的究竟是什么。等水攻的回应真的来了，那一下来得如此突然，尽管他有备在先，仍旧张大嘴愣在了原地。

只见那壳裹着泥沙浊水，简直是爆射而出。一圈带爪的棕色节肢完整地露出来，搅动着向前上方猛冲。可那灌进来的水偏偏搅了它这记冲刺——一道坑壁坍塌，那生灵失了平衡，壳的前端翘离了地面，众腿在空中疯狂乱抓。

===Original===
Shann thrust with the spear, feeling the knife point go home so deeply that he could not pull his improvised weapon free. A limb snapped claws only inches away from his leg as he pushed down on the haft with all his strength. That attack along with the initial upset of balance did the job. The shell flopped over, its rounded hump now embedded in the watery sand of the pit while the frantic struggles of the creature to right itself only buried it the deeper.

The Terran stared down upon a segmented under belly where legs were paired in riblike formation. Shann could locate no head, no good target. But he drew his stunner and beamed at either end of the oval, and then, for good measure, in the middle, hoping in one of those three general blasts to contact the thing’s central nervous system. He was not to know which of those shots did the trick, but the frantic wiggling of the legs slowed and finally ended, as a clockwork toy might run down for want of winding﻿—and at last projected, at crooked angles, completely still. The shell creature might not be dead, but it was tamed for now.
===Chinese===
沙恩挺矛便刺，刀尖扎得那样深，以致他这杆临时武器竟拔不出来。一条节肢的钳爪在他腿边不过几英寸处咔地一合，他把全身力气都压上矛杆。这一击，加上先前那一跤失衡，终于成了事。壳扑通翻倒，浑圆的穹顶栽进坑里渍水的沙中，那生灵拼命挣扎着想翻回身，反倒把自己越埋越深。

地球人俯视着那一节节的腹部，腹上众腿成对排列，肋骨一般。沙恩找不出头在哪里，找不出一个像样的目标。可他还是拔出击晕枪，先照着椭圆形的一端射了一束，又照另一端射一束，末了为保险起见，正中再补一束，指望这三记大而化之的射击里，有一记正好打中这东西的中枢神经。究竟哪一枪见了效，他无从知晓，只看见众腿疯狂的扭动慢了下来，终于停住——像一只忘了上发条的玩具走完了劲，最后僵在几个歪斜的角度上，一动不动。这带壳的东西未必死了，但眼下是被制服了。

===Original===
Taggi had only been waiting for a good chance to do battle. He grabbed one of those legs, worried it, and then leaped to tear at the under body. Unlike the outer shell, this portion of the creature had no proper armor and the wolverine plunged joyfully into the business of the kill, his mate following suit.

The process of butchery was a bloody, even beastly job, and Shann was shaken before it was complete. But he kept at his labors, determined to have that shell, his one chance of escape from the Island. The wolverines feasted on the greenish-white flesh, but he could not bring himself to sample it, climbing to the heights in search of eggs, and making a happy find of a niche filled with the edible moss-fungi.

By late afternoon he had the shell scooped fairly clean and the wolverines had carried away for burial such portions as they had not been able to consume at their first eating. Meanwhile, the leather-headed birds had grown bold enough to snatch up the fragments he tossed out on the water, struggling for that bounty against feeders arising from the depths of the lagoon.
===Chinese===
塔吉本来就在等一个上阵厮杀的好机会。它一口叼住其中一条腿，撕咬一阵，随即扑上去撕扯那腹部。同外头的壳不同，这东西的这一部分没有像样的护甲，公狼獾欢天喜地地投入了这场宰杀，它的配偶紧随其后。

屠宰的活计血腥，甚至可以说野蛮，没等干完，沙恩已经心头发颤。可他仍咬牙撑着——他铁了心要那具壳，那是他逃出这座岛的唯一机会。狼獾们大啖那绿白色的肉，他却下不去口，只管攀上高崖去找鸟蛋，还真叫他快活地寻到一处石龛，里面生满可食的苔藓真菌（moss-fungi）。

到傍晚时分，他已经把壳里的东西掏刮得差不离干净，狼獾们头一顿吃剩的部分，也被它们叼走去埋了贮藏。与此同时，皮头鸟（leather-headed bird）的胆子养大了，竟敢来抢他抛在水面的碎肉，为这点嗟来之食，同从潟湖深处浮上来的觅食者争得不可开交。

===Original===
At the coming of dusk Shann hauled the bloodstained, grisly trophy well up the beach and wedged it among the rocks, determined not to lose his treasure. Then he stripped and washed, first his clothing and then himself, rubbing his hands and arms with sand until his skin was tender. He was still exultant at his luck. The drift would supply him with materials for an outrigger. One more day’s work﻿—or maybe two﻿—and he could leave. He wrung out his blouse and gazed toward the distant line of the shore. Once he had his new canoe ready he would try to make the trip back in the early morning while the mists were still on the sea. That should give him cover against any Throg flight.

That night Shann slept in the deep fog of bodily exhaustion. There were no dreams, nothing but an unconsciousness which even a Throg attack could not have pierced. He roused in the morning with an odd feeling of guilt. The water hole he had scooped in the valley yielded him some swallows tasting of earth, but he had almost forgotten the flavor of a purer liquid. Munching on a fistful of moss, he hurried down to the shore, half fearing to find the shell gone, his luck out once again.
===Chinese===
暮色四合时，沙恩把这件血迹斑斑、狰狞骇人的战利品一路拖上滩头高处，楔进岩石堆里卡牢，铁了心不能丢掉这份财宝。随后他脱光洗净，先洗衣裳，再洗自己，双手和手臂拿沙子搓了又搓，搓得皮肉发嫩。他心里仍自狂喜交加，觉得自家运气不坏。浮木会给他提供搭舷外支架的材料。再干一天——也许两天——他就能动身了。他拧干衬衫，遥遥望向大陆海岸线那一道隐约的长影。等新独木舟一造好，他就赶在清晨海雾未散时启程回渡。有雾色遮掩，斯罗格人的飞行器该发现不了他。

那一夜，沙恩沉睡在浑身力竭的浓雾里。没有梦，只有一片连斯罗格人打上门来都刺不穿的昏沉。早晨醒来，他心头竟萦着一丝莫名的负罪感。他在谷里挖的水坑给了他几大口水，带着泥土味，而更清冽的液体是什么滋味，他几乎已经忘了。他嚼着一把苔藓，匆匆赶下滩去，心里七上八下，生怕壳又没了，他的好运再一次到头。

===Original===
Not only was the shell where he had wedged it, but he had done better than he knew when he had left it exposed in the night. Small things scuttled away from it into hiding, and several birds arose﻿—scavengers had been busy lightening his unwelcome task for that morning. And seeing how the cleanup process had gone, Shann had a second inspiration.

Pushing the thing down the beach, he sank it in the shallows with several rocks to anchor it. Within a few seconds the shell was invaded by a whole school of spiny-tailed fish, that ate greedily. Leaving his find to their cleansing, Shann went back to prospect the pile of raft material, choosing pieces which could serve for an outrigger frame. He was handicapped as he had been all along by the absence of the vines one could use for lashings. And he had reached the point of considering a drastic sacrifice of his clothing to get the necessary strips when he saw Taggi dragging behind him one of the jointed legs the wolverines had put in storage the day before.
===Chinese===
壳不但还卡在他楔的那里，而且他夜里让它露天过夜，倒是歪打正着，干得比他自己知道的还好。一些小东西仓皇四窜，钻进藏身处，几只鸟轰然飞起——食腐者们夜里没闲着，替他把那件不受欢迎的晨活减轻了不少。眼见这番清扫收效之佳，沙恩又生出一计。

他把这东西推下滩去，沉进浅水，又压上几块石头镇住。不出几秒，一整群刺尾鱼（spiny-tailed fish）便涌进壳里，大快朵颐。沙恩把他的宝贝交给它们清扫，自己回头去翻检那堆筏子材料，挑拣能当舷外支架骨架的木料。缺藤条做绑扎，这个老毛病照旧拖着他的后腿。他一路琢磨到了那份上——要不狠狠牺牲一下身上的衣裳，撕成急需的布条——恰在此时，他看见塔吉拖着一条节肢从后面走来，那是狼獾们头天贮藏的存粮。

===Original===
Now and again Taggi laid his prize on the shingle, holding it firmly pinned with his forepaws as he tried to worry loose a section of flesh. But apparently that feat was beyond even his notable teeth, and at length he left it lying there in disgust while he returned to a cache for more palatable fare. Shann went to examine more closely the triple-jointed limb.

The casing was not as hard as horn or shell, he discovered upon testing; it more resembled tough skin laid over bone. With a knife he tried to loosen the skin﻿—a tedious job requiring a great deal of patience, since the tissue tore if pulled away too fast. But with care he acquired a few thongs perhaps a foot long. Using two of these, he made a trial binding of one stick to another, and experimented farther, soaking the whole construction in sea water and then exposing it to the direct rays of the sun.
===Chinese===
塔吉不时把它的战利品搁在砾石滩上，两只前爪死死踩住，想把上面一截肉撕咬下来。可这壮举显然连它那口出了名的好牙也办不到，末了它嫌恶地把腿往那儿一撂，自回它的窖藏去找更可口的东西了。沙恩走过去，细看那条三节的肢足。

一上手试，他就发现这层壳甲不如角或壳那般硬，倒更像是绷在骨头上的一层韧皮。他用刀试着把皮剥离——这活计琐碎磨人，极需耐心，那组织拽得急了就会撕裂。但他小心行事，还真剥下了几条约莫一英尺长的皮条。他拿其中两根，试着把一根木棍绑上另一根，又进一步做试验：把整个绑扎件浸进海水，再摊到烈日底下暴晒。

===Original===
When he examined his test piece an hour later, the skin thongs had set into place with such success that the one piece of wood might have been firmly glued to the other. Shann shuffled his feet in a little dance of triumph as he went on to the lagoon to inspect the waterlogged shell. The scavengers had done well. One scraping, two at the most, would have the whole thing clean and ready to use.

But that night Shann dreamed. No climbing of a skull-shaped mountain this time. Instead, he was again on the beach, laboring under an overwhelming compulsion, building something for an alien purpose he could not understand. And he worked as hopelessly as a beaten slave, knowing that what he made was to his own undoing. Yet he could not halt the making, because just beyond the limit of his vision there stood a dominant will which held him in bondage.
===Chinese===
一小时后他再来查验试件，皮条已经定型咬死，成效好到那两根木头简直像用胶牢牢粘成了一体。沙恩脚下拖沓地跳了一小段胜利之舞，随后往潟湖去查看那具泡透的壳。食腐者们干得不赖。再刮一遍——至多两遍——整具壳就能干净待用了。

可是那天夜里，沙恩做梦了。这一回不是去攀什么骷髅形的山。他梦见自己又回到滩头，在一股不可抗拒的强迫下劳作，为某个异类的、他无从理解的目的建造着什么。他像个被打服的奴隶那样绝望地干着，明知自己造出来的东西正是用来毁掉自己的。然而他停不下这建造，因为在他视野尽头之外，立着一股主宰的意志，把他攥在奴役之中。

===Original===
And he awoke on the beach in the very early dawn, not knowing how he had come there. His body was bathed in sweat, as it had been during his day’s labors under the sun, and his muscles ached with fatigue.

But when he saw what lay at his feet he cringed. The framework of the outrigger, close to completion the night before, was dismantled﻿—smashed. All those strips of hide he had so laboriously culled were cut﻿—into inch-long bits which could be of no service.

Shann whirled, ran to the shell he had the night before pulled from the water and stowed in safety. Its rounded dome was dulled where it had been battered, but there was no break in the surface. He ran his hands anxiously over the curve to make sure. Then, very slowly, he came back to the mess of broken wood and snipped hide. And he was sure, only too sure, of one thing. He, himself, had wrought that destruction. In his dream he had built to satisfy the whim of an enemy; in reality he had destroyed; and that was also, he believed, to satisfy an enemy.
===Chinese===
而他在拂晓前最暗的时分醒在滩头上，不知道自己是怎么到这儿来的。他浑身汗透，同白天顶着日头苦干过一场一般无二，肌肉酸痛，疲乏入骨。

可等他看清脚边躺着的东西，他浑身一缩。舷外支架的骨架——昨夜已近完工——被拆了个七零八落，砸了个稀烂。他辛辛苦苦挑拣出来的那些皮条，全被铰断了——铰成一英寸长的碎段，再派不上任何用场。

沙恩猛地转身，奔向那具他昨夜从水里拖出、妥为收藏的壳。浑圆的穹顶上挨过捶击的地方发了乌，表面却一处破损也没有。他忐忑地双手抚过那道弧线，确认再三。然后，他一步一步挪得很慢很慢，挪回那摊碎木烂皮的狼藉跟前。有一件事他确信，确信得不能再确信：这毁坏，是他亲手干的。在梦里，他为满足一个敌人的心血来潮而建造；在现实中，他毁了它；而这一样是为了——他相信——满足一个敌人。

===Original===
The dream was a part of it. But who or what could set a man dreaming and so take over his body, make him in fact betray himself? But then, what had made Thorvald maroon him here? For the first time, Shann guessed a new, if wild, explanation for the officer’s desertion. Dreams﻿—and the disk which had worked so strangely on Thorvald. Suppose everything the other had surmised was the truth! Then that disk *had* been found on this very island, and here somewhere must lie a clue to the riddle.

Shann licked his lips. Suppose that Thorvald had been sent away under just such a strong compulsion as the one which had ruled Shann last night? Why was he left behind if the other had been moved away to protect some secret? Was it that Shann himself was wanted here, wanted so much that when he at last found a means of escape he was set to destroy it? That act might have been forced upon him for two reasons: to keep him here, and to impress upon him how powerless he was.
===Chinese===
梦是这里面的一环。可是，谁、或者什么东西，能叫一个人做起梦来，就此接管他的身体，让他当真背叛他自己？那么，又是什么，让托尔瓦德把他丢在了这里？头一回，沙恩对军官的离弃猜出了一个全新的、尽管荒唐的解释。梦——还有那只曾在托尔瓦德身上作祟得如此古怪的圆盘。假如对方当时的种种猜测全都是真的呢！那么那只圆盘，*就是*在这座岛上找到的，而这岛上什么地方，必定躺着解开谜团的线索。

沙恩舔了舔嘴唇。假如托尔瓦德也是在这样一股强力强迫下被支走的——正如昨夜支配沙恩的那股？如果说对方被挪走是为了保护某个秘密，那为什么单单把他留下？会不会是沙恩这个人本身就被需要在这里——被需要到了这般地步，以致他终于找到逃生之法时，就有人支使他亲手毁了它？那一手加诸他身，也许出于两个缘由：把他困在此地；再让他看明白，他是何等无力。

===Original===
Powerless! A flicker of stubborn will stirred to respond to that implied challenge. All right, the mysterious *they* had made him do this. But they had underrated him by letting him learn, almost contemptuously, of their presence by that revelation. So warned, he was in a manner armed; he could prepare to fight back.

He squatted by the wreckage as he thought that through, turning over broken pieces. And, Shann realized, he must present at the moment a satisfactory picture of despondency to any spy. A spy, that was it! Someone or something must have him under observation, or his activities of the day before would not have been so summarily countered. And if there was a spy, then there was his answer to the riddle. To trap the trapper. Such action might be a project beyond his resources, but it was his own counterattack.
===Chinese===
无力！一点固执的意志应声而燃，接下了这隐含的挑战。好，神秘的*他们*逼他干了这事。可他们让他借那番暴露知晓了他们的存在——几乎是不屑一顾的疏忽——这便是小瞧了他。既已得了预警，他也算是有了一件武器；他可以备下反击了。

他蹲在那堆残骸旁，把这事从头到尾想了个透，手里翻拣着破木片。而且沙恩意识到，此刻他在任何暗探眼里，都得摆出一副足够消沉丧气的样子来。暗探——对了，就是这个！必定有什么人、什么东西在监视他，否则他头一天的种种举动，不会遭到那般干脆利落的反制。而既然有暗探，谜底也就有了着落：给设陷人设陷。这营生也许超出了他的本事，可这是他自己的反击。

===Original===
So now he had to play a role. Not only must he search the island for the trace of his spy, but he must do it in such a fashion that his purpose would not be plain to the enemy he suspected. The wolverines could help. Shann arose, allowed his shoulders to droop, slouching to the slope with all the air of a beaten man which he could assume, whistling for Taggi and Togi.

When they came, his exploration began. Ostensibly he was hunting for lengths of drift or suitable growing saplings to take the place of those he had destroyed under orders. But he kept a careful watch on the animal pair, hoping by their reactions to pick up a clue to any hidden watcher.
===Chinese===
于是现在，他得演一场戏。他不光要在岛上搜出那个暗探的踪迹，还得搜得让那可疑的敌人看不出他的目的。狼獾们能帮上忙。沙恩站起身，任由两肩垮塌下来，装出一个吃足了败仗的人所能有的全套神气，打着呼哨唤塔吉和托吉，一步拖沓一步地蹭向坡去。

狼獾一到，他的搜索便开始了。明面上，他是在找些够长的浮木，或是就地合适的幼树，来顶替那些他奉命毁掉的材料。暗地里，他却时刻留神那对野兽，指望借它们的反应，捕捉任何潜伏窥伺者留下的线索。

===Original===
The larger of the two beaches marked the point where the Terrans had first landed and where the shell thing had been killed. The smaller was more of a narrow tongue thrust out into the lagoon, much of it choked with sizable boulders. On earlier visits there Taggi and Togi had poked into the hollows among these with their usual curiosity. But now both animals remained upslope, showing no inclination to descend to the water line.

Shann caught hold of Taggi’s scruff, pulling him along. The wolverine twisted and whined, but he did not fight for freedom as he would have upon scenting Throg. Not that the Terran had ever believed one of those aliens was responsible for the happenings on the island.
===Chinese===
两片滩地里，大的那片是两个地球人最初登陆之处，也是宰掉那带壳怪物的刑场。小的那片更像一条探进潟湖的窄长岩舌，大半被不小的巨砾壅塞。早先到那儿去时，塔吉和托吉总要照它们一贯的好奇心，往那些巨砾间的窟穴里嗅探一番。可这一回，两只野兽却滞留坡上，没有半点下到水边去的意思。

沙恩攥住塔吉的颈皮，硬拽着它走。狼獾扭着身子呜呜哀叫，却没有像嗅到斯罗格人时那样拼死挣脱。倒不是这个地球人当真相信过，岛上的这些变故是那伙外星人干的。

===Original===
Taggi came down under Shann’s urging, but he was plainly ill at ease. And at last he snarled a warning when the man would have drawn him closer to two rocks which met overhead in a crude semblance of an arch. There was a stick of drift protruding from that hollow affording Shann a legitimate excuse to venture closer. He dropped his hold on the wolverines, stooped to gather in the length of wood, and at the same time glanced into the pocket.

Water lay just beyond, making this a doorway to the lagoon. The sun had not yet penetrated into the shadow, if it ever did. Shann reached for the wood, at the same time drawing his finger across the flat rock which would furnish a steppingstone for anything using that door as an entrance to the island.
===Chinese===
在沙恩的催逼下，塔吉总算下了坡，却明显浑身不自在。待到人还要把它往两块巨石跟前带时——那两石在头顶相遇，拼出一个粗陋的拱形——它终于低吼出声，示了警。一截浮木从那窟穴里探出来，给了沙恩一个再正当不过的靠近理由。他撒开狼獾，俯身去拾那截木头，眼角却同时扫进了那处穴窝。

穴后就是水，这里俨然是一扇通向潟湖的门。日头还没有照进那片阴影里——只怕它从来就照不进去。沙恩伸手去够浮木，指尖同时从一块扁平的岩石上抹过——凡是从那扇门进岛的东西，都得拿它当垫脚石。

===Original===
Wet! Which might mean his visitor had recently arrived, or else merely that a splotch of spray had landed there not too long before. But in his mind Shann was convinced that he had found the spy’s entrance. Could he turn it into a trap? He added a piece of drift to his bundle and picked up two more before he returned to the cliff ahead.

A trap﻿ ﻿… He revolved in his mind all the traps he knew which could be used here. He already had decided upon the bait﻿—his own work. And if his plans went through﻿—and hope does not die easily﻿—then this time he would not waste his labor either.
===Chinese===
湿的！这也许意味着那位访客方才驾到，也可能不过是没多久前溅上去的一星浪沫。但沙恩心里已经认定：他找到了那暗探的入口。他能把它变成一座陷阱吗？他又往柴捆里添了一截浮木，再捡起两截，才回到前头的崖壁去。

陷阱……他在心里把自知道的陷阱盘了一遍，拣出能在眼下用的。饵，他已经定了——就是他自己手上的活计。而只要他的计划成真——希望这东西，从来不是轻易死得掉的——那么这一回，他的力气也不会白费。

===Original===
So he went back to the same job he had done the day before, making do with skin strips he had considered second-best before, smoothing, cutting. Only the trap occupied his mind, and close to sunset he knew just what he was going to do and how.

Though the Terran did not know the nature of the unseen opponent, he thought he could guess two weaknesses which might deliver the other into his hands. First, the enemy was entirely confident of success in this venture. No being who was able to control Shann as completely and ably as had been done the night before would credit any prey with the power to strike back in force.
===Chinese===
于是他回头干起了昨天的老活计，将就着用那些他先前看不上眼、只当二流的皮条，又刨又裁。他心里只装着那座陷阱。临近日落，该做什么、怎么做，他已了然于胸。

这个地球人虽然摸不清那位看不见的对手的底细，却自信猜得出对方的两个软肋，兴许正可凭这个把对手擒到手。其一，敌人对这桩买卖的成功怀有的是十足的全胜之心。任何一个能把沙恩控制得像昨夜那般彻底、那般漂亮的生灵，都不会相信到手猎物还有力量狠狠还手。

===Original===
Second, such a confident enemy would be unable to resist watching the manipulation of a captive. The Terran was certain that his opponent would be on the scene somewhere when he was led, dreaming, to destroy his work once more.

He might be wrong on both of those counts, but inwardly he didn’t believe so. However, he had to wait until the dark to set up his own answer, one so simple he was certain the enemy would not suspect it at all.
===Chinese===
其二，这样一个自信满满的敌人，是抗拒不了亲眼监看傀儡摆布的诱惑的。地球人确信，等到他被引领着、在梦游中再一次去毁掉自己的活计时，他的对手必定就在近旁某个地方看着。

这两条他也许都料错了，但他心底里并不这么认为。不过，他还得等到天黑，才好布置他自己的答案——那答案简单到了家，他笃定敌人连想都想不到。

## XI The Witch / XI 女巫

===Original===
There were patches of light in the inner valley marking the phosphorescent plants, some creeping at ground level, others tall as saplings. On other nights Shann had welcomed that wan radiance, but now he lay in as relaxed a position as possible, marking each of those potential betrayers as he tried to counterfeit the attitude of sleep and at the same time plan out his route.
===Chinese===
内谷里一斑一斑的光标出那些磷光植物的位置——有的贴地蔓生，有的长得同幼树一般高。换作别的夜晚，沙恩巴不得有这点微光；可此刻他尽可能放松地躺着，一面把每一个可能出卖他的光源一一记下，一面装出酣睡的姿态，同时在心里盘算自己的路线。

===Original===
He had purposely settled in a pool of shadow, the wolverines beside him. And he thought that the bulk of the animal’s bodies would cover his own withdrawal when the time came to move. One arm lying limply across his middle was in reality clutching to him an intricate arrangement of small hide straps which he had made by sacrificing most of the remainder of his painfully acquired thongs. The trap must be set in place soon!
===Chinese===
他特意挑了一潭阴影栖身，狼獾们卧在身旁。他盘算着，到时候真要动身，两具兽躯的块头正好遮掩他的撤离。一条手臂软塌塌横在腹前，实则贴身攥着一副用小皮条结成的繁复绳网——为编它，他搭上了千辛万苦攒下的皮条所剩的大半。这张罗网必须尽快安放到位！

===Original===
Now that he had charted a path to the crucial point avoiding all light plants, Shann was ready to move. The Terran pressed his hand on Taggi’s head in the one imperative command the wolverine was apt to obey﻿—the order to stay where he was.
===Chinese===
既然已经勘定一条避开所有发光植物、直抵要害之处的路线，沙恩便可以行动了。地球人把手按上塔吉的头，打出那条狼獾最肯服从的强制命令——原地待着别动。

===Original===
Shann sat up and gave the same voiceless instruction to Togi. Then he inched out of the hollow, a worm’s progress to that narrow way along the cliff top﻿—the path which anyone or anything coming up from that sea gate on the beach would have to pass in order to witness the shoreline occupied by the half-built outrigger.
===Chinese===
沙恩坐起身，对托吉打出同样的无声指令。随后他从洼坑里一寸寸蹭了出去，像虫子一般挪向崖顶那条窄道——无论何人何物，凡从海滩上那道海门上来的，要看见被那艘造了一半的舷外支架占着的岸线，都得打这条路经过。

===Original===
So much of his plan was based upon luck and guesses, but those were all Shann had. And as he worked at the stretching of his snare, the Terran’s heart pounded, and he tensed at every sound out of the night. Having tested all the anchoring of his net, he tugged at a last knot, and then crouched to listen not only with his ears, but with all his strength of mind and body.
===Chinese===
他的这份计划，大半押在运气和猜测上，可他手里也只剩这两样了。张设罗网的时候，地球人的心怦怦狂跳，夜里传来的每一丝声响都让他绷紧。他把罗网的每个固着点一一试过，又拽紧最后一个绳结，然后蹲下身去，不光用耳朵，还倾尽全部心力与体力去听。

===Original===
Pound of waves, whistle of wind, the sleepy complaint of some bird﻿ ﻿… A regular splashing! One of the fish in the lagoon? Or what he awaited? The Terran retreated as noiselessly as he had come, heading for the hollow where he had bedded down.
===Chinese===
浪头一下一下地砸响，风声呼啸，还有哪只鸟儿睡意朦胧的咕哝……忽而——一阵规律的泼水声！是潟湖里的什么鱼？还是他等候的东西？地球人来时无声，去时也无息，循原路退回自己过夜的那个洼坑。

===Original===
He reached there breathless, his heart pumping, his mouth dry as if he had been racing. Taggi stirred and thrust a nose inquiringly against Shann’s arm. But the wolverine made no sound, as if he, too, realized that some menace lay beyond the rim of the valley. Would that other come up the path Shann had trapped? Or had he been wrong? Was the enemy already stalking him from the other beach? The grip of his stunner was slippery in his damp hand; he hated this waiting.
===Chinese===
到洼坑时他已气喘吁吁，心口突突直跳，嘴里干得像刚跑完一场竞速。塔吉动了动，把鼻子探过来，在沙恩手臂上嗅问。但这只狼獾没有出声，仿佛它也明白，谷缘之外潜着什么威胁。那一位会从沙恩下了罗网的小径上来吗？还是他猜错了？敌人已经从另一片海滩悄悄摸过来了？击晕枪的握把在他汗湿的手里打滑；他恨透了这种等待。

===Original===
The canoe﻿ ﻿… his work on it had been a careless botching. Better to have the job done right. Why, it was perfectly clear now how he had been mistaken! His whole work plan was wrong; he could see the right way of doing things laid out as clear as a blueprint in his mind. A picture in his mind!
===Chinese===
那条独木舟……他先前干的那活儿毛糙得一塌糊涂。还是得把这活儿干像样了才好。瞧啊，他当初错在哪儿，此刻不是一清二楚嘛！整套做工的路数全错了；正确的做法就摊在他的脑子里，清楚得像一张蓝图。一幅印在他脑子里的图！

===Original===
Shann stood up and both wolverines moved uneasily, though neither made a sound. A picture in his mind! But this time he wasn’t asleep; he wasn’t dreaming a dream﻿—to be used for his own defeat. Only (that other could not know this) the pressure which had planted the idea of new work to be done in his mind﻿—an idea one part of him accepted as fact﻿—had not taken warning from his move. He was supposed to be under control; the Terran was sure of that. All right, so he would play that part. He must if he would entice the trapper into his trap.
===Chinese===
沙恩站起身，两只狼獾都不安地动了动，却谁也没出声。一幅印在他脑子里的图！可这一回他没有睡着；他不是在做一场「梦」——不是要被拿来葬送自己。只是（那一位无从知道这一层）那股把「还有新活计要干」的念头栽进他心里的压力——他身上已有一部分把这个念头当作了事实——并未因他的举动而心生戒备。照理说，他该是处在控制之下的；这一点地球人确信不疑。好，那他就把这个角色扮下去。想把设陷人诱进他自己布下的罗网，他就非扮不可。

===Original===
He holstered his stunner, walked out into the open, paying no heed now to the patches of light through which he must pass on his way to the path his own feet had already worn to the boat beach. As he went, Shann tried to counterfeit what he believed would be the gait of a man under compulsion.
===Chinese===
他把击晕枪插回枪套，径直走进开阔地，这会儿全不理会一路上必须穿过的那些光斑——那条通往船滩的小径，他自己的双脚早已把它踩了出来。一路走，沙恩一面竭力摹拟他心目中受制之人的步态。

===Original===
Now he was on the rim fronting the downslope, fighting against his desire to turn and see for himself if anything had climbed behind. The canoe was all wrong, a bad job which he must make better at once so that in the morning he would be free of this island prison.
===Chinese===
此刻他已站在坡顶边缘，迎着下坡路，强压着回头亲眼看看身后是否有什么东西爬上来的欲望。那条独木舟全不对路，是件马虎的糙活，必须马上返工做好，好教他明早便能脱离这座囚岛。

===Original===
The pressure of that other’s will grew stronger. And the Terran read into that the overconfidence which he believed would be part of the enemy’s character. The one who was sending him to destroy his own work had no suspicion that the victim was not entirely malleable, ready to be used as he himself would use a knife or a force ax. Shann strode steadily downslope. With a small spurt of fear he knew that in a way that unseen other was right; the pressure was taking over, even though he was awake this time. The Terran tried to will his hand to his stunner, but his fingers fell instead on the hilt of his knife. He drew the blade as panic seethed in his head, chilling him from within. He had underestimated the other’s power﻿ ﻿…
===Chinese===
那一位意志的压力愈来愈强。地球人从中读出了一种过分自信——他相信那正是敌人性子里的一部分。那个打发他来毁掉自己心血的家伙，一点也没起疑：这个猎物并非全然可捏可塑、任凭驱使，像他自己使唤一柄刀或一柄力场斧那样。沙恩稳步向坡下走去。随即他心里迸出一小股恐惧，因为他明白，在某种意义上，那个看不见的对头并没有错；压力正在接管一切，哪怕这一回他是清醒的。地球人拼命想让手去够击晕枪，手指却落在了刀柄上。恐慌在脑子里翻腾，从里往外把他冻透，他抽出了刀刃。他把那一位的力量估量得太低了……

===Original===
And that panic flared into open fight, making him forget his careful plans. Now he *must* wrench free from this control. The knife was moving to slash a hide lashing, directed by his hand, but not his will.
===Chinese===
那股恐慌轰然烧成一场公开的搏斗，让他把周密的计划忘了个干净。现在他*非*挣脱这份控制不可。刀正在割向一段捆扎的皮索——出手的是他的手，却不是他的意愿。

===Original===
A soundless gasp, a flash of dismay rocked him, but neither was his gasp nor his dismay. That pressure snapped off; he was free. But the other wasn’t! Knife still in fist, Shann turned and ran upslope, his torch in his other hand. He could see a shape now writhing, fighting, outlined against a light bush. And, fearing that the stranger might win free and disappear, the Terran spotlighted the captive in the beam, reckless of Throg or enemy reinforcements.
===Chinese===
一声无声的倒抽气、一道惊慌的闪念把他撼得晃了晃——可那抽气不是他的抽气，惊慌也不是他的惊慌。那股压力戛然而断；他自由了。可对方没有！沙恩手里仍攥着刀，转身朝坡上跑去，另一只手举着原子炬。他看见一团身影正对着一丛发光的灌木扭动、挣扎。生怕那陌生者挣脱跑掉，地球人把光柱直直照住那个落网者，全不顾会不会招来斯罗格人或敌方的接应。

===Original===
The other crouched, plainly startled by the sudden burst of light. Shann stopped abruptly. He had not really built up any mental picture of what he had expected to find in his snare, but this prisoner was as weirdly alien to him as a Throg. The light on the torch was reflected off a skin which glittered as if scaled, glittered with the brilliance of jewels in bands and coils of color spreading from the throat down the chest, spiraling about upper arms, around waist and thighs, as if the stranger wore a treasure house of gems as part of a living body. Except for those patterned loops, coils, and bands, the body had no clothing, though a belt about the slender middle supported a pair of pouches and some odd implements held in loops.
===Chinese===
那一位蹲伏着，显然被这骤然炸开的光惊得怔住了。沙恩猛地停步。他原本并没有在心里勾画出网里会落到个什么，可这名俘虏之诡异地陌生，于他不下于一个斯罗格人。炬光在那身皮肤上反射开来——那皮肤鳞片般闪亮，闪出宝石般的华彩，一道道、一圈圈的彩色纹带自咽喉铺下胸膛，绕着上臂盘旋，环住腰肢和大腿，仿佛这陌生者把一整座宝石宝库当作活生生身体的一部分穿在了身上。除了那些图纹状的环、卷、带，这具身子不着一缕，只是纤细的腰间束着一条腰带，上面挂着两只小袋，还有几件套在环扣里的古怪器具。

===Original===
Roughly the figure was more humanoid than the Throgs. The upper limbs were not too unlike Shann’s arms, though the hands had four digits of equal length instead of five. But the features were nonhuman, closer to saurian in contour. It had large eyes, blazing yellow in the dazzle of the flash, with vertical slits of green for pupils. A nose united with the jaw to make a snout, and above the domed forehead a sharp V-point of raised spiky growth extended back and down until behind the shoulder blades it widened and expanded to resemble a pair of wings.
===Chinese===
大体说来，这身形比斯罗格人更接近人形。上肢同沙恩的手臂相差不远，只是那双手五指成了四指，根根等长。可那副眉眼面目绝非人类，轮廓倒近乎蜥蜴一属。一双大眼在炬光的炫耀里烧成灼亮的黄色，瞳孔是两道竖直的绿缝。鼻子与下颌连成一片吻部，而在那穹隆似的前额上方，一道尖削的 V 形尖刺冠向后下方延展，直到肩胛后方才展宽扩大，活似一对翅膀。

===Original===
The captive no longer struggled, but sat quietly in the tangle of the snare Shann had set, watching the Terran steadily as if there were no difficulty in seeing through the brilliance of the beam to the man who held it. And, oddly enough, Shann experienced no repulsion toward its reptilian appearance as he had upon first sighting the beetle-Throg. On impulse he put down his torch on a rock and walked into the light to face squarely the thing out of the sea.
===Chinese===
俘虏不再挣扎，安静地坐在沙恩布下的罗网缠结里，定定地望着地球人，仿佛要透过来炬光的炫亮、看清持炬的人毫不费事。而说来也怪，面对这副爬虫般的形容，沙恩竟不像初见甲虫般的斯罗格人时那样心生憎恶。他一时冲动，把原子炬搁在一块岩石上，自己走进光里，正面直视这个从海里出来的东西。

===Original===
Still eying Shann, the captive raised one limb and gave an absentminded tug to the belt it wore. Shann, noting that gesture, was struck by a wild surmise, leading him to study the prisoner more narrowly. Allowing for the alien structure of bone, the nonhuman skin; this creature was delicate, graceful, in its way beautiful, with a fragility of limb which backed up his suspicions. Moved by no pressure from the other, but by his own will and sense of fitness, Shann stooped to cut the control line of his snare.
===Chinese===
俘虏仍盯着沙恩，抬起一只上肢，心不在焉地拽了拽腰间的束带。沙恩注意到这个动作，脑中蓦地被一个大胆的猜想击中，便更细细地打量起这名俘虏来。撇开那副异星的骨骼构造和非人的皮肤不谈，这生灵纤巧、优雅，自有一番美丽，四肢的柔弱更坐实了他的疑心。这一回不是出于对方的压力，而是出于自己的意愿和分寸之感，沙恩俯身割断了罗网的控制索。

===Original===
The captive continued to watch as Shann sheathed his blade and then held out his hand. Yellow eyes, never blinking since his initial appearance, regarded him, not with any trace of fear or dismay, but with a calm measurement which was curiosity based upon a strong belief in its own superiority. He did not know how he knew, but Shann was certain that the creature out of the sea was still entirely confident, that it made no fight because it did not conceive of any possible danger from him. And again, oddly enough, he was not irritated by this unconscious arrogance; rather he was intrigued and amused.
===Chinese===
沙恩收刀入鞘，又伸出手去，俘虏依旧看着他。那双黄眼睛自打现身起一眨未眨，此刻端详着他，不带一丝惧意或慌乱，只有一种平静的掂量——那是建立在强烈自恃之上的好奇。他说不上自己是怎么知道的，可沙恩确信，这个从海里来的生灵依旧信心十足；它之所以不反抗，是因为压根设想不出他会构成什么危险。而说来又怪，面对这份浑然不觉的傲慢，他并不着恼，反倒觉得有趣，暗自失笑。

===Original===
“Friends?” Shann used the basic galactic speech devised by Survey and the Free Traders, semantics which depended upon the proper inflection of voice and tone to project meaning when the words were foreign.

The other made no sound, and the Terran began to wonder if his captive had any audible form of speech. He withdrew a step or two then pulled at the snare, drawing the cords away from the creature’s slender ankles. Rolling the thongs into a ball, he tossed the crude net back over his shoulder.
===Chinese===
“朋友？”沙恩用的是勘测队和自由商人（Free Traders）共同创制的基础银河语（basic galactic speech）——这套语汇靠着恰当的声调与语气来传义，哪怕字眼于对方全然陌生。

对方没有出声，地球人不禁犯起了嘀咕：这名俘虏究竟有没有可以听见的言语。他退开一两步，动手去拆那罗网，把绳索从那生灵纤细的脚踝上解下来。他把皮索卷作一团，将这张粗陋的网甩回肩头。

===Original===
“Friends?” he repeated again, showing his empty hands, trying to give that one word the proper inflection, hoping the other could read his peaceful intent in his features if not by his speech.

In one lithe, flowing movement the alien arose. Fully erect, the Warlockian had a frail appearance. Shann, for his breed, was not tall. But the native was still smaller, not more than five feet, that stiff V of head crest just topping Shann’s shoulder. Whether any of those fittings at its belt could be a weapon the Terran had no way of telling. However, the other made no move to draw any of them.
===Chinese===
“朋友？”他又说了一遍，摊开空空的双手，努力给这一个词配上恰当的声调，盼望对方纵然听不懂他的言语，也能从他脸上读出和平的用意。

那外星生灵一个柔韧流畅的动作便站了起来。站直了，这位沃洛克人显得单薄纤弱。就他的族类而论，沙恩个子不高；可这土著还要更矮，至多五英尺，头上那道硬挺的 V 形头冠刚刚够到沙恩的肩头。腰带上那些物件里有没有能充兵器的，地球人无从判别。不过对方丝毫没有伸手去取的意思。

===Original===
Instead, one of the four-digit hands came up. Shann felt the feather touch of strange finger tips on his chin, across his lips, up his cheek, to at last press firmly on his forehead at a spot just between the eyebrows. What followed was communication of a sort, not in words or in any describable flow of thoughts. There was no feeling of enmity﻿—at least nothing strong enough to be called that. Curiosity, yes, and then a growing doubt, not of the Terran himself, but of the other’s preconceived ideas concerning him. Shann was other than the native had judged him, and the stranger was disturbed, that self-confidence a little ruffled. And also Shann was right in his guess. He smiled, his amusement growing﻿—not aimed at his companion on this cliff top, but at himself. For he was dealing with a woman, a very young woman, and someone as fully feminine in her way as any human girl could be.
===Chinese===
然而，举起的是一只四指的手。沙恩感到陌生的指尖如羽毛般触上他的下巴，拂过双唇，抚上脸颊，最终稳稳按在他前额上两眉正中的那一点。随之而来的是某种交流——不在言语之中，也说不上是任何可以描述的思绪流动。其中没有敌意——至少没有什么强到配得上这个名目的东西。有好奇，是的，随后是一缕渐生的疑惑：疑惑的不是地球人本身，而是对方先前对他的成见。沙恩与这土著所以为的不同，这位陌生者不安了，那份自信被搅皱了一点。同时，沙恩的猜想也得了证实。他笑了，觉得越发有趣——这份趣味不是冲着崖顶上这位同伴，而是冲着他自己。因为与他打交道的，是一个女人，一个非常年轻的女人，而且以她自己的方式，其女儿气之足，丝毫不输任何一位人类少女。

===Original===
“Friends?” he asked for the third time.

But the other still exuded a wariness, a wariness mixed with surprise. And the tenuous message which passed between them then astounded Shann. To this Warlockian out of the night he was not following the proper pattern of male behaviour at all; he should have been in awe of the other merely because of her sex. A diffidence rather than an assumption of equality should have colored his response, judged by her standards. At first, he caught a flash of anger at this preposterous attitude of his; then her curiosity won, but there was still no reply to his question.
===Chinese===
“朋友？”他问了第三遍。

可对方仍透着一层戒备，一层掺着惊异的戒备。随后在他们之间传来的那缕微弱的讯息，让沙恩大为骇异。在这位从夜色里走出的沃洛克人看来，他的做派全然不合雄性应有的规矩；仅凭对方的性别，他就该诚惶诚恐才是。按她的标准衡量，给他的回应定调的本该是恭谨谦退，而不是自居对等的架势。起初，他捕捉到她因他这荒谬态度而迸出的一星怒火；随后她的好奇占了上风，可依然没有回答他的问题。

===Original===
The finger tips no longer made contact between them. Stepping back, her hands now reached for one of the pouches at her belt. Shann watched that movement carefully. And because he did not trust her too far, he whistled.
===Chinese===
指尖不再搭着他们之间的这层联络。她退后半步，两手伸向腰间的一只小袋。沙恩小心盯着这个动作。因为他对她信不过几分，便打了个呼哨。

===Original===
Her head came up. She might be dumb, but plainly she was not deaf. And she gazed down into the hollow as the wolverines answered his summons with growls. Her profile reminded Shann of something for an instant; but it should have been golden-yellow instead of silver with two jeweled patterns ringing the snout. Yes, that small plaque he had seen in the cabin of one of the ship’s officers. A very old Terran legend﻿—“Dragon,” the officer had named the creature. Only that one had possessed a serpent’s body, a lizard’s legs and wings.
===Chinese===
她的头扬了起来。她也许不会说话，但显然并不聋。狼獾们应着他的呼哨发出咆哮，她便朝洼坑里望去。她的侧影让沙恩一时想起什么；只是她本该是金黄金黄的，而非这银白之色，吻部还环着两圈宝石纹样。对了，是他在一位飞船军官的舱室里见过的一块小饰板。一个非常古老的地球传说——那位军官管那生物叫「龙」（Dragon）。只不过那一条生着蛇的身躯、蜥蜴的腿和翅膀。

===Original===
Shann gave a sudden start, aware his thoughts had made him careless, or had she in some way led him into that bypath of memory for her own purposes? Because now she held some object in the curve of her curled fingers, regarding him with those unblinking yellow eyes. Eyes﻿ ﻿… eyes﻿ ﻿… Shann dimly heard the alarm cry of the wolverines. He tried to snap draw his stunner, but it was too late.
===Chinese===
沙恩猛地一惊，察觉自己方才走了神、失了防备——抑或，是她为了自己的目的，设法把他引进了那条记忆的岔道？因为此刻她蜷起的指弯里已经攥着一件什么东西，正用那双一眨不眨的黄眼睛望着他。眼睛……眼睛……沙恩恍惚听见狼獾们发出警报的叫吼。他想把击晕枪一把拔出，可已经太迟了。

===Original===
There was a haze about him hiding the rocks, the island valley with its radiant plants, the night sky, the bright beam of the torch. Now he moved through that haze as one walks through a dream approaching nightmare, striding with an effort as if wading through a deterring flood. Sound, sight﻿—one after another those senses were taken from him. Desperately Shann held to one thing, his own sense of identity. He was Shann Lantee, Terran breed, out of Tyr, of the Survey Service. Some part of him repeated those facts with vast urgency against an almost overwhelming force which strove to defeat that awareness of self, making him nothing but a tool﻿—or a weapon﻿—for another’s use.
===Chinese===
一团迷雾罩住了他，遮没岩石，遮没那座亮着发光植物的岛上幽谷，遮没夜空和原子炬明亮的光柱。他在雾中行进，如同走过一场正滑向噩梦的梦，迈步吃力，仿佛正蹚过一道阻人的洪流。听觉、视觉——那些感官一样接一样地被从他身上夺走。沙恩死死抓住一样东西不放：他对自我身份的意识。他是沙恩·兰蒂，地球人的血脉，出身提尔，隶属勘测队。他身体里的某一部分万分急迫地反复念着这些事实，抗御着一股几乎不可抵挡的力量——那力量一心要击溃这份自我认知，把他变成一件工具——或一件兵器——供他人驱使。

===Original===
The Terran fought, soundlessly but fiercely, on a battleground which was within him, knowing in a detached way that his body obeyed another’s commands.
===Chinese===
地球人无声却凶猛地战斗着，战场就在他的身体之内；他以一种抽离旁观的清明知道，自己的肉体正听命于另一个人的号令。

===Original===
“I am Shann﻿—” he cried without audible speech. “I am myself. I have two hands, two legs﻿ ﻿… I think for myself! I am a *man*﻿—”

And to that came an answer of sorts, a blow of will striking at his resistance, a will which struggled to drown him before ebbing, leaving behind it a faint suggestion of bewilderment, of a dawn of concern.
===Chinese===
“我是沙恩——”他不出声地呐喊。“我就是我自己。我有两只手、两条腿……我为我自己思考！我是一个*人*——”

对此而来的算是某种回答：一记意志的重击砸向他的抵抗，那意志挣扎着要淹没他，随后退落下去，留下一丝隐约的迷惑、一丝初升的关切。

===Original===
“I am a *man*!” he hurled that assertion as he might have thrust deep with one of the crude spears he had used against the Throgs. For against what he faced now his weapons were as crude as spears fronting blasters. “I am Shann Lantee, Terran, man﻿ ﻿…” Those were facts; no haze could sweep them from his mind or take away that heritage.

And again there was the lightening of the pressure, the slight recoil, which could only be a prelude to another assault upon his last stronghold. He clutched his three facts to him as a shield, groping for others which might have afforded a weapon of rebuttal.
===Chinese===
“我是一个*人*！”他掷出这声断言，就像用一杆当年对付斯罗格人的粗矛深深捅进敌人身体。因为面对此刻的敌人，他的武器就像长矛对着爆能枪一样原始。“我是沙恩·兰蒂，地球人，人……”这些是事实；任何迷雾都不能把它们从他的心里扫走，也夺不走这份与生俱来的东西。

压力又一次轻了，微微一回缩——那只能是对他最后一座堡垒再度进攻的前奏。他把那三条事实当盾牌紧紧抱在怀里，摸索着别的事实，好凑成一件还击的兵器。

===Original===
Dreams, these Warlockians dealt in and through dreams. And the opposite of dreams are facts! His name, his breed, his sex﻿—these were facts. And Warlock itself was a fact. The earth under his boots was a fact. The water which washed around the island was a fact. The air he breathed was a fact. Flesh, blood, bones﻿—facts, all of them. Now he was a struggling identity imprisoned in a rebel body. But that body was real. He tried to feel it. Blood pumped from his heart, his lungs filled and emptied; he struggled to feel those processes.
===Chinese===
「梦」，这些沃洛克人凭着「梦」打交道、借着「梦」施展手段。而「梦」的对立面是事实！他的名字、他的血脉、他的性别——这些是事实。沃洛克本身就是事实。靴底的大地是事实。环着海岛冲刷的水是事实。他呼吸的空气是事实。血肉、血、骨——全是事实。此刻他是一个被囚禁在叛逆肉体里挣扎的自我。可那肉体是真实的。他努力去感觉它。血液从心脏里泵出，肺叶充盈又排空；他拼命去感觉这些过程。

===Original===
With a terrifying shock, the envelope which had held him vanished. Shann was choking, struggling in water. He flailed out with his arms, kicked his legs. One hand grated painfully against stone. Hardly knowing what he did, but fighting for his life, Shann caught at that rock and drew his head out of water. Coughing and gasping, half drowned, he was weak with the panic of his close brush with death.
===Chinese===
随着一记骇人的震骇，裹住他的那层帷罩消失了。沙恩呛着水，在水里挣扎。他双臂乱拍，双腿乱蹬。一只手擦上石头，痛得火辣辣的。沙恩几乎不知道自己在做什么，只是拼死求生，一把抓住那块岩石，把头拽出水面。他咳嗽着、喘着气，呛了个半死，与死亡擦身而过的惊怖令他浑身发软。

===Original===
For a long moment he could only cling to the rock which had saved him, retching and dazed, as the water washed about his body, a current tugging at his trailing legs. There was light of a sort here, patches of green which glowed with the same subdued light as the bushes of the outer world, for he was no longer under the night sky. A rock-roof was but inches over his head; he must be in some cave or tunnel under the surface of the sea. Again a gust of panic shook him as he felt trapped.
===Chinese===
有好长一阵，他只能抱着那块救命的岩石干呕、发懵，水在他身周荡漾，一股水流拖拽着他漂在后面的双腿。这里有某种光——一斑一斑的绿色，同外面世界那些灌木一样发着收敛的光；他已经不在夜空底下了。一方岩顶距他的头不过几英寸；他必定是在海面之下的某个洞窟或隧道里。一想到自己被困在了此处，又一阵恐慌袭来，攫住他直晃。

===Original===
The water continued to pull at Shann, and in his weakened condition it was a temptation to yield to that pull; the more he fought it the more he was exhausted. At last the Terran turned on his back, trying to float with the stream, sure he could no longer battle it.
===Chinese===
水流不住地拉扯沙恩，以他眼下虚弱的光景，顺从那拉扯真是种诱惑；他越挣扎就越力竭。末了，地球人翻过身来，试着随流仰漂，他确信自己再也斗不过这股水流了。

===Original===
Luckily those few inches of space above the surface of the water continued, and he had air to breathe. But the fear of that ending, of being swept under the surface, chewed at his nerves. And his bodily danger burned away the last of the spell which had held him, brought him into this place, wherever it might be.
===Chinese===
所幸水面上方那几英寸的空隙一直都在，他还有空气可吸。可对那个结局——被卷到水面之下——的恐惧啃噬着他的神经。而肉体上的凶险，烧尽了那道摄住他的魔咒的最后一丝余力——正是它把他带到了此地，管这里到底是哪儿。

===Original===
Was it only his heightened imagination, or had the current grown swifter? Shann tried to gauge the speed of his passage by the way the patches of green light slipped by. Now he turned and began to swim slowly, feeling as if his arms were leaden weights, his ribs a cage to bind his aching lungs.
===Chinese===
只是他绷得过紧的想象，还是水流当真急了？沙恩借着一块块绿光掠过的快慢，试图估出自己的行速。随后他转过身，缓缓游动起来，只觉两条手臂沉得像铅，肋骨成了一具笼子，紧紧箍住他作痛的肺。

===Original===
Another patch of light﻿ ﻿… larger﻿ ﻿… spreading across the roof over head. Then, he was out! Out of the tunnel into a cavern so vast that its arching roof was like a skydome far above his head. But here the patches of light were brighter, and they were arranged in odd groups which had a familiar look to them.
===Chinese===
又一片光……更大了……漫过头顶的岩顶。然后，他出来了！出了隧道，进了一座洞窟——那窟宏大至极，穹隆般的窟顶高高在上，宛如一方天幕。而这里的光斑更亮，而且排成一组组古怪的图形，看着眼熟得很。

===Original===
Only, better than freedom overhead, there was a shore not too distant. Shann swam for that haven, summoning up the last rags of his strength, knowing that if he could not reach it very soon he was finished. Somehow he made it and lay gasping, his cheek resting on sand finer than any of the outer world, his fingers digging into it for purchase to drag his body on. But when he collapsed, his legs were still awash in water.
===Chinese===
只是，比头顶的空阔更妙的是，不远处竟有一线堤岸。沙恩朝那处归宿游去，唤起最后几缕残存的力气，心里明白若不能马上到岸，他就完了。不知怎么他真游到了，趴在那儿喘气，脸颊贴着细沙——比外面世界的任何沙子都细——手指抠进沙里借力，往前拖曳身体。可等他一瘫倒，双腿还浸在水里。

===Original===
No footfall could be heard on that sand. But he knew that he was no longer alone. He braced his hands and with painful effort levered up his body. Somehow he made it to his knees, but he could not stand. Instead he half tumbled back, so that he faced them from a sitting position.
===Chinese===
这样的细沙上传不出脚步声。可他知道，自己已不再是孤身一人。他撑起双手，忍着痛楚使劲把身体撬了起来。他勉强跪直了，却站不起来，反而半跌回去，索性坐着面对他们。

===Original===
*Them*﻿—there were three of them﻿—the dragon-headed ones with their slender, jewel-set bodies glittering even in this subdued light, their yellow eyes fastened on him with a remoteness which did not approach any human emotion, save perhaps that of a cold and limited wonder. But behind them came a fourth, one he knew by the patterns on her body.
===Chinese===
*他们*——一共三个——都是龙首之人，纤细的身躯缀满珠光宝彩，即便在这收敛的光里也熠熠闪烁；黄色的眼睛钉在他身上，目光里带着一种疏远，疏远得近乎不含任何人类情感，至多只有一点冰冷的、有限的惊异。而在他们身后跟着第四个，他凭她身上的纹样认了出来。

===Original===
Shann clasped his hands about his knees to still the trembling of his body, and eyed them back with all the defiance he could muster. Nor did he doubt that he had been brought here, his body as captive to their will, as had been that of their spy or messenger in his crude snare on the island.
===Chinese===
沙恩双手环抱膝盖，压住身体的颤抖，回瞪过去，把能攒起的倔强全都摆上目光。他也毫不怀疑自己是被弄到这儿来的：肉身沦为对方意志的俘虏——正如他们那位探子或信使，落进他在岛上布下的粗陋罗网一样。

===Original===
“Well, you have me,” he said hoarsely. “Now what?”

His words boomed weirdly out over the water, were echoed from the dim outer reaches of the cavern. There was no answer. They merely stood watching him. Shann stiffened, determined to hold to his defiance and to that identity which he now knew was his weapon against the powers they used.
===Chinese===
“好吧，你们逮到我了。”他声音沙哑地说。“接下来呢？”

他的话古怪地轰响着掠过水面，从洞窟幽暗的深处传来回声。没有回答。他们只是站着，望着他。沙恩绷紧了身体，打定主意守住这份倔强，也守住那个身份——他如今明白，那身份正是他对抗他们所操力量的兵器。

===Original===
The one who had somehow drawn him there moved at last, circling around the other three with a suggestion of diffidence in her manner. Shann jerked back his head as her hand stretched to touch his face. And then, guessing that she sought her peculiar form of communication, he submitted to her finger tips, though now his skin crawled under that light but firm pressure and he shrank from the contract.
===Chinese===
那位不知怎的把他引到这里来的，终于动了，绕过另外三个，举止间带着几分恭谨迟疑。她的手伸过来要触他的脸，沙恩猛地把头一缩。随后他猜到她要的是她那套独特的交流，便听任那些指尖落下来——虽然此刻在那轻而稳的一按之下，他的皮肤直起战栗，他畏缩着不愿承受这接触。

===Original===
There were no sensations this time. To his amazement a concrete inquiry shaped itself in his brain, as clear as if the question had been asked aloud: “Who are you?”
===Chinese===
这一回没有任何感觉。令他惊奇的是，一个具体的问句在他脑中自行成形，清晰得如同出声问出来的一样：“你是谁？”

===Original===
“Shann﻿ ﻿…” he began vocally, and then turned words into thoughts. “Shann Lantee, Terran, man.” He made his answer the same which had kept him from succumbing to their complete domination.
===Chinese===
“沙恩……”他先出了声，随即把言语化作思绪。“沙恩·兰蒂，地球人，人。”他作答所用的，正是那句曾撑住他、没让他彻底屈服于他们支配的话。

===Original===
“Name﻿—Shann Lantee, man﻿—yes.” The other accepted those, “Terran?” That was a question.

Did these people have any notion of space travel? Could they understand the concept of another world holding intelligent beings?
===Chinese===
“名字——沙恩·兰蒂，人——是的。”对方接受了这几项。“地球人？”这是一个问句。

这些人有丝毫宇宙航行的概念吗？他们能理解另一个世界也栖居着智慧生灵这个观念吗？

===Original===
“I come from another world﻿ ﻿…” He tried to make a clean-cut picture in his mind﻿—a globe in space, a ship blasting free﻿ ﻿…

“Look!” The fingers still rested between his eyebrows, but with her other hand the Warlockian was pointing up to the dome of the cavern.
===Chinese===
“我来自另一个世界……”他试着在心中造出一幅清晰的图景——太空中的一颗球体，一艘挣脱引力远去的飞船……

“看！”指尖仍停在他的眉心，那沃洛克人却用另一只手指向洞窟的穹顶。

===Original===
Shann followed her order. He studied those patches of light which had seemed so vaguely familiar at his first sighting, studying them closely to know them for what they were. A star map! A map of the heavens as they could be seen from the outer crust of Warlock.
===Chinese===
沙恩依言望去。他细细研究那些初见时便隐隐眼熟的光斑，看个仔细，终于认出了它们的本来面目。一幅星图！一幅从沃洛克的外层地壳上仰望所得的天穹之图。

===Original===
“Yes, I come from the stars,” he answered, booming with his voice.
===Chinese===
“是的，我来自群星。”他提高声音答道，话音在窟中嗡嗡回响。

===Original===
The fingers dropped from his forehead; the scaled head swung around to exchange glances, which were perhaps some unheard communication with the other three. Then the hand was extended again.

“Come!”

Fingers fell from his head to his right wrist, closing there with surprising strength; and some of that strength together with a new energy flowed from them into him, so that he found and kept his feet as the other drew him up.
===Chinese===
指尖从他额头垂落；那颗覆鳞的头颅转了过来，与其余三个交换眼色——那眼色也许正是某种听不见的交流。随后那只手又伸了出来。

“来！”

指头从他头上落到他的右腕，扣在那里，力道大得惊人；而那力道的一部分，连同一般新的活力，从指尖流进了他的身体，于是当对方把他拉起来时，他找到了脚跟，站稳了，没有再倒。

## XII The Veil of Illusion / XII 幻象之幕

===Original===
Perhaps his status was that of a prisoner, but Shann was too tired to press for an explanation. He was content to be left alone in the unusual circular, but roofless, room of the structure to which they had brought him. There was a thick mat-like pallet in one corner, short for the length of his body, but softer than any bed he had rested on since he had left the Terran camp before the coming of the Throgs. Above him glimmered those patches of light symbolizing the lost stars. He blinked at them until they all ran together in bands like the jeweled coils on Warlockian bodies; then he slept﻿—dreamlessly.

The Terran awoke with all his senses alert; some silent alarm might have triggered that instant awareness of himself and his surroundings. There had been no change in the star pattern still overhead; no one had entered the round chamber. Shann rolled over on his mat bed, conscious that all his aches had vanished. Just as his mind was clearly active, so did his body also respond effortlessly to his demands. He was not aware of any hunger or thirst, though a considerable length of time must have passed since he had made his mysteriously contrived exit from the outer world.

In spite of the humidity of the air, his ragged garments had dried on his body. Shann got to his feet, trying to order the sorry remnants of his uniform, eager to be on the move. Though to where and for what purpose he could not have answered.

===Chinese===
他这会儿的身份，说不定就是个囚徒，可沙恩累得没力气去讨一个解释。她们把他带进那座建筑里一间古怪的圆形无顶屋，任他独自待着，他也就知足了。屋角铺着一张厚厚的席垫似的铺位，对他的身量来说短了一截，却比他自打赶在斯罗格人来犯之前离开地球人营地以来睡过的任何一张床都柔软。头顶上，那些象征着失落群星的光斑幽幽闪烁。他对着它们眨眼，直到它们全都连成一条条光带，像沃洛克人身上那一圈圈珠光纹彩；然后他睡了——睡得无梦。

这个地球人一觉醒来，所有感官全都警觉着；也许是什么无声的警报，触发了这刹那间对自身与四周的知觉。头顶的星图纹丝未变，也没有人进过这间圆屋。沙恩在席垫床上翻了个身，发觉周身的酸痛全消了。他的头脑清明活跃，他的身体也同样毫不费力地听从调遣。他感觉不到一丝饥饿或干渴——尽管自从他被那样神秘地安排着送离外界以来，想必已过去了相当长的时日。

尽管空气潮湿，他那身褴褛的衣裳竟已在身上干了。沙恩站起身，整理了一下制服上那些可怜的残余，一心想要动身。虽说要去哪里、所为何来，他自己也答不上来。

===Original===
The door through which he had entered remained closed, refusing to yield to his push. Shann stepped back, eyeing the distance to the top of the partition between the roofless rooms. The walls were smooth with the gloss of a sea shell’s interior, but the exuberant confidence which had been with him since his awakening refused to accept such a minor obstacle.

He made two test leaps, both times his fingers striking the wall well below the top of the partition. Shann gathered himself together as might a cat and tried the third time, putting into that effort every last ounce of strength, determination and will. He made it, though his arms jerked as the weight of his body hung from his hands. Then a scramble, a knee hooked over the top, and he was perched on the wall, able to study the rest of the building.

===Chinese===
他进来的那扇门依旧关着，任他推撞也不肯让步。沙恩退后几步，目测着这些无顶房间之间到隔墙顶端的距离。墙面光滑，泛着贝壳内壁般的光泽，可打从他醒来便充盈周身的那股昂扬自信，拒不把这点小障碍放在眼里。

他试跳了两回，两回手指都够在隔墙顶端以下老远的地方。沙恩像猫一样蓄足了身形，试了第三回，把浑身的力气、决心和意志一滴不剩地全灌了进去。他成功了——虽说当全身的重量都吊在手上时，两条胳膊被拽得一颤一颤。接着一阵攀爬，一条膝盖勾上了墙顶，他已稳稳骑在墙上，得以打量这座建筑的其余部分。

===Original===
In shape, the structure was unlike anything he had seen on his home world or reproduced in any of the tri-dee records of Survey accessible to him. The rooms were either circular or oval, each separated from the next by a short passage, so that the overall impression was that of ten strings of beads radiating from a central knot of one large chamber, all with the uniform nacre walls and a limited amount of furnishings.

===Chinese===
论外形，这座建筑与他母星世界见过的任何东西都不相似，也不像他能查阅的任何勘测队三维影像（tri-dee）资料里收录的模样。房间不是圆形便是椭圆形，彼此之间隔着短短的甬道，因此整体印象便是十条珠串，从当中一大间的结点辐射开去，全都镶着一色的珠母墙壁，摆设寥寥。

===Original===
As he balanced on the narrow perch, Shann could sight no other movement in the nearest line of rooms, those connected by corridors with his own. He got to his feet to walk the tightrope of the upper walls toward that inner chamber which was the heart of the Warlockian﻿—palace? town? apartment dwelling? At least it was the only structure on the island, for he could see the outer rim of that smooth soft sand ringing it about. The island itself was curiously symmetrical, a perfect oval, too perfect to be a natural outcrop of sand and rock.

===Chinese===
沙恩在这狭窄的落脚处稳住身子，望不见最近那排房间——也就是同他这间以甬道相通的那些——里还有什么动静。他站起身，沿着高墙顶端这条钢丝，朝那间内室走去——那里是沃洛克人这座宫殿的心脏；这宫殿，或者该说是城镇？还是群居的宅邸？至少它是岛上唯一的建筑，因为他望得见环抱四周的那一圈光滑细软的沙缘。这座岛本身对称得出奇，是一个完美的椭圆，完美得绝非沙与岩石的天然露头。

===Original===
There was no day or night here in the cavern. The light from the roof patches remained constantly the same, and that flow was abetted within the building by a soft radiation from the walls. Shann reached the next room in line, hunkering down to see within it. To all appearances the chamber was exactly the same as the one he had just left; there were the same unadorned walls, a thick mat bed against the far side, and no indication whether it was in use or had not been entered for days.

He was on the next section of corridor wall when he caught that faint taint in the air, the very familiar scent of wolverines. Now it provided Shann with a guide as well as a promise of allies.

===Chinese===
这洞窟里没有昼夜。顶上光斑洒下的光恒定不变，建筑内部又有墙体柔和的辐光推波助澜。沙恩走到队列中的下一间，蹲下身往里瞧。表面看来，这间屋子同他刚离开的那间一模一样：同样素净无饰的墙，远侧同样铺着一张厚席床，而且看不出它眼下是否有人使用，或是已多日无人踏足。

他正走在下一段甬道墙上，忽然捕捉到空气中一缕淡淡的气息——狼獾的气味，再熟悉不过。此刻它既给沙恩指了路，也给了他盟友的指望。

===Original===
The next bead-room gave him what he wanted. Below him Taggi and Togi paced back and forth. They had already torn to bits the sleeping mat which had been the chamber’s single furnishing, and their temper was none too certain. As Shann squatted well above their range of vision, Taggi reared against the opposite wall, his claws finding no hold on the smooth coating of its surface. They were as competently imprisoned as if they had been dropped into a huge fishbowl, and they were not taking to it kindly.

===Chinese===
下一间珠室给了他想要的答案。就在他脚下，塔吉和托吉来回踱步。屋里唯一的陈设那张睡席，早已被它们撕成了碎片，它们的脾气正没个准头。沙恩蹲在高处、远在它们视野之外，只见塔吉人立而起，扑上对面的墙，爪子在光滑的墙面涂层上找不到半个着力点。它们被这么在行地关了禁闭，活像被丢进一只巨大的鱼缸，而它们对这份待遇可没有半点领情。

===Original===
How had the animals been brought here? Down that water tunnel by the same unknown method he himself had been transported until that almost disastrous awakening in the center of the flood? The Terran did not doubt that the doors of the room were as securely fastened as those of his own further down the corridor. For the moment the wolverines were safe; he could not free them. And he was growing increasingly certain that if he found any of his native jailers, it would be at the center of that wheel of rooms and corridors.

===Chinese===
这两只野兽是怎么给弄到这儿来的？莫非是顺着那条水道，用转运他本人的那同一种未知法子，一直送到洪流中央，直到那回险些酿成大祸的转醒？地球人毫不怀疑，这间屋的屋门同走廊那头他自己那间一样，都闩得严严实实。眼下狼獾们还算安全；他没法放它们出来。而他越来越笃定：要寻到这些土著狱卒中的一个，就得去那座由房间与甬道辐成的轮盘正中。

===Original===
Shann made no attempt to attract the animals’ attention, but kept on along his tightrope path. He passed two more rooms, both empty, both differing in no way from those he had already inspected; and then he came to the central chamber, four times as big as any of the rest and with a much brighter wall light.

The Terran crouched, one hand on the surface of the partition top as an additional balance, the other gripping his stunner. For some reason his captors had not disarmed him. Perhaps they believed they had no necessity to fear his off-world weapon.

===Chinese===
沙恩没有设法去引那两只野兽的注意，只顾沿着他的钢丝继续走。他又经过两间屋子，都空着，同他已经查看过的那些毫无二致；然后他到了中央大室——比其余任何一间都大上四倍，壁光也亮得多。

地球人蹲下身，一只手按在隔墙顶面上借以稳住重心，另一只手攥着击晕枪。不知出于什么缘故，俘获他的人没有解除他的武装。也许她们认定，这件异星武器没什么可怕的。

===Original===
“Have you grown wings?”

The words formed in his brain, bringing with them a sense of calm amusement to reduce all his bold exploration to the level of a child’s first staggering steps. Shann fought his first answering flare of pure irritation. To lose even a fraction of control was to open a door for them. He remained where he was as if he had never “heard” that question, surveying the room below with all the impassiveness he could summon.

===Chinese===
“你长出翅膀来了么？”

那话语在他脑中成形，携着一股平静的莞尔，把他这番大胆探察一下贬低到孩童初次蹒跚学步的地步。沙恩压下了心头第一阵纯属恼怒的火气。哪怕只失掉一分自制，也等于给她们敞开一扇门。他原地未动，仿佛从不曾“听见”那个问题，只拿出他所能聚起的全部漠然，打量底下的屋子。

===Original===
Here the walls were no smooth barrier, but honeycombed with niches in a regular pattern. And in each of the niches rested a polished skull, a nonhuman skull. Only the outlines of those ranked bones were familiar; for just so had looked the great purple-red rock where the wheeling flyers issued from the eye sockets. A rock island had been fashioned into a skull﻿—by design or nature?

And upon closer observation the Terran could see that there was a difference among these ranked skulls, a mutation of coloring from row to row, a softening of outline, perhaps by the wearing of time.

===Chinese===
这里的墙不再是光滑的屏障，而是按规律的纹样蜂巢般嵌满壁龛。每个龛里都安卧着一颗磨光的骷髅，非人的骷髅。唯有这些成列枯骨的轮廓是他熟识的；因为那块紫红色巨岩看去正是这副模样——会飞的东西便从眼窝里进进出出。一座岩岛竟被造就成一颗骷髅——是出自匠心，还是造化使然？

而再细看之下，地球人便发现这些成列的骷髅彼此有别：色泽逐行渐变，轮廓逐渐柔化，或许那是时间磨蚀的痕迹。

===Original===
There was also a table of dull black, rising from the flooring on legs which were not more than a very few inches high, so that from his present perch the board appeared to rest on the pavement itself. Behind the table in a row, as shopkeepers might await a customer, three of the Warlockians, seated cross-legged on mats, their hands folded primly before them. And at the side a fourth, the one whom he had trapped on the island.

===Chinese===
屋里还有一张暗黑色的桌子，从地面撑起的几条桌腿高不过寥寥几英寸，因此从沙恩眼下所处的高度看去，桌面就像是直接搁在地上。桌后一排坐着三名沃洛克人，仿佛店主恭候顾客一般盘腿坐在席上，双手规规矩矩地叠在身前。侧旁还有第四位——正是他在岛上设陷阱擒住的那一个。

===Original===
Not one of those spiked heads rose to view him. But they knew that he was there; perhaps they had known the very instant he had left the room or cell in which they had shut him. And they were so very sure of themselves﻿ ﻿… Once again Shann subdued a spark of anger. That same patience with its core of stubborn determination which had brought him to Warlock backed his moves now. The Terran swung down, landing lightly on his feet, facing the three behind the table, towering well over them as he stood erect, yet gaining no sense of satisfaction from that merely physical fact.

===Chinese===
那些冠刺尖耸的头颅，没有一颗抬起来看他。但她们知道他在那儿；也许他一离开被关押的那间屋子——那间囚室——她们就知道了，连时辰都不差。而她们又是那样地笃定……沙恩又一次按捺下一星怒火。正是那份以倔强决心为核的耐心，当年把他带到了沃洛克，此刻又撑着他的举动。地球人翻身跃下，轻轻落地，面向桌后那三位。他挺身直立，个头远高出她们，可这单纯身量上的事实，没给他带来半点快意。

===Original===
“You have come.” The words sounded as if they might be a part of some polite formula. So he replied in kind and aloud.

“I have come.” Without waiting for their bidding, he dropped into the same cross-legged pose, fronting them now on a more equal level across their dead black table.

===Chinese===
“你来了。”这话听来像是某种客套程式的一部分。于是他也照着程式，出声应答。

“我来了。”不等她们招呼，他便盘腿坐下，摆出同样的坐姿，隔着那张死黑色的桌子，在更对等的层面上与她们相向而坐。

===Original===
“And why have you come, star voyager?” That thought seemed to be a concentrated effort from all three rather than any individual questioning.

“And why did you bring me?” He hesitated, trying to think of some polite form of address. Those he knew which were appropriate to their sex on other worlds seemed incongruous when applied to the bizarre figures now facing him. “Wise ones,” he finally chose.

===Chinese===
“那么你为何而来，星际旅人（star voyager）？”那缕意念似乎是三人合力凝成的，而非任何一人的单独发问。

“那你们又为何带我前来？”他迟疑着，想找一个合乎礼数的称呼。他所知道的、在其他世界上合乎她们性别的那些敬称，安在眼前这几副奇诡的形象身上，怎么看怎么不搭调。“诸位智者，”他最终选定了这个。

===Original===
Those unblinking yellow eyes conveyed no emotion; certainly his human gaze could detect no change of expression on their nonhuman faces.

“You are a male.”

“I am,” he agreed, not seeing just what that fact had to do with either diplomatic fencing or his experiences of the immediate past.

===Chinese===
那些一眨不眨的黄眼睛不透露半点情绪；他那双人类的目光，也确实无从捕捉她们非人面孔上的丝毫变化。

“你是雄性。”

“我是。”他应道，心里却不明白这个事实同这番外交上的机锋交锋有什么相干，也看不出它同他刚刚经历的种种有何关联。

===Original===
“Where then is your thoughtguider?”

Shann puzzled over that conception, guessed at its meaning.

“I am my own thoughtguider,” he returned stoutly, with all the conviction he could manage to put into that reply.

===Chinese===
“那么，你的导思者（thoughtguider）何在？”

沙恩对着这个概念琢磨了一番，猜出了它的意思。

“我自己的导思者，就是我自己。”他理直气壮地答道，把能拿得出的全部信念都灌注进这一答里。

===Original===
Again he met a yellow-green stare, but he sensed a change in them. Some of their complacency had ebbed; his reply had been as a stone dropped into a quiet pool, sending ripples out afar to disturb the customary mirror surface of smooth serenity.

“The star-born one speaks the truth!” That came from the Warlockian who had been his first contact.

“It would appear that he does.” The agreement was measured, and Shann knew that he was meant to “overhear” that.

===Chinese===
他又迎上那黄绿色的凝视，却察觉她们起了变化。她们那股怡然自得消退了几分；他的回答好比一颗石子投进静池，涟漪远远荡开，扰动了那一贯平滑如镜的安然。

“星生者（star-born one）所言不虚！”这话来自当初与他初次接触的那位沃洛克人。

“看来他确是如此。”这声附和字斟句酌，而沙恩明白，这话正是说给他“偷听”的。

===Original===
“It would seem, Readers-of-the-rods”﻿—the middle one of the triumvirate at the table spoke now﻿—“that all living things do not follow our pattern of life. But that is possible. A male who thinks for himself﻿ ﻿… unguided, who dreams perhaps! Or who can understand the truth of dreaming! Strange indeed must be his people. Sharers-of-my-visions, let us consult the Old Ones concerning this.” For the first time one of those crested heads moved, the gaze shifted from Shann to the ranks of the skulls, pausing at one.

===Chinese===
“看来，诸位读签者（Readers-of-the-rods）——”桌前三人团居中的那位此刻开口了——“并非一切生灵都循我们的生活之道。但这毕竟是可能的。一个自行其思的雄性……无人引导，或许竟还会做梦！又或者，竟能明白做梦的真谛！他的族人必定怪异至极。共享幻象的诸位（Sharers-of-my-visions），让我们就此请教古老者（Old Ones）。”那些覆着冠刺的头颅中，头一回有一颗动了一动，目光从沙恩身上移向成排的骷髅，在其中一颗上停了下来。

===Original===
Shann, ready for any wonder, did not betray his amazement when the ivory inhabitant of that particular niche moved, lifted from its small compartment, and drifted buoyantly through the air to settle at the right-hand corner of the table. Only when it had safely grounded did the eyes of the Warlockian move to another niche on the other side of the curving room, this time bringing up from close to floor level a time-darkened skull to occupy the left corner of the table.

There was a third shifting from the weird storehouse, a last skull to place between the other two. And now the youngest native arose from her mat to bring a bowl of green crystal. One of her seniors took it in both hands, making a gesture of offering it to all three skulls, and then gazed over its rim at the Terran.

===Chinese===
沙恩对任何奇景都已有了准备，因此当那处龛格里的象牙色住户动起来、从小小的龛格中升起，轻飘飘地浮过半空、落定在桌子右角时，他没有露出惊诧。直到它稳稳安顿好，那位沃洛克人的目光才移向弧形房间另一侧的另一处壁龛，这一回从贴近地面的低处唤出一颗因岁月而暗沉的骷髅，安在桌子的左角。

第三颗又从这座诡异的库房里挪了位，是最后一颗骷髅，安放在另外两颗之间。接着，最年轻的那位土著从席上起身，捧来一只绿水晶碗。她的一位长辈双手接过，向三颗骷髅一一做出奉献的手势，然后隔着碗沿凝视地球人。

===Original===
“We shall cast the rods, man-who-thinks-without-a-guide. Perhaps then we shall see how strong *your* dreams are﻿—to be bent to your using, or to break you for your impudence.”

Her hands swayed the bowl from side to side, and there was an answering whisper from its interior as if the contents slid loosely there. Then one of her companions reached forward and gave a quick tap to the bottom of that container, spilling out upon the table a shower of brightly colored slivers each an inch or so long.

===Chinese===
“我们要掷签了，无导自思的人（man-who-thinks-without-a-guide）。届时或许便能看出，*你*的梦有多强——是能折得下来供你驱使，还是要因你这份放肆，反过来折断你。”

她双手捧碗左右摇晃，碗里应和着响起一阵窸窣，仿佛所盛之物在里头松松滑动。随后她的一位同伴探身向前，在碗底飞快一叩，一把色彩鲜明的细签便泼洒在桌面上，每根约有一英寸长。

===Original===
Shann, staring at the display in bewilderment, saw that in spite of the seeming carelessness of that toss the small needles had spread out on the blank surface to form a design in arrangement and color. And he wondered how that skillful trick had been accomplished.

All three of the Warlockians bent their heads to study the grouping of the tiny sticks, their young subordinate leaning forward also, her eagerness less well controlled than her elders’. And now it was as if a curtain had fallen between the Terran and the aliens, all sense of communication which had been with him since he had entered the skull-lined chamber was summarily cut off.

===Chinese===
沙恩一头雾水地盯着这场演示，却看出尽管那一泼看似漫不经心，细小的签针竟在空白桌面上铺展开来，以布局与色彩构成了一幅图纹。他不禁纳闷，这套精巧的手法是如何做到的。

三名沃洛克人一齐俯首，研读细签布成的阵形，她们那位年轻的下级也倾身向前，那股急切远不如长辈们把持得住。而此刻，仿佛一道帘幕在地球人与外星人之间垂落，自他踏入这间四壁皆骷髅的屋子以来始终伴随他的那份沟通之感，被断然切断了。

===Original===
A hand moved, making the jeweled pattern﻿—braceleting wrist and extending up the arm﻿—flash subdued fire. Fingers swept the sticks back into the bowl; four pairs of yellow eyes raised to regard Shann once more, but the blanket of their withdrawal still held.

The youngest Warlockian took the bowl from the elder who held it, stood for a long moment with it resting between her palms, fixing Shann with an unreadable stare. Then she came toward him. One of those at the table put out a restraining hand.

===Chinese===
一只手动了，令腕间缠腕、沿臂而上的珠光纹彩闪出幽幽火彩。手指将细签扫回碗里；四对黄眼睛重新抬起，再度望向沙恩，可那层疏离隔绝的罩幕依然罩着未撤。

最年轻的那位沃洛克人从持碗的长辈手里接过碗，双手合捧着站了半晌，以一种读不出深浅的目光盯住沙恩。随后她朝他走来。桌前的一位伸出了拦阻的手。

===Original===
This time Shann did *not* master his start as he heard the first audible voice which had not been his own. The skull at the left hand on the table, by its yellowed color the oldest of those summoned from the niches, was moving, moving because its jaws gaped and then snapped, emitting a faint bleat which might have been a word or two.

She who would have halted the young Warlockian’s advance, withdrew her hand. Then her fingers curled in an unmistakable beckoning gesture. Shann came to the table, but he could not quite force himself near that chattering skull, even though it had stopped its jig of speech.

===Chinese===
这一回，沙恩听见头一个不属于自己的、真正出声的话音时，*没能*按捺住自己的惊跳。桌子左手边的那颗骷髅——论那焦黄的颜色，是从龛中请出的几颗里最老的——动了，动是因为它的颌骨张开又猛地合上，挤出一声细弱的咩叫，兴许算得一两个词。

方才要拦下年轻沃洛克人前行的那位，收回了手。随即她的手指一屈，做出一个再明白不过的召唤手势。沙恩走到桌前，却怎么也没法逼自己凑近那颗聒噪的骷髅——尽管它那阵蹦跳似的絮语已然停歇。

===Original===
The bowl of sticks was offered to him. Still no message from mind to mind, but he could guess at what they wanted of him. The crystal substance was not cool to the touch as he had expected; rather it was warm, as living flesh might feel. And the colored sticks filled about two thirds of the interior, lying all mixed together without any order.

Shann concentrated on recalling the ceremony the Warlockian had used before the first toss. She had offered the bowl to the skulls in turn. The skulls! But he was no consulter of skulls. Still holding the bowl close to his chest, Shann looked up over the roofless walls at the star map on the roof of the cavern. There, that was Rama; and to its left, just a little above, was Tyr’s system where swung the stark world of his birth, and of which he had only few good memories, but of which he was a part. The Terran raised the bowl to that spot of light which marked Tyr’s pale sun.

===Chinese===
那碗签被递到了他面前。心灵之间依旧没有半点讯息传递，但她们的来意他猜得到。那晶体材质摸上去并不像他预想的那般沁凉，倒是温热的，像活人肌肤的手感。彩色细签盛了碗内约莫三分之二，乱纷纷混作一处，毫无章法。

沙恩凝神回想那位沃洛克人在头一掷之前行过的仪轨。她曾把碗依次奉献给诸骷髅。骷髅！可他绝不是个问卜于骷髅的人。沙恩把碗捧在胸前，抬眼越过无顶的墙，望向洞窟穹顶的星图。那边，那是拉玛（Rama）；它左侧略高一点，是提尔的星系——那里面悬着他出生的那个荒凉世界，他对它没几段好回忆，可他终究是它的一部分。地球人把碗举向那一点标着提尔惨白太阳的光斑。

===Original===
Smiling with a wry twist, he lowered the bowl, and on impulse of pure defiance he offered it to the skull that had chattered. Immediately he realized that the move had had an electric effect upon the aliens. Slowly at first, and then faster, he began to swing the bowl from side to side, the needles slipping, mixing within. And as he swung it, Shann held it out over the expanse of the table.

===Chinese===
他嘴角一拧，苦笑了一下，放下碗，随即出于一股纯粹的挑衅冲动，把碗奉献给那颗聒噪过的骷髅。他立刻意识到，这一手在外星人身上激起了电击般的效果。起初缓慢，随即越来越快，他开始左右摇碗，签针在碗里滑窜、翻混。而一边摇着，沙恩一边把碗伸到了大片的桌面之上。

===Original===
The Warlockian who had given him the bowl was the one who struck it on the bottom, causing a rain of splinters. To Shann’s astonishment, mixed as they had been in the container, they once more formed a pattern, and not the same pattern the Warlockians had consulted earlier. The dampening curtain between them vanished; he was in touch mind to mind once again.

“So be it.” The center Warlockian spread out her four-fingered thumbless hands above the scattered needles. “What is read, is read.”

Again a formula. He caught a chorus of answer from the others.

===Chinese===
当初递碗给他的那位沃洛克人在碗底一叩，落下一阵签雨。令沙恩错愕的是，签在容器里明明混作一团，此刻竟又排成一幅图纹，而且同沃洛克人先前问卜的那幅并不相同。隔在双方之间那道窒闷的帘幕消散了；他重新与她们心意相通。

“就这样吧。”居中的那位沃洛克人对着撒开的签针，摊开她那双四指而无拇指的手。“签已阅，即为定。”

又是一句程式。他听到其余几人齐声应和。

===Original===
“What is read, is read. To the dreamer the dream. Let the dream be known for what it is, and there is life. Let the dream encompass the dreamer falsely, and all is lost.”

===Chinese===
“签已阅，即为定。梦归于做梦之人。认清梦的本来面目，便得生路；任梦虚妄地围裹做梦之人，万事皆休。”

===Original===
“Who can question the wisdom of the Old Ones?” asked their leader. “We are those who read the messages they send, out of their mercy. This is a strange thing they bid us do, man﻿—open for you our own initiates’ road to the veil of illusion. That way has never been for males, who dream without set purpose and have not the ability to know true from false, have not the courage to face their dreams to the truth. Do so﻿—if you can!” There was a flash of mockery in that, combined with something else﻿—stronger than distaste, not as strong as hatred, but certainly not friendly.

===Chinese===
“谁敢质疑古老者的智慧？”她们的首领发问。“我们这些人，不过是解读她们出于垂怜所颁讯息的。她们吩咐我们做的这件事委实古怪，人啊——把我们自己那条通往幻象之幕的入门者之路，向你敞开。那条路从来不是为雄性而设：他们做梦没有定旨，没有分辨真假的能耐，也没有直面己梦、洞见真相的胆量。你且走走看——要是你走得了！”这话里闪过一丝讥诮，还掺着别的什么——比嫌恶强烈，不及仇恨酷烈，但断然说不上友善。

===Original===
She held out her hands and Shann saw now, lying on a slowly closing palm, a disk such as the one Thorvald had shown him. The Terran had only one moment of fear and then came blackness, more absolute than the dark of any night he had ever known.

===Chinese===
她伸出双手，沙恩这才看见，一只正缓缓合拢的手掌上躺着一枚圆盘，同托尔瓦德给他看过的那枚如出一辙。地球人只来得及恐惧一瞬，黑暗便压了下来——比他平生所知的任何一个夜晚都更加彻底的黑暗。

===Original===
Light once more, green light with an odd shimmering quality to it. The skull-lined walls were gone; there were no walls, no building held him. Shann strode forward, and his boots sank in sand, that smooth, satin sand which had ringed the island in the cavern. But he was certain he was no longer on that island, even within that cavern, though far above him there was still a dome of roof.

The source of the green shimmer lay to his left. Somehow he found himself reluctant to turn and face it. That would commit him to action. But Shann turned.

===Chinese===
又是光，绿光，带着一种奇异的摇曳微明。四壁皆骷髅的墙不见了；没有墙，也没有任何建筑圈着他。沙恩大步向前，靴子陷进沙里——正是那种光滑如缎的细沙，先前在洞窟里环着那座岛的便是它。但他确信自己已不在那座岛上，甚至不在那座洞窟里，尽管头顶高远处依然罩着一穹顶盖。

那绿光的源头在他左手边。不知怎的，他发现自己竟不愿转身面对它。那一下转身，便是把自己许给了行动。可沙恩还是转过了身。

===Original===
A veil, a veil of rippling green. Material? No, rather mist or light. A veil depending from some source so far over his head that its origin was hidden in the upper gloom, a veil which was a barrier he must cross.

===Chinese===
一道幕，一道涟漪般漾动的绿幕。是实体么？不，更像雾霭，或光。幕从头顶上极高处的某个源头垂悬而下，源头隐没在上方的幽暗里；这道幕，是他必须跨越的一道屏障。

===Original===
With every nerve protesting, Shann walked forward, unable to keep back. He flung up his arm to protect his face as he marched into that stuff. It was warm, and the gas﻿—if gas it was﻿—left no slick of moisture on his skin in spite of its foggy consistency. And it was no veil or curtain, for although he was already well into the murk, he saw no end to it. Blindly he trudged on, unable to sight anything but the rolling billows of green, pausing now and again to go down on one knee and pat the sand underfoot, reassured at the reality of that footing.

===Chinese===
每一根神经都在抗议，沙恩却仍向前走去，止不住自己。他抢起手臂护住脸，一头迈进那团东西。它是温热的，而那气体——如果它是气体的话——尽管质地如雾，却不在他皮肤上留下半点湿滑的水膜。况且它算不上什么幕或帘，因为他虽已深入这团迷浊好一阵，却望不见它的尽头。他盲目前行，目力所及唯有翻涌的绿浪，间或单膝跪下，拍拍脚下的沙，从那落脚处的实实在在里得回一点安心。

===Original===
And when he met nothing menacing, Shann began to relax. His heart no longer labored; he made no move to draw the stunner or knife. Where he was and for what purpose, he had no idea. But there *was* a purpose in this and that the Warlockians were behind it, he did not doubt. The “initiates’ road,” the leader had said, and the conviction was steady in his mind that he faced some test of alien devising.

A cavern with a green veil﻿—his memory awoke. Thorvald’s dream! Shann paused, trying to remember how the other had described this place. So he was enacting Thorvald’s dream! And could the Survey officer now be caught in Shann’s dream in turn, climbing up somewhere into the nose slit of a skull-shaped mountain?

===Chinese===
而当他没有遇上任何威胁时，沙恩渐渐放松下来。心脏不再沉重地擂动；他也没有去拔击晕枪或刀子。他身在何处、所为何来，他一概不知。但这背后确乎*有*一个目的，而幕后是那些沃洛克人，这一点他不怀疑。“入门者之路”，那首领是这样说的；他心里稳稳确信，自己正面对一场出自外星人手笔的试炼。

一座张着绿幕的洞窟——他的记忆醒了。托尔瓦德的梦！沙恩顿住脚，努力回想对方当时如何描述这个地方。这么说，他正在亲身演绎托尔瓦德的梦！那么那位勘测队军官，会不会此刻正反过来困在沙恩的梦里，在什么地方攀爬着钻进一座骷髅状山岳的鼻缝？

===Original===
Green fog without end, and Shann lost in it. How long had he been here? Shann tried to reckon time, the time since his coming into the water-world of the starred cavern. He realized that he had not eaten, nor drank, nor desired to do so either﻿—nor did he now. Yet he was not weak; in fact, he had never felt such tireless energy as possessed his spare body.

Was this *all* a dream? His threatened drowning in the underground stream a nightmare? Yet there was a pattern in this, just as there had been a pattern in the needles he had spilled across the table. One even led to another with discernible logic; because he had tossed that particular pattern he had come here.

===Chinese===
绿雾无穷无尽，沙恩迷失其中。他到这里有多久了？沙恩试图推算时间，推算自从他进入这座缀星洞窟的水世界以来的光阴。他这才发觉，自己不曾吃过，不曾喝过，也未曾起过吃喝之念——此刻也依然不想。可他并不虚弱；事实上，他那精瘦的身体里，从来不曾灌注过这般不知疲倦的精力。

这一切*全都*是梦么？他在地下河里险些没顶，只是一场噩梦？然而这里面有一个格局，正如他泼撒在桌上的签针也曾布成格局。一个甚至循着可辨的逻辑引出另一个：因为他掷出了那样一幅特定的图纹，他才来到了这里。

===Original===
According to the ambiguous instructions or warnings of the Warlockian witch, his safety in this place would depend upon his ability to tell true dreams from false. But how﻿ ﻿… why? So far he had done nothing except walk through a green fog, and for all he knew, he might well be traveling in circles.

Because there was nothing else to do, Shann walked on, his boots pressing sand, rising from each step with a small sucking sound. Then, as he stooped to search for some indication of a path or road which might guide him, his ears caught the slightest of noises﻿—other small sucking whispers. He was not the only wayfarer in this place!

===Chinese===
按那位沃洛克女巫隐晦的指点或警告，他在此地的安危，将系于他分辨真梦与假梦的本事。可是怎么个分法……又为何要分？迄今为止，他除了穿过一片绿雾什么也没做，而且说不定，他早就在原地兜圈子了。

因为没有别的事可做，沙恩继续走，靴子踏进沙里，每一步拔出时都带起一声轻微的吮响。随后，当他俯身去寻找某种能指引他的小径或道路的迹象时，耳朵捕捉到了一丝最细微的声响——另有细小的吮息窸窣。在这地方赶路的，不止他一个！

## XIII He Who Dreams﻿ ﻿… / XIII 做梦的人……

===Original===
The mist was not a quiet thing; it billowed and curled until it appeared to half-conceal darker shadows, any one of which could be an enemy. Shann remained hunkered on the sand, every sense abnormally alert, watching the fog. He was still sure he could hear sounds which marked the progress of another. What other? One of the Warlockians tracking him to spy? Or was there some prisoner like himself lost out there in the murk? Could it be Thorvald?

Now the sound had ceased. He was not even sure from what direction it had first come. Perhaps that other was listening now, as intent upon locating him. Shann ran his tongue over dry lips. The impulse to call out, to try and contact any fellow traveler here, was strong. Only hard-learned caution kept him silent. He got to his hands and knees, uncertain as to his previous direction.

Shann crept. Someone expecting a man walking erect might be suitably distracted by the arrival of a half-seen figure on all fours. He halted again to listen.
===Chinese===
这团雾可不是什么安分的东西；它翻涌着、卷旋着，仿佛半掩着重重更暗的影子，其中随便哪一团都可能是敌人。沙恩依旧蹲伏在沙地上，每一种感官都异样地警醒，盯望着这片雾。他仍确信自己听得见声响——标记着另一个什么东西行进的声响。另一个什么？是追踪他前来窥探的沃洛克人？还是另有哪个像他一样的俘虏，正迷失在外头那片昏蒙里？会不会是托尔瓦德？

这时那声音已经停息。连它起初是从哪个方向来的，他都没有把握。也许那另一位此刻也在侧耳倾听，同样一心想要定他的位。沙恩用舌尖舔了舔干裂的嘴唇。放声呼喊、试着联络这片迷雾里任何一位同路人的冲动十分强烈，只有吃过苦头才学来的谨慎让他闭紧了嘴。他撑起双手双膝，却连自己先前走的是哪个方向也拿不准了。

沙恩匍匐前进。若是有人守候一个直立行走的人，那么一个影影绰绰、四肢着地爬近的身影，或许正够叫他错愕分神。他又停下谛听。

===Original===
He had been right! The sound of a very muffled footfall or footfalls, carried to his ears. He was sure that the sound was louder, that the unknown was approaching. Shann stood, his hand close to his stunner. He was almost tempted to spray that beam blindly before him, hoping to hit the unseen by chance.

A shadow﻿—something more swift than a shadow, more than one of the tricks the curling fog played on eyes﻿—was moving with purpose and straight for him. Still, prudence restrained Shann from calling out.

The figure grew clearer. A Terran! It could be Thorvald! But remembering how they had last parted, Shann did not hurry to meet him.
===Chinese===
他没听错！一串被捂得死死的足音——也许不止一串——传进他耳中。他确信那声音更响了，那个未知者正在逼近。沙恩站起身来，一只手贴着击晕枪。他几乎要受诱惑，朝身前盲目喷扫一道光束，指望侥幸打中那个看不见的家伙。

一道影子——某种比影子更迅捷的东西，不止是卷旋的雾施给眼睛的又一记把戏——正目标明确地移动着，径直朝他而来。然而谨慎依旧按着沙恩，没让他喊出声。

那身影清晰起来。是个地球人！可能是托尔瓦德！可想起两人上次分手时的情形，沙恩没有急着迎上去。

===Original===
That shadow-shape stretched out a long arm in a sweep as if to pull aside some of the vapor concealing them from each other. Then Shann shivered as if that fog had suddenly turned into the drive of frigid snow. For the mist did roll back so that the two of them stood in an irregular clearing in its midst.

And he did not front Thorvald.
===Chinese===
那个影子似的人形挥出长长的手臂，一扫，仿佛要拨开一层把他们彼此遮住的水汽。随即沙恩打了个寒噤，仿佛那片雾骤然变作了寒雪扑面的劲吹。因为雾当真向后卷开，让两人站进雾心一处不成形状的空场。

而他面对的，不是托尔瓦德。

===Original===
Shann was caught up in the ice grip of an old fear, frozen by it, but somehow clinging to a hope that he did not see the unbelievable.

Those hands drawing the lash of a whip back into striking readiness﻿ ﻿… a brutal nose broken askew, a blaster burn puckering across cheek to misshapen ear﻿ ﻿… that, evil, gloating grin of anticipation. Flick, flick, the slight dance of the lash in a master’s hand as those thick fingers tightened about the stock of the whip. In a moment it would whirl up to lay a ribbon of fire about Shann’s defenceless shoulders.
