Then Logally would laugh and laugh, his sadistic mirth echoed by those other men who played jackals to his rogue lion.
===Chinese===
沙恩被一股旧日恐惧的冰钳攥住，整个人冻在其中，却还死死抓着一丝希望——希望自己看见的并非那难以置信之物。

那双手，正把鞭梢收回到待击的位置……一只凶蛮的鼻子歪断在脸上，一道爆能枪的灼痕皱起，从脸颊一路扯到变形的耳朵……还有那副邪气而志得意满的狞笑。啪，啪，厚实的手指收紧在鞭柄上，鞭梢在行家手里轻轻跳动。转眼它就要抡起，在沙恩毫无遮护的肩头勒上一条火带。然后洛加利（Logally）就会笑啊笑啊，他那施虐的快意，还有那群甘当他这头孤恶狮身边豺狼的男人齐声哄和。

===Original===
Other men﻿ ﻿… Shann shook his head dazedly. But he did not stand again in the Dump-size bar of the Big Strike. And he was no longer a terrorized youngster, fit meat for Logally’s amusement. Only the whip rose, the lash curled out, catching Shann just as it had that time years ago, delivering a red slash of pure agony. But Logally was dead, Shann’s mind screamed, fighting frantically against the evidence of his eyes, of that pain in his chest and shoulder. The Dump bully had been spaced by off-world miners, now also dead, whose claims he had tried to jump out in the Ajax system.
===Chinese===
别的男人……沙恩昏沉沉地摇头。可他并没有重新站进「大矿脉」（Big Strike）那间垃圾场尺寸的酒馆。他也早已不是那个被吓破了胆、正好给洛加利当玩意儿的少年。只是那鞭子还是扬了起来，鞭梢窜出，一如多年前那一回般卷住沙恩，抽下一道纯粹的痛楚烙出的红痕。可洛加利死了，沙恩的头脑在尖叫，发疯似的对抗着眼见的证据，对抗着胸口的疼痛、肩头的疼痛。那个垃圾场的恶霸，早被外星来的矿工们抛进太空处死了；那伙矿工如今也都死了——当年在阿贾克斯星系（Ajax system），洛加利妄图侵占的正是他们的矿权。

===Original===
Logally drew back the lash, preparing to strike again. Shann faced a man five years dead who walked and fought. Or, Shann bit hard upon his lower lip, holding desperately to sane reasoning﻿—did he indeed face anything? Logally was the ancient devil of his boyhood produced anew by the witchery of Warlock. Or had Shann himself been led to recreate both the man and the circumstances of their first meeting with fear as a weapon to pull the creator down? Dream true or false. Logally *was* dead; therefore, this dream was false, it had to be.

The Terran began to walk toward that grinning ogre rising out of his old nightmares. His hand was no longer on the butt of his stunner, but swung loosely at his side. He saw the coming lash, the wicked promise in those small narrowed eyes. This was Logally at the acme of his strength, when he was most to be feared, as he had continued to exist over the years in the depths of a boy-child’s memory. But Logally was *not* alive; only in a dream could he be.
===Chinese===
洛加利收鞭回抽，准备再打。沙恩面对的是一个死了五年、却还能走路还能动手的人。又或者——沙恩狠狠咬住下唇，拼命抓住清醒的推理不放——他当真面对着什么吗？洛加利是他少年时代的老魔鬼，被沃洛克的巫术重新造了出来。又或者，是沙恩自己受人引着，把这个人连同他们初次相遇的情景一并重塑出来，让恐惧充当武器，好把造物者拖垮？「梦」之真伪。洛加利*确实*死了；所以这场梦是假的，只能是假的。

地球人朝那狞笑的食人魔走去——那东西正从他旧日的噩梦里拔身而起。他的手不再搭在击晕枪柄上，只松松垂在身侧。他看见抽来的鞭子，看见那双眯起的小眼里包藏的歹毒许诺。这是洛加利全盛气力时的样子，最令人胆寒的时候的样子，正如这些年来它在一个男孩记忆深处持续存活的那样。可洛加利*不*是活人；只有在梦里，他才能存在。

===Original===
For the second time the lash bit at Shann, curling about his body, to dissolve. There was no alteration in Logally’s grin, His muscular arm drew back as he aimed a third blow. Shann continued to walk forward, bringing up one hand, not to strike at that sweating, bristly jaw, but as if to push the other out of his path. And in his mind he held one thought: this was not Logally; it could not be. Ten years had passed since they had met. And for five of those years Logally had been dead. Here was Warlockian witchery, to be met by sane Terran reasoning.
===Chinese===
鞭子第二次咬上沙恩，卷住他的身子，随即消散。洛加利的狞笑分毫未改，他肌肉虬结的手臂向后一摆，瞄好了第三击。沙恩继续向前走，抬起一只手——不是要打那张汗涔涔、鬃硬须粗的颌，倒像是要把对方从自己路上推开。他心里只守着一个念头：这不是洛加利；不可能是。自他们相遇，十年已经过去。这十年里有五年，洛加利是死人。这是沃洛克的巫术，须以地球人清醒的理智来应战。

===Original===
Shann was alone. The mist, which had formed walls, enclosed him again. But still there was a smarting brand across his shoulder. Shann drew aside the rags of his uniform blouse to discover a welt, raw and red. And seeing that, his unbelief was shaken.

When he had believed in Logally and in Logally’s weapon, the other had had reality enough to strike that blow, make the lash cut deep. But when the Terran had faced the phantom with the truth, then neither Logally nor his lash existed, Shann shivered, trying not to think what might lie before him. Visions out of nightmares which could put on substance! He had dreamed of Logally in the past, many times. And he had had other dreams, just as frightening. Must he front those nightmares, all of them﻿—? Why? To amuse his captors, or to prove their contention that he was a fool to challenge the powers of such mistresses of illusion?
===Chinese===
沙恩又是一个人了。那化作四壁的雾重新围拢住他。可他肩头仍横着一道火辣辣的鞭伤。沙恩扯开军上衣的破布条，看见一道红肿破皮的伤痕。看到它，他的不信动摇了。

当他还信着洛加利、信着洛加利的武器时，对方便有足够的真实来落下那一击，叫鞭子抽得深。而当地球人拿真相直面那幻影，洛加利也好、他的鞭子也好，便都不存在了。沙恩发起抖来，竭力不去想前方还可能横着什么。噩梦里的幻象，居然能披上实体！过去他曾多次梦见洛加利。他还做过别的梦，同样骇人。难道他必须直面那些噩梦，一场不落——？为什么？为了给俘获他的人取乐，还是为了坐实她们的断言——竟敢挑战这等幻术女师的力量，他是个傻瓜？

===Original===
How did they know just what dreams to use in order to break him? Or did he himself furnish the actors and the action, projecting old terrors in this mist as a tri-dee tape projected a story in three dimensions for the amusement of the viewer?

Dream true﻿—was this progress through the mist also a dream? Dreams within dreams﻿ ﻿… Shann put his hand to his head, uncertain, badly shaken. But that stubborn core of determination within him was still holding. Next time he would be prepared at once to face down any resurrected memory.
===Chinese===
她们怎么偏偏知道该用哪些「梦」来击垮他？又或者，是他自己供出了演员和戏码，像三维影像带（tri-dee tape）为观者放出一台三维故事那般，把旧日的恐怖投放在这片雾幕上？

真的「梦」——那他这一路穿雾，会不会也是一场梦？梦中之梦……沙恩抬手按住头，心神无主，震骇至深。但他体内那一粒倔强的决心之核还在撑持。下一回，他要立刻严阵以待，直面任何还魂的记忆并把它压下去。

===Original===
Walking slowly, pausing to listen for the slightest sound which might herald the coming of a new illusion, Shann tried to guess which of his nightmares might come to face him. But he was to learn that there was more than one kind of dream. Steeled against old fears, he was met by another emotion altogether.

There was a fluttering in the air, a little crooning cry which pulled at his heart. Without any conscious thought, Shann held out his hands, whistling on two notes a call which his lips appeared to remember more quickly than his mind. The shape which winged through the fog came straight to his waiting hold, tore at long-walled-away hurt with its once familiar beauty. It flew with a list; one of the delicately tinted wings was injured, had never healed straight. But the seraph nestled into the hollow of Shann’s two palms and looked up at him with all the old liquid trust.
===Chinese===
沙恩慢慢走着，不时驻足谛听，捕捉可能宣告又一重幻象来临的最轻的声息，一面猜想着自己的哪一桩噩梦会前来照面。可他即将明白，「梦」不止一种。他对旧日的恐惧披挂严整，迎头撞上的却是一种全然不同的情绪。

空中起了一阵扑翼的轻响，一声柔柔的咕鸣牵动了他的心。沙恩不假思索伸出双手，用两个音调吹出一声召唤——他的嘴唇忆起那声哨，似乎比他的头脑还快。那穿雾振翅而来的身影径直落进他等候的手掌，用它那久违而熟悉的美好，撕扯着他被高墙久久封存的旧痛。它飞得偏斜；一只着色淡雅的翅膀带着伤，从来没有长直。可这只炽鸟还是偎进沙恩双掌合成的窝里，抬眼望他，满含往日那种水盈盈的信任。

===Original===
“Trav! Trav!” He cradled the tiny creature carefully, regarded with joy its feathered body, the curled plumes on its proudly held head, felt the silken patting of those infinitesimal claws against his protecting fingers.

Shann sat down in the sand, hardly daring to breathe. Trav﻿—again! The wonder of this never-to-be-hoped-for return filled him with a surge of happiness almost too great to bear, which hurt in its way with as great a pain as Logally’s lash; it was a pain rooted in love, not fear and hate.
===Chinese===
“特拉夫！特拉夫！”他小心翼翼把这小东西捧在怀里，满心欢喜地端详它那羽毛的身子、那颗神气昂起的小头上绺绺卷曲的冠羽，感受那对细不可察的小爪丝绸似的、一下下轻拍着他护拢的手指。

沙恩在沙地上坐下来，几乎不敢喘气。特拉夫——又一次！这桩绝不敢奢望的回归带来的奇迹，让他胸中涌起一股大得几乎担不下的幸福；那幸福自有一种疼法，疼起来不亚于洛加利的鞭子；只是那种疼扎在爱里，不在恐惧和憎恨里。

===Original===
Logally’s lash﻿ ﻿…

Shann trembled. Trav raised one of those small claws toward the Terran’s face, crooning a soft caressing cry for recognition, for protection, trying to be a part of Shann’s life once more.

Trav! How could he bear to will Trav into nothingness, to bear to summon up another harsh memory which would sweep Trav away? Trav was the only thing Shann had ever known which he could love wholeheartedly, that had answered his love with a return gift of affection so much greater than the light body he now held.
===Chinese===
洛加利的鞭子……

沙恩颤抖起来。特拉夫朝地球人的脸举起一只小爪，柔声咕鸣，那是一声讨认亲、讨庇护的爱抚之音，它想再一回挤进沙恩的生命里去。

特拉夫！他怎么忍下心，用自己的意志把特拉夫送归虚无？怎么忍下心，再唤起一段会把特拉夫卷走的苦痛记忆？特拉夫是沙恩平生唯一识得的、能让他一心一意去爱的生灵；它回应他的爱，回赠的情意之重，远非他此刻捧着的这具轻飘飘的身子所能比。

===Original===
“Trav!” he whispered softly. Then he made his great effort against this second and far more subtle attack. With the same agony which he had known years earlier, he resolutely summoned a bitter memory, sat nursing once more a broken thing which died in pain he could not ease, aware himself of every moment of that pain. And what was worse, this time there clung that nagging little doubt. What if he had not forced the memory? Perhaps he could have taken Trav with him unhurt, alive, at least for a while.

Shann covered his face with his now empty hands. To see a nightmare flicker out after facing squarely up to its terror, that was no great task. To give up a dream which was part of a lost heaven, that cut cruelly deep. The Terran dragged himself to his feet, drained and weary, stumbling on.

Was there no end to this aimless circling through a world of green smoke? He shambled ahead, moving his feet leadenly. How long had he been here? There was no division in time, just the unchanging light which was a part of the fog through which he plodded.
===Chinese===
“特拉夫！”他低低唤道。随后他奋力迎战这第二次、也远为隐微的攻击。怀着多年前尝过的同一种痛楚，他断然唤起一段苦涩的记忆：再坐一回，守着一个破碎的小东西在无从纾解的疼痛里死去，那疼痛的每一刻他都以身心并受。更糟的是，这一回还黏着一缕挥之不去的小小疑虑。倘若他没有强行唤起那段记忆呢？说不定他本可以带着特拉夫同行，不带伤，活生生的，至少能活上一阵。

沙恩用此刻已空了的双手捂住脸。直面一场噩梦的恐怖，看它噗地熄灭，那算不得什么难事。可要放手一个属于失落天堂的梦，这一刀却砍得又狠又深。地球人拖起身子站起来，像被抽空了一般，疲惫不堪，跌跌撞撞往前走。

在这个绿烟世界里漫无目的地绕圈，难道没有尽头？他拖着脚步往前蹭，两脚沉得像灌了铅。他在这儿多久了？时间没有分划，只有那不变的光——它是他跋涉其中的这片雾的一部分。

===Original===
Then he heard more than any shuffle of foot across sand, any crooning of a long dead seraph, the rising and falling of a voice: a human voice﻿—not quite singing or reciting, but something between the two. Shann paused, searching his memory, a memory which seemed bruised, for the proper answer to match that sound.

But, though he recalled scene after scene out of the years, that voice did not trigger any return from his past. He turned toward its source, dully determined to get over quickly the meeting which lay behind that signal. Only, though he walked on and on, Shann did not appear any closer to the man behind the voice, nor was he able to make out separate words composing that chant, a chant broken now and then by pauses, so that the Terran grew aware of the distress of his fellow prisoner. For the impression that he sought another captive came out of nowhere and grew as he cast wider and wider in his quest.
===Chinese===
随后他听见的声音，超出了任何足底擦沙的窸窣、任何一只死去多年的炽鸟的咕鸣——是一个人声的起伏：人的嗓音——不全是唱，也不全是诵，是介于两者之间的什么东西。沙恩驻足，在记忆里搜寻配得上这声音的答案——那记忆仿佛已经撞得青紫。

可是，尽管他把那些年里的场景一幕幕回想了一遍，那声音却引不出他过去的一丝回响。他转向声源，木然打定主意，快些熬过那声号后头必然等着的那场会面。只是，任凭他走啊走啊，沙恩似乎一点也不见逼近那声音后头的人，也辨不出那吟诵里拼组的字句；那吟唱时时被停顿打断，于是地球人渐渐察觉了他的难友那份惶急。因为他寻觅的本是另一个被囚者——这印象不知从哪儿冒出来，随着他越兜越宽，也越来越坐实。

===Original===
Then he might have turned some invisible corner in the mist, for the chant broke out anew in stronger volume, and now he was able to distinguish words he knew.

> “… where blow the winds between the worlds,
 

 And hang the suns in dark of space.
 

 For Power is given a man to use.
 

 Let him do so well before the last accounting﻿—”
===Chinese===
接着，他像是拐过了雾中一道看不见的拐角，那吟唱重新响起，声音更壮，这回他能分辨出认识的词句了。

> “……世界与世界之间，风在那里吹，
> 众太阳在那里高悬于黑暗的太空。
> 力量既已赋予人来运用，
> 愿他善用不辍，直到最后的清算——”

===Original===
The voice was hoarse, cracked, the words spaced with uneven catches of breath, as if they had been repeated many, many times to provide an anchor against madness, form a tie to reality. And hearing that note, Shann slowed his pace. This was out of no memory of his; he was sure of that.

> “… blow the winds between the worlds,
 

 And hang the suns in﻿ ﻿… dark﻿—of﻿—of﻿—”
===Chinese===
那嗓音嘶哑、破裂，字句间隔着不匀的喘息，仿佛已被念过千遍万遍，好拴下一个对抗疯狂的锚，系成一条拴住现实的绳。听出这一层声气，沙恩放慢了脚步。这声音不出自他的任何记忆；这一点他拿得准。

> “……世界与世界之间，风在那里吹，
> 众太阳在那里高悬于……黑暗——的——的——”

===Original===
That harsh croak of voice was running down, as a clock runs down for lack of winding. Shann sped on, reacting to a plea which did not lay in the words themselves.

Once more the mist curled back, provided him with an open space. A man sat on the sand, his fists buried wrist deep in the smooth grains on either side of his body, his eyes set, red-rimmed, glazed, his body rocking back and forth in time to his labored chant.

> “… the dark of space﻿—”
===Chinese===
那种沙哑的嘶声渐渐泄了劲，像一座忘了上发条的钟越走越慢。沙恩加快脚步——他回应的是一种并不在字句本身里的恳求。

雾再一次卷开，让给他一块空地。一个人坐在沙地上，双拳深埋进身体两侧光溜的沙粒里，直没到手腕；他双眼定定地瞪着，眼圈通红，目光发直，身子随着吃力的吟诵前后摇晃。

> “……黑暗的太空——”

===Original===
“Thorvald!” Shann skidded in the sand, went down on his knees. The manner of their last parting was forgotten as he took in the officer’s condition.

The other did not stop his swaying, but his head turned with a stiff jerk, the gray eyes making a visible effort to focus on Shann. Then some of the strain smoothed out of the gaunt features and Thorvald laughed softly.

“Garth!”
===Chinese===
“托尔瓦德！”沙恩在沙里打了个趔趄，跪倒下去。看清军官眼下的状态，两人上回分手那一幕早被他丢在了脑后。

对方没有停下摇晃，脑袋却僵僵地一扭转过来，那双灰眼睛明显费着劲，才把焦点聚到沙恩身上。随即，那清瘦面容上绷紧的神色平复了几分，托尔瓦德轻轻笑了。

“加斯！”

===Original===
Shann stiffened but had no chance to protest that mistaken identification as the other continued: “So you made class one status, boy! I always knew you could if you’d work for it. A couple of black marks on your record, sure. But those can be rubbed out, boy, when you’re willing to try. Thorvalds always have been Survey. Our father would have been proud.”

Thorvald’s voice flattened, his smile faded, there was a growing spark of some emotion in those gray eyes. Unexpectedly, he hurled himself forward, his hands clawing for Shann’s throat. He bore the younger man down under him to the sand where Lantee found himself fighting desperately for his life against a man who could only be mad.
===Chinese===
沙恩僵住了，却来不及抗议这认错了人的称呼，对方自顾自说了下去：“这么说你挣上一级评衔了，小子！我一向知道你行，只要你肯下力气。档案上记着两回过，这不假。可那些都能擦掉，小子，只要你肯来争气。托尔瓦德家的人，祖祖辈辈都是勘测队。咱们的父亲知道了，会骄傲的。”

托尔瓦德的声音平板下来，笑意淡去，那双灰眼睛里某种情绪的火星越蹿越高。冷不防地，他整个人猛扑上前，双手抓挠着探向沙恩的咽喉。他把年轻人压翻在身下的沙地里，兰蒂发觉自己在拼死求生，对手只能是个疯子。

===Original===
Shann used a trick learned on the Dumps, and his opponent doubled up with a gasp of agony to let the younger man break free. He planted a knee on the small of Thorvald’s back, digging the officer into the sand, pinning down his arms in spite of the other’s struggles. Regaining his own breath in gulps, Shann tried to appeal to some spark of reason in the other.

“Thorvald! This is Lantee﻿—Lantee﻿—” His name echoed in the mist-walled void like an unhuman wail.

“Lantee﻿—? No, Throg! Lantee﻿—Throg﻿—killed my brother!”
===Chinese===
沙恩使出在垃圾场学来的一招，对手痛得倒抽一口气蜷起身子，年轻人趁势脱身。他一膝顶在托尔瓦德后腰上，把军官摁进沙里，任对方怎么挣扎也钉死他的双臂。沙恩大口大口把气喘匀，试着唤醒对方心里残存的一点理智火花。

“托尔瓦德！我是兰蒂——兰蒂——”他的名字在雾墙围成的虚空里回荡，像一声非人的哀号。

“兰蒂——？不，斯罗格！兰蒂——斯罗格——杀了我弟弟！”

===Original===
Sand puffed out with the breath, which expelled that indictment. But Thorvald no longer fought, and Shann believed him close to collapse.

Shann relaxed his hold, rolling the other man over. Thorvald obeyed his pull limply, lying face upward, sand in his hair and eyebrows, crusting his slack lips. The younger man brushed the dirt away gently as the other opened his eyes to regard Shann with his old impersonal stare.
===Chinese===
随着吐出这句控诉的那口气，沙粒扑地扬起。但托尔瓦德不再挣扎，沙恩看他眼看就要垮了。

沙恩松开钳制，把对方翻过身来。托尔瓦德软软地由他摆布，仰面躺着，头发里眉毛里全是沙，松弛的嘴唇上也结了一层。年轻人轻轻替他拭去那些沙土，这时对方睁开眼，用他惯常那种不带私意的注视望着沙恩。

===Original===
“You’re alive,” Thorvald stated bleakly. “Garth’s dead. You ought to be dead too.”

Shann drew back, rubbed sand from his hands, his concern dampened by the other’s patent hostility. Only that angry accusation vanished in a blink of those gray eyes. Then there was a warmer recognition in Thorvald’s expression.

“Lantee!” The younger man might just have come into sight. “What are you doing here?”
===Chinese===
“你还活着。”托尔瓦德冷冷地说。“加斯死了。你也本该死了。”

沙恩缩回身，搓掉手上的沙，对方那份明摆着的敌意，浇熄了他几分关切。只不过那怒气冲冲的指控，在灰眼睛一眨之间消散无踪。随即，托尔瓦德的神情里浮起暖意，认出了眼前人。

“兰蒂！”那口气，仿佛年轻人方才走进他的视野。“你在这儿做什么？”

===Original===
Shann tightened his belt. “Just about what you are.” He was still aloof, giving no acknowledgment of difference in rank now. “Running around in this fog hunting the way out.”

Thorvald sat up, surveying the billowing walls of the hole which contained them. Then he reached out a hand to draw fingers down Shann’s forearm.

“You *are* real,” he observed simply, and his voice was warm, welcoming.
===Chinese===
沙恩紧了紧腰带。“跟你做的差不多是同一件事。”他还是淡淡的，此刻丝毫不认什么军衔高下。“在这片雾里转来转去，找一条出去的路。”

托尔瓦德坐起来，打量困住他们的这个坑洞四壁翻涌的雾墙。随后他伸出一只手，手指顺着沙恩的前臂抚下来。

“你是*真*的。”他只平平说了一句，嗓音里透着暖意和欢迎。

===Original===
“Don’t bet on it,” Shann snapped. “The unreal can be mighty real﻿—here.” His hand went up to the smarting brand on his shoulder.

Thorvald nodded. “Masters of illusion,” he murmured.

“Mistresses,” Shann corrected. “This place is run by a gang of pretty smart witches.”
===Chinese===
“这可押不得宝。”沙恩没好气地回。“在这儿，虚的东西能实在得很。”他抬手按上肩头那道灼痛的鞭痕。

托尔瓦德点头。“幻术大师。”他喃喃道。

“是女师。”沙恩纠正。“这地方是一帮精明得很的女巫在当家。”

===Original===
“Witches? You’ve seen them? Where? And what﻿—who are they?” Thorvald pounced with a return of his old-time sharpness.

“They’re females right enough, and they can make the impossible happen. I’d say that classifies them as witches. One of them tried to take me over back on the island. I set a trap and caught her; then somehow she transported me﻿—” Swiftly he outlined the chain of events leading from his sudden awakening in the river tunnel to his present penetration of this fog-world.
===Chinese===
“女巫？你见过她们？在哪儿？还有，她们是什么——什么人？”托尔瓦德扑上来追问，昔日的锋锐又回来了。

“是雌的，千真万确，而且她们能让不可能的事发生。依我说，单凭这一条就该把她们归成女巫。在岛上的时候，她们中间有一个想接管我。我设下陷阱拿住了她；后来不知怎么，她把我传送了过来——”他三言两语，把从自己在那条水道中猛然醒来到此刻闯进这片雾世界的一连串事由，勾勒了一遍。

===Original===
Thorvald listened eagerly. When the story was finished, he rubbed his hands across his drawn face, smearing away the last of the sand. “At least you have some idea of who they are and a suggestion of how you got here. I don’t remember that much about my own arrival. As far as I can remember I went to sleep on the Island and woke up here!”

Shann studied him and knew that Thorvald was telling the truth. He could remember nothing of his departure in the outrigger, the way he had fought Shann in the lagoon. The Survey officer must have been under the control of the Warlockians then. Quickly he gave the older man his version of the other’s actions in the outer world and Thorvald was clearly astounded, though he did not question the facts Shann presented.

“They just *took* me!” Thorvald said in a husky half whisper. “But why? And why are we here? Is this a prison?”
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德听得急切。故事讲完，他双手抹了一把紧绷的脸，把最后一点沙蹭掉。“至少你知道她们是谁，也摸到了自己是怎样到这儿来的头绪。我自己怎么来的，记得的没这么多。就我所能记得的：我在那座岛上睡着了，一觉醒来就在这儿！”

沙恩端详他，知道托尔瓦德说的是实话。他记不得自己乘舷外支架独木舟离去的那一幕，也记不得他在潟湖里同沙恩恶斗的那一场。那位勘测队军官当时必定处在沃洛克人的控制之下。沙恩很快把自己在外界所见对方的一举一动讲给这位年长者听，托尔瓦德显然惊愕不已，但对沙恩摆出的事实并无二话。

“他们就这么把我*掳*来了！”托尔瓦德声音沙哑，半是耳语。“可为什么？我们又为什么在这儿？这是座监狱吗？”

===Original===
Shann shook his head. “I think all this”﻿—a wave of his hand encompassed the green wall, what lay beyond it, and in it﻿—“is a test of some kind. This dream business﻿ ﻿… A little while ago I got to thinking that I wasn’t here at all, that I might be dreaming it all. Then I met you.”

Thorvald understood. “Yes, but this *could* be a dream meeting. How can we tell?” He hesitated, almost diffidently, before he asked: “Have you met anyone else here?”

“Yes.” Shann had no desire to go into that.
===Chinese===
沙恩摇头。“依我看，这一切”——他挥手一挥，把那道绿墙、墙外的天地、墙里的乾坤都圈了进去——“是某种考验。这做梦的名堂……方才有一阵，我都开始琢磨，我根本就不在这儿，这一切兴许全是我做的一场梦。然后我就遇见了你。”

托尔瓦德懂了。“不错，可这场会面*也*可能是一场梦。咱们怎么分辨？”他迟疑了一下，几乎有些踌躇，才问：“在这儿，你还遇见过别的人吗？”

“遇见过。”沙恩无意细谈。

===Original===
“People out of your past life?”

“Yes.” Again he did not elaborate.

“So did I.” Thorvald’s expression was bleak; his encounters in the fog must have proved no more pleasant than Shann’s. “That suggests that we do trigger the hallucinations ourselves. But maybe we can really lick it now.”
===Chinese===
“从你过去日子里来的人？”

“是。”他又一次没有细说。

“我也遇见过。”托尔瓦德的神情冷了下来；他在雾中的遭遇，想必不比沙恩的好受多少。“这说明，那些幻象确是我们自己引出来的。不过也许这一回，咱们真能治住它。”

===Original===
“How?”

“Well, if these phantoms are born of our memories there are about only two or three we could see together﻿—maybe a Throg on the rampage, or that hound we left back in the mountains. And if we do sight anything like that, we’ll know what it is. On the other hand, if we stick together and one of us sees something that the other can’t﻿ ﻿… well, that fact alone will explode the ghost.”
===Chinese===
“怎么治？”

“这么说吧：倘若这些幽灵是从咱们的记忆里生出来的，那么咱们俩能一同看见的，拢共不过两三样——兴许是一个撒开野的斯罗格人，或是咱们撇在山里的那条“猎犬”。真撞见那样的东西，咱们也知道它是什么。反过来，要是咱们守在一处，其中一个看见了什么，另一个却看不见……嘿，光这一条，就够把鬼炸穿。”

===Original===
There was sense in what he said. Shann aided the officer to his feet.

“I must be a better subject for their experiments than you,” the older man remarked ruefully. “They took me over completely at the first.”

“You were carrying that disk,” Shann pointed out. “Maybe that acted as a focusing lens for whatever power they use to make us play trained animals.”
===Chinese===
他这话在理。沙恩扶军官起身。

“拿我给他们做实验，准是比你合适。”年长者自嘲道。“一上来，他们就把我整个儿接管了过去。”

“你当时带着那块圆盘。”沙恩点出来。“不管她们用来驱着咱们演驯兽把戏的是什么力量，那圆盘说不定就充当了一面聚光的透镜。”

===Original===
“Could be!” Thorvald brought out the cloth-wrapped bone coin. “I still have it.” But he made no move to pull off the bit of rag about it. “Now”﻿—he gazed at the wall of green﻿—“which way?”

Shann shrugged. Long ago he had lost any idea of keeping a straight course through the murk. He might have turned around any number of times since he first walked blindly into this place. Then he pointed to the packet Thorvald held.

“Why not flip that?” he asked. “Heads, we go that way﻿—” he indicated the direction in which they were facing﻿—“tails, we do a rightabout-face.”
===Chinese===
“很可能！”托尔瓦德掏出那块布裹的骨圆牌。“我还带着呢。”但他没有动手去解缠在外头的那圈破布。“现在”——他凝望那道绿墙——“往哪边走？”

沙恩耸耸肩。早在先前，他就丢掉了在这片昏蒙里走直线的任何盘算。自打头一回瞎闯进这地方，他兴许已经掉头过不知多少回。随后他指向托尔瓦德捧着的那个小包。

“干吗不掷它一试？”他问。“正面，咱们走这边——”他指了指两人正对着的方向——“反面，咱们来个向后转。”

===Original===
There was an answering grin on Thorvald’s lips. “As good a guide as any we’re likely to find here. We’ll do it.” He pulled away the twist of cloth and with a swift snap, reminiscent of that used by the Warlockian witch to empty the bowl of sticks, he tossed the disk into the air.

It spun, whirled, but﻿—to their open-jawed amazement﻿—it did not fall to the sand. Instead it spun until it looked like a small globe instead of a disk. And it lost its dead white for a glow of green. When that glow became dazzling for Terran eyes the miniature sun swung out, not in orbit but in straight line of flight, heading to their right.

With a muffled cry, Thorvald started in pursuit, Shann running beside him. They were in a tunnel of the fog now, and the pace set by the spinning coin was swift. The Terrans continued to follow it at the best pace they could summon, having no idea of where they were headed, but each with the hope that they finally did have a guide to lead them through this place of confusion and into a sane world where they could face on more equal terms those who had sent them there.
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德唇上回了一笑。“在这儿能指望找着的向导里，这个不比哪个差。就这么办。”他扯掉缠布，手腕干脆一抖——那一抖，令人想起沃洛克女巫倾倒碗中细签的那一叩——把圆牌抛上了半空。

它旋转着、回旋着，可是——两人看得目瞪口呆——它没有落向沙地。它越转越急，直到看上去不再是圆盘，而成了一只小球。它褪去死白色，换上一团绿光。待那绿光亮得叫地球人眼花，这轮小太阳便荡了出去——不入轨道绕行，而是循着笔直的航线，朝他们右方飞去。

托尔瓦德闷哼一声，拔脚就追，沙恩在他身侧并肩疾奔。此刻他们正走在雾的一条隧道里，那枚旋飞的圆牌定下的步子迅疾。两个地球人拿出全副脚力紧追不舍，全然不知前路何方，心里却各怀着同一个指望：他们总算有了一位向导，能领他们走出这片迷乱之地，进入一个神志清明的世界——在那里，同把她们送进这地方的那些角色对垒时，他们总算能争得几分势均力敌。

## XIV Escape / XIV 逃亡

===Original===
“Something ahead!” Thorvald did not slacken the pace set by the brilliant spot of green they trailed. Both of the Terrans feared to fall behind, to lose touch with that guide. Their belief that somehow the traveling disk would bring them to the end of the mist and its attendant illusions had grown firmer with every foot of ground they traversed.

A dark, fixed point, now partly veiled by mist, lay beyond, and it was toward that looming half-shadow that the spinning disk hurtled. Now the mist curled away to display its bulk﻿—larger, blacker and four or five times Thorvald’s height. Both men stopped short, for the disk no longer played pathfinder. It still whirled on its axis in the air, faster and faster, until it appeared to be throwing off sparks, but the sparks faded against a monolith of dark rock unlike the native stone they had seen elsewhere. For it was neither red nor warmly brown, but a dull, dead black. It could have been a huge stone slab, trimmed, smoothed, set up on end as a monument or marker, except that only infinite labor could have accomplished such a task, and there was no valid reason for such toil as far as the Terrans could perceive.
===Chinese===
“前面有东西！”托尔瓦德没有放慢步子——这步子，是照着他们追踪的那一团亮绿光定下的。两个地球人都怕落到后头，怕同那位向导失散。他们相信那只游动的圆盘终归会把自己领到这片雾、连同随之俱来的种种幻象的尽头；脚下的地每走过一尺，这信念便坚定一分。

前方卧着一个黑黢黢的定点，此刻半掩在雾中，那只旋转的圆盘正是朝那团隐隐迫近的半影直扑过去。雾随即卷开，露出它的全貌——比托尔瓦德高出四五倍，更大，更黑。两人猛地收步，因为圆盘不再领路了。它仍在空中绕着轴心回旋，越转越快，直到仿佛迸出点点火花；可那些火花落在一方暗色岩石的独石上便黯然失色——这石头同他们别处见过的本地岩层全然不同：既非红色，也非暖褐，而是一种死气沉沉的哑光黑。它本可以是一块巨大的石板，经修琢、磨平，竖立起来当作纪念碑或界标——只可惜那样一桩工程非无穷无尽的劳力不能成就，而在地球人看来，又实在想不出什么站得住的理由值得下这份苦功。

===Original===
“This is it.” Thorvald moved closer.

By the disk’s action, they deduced that their guide had drawn them to this featureless black steel with the precision of a beam-controlled ship. However, the purpose still eluded them. They had hoped for some exit from the territory of the veil, but now they faced a solid slab of dark stone, neither a conventional exit or entrance, as they proved by circling its base. Beneath their boots was the eternal sand, around them the fog.

“Now what?” Shann asked. They had made their trip about the slab and were back again where the disk whirled with unceasing vigor in a shower of emerald sparks.
===Chinese===
“就是这儿了。”托尔瓦德凑近了些。

从圆盘的举动推断，那位向导把他们引到这块毫无特征的黑色钢材跟前，精准得不亚于一艘波束制导的飞船。可此行的用意依旧叫人捉摸不透。他们本来盼着能寻到「幻象之幕」领地的某个出口，迎面撞上的却是一整块严丝合缝的暗色石板——他们绕着底座转了一圈，证明它既非寻常的出口，也非入口。靴底是那片亘古如斯的沙地，四周还是雾。

“现在怎么办？”沙恩问。两人已绕着石板走过一遭，又回到了原地——那只圆盘仍在一蓬翡翠色的火花里劲头不减地回旋。

===Original===
Thorvald shook his head, scanning the rock face before them glumly. The eagerness had gone out of his expression, a vast weariness replacing it.

“There must have been some purpose in coming here,” he replied, but his tone had lost the assurance of moments earlier.

“Well, if we strike away from here, we’ll just get right back in again.” Shann waved a hand toward the mist, waiting as if with a hunter’s watch upon them. “And we certainly can’t go down.” He dug a boot toe into the sand to demonstrate the folly of that. “So, what about up?”
===Chinese===
托尔瓦德摇了摇头，闷闷地打量面前那面石壁。他脸上的热切已经褪尽，代之而起的是一片深重的倦怠。

“到这儿来总该有个缘故。”他答道，语气里却丢了片刻之前的那份笃定。

“唉，要是从这儿硬闯出去，兜个圈子还是得栽回这雾里。”沙恩朝那片雾挥了挥手——那雾正候着，仿佛猎人一般守望着他们。“往下走是万万不能的。”他把靴尖往沙地里一插，示意那念头何等荒唐。“那么——往上呢？”

===Original===
He ducked under the spinning disk to lay his hands against the surface of the giant slab. And in so doing he made a discovery, revealed to his touch although hidden from sight. For his fingers, running aimlessly across the cold, slightly uneven surface of the stone, slipped into a hollow, quite a deep hollow.

Excited, half fearing that his sudden guess might be wrong, Shann slid his hand higher in line with that hollow, to discover a second. The first had been level with his chest, the second perhaps eighteen inches or so above. He jumped, to draw his fingers down the rock, with damage to his nails but getting his proof. There *was* a third niche, deep enough to hold more than just the toe of a boot, and a fourth above that﻿ ﻿…
===Chinese===
他矮身钻过旋转的圆盘，把双手贴上那块巨石的表面。这一贴便有了个发现——眼睛看不见，触觉却泄露了出来。他的指头在那冰冷而微有不平的石面上漫无目的地游走，忽然滑进了一个凹坑，一个相当深的凹坑。

沙恩心头一热，又半怕这突如其来的猜测落了空，便循着那凹坑的直线把手往上挪，果然又摸到一个。第一个齐着他胸口，第二个约莫高出十八英寸上下。他纵身一跳，手指贴着岩石一路往下捋，指甲都刮坏了，却拿到了实证。第三个凹穴*确实*在，深得足以容纳不止一只靴尖；再往上，还有第四个……

===Original===
“We’ve a ladder of sorts here,” he reported. Without waiting for any answer from Thorvald, Shann began to climb. The holds were so well matched in shape and size that he was sure they could not be natural; they had been bored there for use﻿—the use to which he was now putting them﻿—a ladder to the top of the slab. Though what he might find there was beyond his power to imagine.

The disk did not rise. Shann passed that core of light, climbing above it into the greater gloom. But the holes did not fail him; each was waiting in a direct line with its companion. And to an active man the scramble was not difficult. He reached the summit, glanced around, and made a quick grab for a secure handhold.
===Chinese===
“这儿好歹有架梯子了。”他报告道。不等托尔瓦德答话，沙恩便攀了上去。那些着力点形状大小匀称划一，他敢断定绝非天然生成；那是凿出来派用场的——正是他眼下派上的这个用场——一条通往石板顶端的梯子。至于顶上会有些什么，他连想也无从想起。

圆盘没有升上来。沙恩越过那团光核，攀到它上方更浓的幽暗里。但那些凿孔没有一环失信于他；每一只都在与同伴连成的直线上候着他。对一个身手矫健的人来说，这段攀爬算不得难。他登上了顶端，四下一望，慌忙一把抓牢一个稳当的把手。

===Original===
Waiting for him was no level platform such as he had confidently expected to find. The surface up which he had just made his way fly-fashion was the outer wall of a well or chimney. He looked down now into a pit where black nothingness began within a yard of the top, for the radiance of the mist did not penetrate far into that descent.

Shann fought an attack of giddiness. It would be very easy to lose control, to tumble over and be swallowed up in what might well be a bottomless chasm. And what was the purpose of this well? Was it a trap to entice a prisoner into an unwary climb and then let gravity drag him over? The whole setup was meaningless. Perhaps meaningless only to him, Shann conceded, with a flash of level thinking. The situation could be quite different as far as the natives were concerned. This structure did have a reason, or it would never have been erected in the first place.
===Chinese===
候着他的，并不是他满有把握指望找到的那种平整台面。他方才飞虫一般攀上来的这道面，是一口井——或者说一道烟囱——的外壁。此刻他俯望下去，只见一个深坑，距坑口不出一码，黑色的虚无便已开始，因为雾的辉光透不进那下降的深处多远。

沙恩竭力压下一阵晕眩。一个失控栽下去、叫这多半无底的深渊吞没，实在是太容易了。可这口井究竟是做什么用的？它是个陷阱，专诱俘虏失着攀上来，再让重力把他拖下去？整套布置全无意义可言。也许只是对他而言没有意义——沙恩心头冷静地一闪念，认了这一点。在土著那头，情形或许全然不同。这构造自有它的道理，否则当初根本不会被立起来。

===Original===
“What’s the matter?” Thorvald’s voice was rough with impatience.

“This thing’s a well.” Shann edged about a fraction to call back. “The inside is open and﻿—as far as I can tell﻿—goes clear to the planet’s core.”

“Ladder on the inside too?”
===Chinese===
“怎么回事？”托尔瓦德的嗓音粗厉，透着不耐。

“这东西是口井。”沙恩侧身挪了一小段，回头喊道。“里头是空的，而且——就我判断——一直通到星球的地心。”

“里面也有梯子吗？”

===Original===
Shann squirmed. That was, of course, a very obvious supposition. He kept a tight hold with his left hand, and with the other, he did some exploring. Yes, here was a hollow right enough, twin to those on the outside. But to swing over that narrow edge of safety and begin a descent into the black of the well was far harder than any action he had taken since the morning the Throgs had raided the camp. The green mist could hold no terrors greater than those with which his imagination peopled the depths now waiting to engulf him. But Shann swung over, fitted his boot into the first hollow, and started down.

The only encouragement he gained during that nightmare ordeal was that those holes were regularly spaced. But somehow his confidence did not feed on that fact. There always remained the nagging fear that when he searched for the next it would not be there and he would cling to his perch lacking the needful strength in aching arms and legs to reclimb the inside ladder.
===Chinese===
沙恩局促地扭了一下。这当然是个再明显不过的推断。他左手死死抓牢，用另一只手探了探。不错，这儿果真有个凹坑，同外面那些一模一样。可是要翻过那道窄窄的安全边缘、开始朝井里的黑暗降下去，比斯罗格人袭营地那个清晨以来他做过的任何一件事都难得多。绿雾里纵有千般恐怖，也及不上他想象塞进这片正等着吞噬他的幽深里的种种可怖。但沙恩还是翻了过去，把靴子嵌进第一个凹坑，向下去了。

在那场噩梦般的煎熬里，他得到的唯一宽慰，是那些凿孔间距规整如一。可不知怎么，他的胆气偏偏壮大不起来。心底总盘着那桩磨人的怕：等他摸找下一个孔时，它偏偏不在了，而他吊在原地，酸痛的手臂和双腿再也挤不出力气，沿这内壁的梯子重新攀上去。

===Original===
He was fast losing that sense of well being which had been his during his travels through the fog; a fatigue tugged at his arms and weighed leaden on his shoulders. Mechanically he prospected for the next hold, and then the next. Above, the oblong of half-light grew smaller and smaller, sometimes half blotted out by the movements of Thorvald’s body as the other followed him down that interior way.

How far *was* down? Shann giggled lightheadedly at the humor of that, or what seemed to be humor at the moment. He was certain that they were now below the level of the sand floor outside the slab. And yet no end had come to the well hollow.

No break of light down here; he might have been sightless. But just as the blind develop an extra perceptive sense of unseen obstacles, so did Shann now find that he was aware of a change in the nature of the space about him. His weary arms and legs held him against the solidity of a wall, yet the impression that there was no longer another wall at his back grew stronger with every niche which swung him downward. And he was as sure as if he could see it, that he was now in a wide-open space, another cavern; perhaps, but this one totally dark.
===Chinese===
行经雾中时一路伴着他的那份安适，正飞快地离他而去；一股疲乏拽着他的手臂，铅一般压上他的双肩。他机械地摸索下一个着力点，再下一个。头顶上，那方半明半亮的长方形越缩越小，有时被托尔瓦德的身影遮去一半——后者正顺着这条内里的路跟在他后面下来。

“底下”究竟*有多深*？沙恩头昏脑涨地嗤笑出声，笑这话里的滑稽——或者说，此刻他觉出的滑稽。他敢肯定，他们早已下到石板外那片沙地地面以下了。然而井穴仍不见底。

这下面没有一丝透光的缝隙；他简直同盲人无异。但正像盲人会练出一种感知无形障碍的额外知觉，沙恩此刻也发觉，自己察觉出了周遭空间性质的变化。他疲乏的手臂和双腿撑着他贴住一面实墙，然而背后已没有另一面墙的印象，随着每一个载他向下的凹穴越来越强。他确信得如同亲眼看见：自己此刻身处一片开阔的空间——兴许又是一处洞窟，只是这一处全然漆黑。

===Original===
Deprived of sight, he relied upon his ears. And there was a sound, faint, distorted perhaps by the acoustics of this place, but keeping up a continuous murmur. Water! Not the wash of waves with their persistent beat, but rather the rippling of a running stream. Water must lie below!

And just as his weariness had grown with his leaving behind the fog, so now did both hunger and thirst gnaw at Shann, all the sharper for the delay. The Terran wanted to reach that water, could picture it in his mind, putting away the possibility﻿—the probability﻿—that it might be sea-born and salt, and so unfit to drink.
===Chinese===
视觉既失，他便倚仗耳朵。果然有一线声响——很轻，也许被此地的回声扭曲了，却持续不断地低语着。水！不是浪头那执拗节拍的冲刷，而是一道奔流的潺潺。下面必定有水！

正像他的疲惫是随着把雾抛在身后而步步加深的，此刻饥与渴也一齐啃噬起沙恩来，因这一耽搁而愈发火辣。地球人渴望够到那片水，能在脑子里描出它的模样，一面把那可能性——那十有八九——推到一边不去想：它或许源出海水、又咸又涩，根本没法入口。

===Original===
The upper opening to the cavern of the fog was now so far above him that he had to strain to see it. And that warmth which had been there was gone. A dank chill wrapped him here, dampened the holds to which he clung until he was afraid of slipping. While the murmur of the water grew louder, until its *slap-slap* sounded within arms’ distance. His boot toe skidded from a niche. Shann fought to hold on with numbed fingers. The other foot went. He swung by his hands, kicking vainly to regain a measure of footing.

Then his arms could no longer support him, and he cried out as he fell. Water closed about him with an icy shock which for a moment paralyzed him. He flailed out, fighting the flood to get his head above the surface where he could gasp in precious gulps of air.
===Chinese===
雾之洞窟顶上那道开口，如今已远在他头顶，须得使劲仰看才望得见。先前在那上头的暖意也没有了。这里一股阴湿的寒气裹住他，把他紧攀的把手浸得湿滑，叫他直怕脱手。而水声的低语越来越响，直到那*啪嗒、啪嗒*的声响已近在一臂之内。他的靴尖从一个凹穴里滑了出去。沙恩用冻麻的手指拼命撑住。另一只脚也脱了。他凭双手悬在半空，徒劳地蹬着腿，想再争回一点立足之地。

接着，手臂再也撑不住了，他喊了一声便坠了下去。水四面合拢过来，冰冷的一激，刹那间把他冻得瘫痪。他拼命扑腾，同洪水搏斗，总算把头探出水面，大口大口喘着宝贵的空气。

===Original===
There was a current here, a swiftly running one. Shann remembered the one which had carried him into that cavern in which the Warlockians had their strange dwelling. Although there were no clusters of crystals in this tunnel to supply him with light, the Terran began to nourish a faint hope that he was again in that same stream, that those light crystals would appear, and that he might eventually return to the starting point of this meaningless journey.

So he strove only to keep his head above water. Hearing a splashing behind him, he called out: “Thorvald?”

“Lantee?” The answer came back at once; the splashing grew louder as the other swam to catch up.
===Chinese===
这里有一股水流，一股湍急的水流。沙恩想起先前那一条——正是它把他冲进了沃洛克人安着奇怪居所的那座洞窟。虽然这条隧道里没有成簇的水晶给他照明，地球人心里却渐渐养出一星微弱的指望：也许又是那条水流，也许那些发光的水晶就会出现，也许他到头来能回到这段毫无意义的旅程的起点。

于是他只求脑袋不没进水里。听见身后一阵泼溅声，他扬声喊道：“托尔瓦德？”

“兰蒂？”应声立刻传了回来；那泼溅声越来越响，是同伴奋力游来追赶。

===Original===
Shann swallowed a mouthful of the water lapping against his chin. The taste was brackish, but not entirely salt, and though it stung his lips, the liquid relieved a measure of his thirst.

Only no glowing crystals appeared to stud these walls, and Shann’s hope that they were on their way to the cavern of the island faded. The current grew swifter, and he had to fight to keep his head above water, his tired body reacting sluggishly to commands.

The murmur of the racing flood drummed louder in his ears, or was that sound the same? He could no longer be sure. Shann only knew that it was close to impossible to snatch the necessary breath as he was rolled over and over in the hurrying flood.
===Chinese===
沙恩咽下一口拍打着他下巴的水。味道微咸带涩，倒不算全咸；虽然蜇得嘴唇生疼，这液体总算解了他几分渴。

只是这些壁上始终不见发光的水晶点缀，沙恩那点“正游向岛上洞窟”的希望便淡了下去。水流越发湍急，他不得不苦苦挣扎才能把头保持在水面之上，疲惫的身体对号令的反应已迟钝不堪。

奔腾的洪水在耳中擂得越来越响——还是说那声响根本没有变？他已经拿不准了。沙恩只知道，在急流里被翻过来滚过去，想抢足一口气都近乎不能。

===Original===
In the end he was ejected into blazing, blinding light, into a suffocation of wild water as the bullet in an ancient Terran rifle might have been fired at no specific target. Gasping, beaten, more than half-drowned, Shann was pummeled by waves, literally driven up on a rocky surface which skinned his body cruelly. He lay there, his arms moving feebly until he contrived to raise himself in time to be wretchedly sick. Somehow he crawled on a few feet farther before he subsided again, blinded by the light, flinching from the heat of the rocks on which he lay, but unable to do more for himself.

His first coherent thought was that his speculation concerning the reality of this experience was at last resolved. This could not possibly be an hallucination; at least this particular sequence of events was not. And he was still hazily considering that when a hand fell on his shoulder, fingers biting into his raw flesh.
===Chinese===
到最后，他被抛进一片灼目炫神的光里，抛进一汪憋闷的狂澜——就像老式地球步枪里射出的一颗子弹，本没有射向什么确定的目标。沙恩喘息着、溃败着、淹得半死，被浪头一记记捶打，硬生生推上一片岩石的表面，那岩石毫不留情地磨他的皮肉。他躺在那里，手臂软弱地划动，好不容易才撑起身子，来得及凄凄惨惨地呕吐了一场。他不知打哪儿又挤出一股劲，往前爬了几步，才重新瘫倒下去——光晃得他睁不开眼，身下岩石的烫灼得他直抽缩，可他再也无力多照管自己一分了。

他第一个成形的念头是：关于这段经历是真是幻的揣测，总算有了答案。这不可能是幻觉；至少眼下这一连串变故不是。他还恍恍惚惚地想着这个，一只手落上了他的肩头，指头抠进他破了皮的肉里。

===Original===
Shann snarled, rolled over on his side. Thorvald, water dripping from his rags﻿—or rather steaming from them﻿—his shaggy hair plastered to his skull, sat there.

“You all right?”

Shann sat up in turn, shielding his smarting eyes. He was bruised, battered badly enough, but he could claim no major injuries.
===Chinese===
沙恩低吼一声，翻身侧过。托尔瓦德坐在旁边，破衣烂衫上的水直往下滴——不如说直往上冒汽——一头蓬乱的头发紧贴在脑壳上。

“你没事吧？”

沙恩也坐起身，遮着刺痛的双眼。他遍体青肿，摔打得不轻，但说不上有什么大伤。

===Original===
“I think so. Where are we?”

Thorvald’s lips stretched across his teeth in what was more a grimace than a smile. “Right off the map, any map I know. Take a look.”

They were on a scrap of beach﻿—beach which was more like a reef, for it lacked any covering comparable to sand except for some cupfuls of coarse gravel locked in rock depressions. Rocks, red as the rust of dried blood, rose in fantastic water-sculptured shapes around the small semi-level space they had somehow won.
===Chinese===
“我想没事。咱们这是在哪儿？”

托尔瓦德的嘴唇朝牙齿上一咧，与其说是笑，不如说是龇牙。“地图上根本没有的地方——我知道的任何地图上都没有。你自己瞧吧。”

他们落在巴掌大的一片滩上——说是滩，其实更像一片礁石，上面除了嵌在岩坑里的几捧粗砾，连一点近似沙子的铺盖也没有。四周耸起一圈岩石，红得像干涸的血锈，被水雕琢出光怪陆离的形状，围着他们不知怎么挣下来的这块半平不整的小小空地。

===Original===
This space was V-shaped, washed by equal streams on either side of the prong of rock by water which spouted from the face of a sheer cliff not too far away, with force enough to spray several feet beyond its exit point. Shann seeing that and guessing at its significance, drew a deep breath, and heard the ghost of an answering chuckle from his companion.

“Yes, that’s where we came out, boy. Like to make a return trip?”

Shann shook his head, and then wished that he had not so rashly made that move, for the world swung in a dizzy whirl. Things had happened too fast. For the moment it was enough that they were out of the underground ways, back under the amber sky, feeling the bite of Warlock’s sun.
===Chinese===
这块空地呈 V 字形，岩岬两侧各被一股同样的水流冲刷着——水从不算太远的一道陡直崖壁的壁面上喷出，力道之猛，水花溅出喷口好几英尺远。沙恩看明白了这一点，又猜到了其中的含义，深深吸了一口气，随即听见身旁的同伴回以一声若有若无的轻笑。

“没错，咱们就是从那儿出来的，小子。想不想再来一趟？”

沙恩摇了摇头，随即就后悔不该这么孟浪——整个世界顿时打起晕眩的旋子。事情来得太快了。眼下光是想到他们已脱出地下的通道、重新回到琥珀色的天空底下、重新觉出沃洛克太阳灼人的咬啮，就已经够受用了。

===Original===
Steadying his head with both hands, Shann turned slowly, to survey what might lie at their backs. The water, pouring by on either side, suggested that they were again on an island. Warlock, he thought gloomily, seemed to be for Terrans a succession of islands, all hard to escape.

The tangle of rocks did not encourage any exploration. Just gazing at them added to his weariness. They rose, tier by tier, to a ragged crown against the sky. Shann continued to sit staring at them.
===Chinese===
沙恩双手捧住脑袋把它稳住，缓缓转身，打量他们背后可能有些什么。两侧奔泻而过的水表明，他们又落在一座岛上了。沃洛克啊，他悻悻地想，对地球人来说似乎注定是一连串的岛，座座都难以脱身。

那片乱石堆根本鼓不起人去探路的兴致。光是这样望着，他的疲乏便又添了几分。岩石一层高过一层，直垒到天际，顶着参差的冠。沙恩依旧坐着，怔怔地盯住它们。

===Original===
“To climb that﻿ ﻿…” His voice trailed into the silence of complete discouragement.

“You climb﻿—or swim,” Thorvald stated. But, Shann noted, the Survey officer was not in a hurry to make either move.

Nowhere in that wilderness of rock was there the least relieving bit of purple foliage. Nor did any clak-claks or leather-headed birds tour the sky over their heads. Shann’s thirst might have been partially assuaged, but his hunger remained. And it was that need which forced him at last into action. The barren heights promised nothing in the way of food, but remembering the harvest the wolverines had taken from under the rocks along the river, he got to his feet and lurched out on the reef which had been their salvation, hunting some pool which might hold an edible captive or two.
===Chinese===
“攀那玩意儿……”他的声音渐渐低下去，没入彻底泄气的沉默里。

“要么攀——要么游。”托尔瓦德说得干脆。可沙恩注意到，勘测队军官自己两样都并不急着干。

那片岩石的荒野里，连一丝能叫人眼前一亮的紫色枝叶也没有。头顶的天上，也不见克拉克鸟或皮头鸟巡游。沙恩的渴或许解了一半，饿却原封不动地留着。正是这份需要最后逼他动了身。荒瘠的高处在吃食上半点指望也没有，可他想起狼獾们当年在河边石头底下捞到的那份收成，便站起身，踉跄着走上那片救了他们性命的礁石，寻摸哪个水洼里兴许圈着一两个能进口的俘虏。

===Original===
So it was that Shann made the discovery of a possible path consisting of a ledge running toward the other end of the island, if this were an island where they had taken refuge. The spray of the water drenched that way, feeding small pools in the uneven surface, and strips of yellow weed trailed in slimy ribbons back below the surface of the waves.

He called to Thorvald and gestured to his find. And then, close together, linking hands when the going became hazardous, the men followed the path. Twice they made finds in the pools, finned or clawed grotesque creatures, which they killed and ate, wolfing down the few fragments of odd-tasting flesh. Then, in a small crevice, which could hardly be dignified by the designation of “cave,” Thorvald chanced upon a quite exciting discovery﻿—a clutch of four greenish eggs, each as large as his doubled fist.
===Chinese===
就这样，沙恩发现了一条似乎可行的路——一道石台，朝小岛的另一头延伸过去，倘若他们避难的这块地方算得上岛的话。水花把那条道淋得透湿，在不平整的台面上积出一个个小水洼，一条条黄色的海草像滑腻的缎带，曳在浪面之下。

他唤来托尔瓦德，朝自己的发现比划了一下。随后两人紧紧相随，遇着险处便手挽手，沿那条道前行。他们在水洼里两番有所斩获——些个长鳍的或带爪的怪模怪样的生灵——宰了便吃，狼吞虎咽咽下那几口味道古怪的肉。随后，在一条当不起“洞窟”二字的小石缝里，托尔瓦德撞上一桩相当叫人振奋的发现——一窝四枚泛绿的蛋，每一枚都有他攥起的拳头两个那么大。

===Original===
Their outer covering was more like tough membrane than true shell, and the Terrans worried it open with difficulty. Shann shut his eyes, trying not to think of what he mouthed as he sucked his share dry. At least that semiliquid stayed put in his middle, though he expected disastrous results from the experiment.

More than a little heartened by this piece of luck, they kept on, though the ledge changed from a reasonably level surface to a series of rising, unequal steps, drawing them away from the water. At long last they came to the end of that path. Shann leaned back against a convenient spur of rock.
===Chinese===
蛋的外壳与其说是真正的蛋壳，不如说更像一层坚韧的膜，两个地球人费了好大的劲才把它撕开。沙恩闭起眼睛，一面吮干自己那一份，一面竭力不去想嘴里含的是什么。至少那半流质的东西在他肚里安安分分待住了，尽管他原指望这场实验准会闹出什么灾祸性的结果来。

这一下运气叫他们大为振奋，两人接着前行，尽管那道石台从还算平整的路面渐渐变成一连串步步上升、高高低低的台阶，把他们带离水边。好容易，他们走到了那条道的尽头。沙恩往后一靠，正靠上一处现成的岩角。

===Original===
“Company!” he alerted Thorvald.

The Survey officer joined him to share an outcrop of rock from which they were provided with an excellent view of the scene below, and it was a scene to hold their full attention.
===Chinese===
“有客！”他向托尔瓦德示警。

勘测队军官凑到他身边，同他分享一处凸岩；从那里，脚下的景象尽收眼底——而那景象，牢牢攫住了他们全部的注意力。

===Original===
That soft sweep of sand which had floored the cavern of the fog lay here also, a gray-blue carpet sloping gently out of the sea. For Shann had no doubt that the wide stretch of water before them was the western ocean. Walling the beach on either side, and extending well out into the water so that the farthest piles were awash except for their crowns, were pillars of stone, shaped with the same finish as that slab which had provided them a ladder of escape. And because of the regularity of their spacing, Shann did not believe them works of nature.

Grouped between them now were the players of the drama. One of the Warlockian witches, her gem body patterns glittering in the sunlight, was walking backward out of the sea, her hands held palms together, breast high, in a Terran attitude of prayer. And following her something swam in the water, clearly not another of her own species. But her actions suggested that by some invisible means she was drawing that water dweller after her. Waiting on shore were two others of her kind, viewing her actions with close attention, the attention of scholars for an instructor.
===Chinese===
铺在雾之洞窟底上的那层绵软沙地，这里也有，一张灰蓝色的地毯，从海里缓缓斜升上来。沙恩毫不怀疑，眼前这片宽阔的水面就是西边的大洋。海滩两侧各立着一列石柱，一路远远探进水里，最远的那几根只余柱冠露在水面之上；石柱修凿得同那块给他们当了逃生天梯的石板一般齐整。而正因为它们排列得如此规整，沙恩不信这是天然的作品。

好戏的角色此刻正聚在石柱之间。一名沃洛克女巫正倒退着走出海来，她身上宝石般的纹饰在日光下粼粼发亮；双手合十举在胸前，摆出一副地球人祈祷的姿势。她身后的水里游着个东西，显然不是她的同类。但她的举止表明，她正凭某种看不见的手段，把那个水中的居民一路牵引过来。岸上还候着她的两个同类，密切注视着她的一举一动——那是学生注视教师的专注。

===Original===
“Wyverns!”

Shann looked inquiringly at his companion. Thorvald added a whisper of explanation. “A legend of Terra﻿—they were supposed to have a snake’s tail instead of hind legs, but the heads﻿ ﻿… They’re Wyverns!”
===Chinese===
“翼蜥族！”

沙恩疑问地望向同伴。托尔瓦德压低嗓门补了一句解释。“地球的一则古老传说——据说它们不长后腿，只拖着一条蛇尾，可那些脑袋……她们正是翼蜥族！”

===Original===
Wyverns. Shann liked the sound of that word; to his mind it well fitted the Warlockian witches. And the one they were watching in action continued her steady backward retreat, rolling her bemused captive out of the water. What emerged into the blaze of sunlight was one of those fork-tailed sea dwellers such as the Terrans had seen die after the storm. The thing crawled out of the shallows, its eyes focused in a blind stare on the praying hands of the Wyvern.

She halted, well up on the sand, when the body of her victim or prisoner﻿—Shann was certain that the fork-tail was one or the other﻿—was completely out of the water. Then, with lightning speed, she dropped her hands.
===Chinese===
翼蜥族。沙恩喜欢这个词的音色；在他听来，它配沃洛克女巫正相宜。他们注视着的这一位仍在施展手段，继续稳稳地倒退，把她那神魂迷乱的俘虏从水里滚带出来。跃入烈日光焰里的，正是那样一种叉尾的海中居民——同地球人见过风暴过后死去的那几条一路货色。那东西爬出浅水，一双眼睛直勾勾地、茫然死盯着翼蜥族那双祈祷的手。

等她的猎物——或者说囚徒，沙恩确信叉尾兽二者必居其一——整个儿离了水，她便停住脚，稳稳站在沙地高处。随即，快如闪电，她垂下了双手。

===Original===
Instantly fork-tail came to life. Fanged jaws snapped. Aroused, the beast was the incarnation of evil rage, a rage which had a measure of intelligence to direct it into deadly action. And facing it, seemingly unarmed and defenseless, were the slender, fragile Wyverns.

Yet none of the small group of natives made any attempt to escape. Shann thought them suicidal in their indifference as fork-tail, short legs sending the fine sand flying in a dust cloud, made a rush toward its enemies.
===Chinese===
叉尾兽霎时活了过来。带獠牙的巨口咔地一咬。惊醒的巨兽成了暴怒的化身，而那暴怒里又掺着几分智性，足以把它引向致命的行动。同它对峙的，是那些看不出带兵刃、也看不出有防身的纤细脆弱的翼蜥族。

可这一小群土著谁也没有半点逃走的意思。沙恩只觉得她们这份无动于衷简直是自寻死路——叉尾兽四条短腿把细沙踢得漫天扬尘，朝它的敌人们猛冲过去。

===Original===
The Wyvern who had led the beast ashore did not move. But one of her companions swung up a hand, as if negligently waving the monster to a stop. Between her first two digits was a disk. Thorvald caught at Shann’s arm.

“See that! It’s a copy of the one I had; it must be!”
===Chinese===
引兽上岸的那位翼蜥族一动不动。倒是她的一个同伴扬起一只手，仿佛漫不经心地挥示那怪物止步。她前两根指头之间，夹着一只圆盘。托尔瓦德一把抓住沙恩的手臂。

“看见没有！那是我那块的复制品，准没错！”

===Original===
They were too far away to be sure it was a duplicate, but it was coin-shaped and bone-white. And now the Wyvern swung it back and forth in a metronome sweep. Fork-tail skidded to a stop, its head beginning﻿—reluctantly at first, and then, with increasing speed﻿—to echo that left-right sweep. This Wyvern had the sea beast under control, even as her companion had earlier held it.

Chance dictated what happened next. As had her sister charmer, the Wyvern began a backward withdrawal up the length of the beach, drawing the sea thing in her wake. They were very close to the foot of the drop above which the Terrans stood, fascinated, when the sand betrayed the witch. Her foot slipped into a hole and she was thrown backward, her control disk spinning out of her fingers.
===Chinese===
隔得这样远，没人敢断定那真是同一款，但它是硬币形状、骨头白色。此刻那翼蜥族把它像节拍器一样左右摆动。叉尾兽一个急停刹住，脑袋开始——起初老大不情愿，随后越来越快——应和着那左一摆右一摆。这位翼蜥族把海中巨兽制得服服帖帖，正像先前她同伴拿住它时一样。

接下来发生的事，全由偶然摆布。同她那位驭兽的姊妹先前一样，这位翼蜥族开始沿沙滩一路倒退，把那海中怪物牵在身后。她们已退到地球人脚下那道陡坎的坎脚近旁——两人正看得目眩神驰——就在这时，沙地出卖了女巫。她一脚踩进一个坑洞，整个人向后跌去，那只控制圆盘从指间旋飞了出去。

===Original===
At once the monster she had charmed shot forth its head, snapped at that spinning trifle﻿—and swallowed it. Then the fork-tail hunched in a posture Shann had seen the wolverines use when they were about to spring. The weaponless Wyvern was the prey, and both her companions were too far away to interfere.

Why he moved he could not have explained. There was no reason for him to go to the aid of the Warlockian, one of the same breed who had ruled him against his will. But Shann sprang, landing in the sand on his hands and knees.
===Chinese===
被她魅住的那头怪物立刻探出脑袋，一口咬住那旋转的小玩意——吞了下去。随即叉尾兽弓起身子，摆出一副沙恩见狼獾们蓄势欲扑时的架势。失了兵刃的翼蜥族成了猎物，而她的两个同伴都隔得太远，来不及插手。

他自己也没法解释为什么要动。他没有任何理由去援救那个沃洛克人——那正是曾违逆他的意愿统治过他的同族。可沙恩还是纵身跃了下去，手脚着地落在沙地上。

===Original===
The sea thing whipped around, undecided between two possible victims. Shann had his knife free, was on his feet, his eyes on the beast’s, knowing that he had appointed himself dragon slayer for no good reason.
===Chinese===
那海中怪物唰地转过身来，在两个可能的猎物之间拿不定主意。沙恩已拔刀在手，站直了身子，眼睛盯住巨兽的眼睛，心里明白：自己已无缘无故自封起了屠龙者。

## XV Dragon Slayer / XV 屠龙者

===Original===
“Ayeeee!” Sheer defiance, not only of the beast he fronted, but of the Wyverns as well, brought that old rallying cry to his lips﻿—the call used on the Dumps of Tyr to summon gang aid against outsiders. Fork-tail had crouched again for a spring, but that throat-crackling blast appeared to startle it.

Shann, blade ready, took a dancing step to the right. The thing was scaled, perhaps as well armored against frontal attack as was the shell-creature he had fought with the aid of the wolverines. He wished he had the Terran animals now﻿—with Taggi and his mate to tease and feint about the monster, as they had done with the Throg hound﻿—for he would have a better chance. If only the animals were here!

===Chinese===
“啊咿——！”纯然是挑衅——既冲着他面前的那头巨兽，也冲着翼蜥族——把那声古老的集结呐喊送上了他的唇边；那是在提尔的垃圾场上，召唤帮伙同援、共御外来者时用的号子。叉尾兽又蹲伏下去蓄势欲扑，可那一声劈裂了嗓子的呼喝，似乎惊了它一跳。

沙恩持刀待战，朝右侧滑出一步轻快的舞步。这东西周身覆鳞，正面强攻，其装甲之坚也许不亚于他当初靠狼獾相助斗过的那头壳兽。他多希望此刻那两只地球动物就在手边——有塔吉和它的伴侣围着怪物挑逗佯攻，就像它们对付斯罗格“猎犬”那样——他的胜算就会大得多。要是动物们在这儿就好了！

===Original===
Those eyes﻿—red-pitted eyes in a gargoyle head following his every movement﻿—perhaps those were the only vulnerable points.

Muscles tensed beneath that scaled hide. The Terran readied himself for a sidewise leap, his knife hand raised to rake at those eyes. A brown shape with a V of lighter fur banding its back crossed the far range of Shann’s vision. He could not believe what he saw, not even when a snarling animal, slavering with rage, came at a lumbering gallop to stand beside him, a second animal on its heels.

===Chinese===
那双眼睛——嵌在一颗石像鬼般的头颅里、一窝红坑似的眼睛，追踪着他的一举一动——也许那是仅有的几处破绽。

鳞甲之下，肌肉绷紧了。地球人蓄势待发，准备向侧面跃出，握刀的手高高扬起，要去抓那双眼睛。一个褐色的身影——背上横着一道V形的浅色毛纹——掠过沙恩视野的远端。他不敢相信自己的眼睛；哪怕一头咆哮不休、怒涎横飞的野兽迈着笨重的大步奔到他身旁站定，第二头紧随其后，他仍旧不敢信。

===Original===
Uttering his own battle cry, Taggi attacked. The fork-tail’s head swung, imitating the movements of the wolverine as it had earlier mimicked the swaying of the disk in the Wyvern’s hand. Togi came in from the other side. They might have been hounds keeping a bull in play. And never had they shown such perfect team work, almost as if they could sense what Shann desired of them.

That forked tail lashed viciously, a formidable weapon. Bone, muscles, scaled flesh, half buried in the sand, swept up a cloud of grit into the face of the man and the animals. Shann fell back, pawing with his free hand at his eyes. The wolverines circled warily, trying for the attack they favored﻿—the spring to the shoulders, the usually fatal assault on the spine behind the neck. But the armored head of the fork-tail, slung low, warned them off. Again the tail lashed, and this time Taggi was caught and hurled across the beach.

===Chinese===
塔吉发出它自己的战吼，扑了上去。叉尾兽的头颅随之摆动，模仿起狼獾的动作，正如它方才模仿翼蜥族手中圆盘的摇曳一般。托吉从另一侧包抄上来。它们活像一对逗弄公牛的猎犬。而它们配合之默契，向来不曾这般天衣无缝，简直像是能察觉沙恩想让它们做什么。

那条分叉的尾巴凶狠地横扫过来，是一件了不得的兵器。骨、肉、鳞甲，半埋在沙里，一扫便卷起漫天砂砾，扑向人和兽的脸。沙恩连连后退，用空着的那只手直抹眼睛。狼獾们小心翼翼地兜着圈子，寻机施展它们惯用的一手——跃上肩头，扑咬颈后脊椎，那一口往往致命。可叉尾兽压低了那颗装甲的头颅，逼得它们不敢近前。尾巴再度横扫，这一回塔吉被扫个正着，整个儿抛过了海滩。

===Original===
Togi uttered a challenge, made a reckless dash, and raked down the length of the fork-tail’s body, fastening on that tail, weighing it to earth with her own poundage while the sea creature fought to dislodge her. Shann, his eyes watering from the sand, but able to see, watched that battle for a long second, judging that fork-tail was completely engaged in trying to free its best weapon from the grip of the wolverine. The latter clawed and bit with a fury which suggested Togi intended to immobilize that weapon by tearing it to shreds.

Fork-tail wrenched its body, striving to reach its tormentor with fangs or clawed feet. And in that struggle to achieve an impossible position, its head slued far about, uncovering the unprotected area behind the skull base which usually lay under the spiny collar about its shoulders.

===Chinese===
托吉发出一声宣战的挑衅，不管不顾地猛冲过去，顺着叉尾兽的身子一路抓挠到尾端，一口咬住那条尾巴，拿自己的体重把它往地上坠，海兽则拼命要甩脱她。沙恩叫砂子迷得直流眼泪，却还看得见。他久久注视着这场搏斗，判定叉尾兽已把全副心思扑在一件事上：把最好的兵器从狼獾口中解救出来。而后者那股疯狂抓咬的劲头表明，托吉是打算把那件兵器撕成碎条，叫它彻底动弹不得。

叉尾兽拼命拧动身躯，想用尖牙或利爪够到折磨它的仇家。而在这一番强扭成不可能姿势的挣扎里，它的头颅拧转过了头，暴露出颅基后侧那片没有遮护的所在——平日里，那里一直掩在肩周那圈棘领底下。

===Original===
Shann went in. With one hand he gripped the edge of that collar﻿—its serrations tearing his flesh﻿—and at the same time he drove his knife blade deep into the soft underfolds, ripping on toward the spinal column. The blade nicked against bone as the fork-tail’s head slammed back, catching Shann’s hand and knife together in a trap. The Terran was jerked from his feet, and flung to one side with the force of the beast’s reaction.

Blood spurted up, his own blood mingled with that of the monster. Only Togi’s riding of the tail prevented Shann’s being beaten to death. The armored snout pointed skyward as the creature ground the sharp edge of its collar down on the Terran’s arm. Shann, frantic with pain, drove his free fist into one of those eyes.

===Chinese===
沙恩欺身而入。他一手扣住那圈棘领的边缘——领上的锯齿撕开他的皮肉——与此同时，刀刃深深刺入底下柔软的褶肉，一路豁向脊椎。叉尾兽的头颅猛地砸了回来，刀锋磕在骨头上，把沙恩的手连同刀一起关进了这具陷阱。地球人被掀离双脚，随着巨兽反扑的力道甩向一边。

血喷涌上来，他自己的血混着怪物的血。多亏托吉死死骑住那条尾巴，沙恩才没被活活打死。那具装甲的口鼻直指天空，怪物把棘领锋利的棱缘往地球人的臂上碾压。沙恩痛得发了狂，抡起空着的那只拳头，狠狠捣进那双眼睛中的一只。

===Original===
Fork-tail jerked convulsively; its head snapped down again and Shann was free. The Terran threw himself back, keeping his feet with an effort. Fork-tail was writhing, churning up the sand in a cloud. But it could not rid itself of the knife Shann had planted with all his strength, and which the blows of its own armored collar were now driving deeper and deeper into its back.

It howled thinly, with an abnormal shrilling. Shann, nursing his bleeding forearm against his chest, rolled free from the waves of sand it threw about, bringing up against one of the rock pillars. With that to steady him, he somehow found his feet, and stood weaving, trying to see through the rain of dust.

===Chinese===
叉尾兽痉挛般猛地一挣；头颅再度砸落，沙恩脱身了。地球人向后纵开，好歹站稳了脚。叉尾兽翻滚扭动，搅得沙尘腾起如云。可它甩不脱那把沙恩拼尽全力钉进去的刀，而它自己装甲棘领的一次次撞击，正把那刀越撞越深地楔进它的背脊。

它嗥出一线又细又尖、反常至极的长音。沙恩把流血的前臂拢在胸前护着，从巨兽掀起的沙浪里翻滚脱身，撞上其中一根石柱才收住。他撑着石柱，好歹站起身来，摇摇晃晃地立着，竭力想穿透那阵尘雨看清东西。

===Original===
The convulsions which churned up that concealing cloud were growing more feeble. Then Shann heard the triumphant squall from Togi, saw her brown body still on the torn tail just above the forking. The wolverine used her claws to hitch her way up the spine of the sea monster, heading for the mountain of blood spouting from behind the head. Fork-tail fought to raise that head once more; then the massive jaw thudded into the sand, teeth snapping fruitlessly as a flood of grit overrode the tongue, packed into the gaping mouth.

How long had it taken﻿—that frenzy of battle on the bloodstained beach? Shann could have set no limit in clock-ruled time. He pressed his wounded arm tighter to him, lurched past the still twitching sea thing to that splotch of brown fur on the sand, shaping the wolverine’s whistle with dry lips. Togi was still busy with the kill, but Taggi lay where that murderous tail had thrown him.

===Chinese===
搅起这片遮人耳目之尘云的痉挛，越来越微弱了。随后沙恩听见托吉得意的尖叫，看见她那褐色的身子还伏在尾巴分叉上方那截撕裂的尾根上。狼獾用爪子勾挂，一寸寸攀上海兽的脊背，朝头颅后方那座喷涌的血山而去。叉尾兽还想再昂起那颗头；随即那副沉重的巨颌砰地砸进沙里，牙齿空自咬合——一股砂砾漫过它的舌头，塞满了那张大张的嘴。

这场鏖战狂热——血染海滩上的这一场——前后有多久？沙恩说不出个用钟表刻度丈量的界限。他把伤臂搂得更紧，踉跄着绕过那具还在抽搐的海中巨兽，走向沙地上那团褐色的毛影，干裂的嘴唇撮出唤狼獾的呼哨。托吉还在忙着对付猎获物，塔吉却躺在那条凶尾把它摔落的地方。

===Original===
Shann fell on his knees, as the beach around him developed a curious tendency to sway. He put his good hand to the ruffled back fur of the motionless wolverine.

“Taggi!”

===Chinese===
沙恩双膝一软跪了下去，只觉四周的海滩生出一种古怪的摇晃劲儿。他把完好的那只手，按在一动不动的狼獾那蓬乱竖起的背毛上。

“塔吉！”

===Original===
A slight quiver answered. Shann tried awkwardly to raise the animal’s head with his own hand. As far as he could see, there were no open wounds; but there might be broken bones, internal injuries he did not have the skill to heal.

“Taggi?” He called again gently, striving to bring that heavy head up on his knee.

“The furred one is not dead.”

===Chinese===
一阵轻微的颤动回答了他。沙恩笨拙地想用那只手托起兽头。就他眼力所及，没有破开的伤口；可也许有断骨，有他没本事医治的内伤。

“塔吉？”他又轻轻地唤了一声，竭力把那颗沉甸甸的脑袋挪上自己的膝头。

“这毛茸茸的没有死。”

===Original===
For a moment Shann was not aware that those words had formed in his mind, had not been heard by his ears. He looked up, eyes blazing at the Wyvern coming toward him in a graceful glide across the crimsoned sand. And in a space of heartbeats his thrust of anger cooled into a stubborn enmity.

“No thanks to you,” he said deliberately aloud. If the Wyvern witch wanted to understand him, let her make the effort; he did not try to touch her thoughts with his.

===Chinese===
有一瞬，沙恩没有察觉那些字句是在他心里成形的，并非耳朵听来的。他抬起头，目光灼灼，逼视着那位翼蜥族——她正优雅地滑行过染红的沙地，向他走来。而不过几次心跳的工夫，他那一腔迸发的怒火，便冷凝成一种固执的敌意。

“这可谢不着你。”他一字一顿，故意说出声来。那位翼蜥族女巫若想弄懂他的话，就让她费这个力气；他才不去用自己的念头碰她的念头。

===Original===
Taggi stirred again, and Shann glanced down quickly. The wolverine gasped, opened his eyes, shook his miniature bear head, scattering pellets of sand. He sniffed at a dollop of blood, the dark, alien blood, spattered on Shann’s breeches, and then his head came up with a reassuring alertness as he looked to where his mate was still worrying the now quiet fork-tail.

With an effort, Taggi got to his feet, Shann aiding him. The man ran his hand down over ribs, seeking any broken bones. Taggi growled a warning once when that examination brought pain in its wake, but Shann could detect no real damage. As might a cat, the wolverine must have met the shock of that whip-tail stroke relaxed enough to escape serious injury. Taggi had been knocked out, but now he was able to navigate again. He pulled free from Shann’s grip, lumbering across the sand to the kill.

===Chinese===
塔吉又动了动，沙恩赶紧低头。狼獾倒抽一口气，睁开眼睛，摇了摇那颗小熊似的脑袋，抖落一粒粒砂尘。它嗅了嗅溅在沙恩马裤上的一摊血——暗色的、异星的血——随后脑袋一扬，透出一股叫人放心的警醒，望向它的伴侣仍在撕弄那头已然安分的叉尾兽的方向。

塔吉费了一番力气才站起来，沙恩从旁搀扶。人的手掌顺着肋腹一路摸下去，探查有无断骨。查到痛处时，塔吉低吼了一声示警，但沙恩摸不出真正的损伤。狼獾想是像猫一样，挨那记鞭尾抽击时身子松得恰到好处，竟躲过了重伤。塔吉是被打晕了，可这会儿又能走动了。它从沙恩手里挣脱出来，迈着笨重的步子穿过沙地，朝猎获物走去。

===Original===
Someone else was crossing that strip of beach. Passing the Wyvern as if he did not see them, Thorvald came directly to Shann. A few seconds later he had the torn arm stretched across his own bent knee, examining the still bleeding hurt.

“That’s a nasty one,” he commented.

===Chinese===
还有人正穿过那片海滩。托尔瓦德从翼蜥族身边走过，仿佛没看见她们，径直来到沙恩跟前。几秒钟后，那条皮开肉绽的手臂已经摊上他弓起的膝头，他察看着那处仍在外渗的伤。

“这一下可够呛。”他评了一句。

===Original===
Shann heard the words and they made sense, but the instability of his surroundings was increasing, while Thorvald’s handling sent sharp stabs of pain up his arm and somehow into his head, where they ended in red bursts to cloud his sight.

Out of the reddish mist which had fogged most of the landscape there emerged a single object, a round white disk. And in Shann’s clouded mind a well-rooted apprehension stirred. He struck out with his one hand, and through luck connected. The disk flew out of sight. His vision cleared enough so he could sight the Wyvern who had been leaning over Thorvald’s shoulder centering her weird weapon on him. Making a great effort, Shann got out the words, words which he also shaped in his mind as he said them aloud: “You’re not taking me over﻿—again!”

===Chinese===
沙恩听见了这些话，也明白它们的意思，可四周景物的摇晃越来越厉害，托尔瓦德的摆弄又送来一阵阵刀扎似的剧痛，顺着手臂向上，不知怎么钻进脑袋，在那儿炸开一团团红雾，糊住他的视线。

在蒙住大半景物的淡红雾霭里，浮出了唯一一样东西——一枚白色的圆盘。沙恩昏沉的头脑里，一株根深蒂固的忧惧动了动。他抡起独臂挥出去，侥幸打了个正着。圆盘飞得没了影。他的视力恢复了几分，看清了那位翼蜥族——她方才正从托尔瓦德肩头探过身来，把那件古怪的武器对准他。沙恩费了老大劲，吐出一句话，一边说出声、一边也在脑子里把字句成形：“你们别想再摆布我——别想再来第二回！”

===Original===
There was no emotion to be read on that jewel-banded face or in her unblinking eyes. He caught at Thorvald, determined to get across his warning.

“Don’t let them use those disks on us!”

“I’ll do my best.”

===Chinese===
那张缀满珠光纹带的脸上、那双一眨不眨的眼睛里，都读不出半点情绪。他一把抓住托尔瓦德，打定主意要把警告传到他心里。

“别让她们拿那些圆盘对付我们！”

“我尽力。”

===Original===
Only the haze had taken Thorvald again. Did one of the Wyverns have a disk focused on them? Were they being pulled into one of those blank periods, to awaken as prisoners once more﻿—say, in the cavern of the veil? The Terran fought with every ounce of will power to escape unconsciousness, but he failed.

===Chinese===
只是，那片迷雾又把托尔瓦德吞了下去。是不是有哪个翼蜥族正拿圆盘对准了他们？他们是不是正被拖进又一段空白的时辰，好再度作为囚徒醒转——比方说，在幕之洞窟里？地球人使出每一分意志力对抗昏迷，却还是败下阵来。

===Original===
This time he did not awaken half-drowning in an underground stream or facing a green mist. And there was an ache in his arm which was somehow reassuring with the very insistence of pain. Before opening his eyes, his fingers crossed the smooth slick of a bandage there, went on to investigate by touch a sleep mat such as he had found in the cavern structure. Was he back in that web of rooms and corridors?

Shann delayed opening his eyes until a kind of shame drove him to it. He first saw an oval opening almost the length of his body as it was stretched only a foot of two below the sill of that window. And through its transparent surface came the golden light of the sun﻿—no green mist, no crystals mocking the stars.

===Chinese===
这一回，他醒来时既没有半淹在地下河里，也没有面对一片绿雾。手臂里有一阵疼痛，而那疼痛执拗得很，反倒叫人安心。睁开眼之前，他的手指先掠过臂上绷带那圈光滑的包覆，又继续摸下去，探到一张睡席——同他在洞窟那座建筑里见过的一样。他是不是又回到了那张由房间与甬道织成的网里？

沙恩迟迟不肯睁眼，直到一股近乎羞愧的心情逼他睁开。他最先看见一道椭圆形的开口，几乎有他伸直的身体那么长——他躺平的地方，离那道窗台只有一两英尺。透过那透明的窗面，洒进来金色的阳光——没有绿雾，也没有摹拟星辰的水晶。

===Original===
The room in which he lay was small with smooth walls, much like that in which he had been imprisoned on the island. And there were no other furnishings save the mat on which he rested. Over him was a light cover netted of fibers resembling yarn, with feathers knotted into it to provide a downy upper surface. His clothing was gone, but the single covering was too warm and he pushed it away from his shoulders and chest as he wriggled up to see the view beyond the window.

===Chinese===
他躺着的这间屋子很小，墙壁光滑，很像他被囚在岛上时的那一间。屋里除了他身下的睡席，再无别的陈设。他身上盖着一条轻罩，是纱线似的纤维结成的网，网上又结进一簇簇羽毛，织出一片绒乎乎的表面。他的衣裳不见了，可单是这一条罩着已嫌太暖，他撑起半身去看窗外景致时，便把它从肩头胸口推了下去。

===Original===
His torn arm came into full view. From wrist to elbow it was encased in an opaque skin sheath, unlike any bandage of his own world. Surely that had not come out of any Survey aid pack. Shann gazed toward the window, but beyond lay only a reach of sky. Except for a lemon cloud or two ruffled high above the horizon, nothing broke that soft amber curtain. He might be quartered in a tower well above ground level, which did not match his former experience with Wyvern accommodations.

===Chinese===
那条皮开肉绽的手臂整个儿露在眼前。从手腕到肘部，它封在一层不透光的皮鞘里，同他本星的任何绷带都不一样。那肯定不是从勘测队的急救包里取出来的。沙恩望向窗口，可窗外唯有一大片天空。除了地平线上高处被风揉皱的一两朵柠檬色的云，再没有什么能划破那道柔和的琥珀色天幕。他也许被安置在一座远高出地面的塔楼里——这与他先前领教过的翼蜥族住处可不相符。

===Original===
“Back with us again?” Thorvald, one hand lifting a door panel, came in. His ragged uniform was gone, and he wore only breeches of a sleek green material and his own scuffed-and-battered boots.

Shann settled back on the mat. “Where are we?”

===Chinese===
“又回到我们中间了？”托尔瓦德一手掀起门板，走了进来。他那身褴褛的制服不见了，只穿一条光滑的绿色料子的裤子，脚上还是他自己那双磨蹭碰损的旧靴。

沙恩在睡席上坐定。“我们这是在哪儿？”

===Original===
“I think you might term this the capital city,” Thorvald answered. “In relation to the mainland, we’re on an island well out to sea﻿—westward.”

“How did we get here?” That climb in the slab, the stream underground﻿ ﻿… Had it been an interior river running under the bed of the sea? But Shann was not prepared for the other’s reply.

“By wishing.”

===Chinese===
“我想你可以管这儿叫都城。”托尔瓦德答道，“以大陆为参照，我们在一座远出海面的岛上——朝西。”

“我们怎么到这儿来的？”攀那块石板，地下河那一趟……莫非那是一条在海底底下奔流的内河？可对方的回答，沙恩还是没料到。

“靠许愿。”

===Original===
“By *what*?”

Thorvald nodded, his expression serious. “They wished us here. Listen, Lantee, when you jumped down to mix it with that fork-tailed thing, did you wish you had the wolverines with you?”

===Chinese===
“靠*什么*？”

托尔瓦德点点头，神情认真。“她们把我们‘愿’到了这儿。听着，兰蒂，你跳下去跟那条叉尾家伙搅作一团的时候，是不是盼着狼獾就在你身边？”

===Original===
Shann thought back; his memories of what had occurred before that battle were none too clear. But, yes, he had wished Taggi and Togi present at that moment to distract the enraged beast.

“You mean I wished them?” The whole idea was probably a part of the Wyvern jargon of dreaming and he added, “Or did I just dream everything?” There was the bandage on his arm, the soreness under that bandage. But also there had been Logally’s lash brand back in the cavern, which had bitten into his flesh with the pain of a real blow.

===Chinese===
沙恩回想了一番；他对那场战斗之前的事，记忆已不甚清楚。可是——没错，他当时确实盼着塔吉和托吉就在跟前，好牵制那头暴怒的巨兽。

“你是说，是我把它们愿来的？”这整个念头，多半也是翼蜥族那套“梦”的行话的一部分，于是他添了一句：“要不，就是我把这一切都梦出来了？”臂上明明有绷带，绷带底下明明有酸痛。可当初在洞窟里，洛加利那一鞭的烙痕也实实在在，咬进他皮肉里的痛，不亚于真实的一击。

===Original===
“No, you weren’t dreaming. You happened to be tuned in one of those handy little gadgets our lady friends here use. And, so tuned in, your desire for the wolverines being pretty powerful just then, they came.”

Shann grimaced. This was unbelievable. Yet there were his meetings with Logally and Trav. How could anyone rationally explain them? And how had he, in the beginning, been jumped from the top of the cliff on the island of his marooning into the midst of an underground flood without any conscious memory of an intermediate journey?

===Chinese===
“不，你不是在做梦。你恰好调准了咱们这些女士朋友所用的一件趁手小玩意儿的频率。而这么一调准，你盼狼獾的念头当时又正强得很——它们就来了。”

沙恩做了个苦脸。这简直难以置信。可是，他同洛加利、同特拉夫的那几番相遇，又怎么解释？谁能理性地说明它们？还有，起初他怎么会从流落受困那座岛的崖顶，一跳便跳进地下洪水的正中央，中间那段路程，竟没有半点有意识的记忆？

===Original===
“How does it work?” he asked simply.

Thorvald laughed. “You tell me. They have these disks, one to a Wyvern, and they control forces with them. Back there on the beach we interrupted a class in such control; they were the novices learning their trade. We’ve stumbled on something here which can’t be defined or understood by any of our previous standards of comparison. It’s frankly magic, judged by our terms.”

===Chinese===
“这东西是怎么运作的？”他直截了当地问。

托尔瓦德笑了。“这你倒得给我讲讲。她们有这些圆盘，每个翼蜥族一枚，她们拿它驾驭种种力量。方才在海滩上，我们打断了人家一堂驾驭课；那是些还在学本行的学徒。我们在这儿撞见的东西，没法用我们先前的任何比较标准去界定、去理解。照我们的说法，这干脆就是魔法。”

===Original===
“Are we prisoners?” Shann wanted to know.

“Ask me something I’m sure of. I’ve been free to come and go within limits. No one’s exhibited any signs of hostility; most of them simply ignore me. I’ve had two interviews, via this mind-reading act of theirs, with their rulers, or elders, or chief sorceresses﻿—all three titles seem to apply. They ask questions, I answer as best I can, but sometimes we appear to have no common meeting ground. Then I ask some questions, they evade gracefully, or reply in a kind of unintelligible double-talk, and that’s as far as our communication has progressed so far.”

===Chinese===
“我们是囚徒吗？”沙恩想知道。

“问点我有把握的吧。我一直在限定的范围内来去自由。没人露出过半点敌意；她们多半干脆当我不存在。我已经借她们那套读心的把戏，同她们的统治者——或者长老，或者首席女巫——交谈过两次，这三个头衔好像全都适用。她们提问，我竭尽所能地答，可有时候我们似乎全无共通的交点。然后我也提些问题，她们要么优雅地回避，要么用一种不知所云的模棱话应付——咱们两家的交流，至今也就进展到这一步。”

===Original===
“Taggi and Togi?”

“Have a run of their own and as far as I can tell are better satisfied with life than I am. Oddly enough, they respond more quickly and more intelligently to orders. Perhaps this business of being shunted around by the disks has conditioned them in some way.”

===Chinese===
“塔吉和托吉呢？”

“自有它们撒欢的地方，而且据我看，它们对这日子的满意劲儿还在我之上。怪的是，它们对命令应得更快、更聪明。兴许是被圆盘搬来搬去的这档子事，以某种方式调教出了它们。”

===Original===
“What about these Wyverns? Are they all female?”

“No, but their tribal system is strictly matriarchal, which follows a pattern even Terra once knew: the fertile earth mother and her priestesses, who became the witches when the gods overruled the goddesses. The males are few in number and lack the power to activate the disks. In fact,” Thorvald laughed ruefully, “one gathers that in this civilization our opposite numbers have, more or less, the status of pets at the best, and necessary evils at the worst. Which put *us* at a disadvantage from the start.”

===Chinese===
“这些翼蜥族到底是怎么回事？她们全是雌性？”

“不，可她们的部族体系是严格的母系制，走的是连地球也曾经懂的路子：多产的大地之母和她的女祭司们——待到诸神压倒诸女神，她们便成了女巫。雄性数目寥寥，也没有驱动圆盘的能耐。说实在的，”托尔瓦德苦笑道，“听那意思，在这么个文明里，咱们男性的对等角色，往好里说近乎宠物，往坏里说算个少不了的祸害。这一条，可叫*我们*打从一开始就矮了一头。”

===Original===
“You think that they won’t take us seriously because we are males?”

“Might just work out that way. I’ve tried to get through to them about danger from the Throgs, telling them what it would mean to them to have the beetle-heads settle in here for good. They just brush aside the whole idea.”

===Chinese===
“你觉得她们就因为我们俩是雄性，便不会拿我们当真？”

“没准儿真就落成这样。我试着让她们明白斯罗格人的危险，跟她们讲，要是任凭那些甲虫脑袋在这儿永久扎下根来，对她们意味着什么。她们只把这一整套念头整个儿拨到一边。”

===Original===
“Can’t you argue that the Throgs are males, too? Or aren’t they?”

The Survey officer shook his head. “That’s a point no human can answer. We’ve been sparring with Throgs for years and there have been libraries of reports written about them and their behavior patterns, all of which add up to about two paragraphs of proven facts and hundreds of surmises beginning with the probable and skimming out into the wild fantastic. You can claim anything about a Throg and find a lot of very intelligent souls ready to believe you. But whether those beetle-heads squatting over on the mainland are able to answer to ‘he,’ ‘she,’ or ‘it,’ your solution is just as good as mine. We’ve always considered the ones we fight to be males, but they might just as possibly be amazons. Frankly, these Wyverns couldn’t care less either; at least that’s the impression they give.”

===Chinese===
“你不能跟她们争辩，说斯罗格人也是雄性？还是说他们不是？”

勘测队军官摇了摇头。“这一点，没有哪个人类答得上来。我们同斯罗格人过招已有多年，写下的关于他们及其行为模式的报告能堆满一座座图书馆，而这一切加起来，不过凑出大约两段经证实的事实，外加成百上千条猜想——从或然的起点，一路滑进狂野的奇谈。关于斯罗格人，随便你怎么说，都有一大群非常聪明的头脑乐意信你。可蹲在大陆那头的那帮甲虫脑袋，究竟该应‘他’、‘她’还是‘它’，你的答案同我的一样作数。我们一向把交手的那些当作雄性，但他们同样可能是一帮亚马逊女战士。老实说，这些翼蜥族对此也压根无所谓；至少她们给人的印象如此。”

===Original===
“But anyway,” Shann observed, “it hasn’t come to ‘we’re all girls together’ either.”

Thorvald laughed again. “Not so you can notice. We’re not the only unwilling visitor in the vicinity.”

===Chinese===
“可不管怎么说，”沙恩说，“她们也还没落到‘咱们全是姐妹’那一步。”

托尔瓦德又笑了。“眼下还瞧不出来。在这一带，来得不情愿的客人，可不是只有咱们。”

===Original===
Shann sat up. “A Throg?”

“A something. Non-Warlockian, or non-Wyvern. And perhaps trouble for us.”

“You haven’t seen this other?”

===Chinese===
沙恩坐起身。“斯罗格人？”

“是个‘什么’。非沃洛克本地，或者说非翼蜥族。而且说不定是咱们的麻烦。”

“你没见过这‘另一位’？”

===Original===
Thorvald sat down cross-legged. The amber light from the window made red-gold of his hair, added ruddiness to his less-gaunt features.

“No, I haven’t. As far as I can tell, the stranger’s not right here. I caught stray thought beams twice﻿—surprise expressed by newly arrived Wyverns who met me and apparently expected to be fronted by something quite physically different.”

===Chinese===
托尔瓦德盘腿坐下。窗口的琥珀色光把他的头发照成了红金色，也给他那张不再那么瘦削的脸添了几分红润。

“没，没见过。据我判断，那位陌生者不在近处。我两次捕捉到零散的思维波束——是新到的翼蜥族表达出的惊讶；她们撞见了我，显然满以为迎面该出现一个身量外形大不相同的东西。”

===Original===
“Another Terran scout?”

“No. I imagine that to the Wyverns we must look a lot alike. Just as we couldn’t tell one of them from her sister if their body patterns didn’t differ. Discovered one thing about those patterns﻿—the more intricate they run, the higher the ‘power,’ not of the immediate wearer, but of her ancestors. They’re marked when they qualify for their disk and presented with the rating of the greatest witch in their family line as an inducement to live up to those deeds and surpass them if possible. Quite a bit of logic to that. Given the right conditioning, such a system might even work in our service.”

===Chinese===
“又一个地球侦察员？”

“不是。我想，在翼蜥族眼里，咱们俩的模样准差不离。正像她们若没有体纹之别，咱们也分不出谁是谁的姐妹。我倒是摸出了那些体纹的一条门道——纹路越繁复，‘法力’越高，倒不是佩戴者本人的法力，而是她祖先的。她们考领圆盘的那天便绘上体纹，还领受自家血脉中最伟大的女巫的名号，以此激励她们不负那些功业，可能的话更加以超越。这里头颇有一番道理。调教得当的话，这样一套法子，说不定在咱们的队伍里也行得通。”

===Original===
That nugget of information was the stuff from which Survey reports were made. But at the moment the information concerning the other captive was of more value to Shann. He steadied his body against the wall with his good hand and got to his feet. Thorvald watched him.

“I take it you have visions of action. Tell me, Lantee, why *did* you take that header off the cliff to mix it with fork-tail?”

===Chinese===
这一星半点的信息金矿，正是勘测队报告赖以织成的素材。可此刻，对沙恩来说，关于另一个俘虏的消息更要紧。他用那只完好的手撑着墙站起来。托尔瓦德望着他。

“我猜你是起了大干一场的念头。告诉我，兰蒂，你*到底*为什么一头栽下悬崖，去跟叉尾兽搅作一团？”

===Original===
Shann wondered himself. He had no reason for that impulsive act. “I don’t know﻿—”

“Chivalry? Fair Wyvern in distress?” the other prodded. “Or did the back lash from one of those disks draw you in?”

===Chinese===
沙恩自己也纳闷。那阵冲动的举动，他给不出缘由。“我不知道——”

“骑士精神？落难的翼蜥族美人？”对方追问，“还是说，是哪枚圆盘的回波，把你卷了进去？”

===Original===
“I don’t know﻿—”

“And why did you use your knife instead of your stunner?”

===Chinese===
“我不知道——”

“还有，你为什么用刀，不用击晕枪？”

===Original===
Shann was startled. For the first time he realized that he had fronted the greatest native menace they had discovered on Warlock with the more primitive of his weapons. Why had he not tried the stunner on the beast? He had just never thought of it when he had taken that leap into the role of dragon slayer.

===Chinese===
沙恩怔住了。他头一回意识到：面对他们在沃洛克所发现的最厉害的土生威胁，他手里拿的，竟是两件武器里较原始的那一件。他怎么没对那头巨兽试试击晕枪？跳下去扮演屠龙者的那一刻，他压根就没想起它。

===Original===
“Not that it would have done you any good to try the ray; it has no effect on fork-tail.”

“You tried it?”

===Chinese===
“倒不是说试了那射线就能帮你；它对叉尾兽毫无效果。”

“你试过？”

===Original===
“Naturally. But you didn’t know that, or did you pick up that information earlier?”

“No,” answer Shann slowly. “No, I don’t know why I used the knife. The stunner would have been more natural.” Suddenly he shivered, and the face he turned to Thorvald was very sober.

===Chinese===
“自然。可你原先并不知道这一点——还是说，你早先就拾得了这条消息？”

“不，”沙恩缓缓答道。“不，我不知道自己为什么用刀。照理说，用击晕枪才更顺。”他突然打了个寒噤，转向托尔瓦德的那张脸，异常凝重。

===Original===
“How much do they control us?” he asked, his voice dropping to a half whisper as if the walls about them could pick up those words and relay them to other ears. “What can they do?”

===Chinese===
“她们在多大程度上控制着我们？”他问，声音低成了半个耳语，仿佛四周的墙壁能拾起这些字句，转发给别的耳朵。“她们能做什么？”

===Original===
“A good question.” Thorvald lost his light tone. “Yes, what can they feed into our minds without our knowing? Perhaps those disks are only window dressing, and they can work without them. A great deal will depend upon the impression we can make on these witches.” He began to smile again, more wryly. “The name we gave this planet is certainly a misnomer. A warlock is a male sorcerer, not a witch.”

===Chinese===
“问得好。”托尔瓦德收起了轻快的口吻。“是啊，她们能趁我们不知不觉，往咱们脑子里灌些什么？也许那些圆盘不过是装点门面，没有它们，她们照样使得动。往后的事，多半要看咱们能在这些女巫心里留下什么印象。”他又笑了，笑得更涩了些。“咱们给这颗行星起的名字，实在是名不副实。男巫（warlock）是男性的巫师，可不是女巫。”

===Original===
“And what are the chances of our becoming warlocks ourselves?”

Again Thorvald’s smile faded, but he gave a curt little nod to Shann as if approving that thought. “That is something we are going to look into, and now! If we have to convince some stubborn females, as well as fight Throgs, well”﻿—he shrugged﻿—“we’ll have a busy, busy, time.”

===Chinese===
“那咱们自己当上男巫的胜算，又有几何？”

托尔瓦德的笑容又一次淡了下去，但他朝沙恩利落地一点头，像是赞许这个念头。“这正是咱们要去查个明白的，马上就去！要是咱们不但得跟斯罗格人打，还得先说服几位固执的女性——唔，”他耸了耸肩，“那咱们可有的忙、有的忙了。”

## XVI Third Prisoner / XVI 第三个俘虏
===Original===
“Well, it works as good as new.” Shann held his hand and arm out into the full path of the sun. He had just stripped off the skin-case bandage, to show the raw seam of a half-healed scar, but as he flexed muscles, bent and twisted his arm, there was only a small residue of soreness left.

“Now what, or where?” he asked Thorvald with some eagerness. Several days’ imprisonment in this room had made him impatient for the outer world again. Like the officer, he now wore breeches of the green fabric, the only material known to the Wyverns, and his own badly worn boots. Oddly enough, the Terrans’ weapons, stunner and knife, had been left to them, a point which made them uneasy, since it suggested that the Wyverns believed they had nothing to fear from clumsy alien arms.

“Your guess is as good as mine,” Thorvald answered that double question. “But it is you they want to see; they insisted upon it, rather emphatically in fact.”

===Chinese===
“嗯，这条胳膊使起来跟新的一样。”沙恩把手臂伸进太阳直泻的光路里。他刚拆掉那层皮套似的绷带，露出半愈疤痕的嫩棱；可他屈伸肌肉、弯转扭动手臂时，残余的酸痛已所剩无几。

“接下来做什么，要么去哪儿？”他带着几分急切问托尔瓦德。在这间屋子里囚了好几天，他又对外面的世界心痒难耐起来。同那位军官一样，他如今穿着绿色织物做的裤子——那是翼蜥族所知的唯一衣料——脚上还是自己那双磨损得厉害的靴子。奇怪的是，两个地球人的武器，击晕枪和刀，竟被留在了他们身边。这一点令他们隐隐不安，因为它暗示着，翼蜥族相信自己丝毫不必惧怕外星人那点笨拙的武器。

“我猜的未必比你高明。”托尔瓦德回答了这句二合一的问话。“不过她们要见的是你；她们坚持如此，而且语气着实不容分说。”

===Original===
The Wyvern city existed as a series of cell-like hollows in the interior of a rock-walled island. Outside there had been no tampering with the natural rugged features of the escarpment, and within, the silence was almost complete. For all the Terrans could learn, the population of the stone-walled hive might have been several thousand, or just the handful that they had seen with their own eyes along the passages which had been declared open territory for them.

Shann half expected to find again a skull-walled chamber where witches tossed colored sticks to determine his future. But he came with Thorvald into an oval room in which most of the outer wall was a window. And seeing what lay framed in that, Shann halted, again uncertain as to whether he actually saw that, or whether he was willed into visualizing a scene by the choice of his hostesses.

They were lower now than the room in which he had nursed his wound, not far above water level. And this window faced the sea. Across a stretch of green water was his red-purple skull, the waves lapping its lower jaw, spreading their foam in between the gaping rock-fringe which formed its teeth. And from the eye hollows flapped the clak-claks of the sea coast, coming and going as if they carried to some imprisoned brain within that giant bone case messages from the outer world.

===Chinese===
翼蜥族的城，是一连串小室似的凹穴，开在一座石壁岛屿的内部。岛外的陡崖一任天然嶙峋，未见丝毫人工斧凿；岛内的寂静则近乎彻底。就两个地球人所能探知的而言，这座石壁蜂巢里的居民，可能多达数千，也可能就只有他们亲眼见过的那一小撮——在那些被宣布为他们可自由通行的廊道沿线上。

沙恩半以为又要走进一间骷髅砌壁的厅堂，看女巫们抛掷彩签来判定他的前程。可他随托尔瓦德进的是一间椭圆的屋子，外墙的大半是一整面窗。望见框在窗中的景象，沙恩顿住了脚，又一次拿不准：那究竟是他亲眼所见，还是女主人们循着自家的心意，让他“看见”的一幕。

这里的位置比他养伤的那间屋子低，离水面不远。这扇窗面朝大海。一片绿水之外，便是他那具红紫色的骷髅：浪涛舔舐着它的下颌，把泡沫泼溅进一格格豁张的岩缝——那便是它的牙齿。眼窝的空洞里，海边的克拉克鸟扑扇起落，来来去去，仿佛在给那巨大骨壳里某个遭囚的头脑，捎去外面世界的音信。

===Original===
“My dream﻿—” Shann said.

“Your dream.” Thorvald had not echoed that; the answer had come in his brain.

===Chinese===
“我的「梦」——”沙恩说。

“你的「梦」。”这话不是托尔瓦德的应和；那答案是径直来到他脑子里的。

===Original===
